and this does not depend on the choice of
a\in\Omega
.

Especially, for
f(x)\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{m}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})
we have

\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{-\lambda+\frac{n}{r}}\frac{\Gamma_{r}^{d}(\lambda)}{(2\pi\sqrt{-1})^{n}}\int_{a+\sqrt{-1}\mathfrak{n}^{+}}{\rm e}^{(z|w)_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}}f\big(w^{-\mathit{1}}\big)\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(w)^{-\lambda}{\rm d}w=\frac{1}{(\lambda)_{\mathbf{m},d}}f(z),

and comparing this with Corollary
2.10

\big\langle f,{\rm e}^{(\cdot|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda}=\frac{1}{(\lambda)_{\mathbf{m},d}}f(z),

we get the following.

Corollary 4.3
.

For
\operatorname{Re}\lambda>\frac{2n}{r}-1
,
f\in\mathcal{P}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})
,
z,a\in\Omega
we have

\big\langle f,{\rm e}^{(\cdot|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda}=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{-\lambda+\frac{n}{r}}\frac{\Gamma_{r}^{d}(\lambda)}{(2\pi\sqrt{-1})^{n}}\int_{a+\sqrt{-1}\mathfrak{n}^{+}}{\rm e}^{(z|w)_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}}f\big(w^{-\mathit{1}}\big)\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(w)^{-\lambda}{\rm d}w.

Proof of Theorem
4.1
.

Let
f_{1},f_{2}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})
. Then by using Corollary
4.3
twice, for
\operatorname{Re}\lambda>\frac{2n}{r}-1
,
z\in\Omega
we get

\displaystyle\big\langle f_{1}(x)f_{2}(x),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{-\lambda+\frac{n}{r}}\frac{\Gamma_{r}^{d}(\lambda)}{(2\pi\sqrt{-1})^{n}}\int_{a+\sqrt{-1}\mathfrak{n}^{+}}{\rm e}^{(z|w)_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}}f_{1}\big(w^{-\mathit{1}}\big)f_{2}\big(w^{-\mathit{1}}\big)\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(w)^{-\lambda}{\rm d}w

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{-\lambda+\frac{n}{r}}\frac{\Gamma_{r}^{d}(\lambda)}{(2\pi\sqrt{-1})^{n}}\int_{a+\sqrt{-1}\mathfrak{n}^{+}}{\rm e}^{(z|w)_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}}f_{1}^{\sharp(k)}(w)f_{2}\big(w^{-\mathit{1}}\big)\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(w)^{-\lambda-k}{\rm d}w

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{-\lambda+\frac{n}{r}}f_{1}^{\sharp(k)}\bigg(\frac{\partial}{\partial z}\bigg)\frac{\Gamma_{r}^{d}(\lambda)}{(2\pi\sqrt{-1})^{n}}\int_{a+\sqrt{-1}\mathfrak{n}^{+}}{\rm e}^{(z|w)_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}}f_{2}\big(w^{-\mathit{1}}\big)\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(w)^{-\lambda-k}{\rm d}w

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{-\lambda+\frac{n}{r}}f_{1}^{\sharp(k)}\bigg(\frac{\partial}{\partial z}\bigg)\frac{\Gamma_{r}^{d}(\lambda)}{\Gamma_{r}^{d}(\lambda+k)}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{\lambda+k-\frac{n}{r}}\big\langle f_{2}(x),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda+k,x}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{1}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k}_{r},d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{-\lambda+\frac{n}{r}}f_{1}^{\sharp(k)}\bigg(\frac{\partial}{\partial z}\bigg)\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{\lambda+k-\frac{n}{r}}\big\langle f_{2}(x),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda+k,x}.\qed

∎

Next we consider an involution
\sigma
on
\mathfrak{p}^{+}
, and let
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1}:=(\mathfrak{p}^{+})^{\sigma}
,
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}:=(\mathfrak{p}^{+})^{-\sigma}
.
We take
\varepsilon_{2}\in\{1,2\}
such that

(x_{2}|\overline{y_{2}})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}=\varepsilon_{2}(x_{2}|\overline{y_{2}})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}},\qquad x_{2},y_{2}\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}

(4.2)

holds. In this section we assume that
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}
is also of tube type.
We take a maximal tripotent
e\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}
. Then
e
also becomes a maximal tripotent of
\mathfrak{p}^{+}
.
We consider the corresponding Jordan algebra structure on
\mathfrak{p}^{+}
,
let
\mathfrak{n}^{+}\subset\mathfrak{p}^{+}
,
\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}\subset\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}
be the Euclidean real forms,
and let
\Omega\subset\mathfrak{n}^{+}
,
\Omega_{2}\subset\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}
be the symmetric cones. Then we have

\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(x_{2})=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(x_{2})^{\varepsilon_{2}},\qquad x_{2}\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}.

In addition, if
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}
is a direct sum of two Jordan triple subsystems,
then we write
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}=:\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}\oplus\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}
,
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1}=:\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{12}
,
\operatorname{rank}\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}=:r^{\prime}
,
\operatorname{rank}\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}=:r^{\prime\prime}
.
In this case always
\varepsilon_{2}=1
holds, and
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}=\mathfrak{n}^{+\mathbb{C}}_{11}
and
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}=\mathfrak{n}^{+\mathbb{C}}_{22}
are also of tube type.
Now we want to compute

\big\langle F(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x},\qquad F(x_{2})\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\big)

for
\mathfrak{p}^{+}=\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1}\oplus\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}
case, and

\big\langle f_{1}(x_{11})f_{2}(x_{22}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x},\qquad f_{1}(x_{11})\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}),\quad f_{2}(x_{22})\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}\big)

for
\mathfrak{p}^{+}=\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}\oplus\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{12}\oplus\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}
case.
However, it seems difficult to compute these for general case.
From now on we assume
F(x_{2})=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(x_{2})^{k}f(x_{2})
,
f(x_{2})\in\mathcal{P}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2})\cap\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})
for the former case,
and
f_{1}(x_{11})=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}(x_{11})^{k}\in\mathcal{P}_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime}}}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}\big)
for the latter case.

Theorem 4.4
.

1.

For
\mathfrak{p}^{+}=\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1}\oplus\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}
case, let
\operatorname{Re}\lambda>\frac{2n}{r}-1
,
k\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
,
\mathbf{l}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}
,
and
f\in\mathcal{P}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\big)\cap\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})
. Then for
z=z_{1}+z_{2}\in\Omega\subset\mathfrak{n}^{+}\subset\mathfrak{p}^{+}
we have

\displaystyle\big\langle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(x_{2})^{k}f(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{1}{(\lambda)_{\underline{2k/\varepsilon_{2}}_{r}+\mathbf{l},d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{-\lambda+\frac{n}{r}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}\bigg(\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{2}}\frac{\partial}{\partial z_{2}}\bigg)^{k}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{\lambda+\frac{2k}{\varepsilon_{2}}-\frac{n}{r}}f(z_{2}).

2.

For
\mathfrak{p}^{+}=\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}\oplus\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{12}\oplus\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}
case, let
\operatorname{Re}\lambda>\frac{2n}{r}-1
,
k\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
,
\mathbf{l}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r^{\prime\prime}}
,
and
f\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}\big)
. Then for
z=z_{11}+z_{12}+z_{22}\in\Omega\subset\mathfrak{n}^{+}\subset\mathfrak{p}^{+}
we have

\displaystyle\big\langle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}(x_{11})^{k}f(x_{22}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{1}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k}_{r}+\mathbf{l},d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{-\lambda+\frac{n}{r}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}\bigg(\frac{\partial}{\partial z_{22}}\bigg)^{k}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{\lambda+k-\frac{n}{r}}f(z_{22}).

Here we normalize
\frac{\partial}{\partial z_{2}}
,
\frac{\partial}{\partial z_{22}}
with respect to the bilinear forms

(\cdot|\cdot)_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}\colon\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\times\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\to\mathbb{C}
,
(\cdot|\cdot)_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}\colon\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}\times\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}\to\mathbb{C}
,
and identify
\mathbf{l}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r^{\prime\prime}}
and
(l_{1},\dots,l_{r^{\prime\prime}},0,\dots,0)\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}
.
This theorem is regarded as an analogue of the Rodrigues formulas for Jacobi polynomials.
For such formulas for symmetry breaking operators in tensor product case, see also
[
4
]
.

Proof.

(1) Let

\operatorname{Proj}_{2}\colon\quad\mathfrak{p}^{+}=\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1}\oplus\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\longrightarrow\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2},\qquad(z_{1},z_{2})\longmapsto z_{2}

be the orthogonal projection, and for
k\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
,
\mathbf{l}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}
,
f\in\mathcal{P}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2})\cap\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})
, let

f_{1}(x):=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(x_{2})^{k}=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(\operatorname{Proj}_{2}(x))^{k},\qquad f_{2}(x):=f(x_{2})=f(\operatorname{Proj}_{2}(x)).

Then by Proposition
2.4
(1), for
x=x_{1}+x_{2}\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}=\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1}\oplus\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}
we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\operatorname{Proj}_{2}\big(x^{-\mathit{1}}\big)=\big(x_{2}-P(x_{1})x_{2}^{-\mathit{1}}\big)^{-\mathit{1}},\\
&\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(x)^{2}=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}\big(x_{2}-P(x_{1})x_{2}^{-\mathit{1}}\big)\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(x_{2}),\end{split}

and hence

\displaystyle f_{1}^{\sharp(2k/\varepsilon_{2})}(x)

\displaystyle=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(x)^{2k/\varepsilon_{2}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}\big(\operatorname{Proj}_{2}\big(x^{-\mathit{1}}\big)\big)^{k}

\displaystyle=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(x_{2})^{k/\varepsilon_{2}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}\big(x_{2}-P(x_{1})x_{2}^{-\mathit{1}}\big)^{k/\varepsilon_{2}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}\big(x_{2}-P(x_{1})x_{2}^{-\mathit{1}}\big)^{-k}

\displaystyle=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(x_{2})^{k}

holds. Also, by Corollary
2.10
we have

\big\langle f_{2}(x),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda+2k/\varepsilon_{2},x}=\big\langle f(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda+2k/\varepsilon_{2},x}=\frac{1}{\big(\lambda+\frac{2k}{\varepsilon_{2}}\big)_{\mathbf{l},d}}f(z_{2}).

Therefore by Theorem
4.1
we get

\displaystyle\big\langle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(x_{2})^{k}f(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{1}{(\lambda)_{\underline{2k/\varepsilon_{2}}_{r},d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{-\lambda+\frac{n}{r}}f_{1}^{\sharp(2k/\varepsilon_{2})}\bigg(\frac{\partial}{\partial z}\bigg)\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{\lambda+\frac{2k}{\varepsilon_{2}}-\frac{n}{r}}\big\langle f_{2}(x),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda+2k/\varepsilon_{2},x}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{1}{(\lambda)_{\underline{2k/\varepsilon_{2}}_{r},d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{-\lambda+\frac{n}{r}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}\bigg(\operatorname{Proj}_{2}\bigg(\frac{\partial}{\partial z}\bigg)\bigg)^{k}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{\lambda+\frac{2k}{\varepsilon_{2}}-\frac{n}{r}}\frac{1}{\big(\lambda+\frac{2k}{\varepsilon_{2}}\big)_{\mathbf{l},d}}f(z_{2})

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{1}{(\lambda)_{\underline{2k/\varepsilon_{2}}_{r}+\mathbf{l},d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{-\lambda+\frac{n}{r}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}\bigg(\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{2}}\frac{\partial}{\partial z_{2}}\bigg)^{k}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{\lambda+\frac{2k}{\varepsilon_{2}}-\frac{n}{r}}f(z_{2}).

(2) Let

\operatorname{Proj}_{ij}\colon\quad\mathfrak{p}^{+}=\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}\oplus\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{12}\oplus\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}\longrightarrow\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{ij},\qquad(z_{11},z_{12},z_{22})\longmapsto z_{ij}

be the orthogonal projection, and for
k\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
,
\mathbf{l}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r^{\prime\prime}}
,
f\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22})
, let

f_{1}(x):=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}(x_{11})^{k}=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}(\operatorname{Proj}_{11}(x))^{k},\qquad f_{2}(x):=f(x_{22})=f(\operatorname{Proj}_{22}(x)).

Then by Proposition
2.4
(2), for
x=x_{11}+x_{12}+x_{22}\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}\oplus\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{12}\oplus\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}
we have

\displaystyle\operatorname{Proj}_{11}\big(x^{-\mathit{1}}\big)=\big(x_{11}-P(x_{12})x_{22}^{-\mathit{1}}\big)^{-\mathit{1}},

\displaystyle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(x)=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}\big(x_{11}-P(x_{12})x_{22}^{-\mathit{1}}\big)\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}(x_{22}),

and hence

\displaystyle f_{1}^{\sharp(k)}(x)

\displaystyle=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(x)^{k}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}\big(\operatorname{Proj}_{11}\big(x^{-\mathit{1}}\big)\big)^{k}

\displaystyle=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}(x_{22})^{k}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}\big(x_{11}-P(x_{12})x_{22}^{-\mathit{1}}\big)^{k}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}\big(x_{11}-P(x_{12})x_{22}^{-\mathit{1}}\big)^{-k}

\displaystyle=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}(x_{22})^{k}

(4.3)

holds. Also, since
f_{2}(x)=f(x_{22})\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22})\subset\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})
holds, by Corollary
2.10
we have

\big\langle f_{2}(x),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda+k,x}=\big\langle f(x_{22}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda+k,x}=\frac{1}{(\lambda+k)_{\mathbf{l},d}}f(z_{22}).

Therefore by Theorem
4.1
we get

\displaystyle\big\langle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}(x_{11})^{k}f(x_{22}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}

\displaystyle{}=\frac{1}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k}_{r},d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{-\lambda+\frac{n}{r}}f_{1}^{\sharp(k)}\bigg(\frac{\partial}{\partial z}\bigg)\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{\lambda+k-\frac{n}{r}}\big\langle f_{2}(x),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda+k,x}

\displaystyle{}=\frac{1}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k}_{r},d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{-\lambda+\frac{n}{r}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}\bigg(\operatorname{Proj}_{22}\bigg(\frac{\partial}{\partial z}\bigg)\bigg)^{k}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{\lambda+k-\frac{n}{r}}\frac{1}{(\lambda+k)_{\mathbf{l},d}}f(z_{22})

\displaystyle{}=\frac{1}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k}_{r}+\mathbf{l},d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{-\lambda+\frac{n}{r}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}\bigg(\frac{\partial}{\partial z_{22}}\bigg)^{k}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{\lambda+k-\frac{n}{r}}f(z_{22}).

∎

5
Computation for non-simple
\boldsymbol{\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}}

In this section we treat
\mathfrak{p}^{+}=\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1}\oplus\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}
such that
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}
is non-simple,
and write
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}=:\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}\oplus\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}
,
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1}=:\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{12}
, so that

\displaystyle\big(\mathfrak{p}^{+},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{12},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}\big)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\begin{cases}\big(\mathbb{C}^{d+2},\mathbb{C},\mathbb{C}^{d},\mathbb{C}\big)&(\textit{Case }1),\\
\big({\rm Sym}(r,\mathbb{C}),\operatorname{Sym}(r^{\prime},\mathbb{C}),M(r^{\prime},r^{\prime\prime};\mathbb{C}),\operatorname{Sym}(r^{\prime\prime},\mathbb{C})\big)&(\textit{Case }2),\\
\big(M(q,s;\mathbb{C}),M(q^{\prime},s^{\prime};\mathbb{C}),M(q^{\prime},s^{\prime\prime};\mathbb{C})\oplus M(q^{\prime\prime},s^{\prime};\mathbb{C}),M(q^{\prime\prime},s^{\prime\prime};\mathbb{C})\big)&(\textit{Case }3),\\
\big({\rm Skew}(s,\mathbb{C}),\operatorname{Skew}(s^{\prime},\mathbb{C}),M(s^{\prime},s^{\prime\prime};\mathbb{C}),\operatorname{Skew}(s^{\prime\prime},\mathbb{C})\big)&(\textit{Case }4),\\
\big({\rm Herm}(3,\mathbb{O})^{\mathbb{C}},\mathbb{C},M(1,2;\mathbb{O})^{\mathbb{C}},\operatorname{Herm}(2,\mathbb{O})^{\mathbb{C}}\big)&(\textit{Case }5),\\
\big(M(1,2;\mathbb{O})^{\mathbb{C}},\mathbb{C},\operatorname{Skew}(5,\mathbb{C}),M(1,5;\mathbb{C})\big)&(\textit{Case }6)\end{cases}

with
r=r^{\prime}+r^{\prime\prime}
,
q=q^{\prime}+q^{\prime\prime}
,
s=s^{\prime}+s^{\prime\prime}
.
Then the corresponding symmetric pairs are

(G,G_{1})=\begin{cases}\big({\rm SO}_{0}(2,d+2),{\rm SO}_{0}(2,d)\times{\rm SO}(2)\big)&(\textit{Case }1),\\
\big({\rm Sp}(r,\mathbb{R}),{\rm U}(r^{\prime},r^{\prime\prime})\big)&(\textit{Case }2),\\
\big({\rm SU}(q,s),{\rm S}({\rm U}(q^{\prime},s^{\prime\prime})\times{\rm U}(q^{\prime\prime},s^{\prime}))\big)&(\textit{Case }3),\\
\big({\rm SO}^{*}(2s),{\rm U}(s^{\prime},s^{\prime\prime})\big)&(\textit{Case }4),\\
\big(E_{7(-25)},{\rm U}(1)\times E_{6(-14)}\big)&(\textit{Case }5),\\
\big(E_{6(-14)},{\rm U}(1)\times{\rm SO}^{*}(10)\big)&(\textit{Case }6)\end{cases}

(up to covering).
Let
\dim\mathfrak{p}^{+}=:n
,
\dim\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}=:n^{\prime}
,
\dim\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}=:n^{\prime\prime}
,
\operatorname{rank}\mathfrak{p}^{+}=:r
,
\operatorname{rank}\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}=:r^{\prime}
,
\operatorname{rank}\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}=:r^{\prime\prime}
,
and let
d
,
d^{\prime}
,
d^{\prime\prime}
be the numbers defined in (
2.3
) for
\mathfrak{p}^{+}
,
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}
,
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}
respectively.
Then the numbers
(r,r^{\prime},r^{\prime\prime},d)
are given by

(r,r^{\prime},r^{\prime\prime},d)=\begin{cases}(2,1,1,d)&(\textit{Case }1),\\
(r,r^{\prime},r^{\prime\prime},1)&(\textit{Case }2),\\
(\min\{q,s\},\min\{q^{\prime},s^{\prime}\},\min\{q^{\prime\prime},s^{\prime\prime}\},2)&(\textit{Case }3),\\
(\lfloor s/2\rfloor,\lfloor s^{\prime}/2\rfloor,\lfloor s^{\prime\prime}/2\rfloor,4)&(\textit{Case }4),\\
(3,1,2,8)&(\textit{Case }5),\\
(2,1,1,6)&(\textit{Case }6),\end{cases}

and we have
d=d^{\prime}=d^{\prime\prime}
if
r^{\prime},r^{\prime\prime}\neq 1
. Even when
r^{\prime}
or
r^{\prime\prime}=1
, since
d^{\prime}
,
d^{\prime\prime}
are not determined uniquely and any numbers are allowed,
we may assume
d=d^{\prime}=d^{\prime\prime}
.

5.1
Main theorem

In this section we assume
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}
is of tube type, that is, we assume
q^{\prime}=s^{\prime}=r^{\prime}
for
Case
3, and
s^{\prime}=2r^{\prime}
for
Case
4.
We fix a maximal tripotent
e^{\prime}\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}
, regard
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}
as a Jordan algebra,
and let
\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}
be the corresponding Euclidean real form of
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}
.

Theorem 5.1
.

Assume
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}
is of tube type.
Let
\operatorname{Re}\lambda>p-1
,
k\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
,
\mathbf{l}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r^{\prime\prime}}
, and
f\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22})
.
For
\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r^{\prime\prime}}
, we define the polynomials
\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{m}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22})\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{n}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22})
by

\sum_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r^{\prime\prime}}}\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}(x_{22},y_{22})=f(x_{22}+y_{22}).

(5.1)

We write
z=z_{11}+z_{12}+z_{22}\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}=\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}\oplus\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{12}\oplus\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}
.
Then we have

\displaystyle\big\langle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}(x_{11})^{k}f(x_{22}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}

\displaystyle=\frac{\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\big)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}},d}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k}_{r}+\mathbf{l},d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}(z_{11})^{k}\sum_{\mathbf{m}}\frac{(-k)_{\mathbf{m},d}}{\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\big)_{\mathbf{m},d}}\sum_{\mathbf{n}}\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}\big(Q(z_{12}){}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{11}^{-\mathit{1}}-z_{22},z_{22}\big)

\displaystyle=\frac{\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\big)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}},d}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k}_{r}+\mathbf{l},d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}(z_{11})^{k}\sum_{\mathbf{n}}\frac{\big(\lambda+k-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\big)_{\mathbf{n},d}}{\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\big)_{\mathbf{n},d}}\sum_{\mathbf{m}}\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}\big(Q(z_{12}){}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{11}^{-\mathit{1}},z_{22}-Q(z_{12}){}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{11}^{-\mathit{1}}\big)

\displaystyle=\frac{(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime})_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}},d}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k}_{r}+\mathbf{l},d}\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\big)_{\mathbf{l},d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}(z_{11})^{k}\sum_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}(-k)_{\mathbf{m},d}\bigg(\lambda+k-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\bigg)_{\mathbf{n},d}\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}\big(Q(z_{12}){}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{11}^{-\mathit{1}},z_{22}\big).

Here, since
f(x_{22}+y_{22})
is a homogeneous polynomial of degree
|\mathbf{l}|
,
\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}
is non-zero only if
|\mathbf{m}|+|\mathbf{n}|=|\mathbf{l}|
,
and these are finite sums. Moreover, if
r^{\prime}<r^{\prime\prime}
, then since the rank of
Q(z_{12}){}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{11}^{-\mathit{1}}
is at most
r^{\prime}
,

\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}\big(Q(z_{12}){}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{11}^{-\mathit{1}},*\big)
is non-zero only if
\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r^{\prime}}
.

To prove the theorem, first we assume
\mathfrak{p}^{+}
,
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}
are also of tube type, fix a maximal tripotent

e=e^{\prime}+e^{\prime\prime}\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}\oplus\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}\subset\mathfrak{p}^{+}
, regard
\mathfrak{p}^{+}
,
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}
,
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}
as Jordan algebras,
and let
\Omega\subset\mathfrak{n}^{+}\subset\mathfrak{p}^{+}
,
\Omega_{jj}\subset\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{jj}\subset\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{jj}
be the corresponding Euclidean real forms
and the symmetric cones.
Then by Theorem
4.4
(2) and Proposition
2.4
(2), for
z\in\Omega\subset\mathfrak{n}^{+}\subset\mathfrak{p}^{+}
we have

\displaystyle\big\langle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}(x_{11})^{k}f(x_{22}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{1}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k}_{r}+\mathbf{l},d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{-\lambda+\frac{n}{r}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}\bigg(\frac{\partial}{\partial z_{22}}\bigg)^{k}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{\lambda+k-\frac{n}{r}}f(z_{22})

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{1}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k}_{r}+\mathbf{l},d}}\big(\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}(z_{11})\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}(z_{22}-P(z_{12})z_{11}^{-\mathit{1}})\big)^{-\lambda+\frac{n}{r}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}\bigg(\frac{\partial}{\partial z_{22}}\bigg)^{k}

\displaystyle\qquad\hphantom{=}{}\ \times\big(\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}(z_{11})\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}\big(z_{22}-P(z_{12})z_{11}^{-\mathit{1}}\big)\big)^{\lambda+k-\frac{n}{r}}f(z_{22})

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{1}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k}_{r}+\mathbf{l},d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}(z_{11})^{k}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}\big(z_{22}-Q(z_{12}){}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{11}^{-\mathit{1}}\big)^{-\lambda+\frac{n}{r}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}\bigg(\frac{\partial}{\partial z_{22}}\bigg)^{k}

\displaystyle\qquad\hphantom{=}{}\ \times\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}\big(z_{22}-Q(z_{12}){}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{11}^{-\mathit{1}}\big)^{\lambda+k-\frac{n}{r}}f(z_{22}).

(5.2)

We note that since for
z=z_{11}+z_{12}+z_{22}\in\Omega
we have

\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}(z_{11})\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}\big(z_{22}-Q(z_{12}){}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{11}^{-\mathit{1}}\big)>0,

and since
\Omega
is connected,
z_{11}\in\Omega_{11}
,
z_{22}-Q(z_{12}){}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{11}^{-\mathit{1}}\in\Omega_{22}
hold.

Next we recall the
Riesz integral
from
[
11
, Theorem VII.2.2]
.
For a closed set
S\subset\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}
and for
k\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
let

C^{k}_{S}(\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}):=\big\{F\in C^{k}(\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22})\mid\operatorname{supp}F\subset S\big\}.

Let
\Omega_{22}^{cl}\subset\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}
be the closure of the symmetric cone
\Omega_{22}
.
When
S\cap\Omega^{cl}_{22}
is compact, for
F\in C^{0}_{S}(\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22})
the Riesz integral
R_{\lambda}
is defined by

R_{\lambda}(F):=\frac{1}{\Gamma_{r^{\prime\prime}}^{d}(\lambda)}\int_{\Omega_{22}}F(w_{22})\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}(w_{22})^{\lambda-\frac{n^{\prime\prime}}{r^{\prime\prime}}}{\rm d}w_{22},\qquad\operatorname{Re}\lambda>\frac{n^{\prime\prime}}{r^{\prime\prime}}-1,

where
\Gamma_{r^{\prime\prime}}^{d}(\lambda)
is as (
4.1
). Then if
F\in C^{lr^{\prime\prime}}_{S}(\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22})
,
k\leq l
, we have

R_{\lambda}(F)=R_{\lambda+k}\bigg(\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}\bigg({-}\frac{\partial}{\partial w_{22}}\bigg)^{k}F\bigg),

and this is analytically continued for
\operatorname{Re}\lambda>\frac{n^{\prime\prime}}{r^{\prime\prime}}-l-1
.
Moreover, since
R_{0}=\delta_{0}
, for
k\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
with
k<-\frac{n^{\prime\prime}}{r^{\prime\prime}}+l+1
we have

R_{-k}(F)=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}\bigg({-}\frac{\partial}{\partial w_{22}}\bigg)^{k}F(w_{22})\biggr|_{w_{22}=0}=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}\bigg(\frac{\partial}{\partial w_{22}}\bigg)^{k}F(-w_{22})\biggr|_{w_{22}=0}.

In the following we set
Q(z_{12}){}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{11}^{-\mathit{1}}=:a_{22}
.
Then for
z_{22},a_{22}\in\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}
with
z_{22}-a_{22}\in\Omega_{22}
, by putting

F(w_{22}):=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}(z_{22}-a_{22}-w_{22})^{\lambda+k-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}-\frac{n^{\prime\prime}}{r^{\prime\prime}}}f(z_{22}-w_{22})1_{z_{22}-a_{22}-\Omega_{22}}(w_{22})

in the above formula,

\displaystyle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}(z_{22}-a_{22})^{-\lambda+\frac{n}{r}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}\bigg(\frac{\partial}{\partial z_{22}}\bigg)^{k}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}(z_{22}-a_{22})^{\lambda+k-\frac{n}{r}}f(z_{22})

\displaystyle{}=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}(z_{22}-a_{22})^{-\lambda+\frac{n}{r}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}\bigg(\frac{\partial}{\partial w_{22}}\bigg)^{k}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}(z_{22}+w_{22}-a_{22})^{\lambda+k-\frac{n}{r}}f(z_{22}+w_{22})\biggr|_{w_{22}=0}

is given by the analytic continuation of

\displaystyle\frac{\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}(z_{22}-a_{22})^{-\lambda+\frac{n}{r}}}{\Gamma_{r^{\prime\prime}}^{d}(-k)}\int_{\Omega_{22}\cap(z_{22}-a_{22}-\Omega_{22})}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}(w_{22})^{-k-\frac{n^{\prime\prime}}{r^{\prime\prime}}}

\displaystyle\qquad\hphantom{=}{}\ \times\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}(z_{22}-a_{22}-w_{22})^{\lambda+k-\frac{n}{r}}f(z_{22}-w_{22})\,{\rm d}w_{22}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}(z_{22}-a_{22})^{-\lambda+\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}+\frac{n^{\prime\prime}}{r^{\prime\prime}}}}{\Gamma_{r^{\prime\prime}}^{d}(-k)}\int_{\Omega_{22}\cap(z_{22}-a_{22}-\Omega_{22})}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}(w_{22})^{-k-\frac{n^{\prime\prime}}{r^{\prime\prime}}}

\displaystyle\qquad\hphantom{=}{}\ \times\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}(z_{22}-a_{22}-w_{22})^{\lambda+k-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}-\frac{n^{\prime\prime}}{r^{\prime\prime}}}f(z_{22}-w_{22})\,{\rm d}w_{22},

which originally converges when
-\operatorname{Re}k>\frac{n^{\prime\prime}}{r^{\prime\prime}}-1
,
\operatorname{Re}\left(\lambda+k-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\right)>\frac{n^{\prime\prime}}{r^{\prime\prime}}-1
.
Now for a while we omit the subscript 22, and prove the following.

Proposition 5.2
.

Let
\operatorname{Re}\mu>\frac{n}{r}-1
,
\operatorname{Re}\nu>\frac{n}{r}-1
,
\mathbf{l}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}
and
f\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})
.
We define
\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{m}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{n}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})
as (
5.1
).
Then for
z,a\in\mathfrak{n}^{+}
with
z-a\in\Omega
, we have

\displaystyle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z-a)^{-\mu-\nu+\frac{n}{r}}\int_{(a+\Omega)\cap(z-\Omega)}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(w-a)^{\mu-\frac{n}{r}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z-w)^{\nu-\frac{n}{r}}f(z+a-w)\,{\rm d}w

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z-a)^{-\mu-\nu+\frac{n}{r}}\int_{\Omega\cap(z-a-\Omega)}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(w)^{\mu-\frac{n}{r}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z-a-w)^{\nu-\frac{n}{r}}f(z-w)\,{\rm d}w

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{\Gamma_{r}^{d}(\mu)\Gamma_{r}^{d}(\nu)}{\Gamma_{r}^{d}(\mu+\nu)}\sum_{\mathbf{m}}\frac{(\mu)_{\mathbf{m},d}}{(\mu+\nu)_{\mathbf{m},d}}\sum_{\mathbf{n}}\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}(a-z,z)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{\Gamma_{r}^{d}(\mu)\Gamma_{r}^{d}(\nu)}{\Gamma_{r}^{d}(\mu+\nu)}\sum_{\mathbf{n}}\frac{(\nu)_{\mathbf{n},d}}{(\mu+\nu)_{\mathbf{n},d}}\sum_{\mathbf{m}}\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}(a,z-a)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{\Gamma_{r}^{d}(\mu)\Gamma_{r}^{d}(\nu)}{\Gamma_{r}^{d}(\mu+\nu+\mathbf{l})}\sum_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}(\mu)_{\mathbf{m},d}(\nu)_{\mathbf{n},d}\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}(a,z).

Then Theorem
5.1
for tube type
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}
is proved from Proposition
5.2

by dividing each side by
\Gamma_{r^{\prime\prime}}^{d}(\mu)
and putting
\mu=-k
,
\nu=\lambda+k-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}
, so that

\displaystyle\frac{\Gamma_{r^{\prime\prime}}^{d}(\nu)}{\Gamma_{r^{\prime\prime}}^{d}(\mu+\nu)}=\frac{\Gamma_{r^{\prime\prime}}^{d}\big(\lambda+k-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\big)}{\Gamma_{r^{\prime\prime}}^{d}\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\big)}=\bigg(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\bigg)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}},d},

\displaystyle\frac{\Gamma_{r^{\prime\prime}}^{d}(\nu)}{\Gamma_{r^{\prime\prime}}^{d}(\mu+\nu+\mathbf{l})}=\frac{\Gamma_{r^{\prime\prime}}^{d}\big(\lambda+k-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\big)}{\Gamma_{r^{\prime\prime}}^{d}\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\big)}\frac{\Gamma_{r^{\prime\prime}}^{d}\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\big)}{\Gamma_{r^{\prime\prime}}^{d}\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}+\mathbf{l}\big)}=\frac{\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\big)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}},d}}{\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\big)_{\mathbf{l},d}},

and since each side of Theorem
5.1
is a polynomial in
z
, the equalities hold not only for
z\in\Omega

but also for all
z\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}
by analytic continuation.

For the proof of Proposition
5.2
, we prepare some notations.
For
\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}
, we decompose
\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{m}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{n}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})\subset\mathcal{P}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}\oplus\mathfrak{p}^{+})
as

\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{m}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{n}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})=\bigoplus_{\mathbf{l}\in\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}^{\mathbf{l}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}\oplus\mathfrak{p}^{+}),

where
\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}^{\mathbf{l}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}\oplus\mathfrak{p}^{+})
is the sum of
L
-submodules in
\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{m}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{n}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})

with the restricted lowest weight
-l_{1}\gamma_{1}-\cdots-l_{r}\gamma_{r}
under the diagonal action of
L
,
where
L
is the real form of
K^{\mathbb{C}}
given in Section
2.4
,
and
\{\gamma_{j}\}\subset\mathfrak{a}_{\mathfrak{l}}^{\vee}
is as in Section
2.5
.
Then we have the following formula, which is a generalization of the
beta integral

[
11
, Theorem VII.1.7]
.

Proposition 5.3
.

Let
\operatorname{Re}\mu>\frac{n}{r}-1
,
\operatorname{Re}\nu>\frac{n}{r}-1
,
\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}
,
\mathbf{l}\in\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}
.
Then for any
\tilde{f}(x,y)\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}^{\mathbf{l}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}\oplus\mathfrak{p}^{+})
,
z\in\Omega
, we have

\displaystyle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{-\mu-\nu+\frac{n}{r}}\int_{\Omega\cap(z-\Omega)}\tilde{f}(w,z-w)\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(w)^{\mu-\frac{n}{r}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z-w)^{\nu-\frac{n}{r}}{\rm d}w

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{\Gamma_{r}^{d}(\mu+\mathbf{m})\Gamma_{r}^{d}(\nu+\mathbf{n})}{\Gamma_{r}^{d}(\mu+\nu+\mathbf{l})}\tilde{f}(z,z).

Especially, if
\mathbf{l}\notin\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}
, then
\tilde{f}(z,z)=0\in\mathcal{P}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})
and the above integral vanishes.

Proof.

For
\tilde{f}(x,y)\in\mathcal{P}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})\otimes\mathcal{P}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})
we define a function
\tilde{f}^{\wedge}(z)\in C(\Omega)
by

\tilde{f}^{\wedge}(z):=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{-\mu-\nu+\frac{n}{r}}\int_{\Omega\cap(z-\Omega)}\tilde{f}(w,z-w)\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(w)^{\mu-\frac{n}{r}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z-w)^{\nu-\frac{n}{r}}{\rm d}w.

Then for
l\in L
we have

\displaystyle(\tilde{f}(l\cdot,l\cdot))^{\wedge}(z)={}

\displaystyle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{-\mu-\nu+\frac{n}{r}}\int_{\Omega\cap(z-\Omega)}\!\!\!\!\!\!\tilde{f}(lw,l(z\!-w))\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(w)^{\mu\!-\frac{n}{r}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z\!-w)^{\nu-\frac{n}{r}}{\rm d}w

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{-\mu-\nu+\frac{n}{r}}\int_{\Omega\cap(lz-\Omega)}\tilde{f}(w,lz-w)\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}\big(l^{-1}w\big)^{\mu-\frac{n}{r}}

\displaystyle\times\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}\big(z-l^{-1}w\big)^{\nu-\frac{n}{r}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}\big(l^{-1}e\big)^{\frac{n}{r}}{\rm d}w

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(lz)^{-\mu-\nu+\frac{n}{r}}\int_{\Omega\cap(lz-\Omega)}\!\!\!\!\!\tilde{f}(w,lz-w)\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(w)^{\mu-\frac{n}{r}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(lz-w)^{\nu-\frac{n}{r}}{\rm d}w

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle\tilde{f}^{\wedge}(lz).

Therefore
\tilde{f}(x,y)\mapsto\tilde{f}^{\wedge}(z)
is
L
-equivariant.
Now we compute
\tilde{f}^{\wedge}(z)
when
\tilde{f}(x,y)\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}^{\mathbf{l}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}\allowbreak\oplus\mathfrak{p}^{+})
is a lowest weight vector.
Then
\tilde{f}(x,y)
satisfies
\tilde{f}(lx,ly)=\Delta_{\mathbf{l}}(le)\tilde{f}(x,y)
for
l\in A_{L}N_{L}^{\top}
.
In this case, when
z=le
with
l\in A_{L}N_{L}^{\top}
, we have

\displaystyle\tilde{f}^{\wedge}(z)=\tilde{f}^{\wedge}(le)=\big(\tilde{f}(l\cdot,l\cdot)\big)^{\wedge}(e)=\big(\Delta_{\mathbf{l}}(le)\tilde{f}(\cdot,\cdot)\big)^{\wedge}(e)=\Delta_{\mathbf{l}}(z)\tilde{f}^{\wedge}(e),

(5.3)

that is,

\tilde{f}^{\wedge}(e)\Delta_{\mathbf{l}}(z)\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{\mu+\nu-\frac{n}{r}}=\int_{\Omega\cap(z-\Omega)}\tilde{f}(w,z-w)\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(w)^{\mu-\frac{n}{r}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z-w)^{\nu-\frac{n}{r}}{\rm d}w

holds. Then by multiplying
{\rm e}^{-\operatorname{tr}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)}
and integrating on
\Omega
with respect to
z
,
the left hand side is computed by using Theorem
4.2
(1) as

\int_{\Omega}{\rm e}^{-\operatorname{tr}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)}\tilde{f}^{\wedge}(e)\Delta_{\mathbf{l}}(z)\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{\mu+\nu-\frac{n}{r}}{\rm d}z=\Gamma_{r}^{d}(\mu+\nu+\mathbf{l})\tilde{f}^{\wedge}(e),

and the right hand side is computed by using the variable change
z=x+y
,
w=x
and again by Theorem
4.2
(1) as

\displaystyle\iint_{\begin{subarray}{c}z\in\Omega,w\in\Omega\cap(z-\Omega)\end{subarray}}{\rm e}^{-\operatorname{tr}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)}\tilde{f}(w,z-w)\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(w)^{\mu-\frac{n}{r}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z-w)^{\nu-\frac{n}{r}}{\rm d}w{\rm d}z

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\iint_{\begin{subarray}{c}x\in\Omega,y\in\Omega\end{subarray}}{\rm e}^{-\operatorname{tr}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(x+y)}\tilde{f}(x,y)\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(x)^{\mu-\frac{n}{r}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(y)^{\nu-\frac{n}{r}}{\rm d}x{\rm d}y

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\Gamma_{r}^{d}(\mu+\mathbf{m})\Gamma_{r}^{d}(\nu+\mathbf{n})\tilde{f}(e,e).

Therefore we have

\tilde{f}^{\wedge}(e)=\frac{\Gamma_{r}^{d}(\mu+\mathbf{m})\Gamma_{r}^{d}(\nu+\mathbf{n})}{\Gamma_{r}^{d}(\mu+\nu+\mathbf{l})}\tilde{f}(e,e),

and by (
5.3
), we get

\displaystyle\tilde{f}^{\wedge}(z)=\Delta_{\mathbf{l}}(z)\tilde{f}^{\wedge}(e)=\Delta_{\mathbf{l}}(z)\frac{\Gamma_{r}^{d}(\mu+\mathbf{m})\Gamma_{r}^{d}(\nu+\mathbf{n})}{\Gamma_{r}^{d}(\mu+\nu+\mathbf{l})}\tilde{f}(e,e)=\frac{\Gamma_{r}^{d}(\mu+\mathbf{m})\Gamma_{r}^{d}(\nu+\mathbf{n})}{\Gamma_{r}^{d}(\mu+\nu+\mathbf{l})}\tilde{f}(z,z).

By the
L
-equivariance, this holds for all
\tilde{f}(x,y)\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}^{\mathbf{l}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}\oplus\mathfrak{p}^{+})
.
∎

Proof of Proposition
5.2
.

The 1st equality is clear. Next we prove the 2nd equality. Since as a function of
w
,
\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}(-w,z)\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{m}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})=\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{m},\underline{0}_{r}}^{\mathbf{m}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}\oplus\mathfrak{p}^{+})
holds, we have

\displaystyle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z-a)^{-\mu-\nu+\frac{n}{r}}\int_{\Omega\cap(z-a-\Omega)}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(w)^{\mu-\frac{n}{r}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z-a-w)^{\nu-\frac{n}{r}}f(z-w)\,{\rm d}w

\displaystyle{}=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z-a)^{-\mu-\nu+\frac{n}{r}}\sum_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}\int_{\Omega\cap(z-a-\Omega)}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(w)^{\mu-\frac{n}{r}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z-a-w)^{\nu-\frac{n}{r}}\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}(-w,z)\,{\rm d}w

\displaystyle{}=\sum_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}\frac{\Gamma_{r}^{d}(\mu+\mathbf{m})\Gamma_{r}^{d}(\nu)}{\Gamma_{r}^{d}(\mu+\nu+\mathbf{m})}\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}(-(z-a),z)=\frac{\Gamma_{r}^{d}(\mu)\Gamma_{r}^{d}(\nu)}{\Gamma_{r}^{d}(\mu+\nu)}\sum_{\mathbf{m}}\frac{(\mu)_{\mathbf{m},d}}{(\mu+\nu)_{\mathbf{m},d}}\sum_{\mathbf{n}}\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}(a-z,z).

The 3rd equality is also proved similarly

\displaystyle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z-a)^{-\mu-\nu+\frac{n}{r}}\int_{\Omega\cap(z-a-\Omega)}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(w)^{\mu-\frac{n}{r}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z-a-w)^{\nu-\frac{n}{r}}f(z-w)\,{\rm d}w

\displaystyle{}=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z-a)^{-\mu-\nu+\frac{n}{r}}\int_{\Omega\cap(z-a-\Omega)}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(w)^{\mu-\frac{n}{r}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z-a-w)^{\nu-\frac{n}{r}}f(a+z-a-w)\,{\rm d}w

\displaystyle{}=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z-a)^{-\mu-\nu+\frac{n}{r}}\sum_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}\int_{\Omega\cap(z-a-\Omega)}\hskip-25.60747pt\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(w)^{\mu-\frac{n}{r}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z-a-w)^{\nu-\frac{n}{r}}\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}(a,z-a-w)\,{\rm d}w

\displaystyle{}=\sum_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}\frac{\Gamma_{r}^{d}(\mu)\Gamma_{r}^{d}(\nu+\mathbf{n})}{\Gamma_{r}^{d}(\mu+\nu+\mathbf{n})}\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}(a,z-a)

\displaystyle{}=\frac{\Gamma_{r}^{d}(\mu)\Gamma_{r}^{d}(\nu)}{\Gamma_{r}^{d}(\mu+\nu)}\sum_{\mathbf{n}}\frac{(\nu)_{\mathbf{n},d}}{(\mu+\nu)_{\mathbf{n},d}}\sum_{\mathbf{m}}\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}(a,z-a).

Finally we prove the 4th equality. Since the map
\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})\ni f\mapsto\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{m}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{n}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})
is
L\simeq\Delta L
-equivariant,
we may assume
f=\tilde{\Phi}_{\mathbf{l}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})^{K_{L}}
(see (
2.6
)).
We sum up the left hand side with respect to
\mathbf{l}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}
. Then by (
2.9
) we have

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathbf{l}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z-a)^{-\mu-\nu+\frac{n}{r}}\int_{\Omega\cap(z-a-\Omega)}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(w)^{\mu-\frac{n}{r}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z-a-w)^{\nu-\frac{n}{r}}\tilde{\Phi}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}_{\mathbf{l}}(z-w)\,{\rm d}w

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z-a)^{-\mu-\nu+\frac{n}{r}}\int_{\Omega\cap(z-a-\Omega)}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(w)^{\mu-\frac{n}{r}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z-a-w)^{\nu-\frac{n}{r}}{\rm e}^{\operatorname{tr}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z-w)}{\rm d}w

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z-a)^{-\mu-\nu+\frac{n}{r}}\int_{\Omega\cap(e-\Omega)}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}\big(P\big((z-a)^{\mathit{1/2}}\big)w\big)^{\mu-\frac{n}{r}}

\displaystyle\qquad\hphantom{=}{}\ \times\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}\big(z-a-P\big((z-a)^{\mathit{1/2}}\big)w\big)^{\nu-\frac{n}{r}}{\rm e}^{\operatorname{tr}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z-P((z-a)^{\mathit{1/2}})w)}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z-a)^{\frac{n}{r}}{\rm d}w

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\int_{\Omega\cap(e-\Omega)}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(w)^{\mu-\frac{n}{r}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(e-w)^{\nu-\frac{n}{r}}{\rm e}^{(z|e)_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}-(z-a|w)_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}}{\rm d}w

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\int_{\Omega\cap(e-\Omega)}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(w)^{\mu-\frac{n}{r}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(e-w)^{\nu-\frac{n}{r}}{\rm e}^{(a|w)_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}+(z|e-w)_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}}{\rm d}w.

For
\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}
,
\mathbf{l}\in\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}
,
we define
K_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}^{\mathbf{l}}(a,b;c,d)\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}^{\mathbf{l}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}\oplus\mathfrak{p}^{+})_{a,b}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}^{\mathbf{l}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}\oplus\mathfrak{p}^{+})_{c,d}
by

{\rm e}^{(a|c)_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}+(b|d)_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}}=\sum_{\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}}\sum_{\mathbf{n}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}}\sum_{\mathbf{l}\in\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}}K_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}^{\mathbf{l}}(a,b;c,d).

Then we have

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega\cap(e-\Omega)}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(w)^{\mu-\frac{n}{r}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(e-w)^{\nu-\frac{n}{r}}{\rm e}^{(a|w)_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}+(z|e-w)_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}}{\rm d}w

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\sum_{\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}}\sum_{\mathbf{n}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}}\sum_{\mathbf{l}\in\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}}\int_{\Omega\cap(e-\Omega)}\!\!\!\!\!\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(w)^{\mu-\frac{n}{r}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(e-w)^{\nu-\frac{n}{r}}K_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}^{\mathbf{l}}(a,z;w,e-w)\,{\rm d}w

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\sum_{\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}}\sum_{\mathbf{n}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}}\sum_{\mathbf{l}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}}\frac{\Gamma_{r}^{d}(\mu+\mathbf{m})\Gamma_{r}^{d}(\nu+\mathbf{n})}{\Gamma_{r}^{d}(\mu+\nu+\mathbf{l})}K_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}^{\mathbf{l}}(a,z;e,e)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\sum_{\mathbf{l}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}}\frac{\Gamma_{r}^{d}(\mu)\Gamma_{r}^{d}(\nu)}{\Gamma_{r}^{d}(\mu+\nu+\mathbf{l})}\sum_{\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}}\sum_{\mathbf{n}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}}(\mu)_{\mathbf{m},d}(\nu)_{\mathbf{n},d}K_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}^{\mathbf{l}}(a,z;e,e).

Now since
K_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}^{\mathbf{l}}(a,z;e,e)=(\tilde{\Phi}_{\mathbf{l}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}})^{\sim}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}(a,z)
holds, we get

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathbf{l}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z-a)^{-\mu-\nu+\frac{n}{r}}\int_{\Omega\cap(z-a-\Omega)}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(w)^{\mu-\frac{n}{r}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z-a-w)^{\nu-\frac{n}{r}}\tilde{\Phi}_{\mathbf{l}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z-w)\,{\rm d}w

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\sum_{\mathbf{l}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}}\frac{\Gamma_{r}^{d}(\mu)\Gamma_{r}^{d}(\nu)}{\Gamma_{r}^{d}(\mu+\nu+\mathbf{l})}\sum_{\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}}\sum_{\mathbf{n}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}}(\mu)_{\mathbf{m},d}(\nu)_{\mathbf{n},d}\big(\tilde{\Phi}_{\mathbf{l}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}\big)^{\sim}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}(a,z).

Then by projecting both sides to
\bigoplus_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}^{\mathbf{l}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}\oplus\mathfrak{p}^{+})
, we get the 4th equality
for
f=\tilde{\Phi}_{\mathbf{l}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}
, and by the
L
-equivariace, this holds for all
f\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})
.
∎

This completes the proof of Theorem
5.1
for tube type
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}
.
When
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}
is of non-tube type, we take maximal tripotents
e^{\prime}\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}
,
e^{\prime\prime}\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}
,
and let
\mathfrak{p}^{+\prime}:=\mathfrak{p}^{+}(e^{\prime}+e^{\prime\prime})_{2}
,
\mathfrak{p}^{+\prime}_{22}:=\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}(e^{\prime\prime})_{2}
,
\mathfrak{p}^{+\prime}_{12}:=\mathfrak{p}^{+\prime}\cap\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{12}
,
so that
\mathfrak{p}^{+\prime}=\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}\oplus\mathfrak{p}^{+\prime}_{12}\oplus\mathfrak{p}^{+\prime}_{22}
holds.
For
x\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}
, let
x^{\prime}\in\mathfrak{p}^{+\prime}
be the orthogonal projection of
x
onto
\mathfrak{p}^{+\prime}
.
Now we take a polynomial
f(x^{\prime}_{22})\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+\prime}_{22})
, and extend to a polynomial
f(x_{22})\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22})

on
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}
. Then we have

\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}(x_{22},y_{22})=\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}(x_{22}^{\prime},y_{22}^{\prime})\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{m}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+\prime}_{22})\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{n}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+\prime}_{22})\subset\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{m}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22})\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{n}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}),

and since
\big(Q(z_{12}){}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{11}^{-\mathit{1}}\big)^{\prime}=Q(z_{12}^{\prime}){}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{11}^{-\mathit{1}}
holds,
by Corollary
2.11
we get

\displaystyle\big\langle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}(x_{11})^{k}f(x_{22}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x,\mathfrak{p}^{+}}=\big\langle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}(x_{11})^{k}f(x_{22}^{\prime}),{\rm e}^{(x^{\prime}|\overline{z^{\prime}})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+\prime}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x^{\prime},\mathfrak{p}^{+\prime}}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\big)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}},d}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k}_{r}+\mathbf{l},d}\big(\lambda\!-\!\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\big)_{\mathbf{l},d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}(z_{11})^{k}\sum_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}(-k)_{\mathbf{m},d}\bigg(\lambda\!+\!k\!-\!\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\bigg)_{\mathbf{n},d}\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}\big(Q(z_{12}^{\prime}){}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{11}^{-\mathit{1}},z_{22}^{\prime}\big)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\big)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}},d}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k}_{r}+\mathbf{l},d}\big(\lambda\!-\!\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\big)_{\mathbf{l},d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}(z_{11})^{k}\sum_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}(-k)_{\mathbf{m},d}\bigg(\lambda\!+\!k\!-\!\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\bigg)_{\mathbf{n},d}\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}\big(Q(z_{12}){}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{11}^{-\mathit{1}},z_{22}\big).

Now let
\mathfrak{k}^{\mathbb{C}}_{22}:=[\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22},\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{22}]\subset\mathfrak{k}^{\mathbb{C}}
, and let
K_{22}^{\mathbb{C}}\subset K^{\mathbb{C}}
be the corresponding connected subgroup.
Then since both

\mathcal{P}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22})\longrightarrow\mathcal{P}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}),\qquad f(x_{22})\mapsto\big\langle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}(x_{11})^{k}f(x_{22}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}

and

\mathcal{P}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22})\longrightarrow\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{m}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22})\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{n}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}),\qquad f(x_{22})\mapsto\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}(x_{22},y_{22})

are
K_{22}^{\mathbb{C}}
-equivariant, and since
\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22})
is generated by
\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+\prime}_{22})
as a
K^{\mathbb{C}}_{22}
-module,
the above formula holds for all
f\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22})
. This completes the proof of Theorem
5.1
for non-tube type
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}
.

Especially if both
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}
,
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}
are of tube type (i.e.,
Cases
1, 2, 5,
Case
3 with
q^{\prime}=s^{\prime}=r^{\prime}
,
q^{\prime\prime}=s^{\prime\prime}=r^{\prime\prime}
,
and
Case
4 with
s^{\prime}=2r^{\prime}
,
s^{\prime\prime}=2r^{\prime\prime}
), and if
\mathbf{l}=\underline{l}_{r^{\prime\prime}}
,
f(x_{22})=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}(x_{22})^{l}
,
then since by (
2.4
) and (
2.11
),

\displaystyle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}(x_{22}+y_{22})^{l}

\displaystyle=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}(y_{22})^{l}h_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}}\big({-}x_{22},{}^{t}\hskip-1.0pty_{22}^{-\mathit{1}}\big)^{l}

\displaystyle=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}(y_{22})^{l}\sum_{\mathbf{m}}(-1)^{|\mathbf{m}|}(-l)_{\mathbf{m},d}\tilde{\Phi}_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}}\big(x_{22},{}^{t}\hskip-1.0pty_{22}^{-\mathit{1}}\big)

holds, where
\tilde{\Phi}_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}}
is as in (
2.8
), we have

\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}(x_{22},y_{22})=\begin{cases}\displaystyle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}(y_{22})^{l}(-1)^{|\mathbf{m}|}(-l)_{\mathbf{m},d}\tilde{\Phi}_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}}\big(x_{22},{}^{t}\hskip-1.0pty_{22}^{-\mathit{1}}\big),&\mathbf{n}=\underline{l}_{r^{\prime\prime}}-\mathbf{m}^{\vee},\\
0,&\mathbf{n}\neq\underline{l}_{r^{\prime\prime}}-\mathbf{m}^{\vee},\end{cases}

where
\mathbf{m}^{\vee}=(m_{r^{\prime\prime}},m_{r^{\prime\prime}-1},\dots,m_{1})
. Therefore we get

\displaystyle\big\langle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}(x_{11})^{k}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}(x_{22})^{l},{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\big)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}},d}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k}_{r}+\underline{l}_{r^{\prime\prime}},d}\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\big)_{\underline{l}_{r^{\prime\prime}},d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}(z_{11})^{k}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}(z_{22})^{l}

\displaystyle\qquad\hphantom{=}{}\ \times\sum_{\mathbf{m}}(-1)^{|\mathbf{m}|}(-k)_{\mathbf{m},d}(-l)_{\mathbf{m},d}\bigg(\lambda+k-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\bigg)_{\underline{l}_{r^{\prime\prime}}-\mathbf{m}^{\vee},d}\tilde{\Phi}_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}}\big(Q(z_{12}){}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{11}^{-\mathit{1}},{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{22}^{-\mathit{1}}\big)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\big)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}},d}\big(\lambda+k-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\big)_{\underline{l}_{r^{\prime\prime}},d}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k}_{r}+\underline{l}_{r^{\prime\prime}},d}\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\big)_{\underline{l}_{r^{\prime\prime}},d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}(z_{11})^{k}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}(z_{22})^{l}

\displaystyle\qquad\hphantom{=}{}\ \times\sum_{\mathbf{m}}\frac{(-k)_{\mathbf{m},d}(-l)_{\mathbf{m},d}}{\left(-\lambda-k-l+\frac{n}{r}\right)_{\mathbf{m},d}}\tilde{\Phi}_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}}\big(Q(z_{12}){}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{11}^{-\mathit{1}},{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{22}^{-\mathit{1}}\big),

and the constant is computed as

\displaystyle\frac{\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\big)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}},d}\big(\lambda+k-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\big)_{\underline{l}_{r^{\prime\prime}},d}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k}_{r}+\underline{l}_{r^{\prime\prime}},d}\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\big)_{\underline{l}_{r^{\prime\prime}},d}}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\big)_{\underline{k+l}_{r^{\prime\prime}},d}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k+l}_{r^{\prime\prime}},d}\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime\prime}\big)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime}},d}\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\big)_{\underline{l}_{r^{\prime\prime}},d}}=\frac{\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime\prime}\big)_{\underline{k+l}_{r^{\prime}},d}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k+l}_{r^{\prime}},d}\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime\prime}\big)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime}},d}\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\big)_{\underline{l}_{r^{\prime\prime}},d}}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{\big(\lambda+l-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\big)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}},d}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k+l}_{r^{\prime\prime}},d}\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime\prime}\big)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime}},d}}=\frac{\big(\lambda+k-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime\prime}\big)_{\underline{l}_{r^{\prime}},d}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k+l}_{r^{\prime}},d}\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\big)_{\underline{l}_{r^{\prime\prime}},d}}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\begin{cases}\displaystyle\frac{\big(\lambda+\max\{k,l\}-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\big)_{\underline{\min\{k,l\}}_{r^{\prime\prime}},d}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k+l}_{r^{\prime\prime}},d}\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime\prime}\big)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime}-r^{\prime\prime}},d}\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\big)_{\underline{\min\{k,l\}}_{r^{\prime\prime}},d}},&r^{\prime}\geq r^{\prime\prime},\\
\displaystyle\frac{\big(\lambda+\max\{k,l\}-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime\prime}\big)_{\underline{\min\{k,l\}}_{r^{\prime}},d}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k+l}_{r^{\prime}},d}\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\big)_{\underline{l}_{r^{\prime\prime}-r^{\prime}},d}\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime\prime}\big)_{\underline{\min\{k,l\}}_{r^{\prime}},d}},&r^{\prime}\leq r^{\prime\prime}.\end{cases}

(5.4)

Also, by Proposition
2.4
(2) we have

t^{r}h_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}}\big(t^{-1}Q(z_{12}){}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{11}^{-\mathit{1}},{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{22}^{-\mathit{1}}\big)=t^{r}h_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}}\big(t^{-1}Q(z_{12}){}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{22}^{-\mathit{1}},{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{11}^{-\mathit{1}}\big)\in\mathbb{C}[t],

and since
\tilde{\Phi}_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}}\big(Q(z_{12}){}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{11}^{-\mathit{1}},{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{22}^{-\mathit{1}}\big)
depends only on the roots of
this polynomial, we have

\tilde{\Phi}_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}}\big(Q(z_{12}){}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{11}^{-\mathit{1}},{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{22}^{-\mathit{1}}\big)=\tilde{\Phi}_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}}\big(Q(z_{12}){}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{22}^{-\mathit{1}},{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{11}^{-\mathit{1}}\big).

Therefore we have the following.

Corollary 5.4
.

Assume
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}
,
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}
are of tube type, and let

\operatorname{Re}\lambda>\frac{2n}{r}-1
,
k,l\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
.
Then for
z=z_{11}+z_{12}+z_{22}\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}
, we have

\displaystyle\big\langle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}(x_{11})^{k}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}(x_{22})^{l},{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\begin{cases}\displaystyle\frac{\big(\lambda+\max\{k,l\}-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\big)_{\underline{\min\{k,l\}}_{r^{\prime\prime}},d}}{(\lambda)_{(\underline{k+l}_{r^{\prime\prime}},\underline{k}_{r^{\prime}-r^{\prime\prime}},\underline{\min\{k,l\}}_{r^{\prime\prime}}),d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}(z_{11})^{k}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}(z_{22})^{l}\\
\qquad\displaystyle{}\times{}_{2}F_{1}^{\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}}\left(\begin{matrix}-k,-l\\
-\lambda-k-l+\frac{n}{r}\end{matrix};Q(z_{12}){}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{11}^{-\mathit{1}},{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{22}^{-\mathit{1}}\right)\!,&r^{\prime}\geq r^{\prime\prime},\\
\displaystyle\frac{\big(\lambda+\max\{k,l\}-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime\prime}\big)_{\underline{\min\{k,l\}}_{r^{\prime}},d}}{(\lambda)_{(\underline{k+l}_{r^{\prime}},\underline{l}_{r^{\prime\prime}-r^{\prime}},\underline{\min\{k,l\}}_{r^{\prime}}),d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}(z_{11})^{k}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}(z_{22})^{l}\\
\qquad\displaystyle{}\times{}_{2}F_{1}^{\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}}\left(\begin{matrix}-k,-l\\
-\lambda-k-l+\frac{n}{r}\end{matrix};Q(z_{12}){}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{22}^{-\mathit{1}},{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{11}^{-\mathit{1}}\right)\!,&r^{\prime}\leq r^{\prime\prime}.\end{cases}

Here
{}_{2}F_{1}^{\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{jj}}
is as in (
2.14
).
By this corollary and by Remark
2.8
, the inner product

\big\langle\!\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}\hskip-2.0pt(x_{11})^{k}\allowbreak\times\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}(x_{22})^{l},{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}
is
given by using a Heckman–Opdam’s multivariate hypergeometric polynomial of type
BC_{\min\{r^{\prime},r^{\prime\prime}\}}
. On the other hand, for general
\mathbf{l}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r^{\prime\prime}}
,
f(x_{22})\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22})
, the inner product

\big\langle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}(x_{11})^{k}f(x_{22}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}
is not always
given by that, since the generalization of
\underline{l}_{r^{\prime\prime}}
to
\mathbf{l}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r^{\prime\prime}}
in the above formula
does not fit with (
2.17
).

For later use we prove the following. Suppose
\mathfrak{p}^{+}=\mathfrak{n}^{+\mathbb{C}}
is simple and of tube type.

Proposition 5.5
.

Let
\mathbf{l}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}
,
j\in\{0,1,\dots,l_{r}\}
. For
f(x)\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})
,
we put
g(x):=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(x)^{-j}f(x)\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}-\underline{j}_{r}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})
.

1.

Let
\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}
. Then for
x,y\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}
we have

\displaystyle\bigg(\frac{n}{r}-j+\mathbf{l}\bigg)_{\underline{j}_{r},d}\tilde{g}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}(x,y)

\displaystyle=\bigg(\frac{n}{r}+\mathbf{m}\bigg)_{\underline{j}_{r},d}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(x)^{-j}\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m}+\underline{j}_{r},\mathbf{n}}(x,y)

\displaystyle=\bigg(\frac{n}{r}+\mathbf{n}\bigg)_{\underline{j}_{r},d}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(y)^{-j}\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}+\underline{j}_{r}}(x,y).

2.

Let
k\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
,
k\geq j
. Then for
z,a\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}
we have

\displaystyle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}\bigg(\frac{\partial}{\partial z}\bigg)^{k}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z-a)^{k-j}f(z)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\bigg(\frac{n}{r}-j+\mathbf{l}\bigg)_{\underline{j}_{r},d}\!\!\!\!\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z-a)^{-j}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}\bigg(\frac{\partial}{\partial z}\bigg)^{k-j}\!\!\!\!\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z-a)^{k}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{-j}f(z).

Proof.

(1) We compute
\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}\big(\frac{\partial}{\partial x}\big)^{j}f(x+y)
in two ways. By
[
11
, Proposition VII.1.6]
,

\displaystyle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}\bigg(\frac{\partial}{\partial x}\bigg)^{j}\!f(x\!+\!y)={}

\displaystyle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}\bigg(\frac{\partial}{\partial w}\bigg)^{j}f(w)\biggl|_{w=x+y}=\bigg(\frac{n}{r}\!-\!j\!+\!\mathbf{l}\bigg)_{\underline{j}_{r},d}\!\!\!\!\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(x+y)^{-j}f(x+y)

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle\bigg(\frac{n}{r}-j+\mathbf{l}\bigg)_{\underline{j}_{r},d}\sum_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}\tilde{g}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}(x,y),

\displaystyle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}\bigg(\frac{\partial}{\partial x}\bigg)^{j}\!f(x\!+\!y)={}

\displaystyle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}\bigg(\frac{\partial}{\partial x}\bigg)^{j}\sum_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}(x,y)

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(x)^{-j}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{m}\\
m_{r}\geq j\end{subarray}}\bigg(\frac{n}{r}-j+\mathbf{m}\bigg)_{\underline{j}_{r},d}\sum_{\mathbf{n}}\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}(x,y)

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(x)^{-j}\sum_{\mathbf{m}}\bigg(\frac{n}{r}+\mathbf{m}\bigg)_{\underline{j}_{r},d}\sum_{\mathbf{n}}\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m}+\underline{j}_{r},\mathbf{n}}(x,y)

hold. We note that
\left(\frac{n}{r}-j+\mathbf{m}\right)_{\underline{j}_{r},d}=0
holds if
m_{r}<j
.
Then projecting both onto
\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{m}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{n}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})
, we get the 1st equality. The 2nd equality is also proved similarly.

(2) By dividing the 3rd equality of Proposition
5.2
by
\Gamma_{r}^{d}(\mu)
and putting
\mu=-k
,

\displaystyle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z-a)^{k-\nu+\frac{n}{r}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}\bigg(\frac{\partial}{\partial z}\bigg)^{k}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z-a)^{\nu-\frac{n}{r}}f(z)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=(\nu-k)_{\underline{k}_{r},d}\sum_{\mathbf{n}}\frac{(\nu)_{\mathbf{n},d}}{(\nu-k)_{\mathbf{n},d}}\sum_{\mathbf{m}}\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}(a,z-a)=\sum_{\mathbf{n}}(\nu-k+\mathbf{n})_{\underline{k}_{r},d}\sum_{\mathbf{m}}\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}(a,z-a)

holds, and putting
\nu=k-j+\frac{n}{r}
, we get

\displaystyle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}\bigg(\frac{\partial}{\partial z}\bigg)^{k}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z-a)^{k-j}f(z)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z-a)^{-j}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{n}\\
n_{r}\geq j\end{subarray}}\bigg(\frac{n}{r}-j+\mathbf{n}\bigg)_{\underline{k}_{r},d}\sum_{\mathbf{m}}\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}(a,z-a)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z-a)^{-j}\sum_{\mathbf{n}}\bigg(\frac{n}{r}+\mathbf{n}\bigg)_{\underline{k}_{r},d}\sum_{\mathbf{m}}\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}+\underline{j}_{r}}(a,z-a)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z-a)^{-j}\sum_{\mathbf{n}}\bigg(\frac{n}{r}+j+\mathbf{n}\bigg)_{\underline{k-j}_{r},d}\bigg(\frac{n}{r}+\mathbf{n}\bigg)_{\underline{j}_{r},d}\sum_{\mathbf{m}}\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}+\underline{j}_{r}}(a,z-a).

Similarly, for
g(x):=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(x)^{-j}f(x)
we have

\displaystyle\bigg(\frac{n}{r}-j+\mathbf{l}\bigg)_{\underline{j}_{r},d}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z-a)^{-j}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}\bigg(\frac{\partial}{\partial z}\bigg)^{k-j}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z-a)^{k}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{-j}f(z)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\bigg(\frac{n}{r}-j+\mathbf{l}\bigg)_{\underline{j}_{r},d}\sum_{\mathbf{n}}\bigg(\frac{n}{r}+j+\mathbf{n}\bigg)_{\underline{k-j}_{r},d}\sum_{\mathbf{m}}\tilde{g}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}(a,z-a).

Then comparing these and combining with (1), we get the desired formula.
∎

If
\mathbf{l}=\underline{l}_{r}
for some
l\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
, then Proposition
5.5
(2) also follows from (
2.16
).
By Propositions
5.5
(2) and
2.4
(2), easily we get the following.

Corollary 5.6
.

Suppose
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}
,
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}
are of tube type. Let
j,k\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
,
\mathbf{l}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r^{\prime\prime}}
,
j\leq\min\{k,l_{r^{\prime\prime}}\}

and let
f(x_{22})\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22})
. Then for
z=z_{11}+z_{12}+z_{22}\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}=\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}\oplus\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{12}\oplus\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}
we have

\displaystyle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}\bigg(\frac{\partial}{\partial z_{22}}\bigg)^{k}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{k-j}f(z_{22})

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\bigg(\frac{n^{\prime\prime}}{r^{\prime\prime}}-j+\mathbf{l}\bigg)_{\underline{j}_{r^{\prime\prime}},d}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{-j}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}\bigg(\frac{\partial}{\partial z_{22}}\bigg)^{k-j}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{k}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}(z_{22})^{-j}f(z_{22}).

5.2
Computation of poles

By Theorem
5.1
, we can compute the poles of
\big\langle f_{1}(x_{11})f_{2}(x_{22}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}

for
f_{1}(x_{11})\in\mathcal{P}_{\underline{k}_{a}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11})
,
f_{2}(x_{22})\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22})

with
k\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
,
1\leq a\leq r^{\prime}
,
\mathbf{l}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r^{\prime\prime}}
, without the assumption that
\mathfrak{p}^{+}
,

\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}
and
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}
are of tube type.
In the following, for
\mathbf{k}=(k_{1},\dots,k_{r})
,
\mathbf{l}=(l_{1},\dots,l_{r})\in\mathbb{Z}^{r}
,
we write
\min\{\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l}\}:=(\min\{k_{1},l_{1}\},\dots,\min\{k_{r},l_{r}\})
,
\max\{\mathbf{k},\mathbf{l}\}:=(\max\{k_{1},l_{1}\},\dots,\max\{k_{r},l_{r}\})\in\mathbb{Z}^{r}
,
and write
\mathbf{k}\leq\mathbf{l}
if
k_{j}\leq l_{j}
for all
1\leq j\leq r
.

Corollary 5.7
.

For
k\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
,
1\leq a\leq r^{\prime}
,
\mathbf{l}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r^{\prime\prime}}
, let

\displaystyle\mathbf{l}^{\prime}:=\begin{cases}(l_{1},\dots,l_{r^{\prime\prime}},0,\dots,0),&a\geq r^{\prime\prime}\\
(l_{1},\dots,l_{a}),&a\leq r^{\prime\prime}\end{cases}\quad\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{a},

\displaystyle\mathbf{l}^{\prime\prime}:=\begin{cases}(0,\dots,0),&a\geq r^{\prime\prime}\\
(l_{a+1},\dots,l_{r^{\prime\prime}},0,\dots,0),&a\leq r^{\prime\prime}\end{cases}\quad\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r^{\prime\prime}},

(5.5)

and let
f_{1}(x_{11})\in\mathcal{P}_{\underline{k}_{a}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11})
,
f_{2}(x_{22})\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22})
.

1.

When
z_{12}=0
, we have

\displaystyle\big\langle f_{1}(x_{11})f_{2}(x_{22}),{\rm e}^{(x_{11}+x_{22}|\overline{z_{11}+z_{22}})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}a\big)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}}+\mathbf{l},d}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k}_{a+r^{\prime\prime}}+\mathbf{l},d}\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}a\big)_{\mathbf{l},d}}f_{1}(z_{11})f_{2}(z_{22})

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}a+\max\{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}}+\mathbf{l}^{\prime\prime},\mathbf{l}\}\big)_{\min\{\mathbf{l}-\mathbf{l}^{\prime\prime},\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}}\},d}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k}_{a}+\mathbf{l}^{\prime},d}\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}a\big)_{\min\{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}}+\mathbf{l}^{\prime\prime},\mathbf{l}\},d}}f_{1}(z_{11})f_{2}(z_{22}).

2.

As a function of
\lambda
,

\displaystyle(\lambda)_{\underline{k}_{a}+\mathbf{l}^{\prime},d}\bigg(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}a\bigg)_{\min\{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}}+\mathbf{l}^{\prime\prime},\mathbf{l}\},d}\big\langle f_{1}(x_{11})f_{2}(x_{22}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=(\lambda)_{(\underline{k}_{a}+\mathbf{l}^{\prime},\min\{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}}+\mathbf{l}^{\prime\prime},\mathbf{l}\}),d}\big\langle f_{1}(x_{11})f_{2}(x_{22}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}

is holomorphically continued for all
\lambda\in\mathbb{C}
.

3.

Moreover, when
f_{1}(x_{11})f_{2}(x_{22})\neq 0
,
if
l_{a+1}=0
or
k=0
or
\mathbf{l}=\underline{l}_{a^{\prime}}

(l\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
,
1\leq a^{\prime}\leq r^{\prime\prime})
,
then the above formula gives a non-zero polynomial in
z\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}
for all
\lambda\in\mathbb{C}
.

Proof.

(1) First we assume
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}
is of tube type,
a=r^{\prime}
,
f_{1}(x_{11})=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}(x_{11})^{k}
,
and write
f_{2}(x_{22})=f(x_{22})\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22})
. In the 3rd equality of Theorem
5.1
,
if we substitute
z_{12}=0
, then only
\mathbf{m}=\underline{0}_{r^{\prime}}
,
\mathbf{n}=\mathbf{l}
term remains, and we have

\displaystyle\big\langle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}(x_{11})^{k}f(x_{22}),{\rm e}^{(x_{11}+x_{22}|\overline{z_{11}+z_{22}})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\big)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}},d}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k}_{r}+\mathbf{l},d}\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\big)_{\mathbf{l},d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}(z_{11})^{k}(-k)_{\underline{0}_{r^{\prime\prime}},d}\bigg(\lambda+k-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\bigg)_{\mathbf{l},d}\tilde{f}_{\underline{0}_{r^{\prime\prime}},\mathbf{l}}\big(Q(0){}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{11}^{-\mathit{1}},z_{22}\big)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\big)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}}+\mathbf{l},d}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k}_{r}+\mathbf{l},d}\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\big)_{\mathbf{l},d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}(z_{11})^{k}f(z_{22}).

Next we consider general
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}
and
1\leq a\leq r^{\prime}
. We take a tripotent
e^{\prime}\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}
of rank
a
,
a maximal tripotent
e^{\prime\prime}\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}
, and let
\mathfrak{p}^{+\prime}:=\mathfrak{p}^{+}(e^{\prime}+e^{\prime\prime})_{2}
,
\mathfrak{p}^{+\prime}_{ij}:=\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{ij}\cap\mathfrak{p}^{+\prime}
.
For
z\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}
, let
z^{\prime}\in\mathfrak{p}^{+\prime}
be the projection of
z
onto
\mathfrak{p}^{+\prime}
.
Then for
f_{1}(x_{11})=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+\prime}_{11}}(x_{11}^{\prime})^{k}\in\mathcal{P}_{\underline{k}_{a}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11})
,

f_{2}(x_{22})=f(x_{22}^{\prime})\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{+\prime}_{22}\big)\subset\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}\big)
, by Corollary
2.11
we have

\displaystyle\big\langle f_{1}(x_{11})f_{2}(x_{22}),{\rm e}^{(x_{11}+x_{22}|\overline{z_{11}+z_{22}})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x,\mathfrak{p}^{+}}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\big\langle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+\prime}_{11}}(x_{11}^{\prime})^{k}f(x_{22}^{\prime}),{\rm e}^{(x_{11}^{\prime}+x_{22}^{\prime}|\overline{z_{11}^{\prime}+z_{22}^{\prime}})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+\prime}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x^{\prime},\mathfrak{p}^{+\prime}}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}a\big)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}}+\mathbf{l},d}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k}_{a+r^{\prime\prime}}+\mathbf{l},d}\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}a\big)_{\mathbf{l},d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+\prime}_{11}}(z_{11}^{\prime})^{k}f(z_{22}^{\prime})=\frac{\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}a\big)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}}+\mathbf{l},d}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k}_{a+r^{\prime\prime}}+\mathbf{l},d}\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}a\big)_{\mathbf{l},d}}f_{1}(z_{11})f_{2}(z_{22}),

and since
f_{1}(x_{11})f_{2}(x_{22})\mapsto\big\langle f_{1}(x_{11})f_{2}(x_{22}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}
is

K_{1}
-equivariant, this holds for all
f_{1}(x_{11})\in\mathcal{P}_{\underline{k}_{a}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11})
,
f_{2}(x_{22})\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}\big)
.
Finally, the constant is computed as

\displaystyle\frac{\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}a\big)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}}+\mathbf{l},d}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k}_{a+r^{\prime\prime}}+\mathbf{l},d}\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}a\big)_{\mathbf{l},d}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}a\big)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}}+\mathbf{l},d}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k}_{a}+\mathbf{l}^{\prime},d}\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}a\big)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}}+\mathbf{l}^{\prime\prime},d}\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}a\big)_{\mathbf{l},d}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}a+\max\{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}}+\mathbf{l}^{\prime\prime},\mathbf{l}\}\big)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}}+\mathbf{l}-\max\{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}}+\mathbf{l}^{\prime\prime},\mathbf{l}\},d}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k}_{a}+\mathbf{l}^{\prime},d}\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}a\big)_{\min\{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}}+\mathbf{l}^{\prime\prime},\mathbf{l}\},d}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}a+\max\{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}}+\mathbf{l}^{\prime\prime},\mathbf{l}\}\big)_{\min\{\mathbf{l}-\mathbf{l}^{\prime\prime},\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}}\},d}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k}_{a}+\mathbf{l}^{\prime},d}\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}a\big)_{\min\{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}}+\mathbf{l}^{\prime\prime},\mathbf{l}\},d}}.

(2) Again we assume
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}
is of tube type,
a=r^{\prime}
,
f_{1}(x_{11})=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}(x_{11})^{k}
,
and write
f_{2}(x_{22})=f(x_{22})\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22})
.
First let
r^{\prime}\geq r^{\prime\prime}
. Then by the 1st equality of Theorem
5.1
we have

\displaystyle\big\langle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}(x_{11})^{k}f(x_{22}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\big)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}},d}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k}_{r}+\mathbf{l},d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}(z_{11})^{k}\sum_{\mathbf{m}}\frac{(-k)_{\mathbf{m},d}}{\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\big)_{\mathbf{m},d}}\sum_{\mathbf{n}}\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}\big(Q(z_{12}){}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{11}^{-\mathit{1}}-z_{22},z_{22}\big)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{1}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime}}+\mathbf{l},d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}(z_{11})^{k}\sum_{\mathbf{m}}\frac{(-k)_{\mathbf{m},d}}{\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\big)_{\mathbf{m},d}}\sum_{\mathbf{n}}\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}\big(Q(z_{12}){}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{11}^{-\mathit{1}}-z_{22},z_{22}\big).

Then since
(-k)_{\mathbf{m},d}
is non-zero only if
m_{j}\leq k
, and

\sum_{\mathbf{n}}\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}\left(Q(z_{12}){}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{11}^{-\mathit{1}}-z_{22},z_{22}\right)
is non-zero only if
m_{j}\leq l_{j}
, we have

\displaystyle(\lambda)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime}}+\mathbf{l},d}\bigg(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\bigg)_{\min\{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}},\mathbf{l}\},d}\big\langle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}(x_{11})^{k}f(x_{22}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}(z_{11})^{k}\sum_{\mathbf{m}\leq\min\{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}},\mathbf{l}\}}(-k)_{\mathbf{m},d}\bigg(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}+\mathbf{m}\bigg)_{\min\{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}},\mathbf{l}\}-\mathbf{m},d}

\displaystyle\qquad\hphantom{=}{}\ \times\sum_{\mathbf{n}}\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}\big(Q(z_{12}){}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{11}^{-\mathit{1}}-z_{22},z_{22}\big).

(5.6)

Therefore the claim follows. Next let
r^{\prime}<r^{\prime\prime}
.
Then since
\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}\left(Q(z_{12}){}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{11}^{-\mathit{1}}-z_{22},z_{22}\right)
are not
linearly independent in general, we use the 2nd equality of the theorem instead. Then we have

\displaystyle\big\langle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}(x_{11})^{k}f(x_{22}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}

\displaystyle=\frac{\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\big)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}},d}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k}_{r}+\mathbf{l},d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}(z_{11})^{k}\sum_{\mathbf{n}}\frac{\big(\lambda+k-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\big)_{\mathbf{n},d}}{\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\big)_{\mathbf{n},d}}\sum_{\mathbf{m}}\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}\big(Q(z_{12}){}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{11}^{-\mathit{1}},z_{22}-Q(z_{12}){}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{11}^{-\mathit{1}}\big)

\displaystyle=\frac{\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}(z_{11})^{k}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime}}+\mathbf{l}^{\prime},d}}\sum_{\mathbf{n}}\frac{\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\big)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}}+\mathbf{n},d}}{\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\big)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}}+\mathbf{l}^{\prime\prime},d}\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\big)_{\mathbf{n},d}}\sum_{\mathbf{m}}\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}\big(Q(z_{12}){}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{11}^{-\mathit{1}},z_{22}-Q(z_{12}){}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{11}^{-\mathit{1}}\big).

Then since the rank of
Q(z_{12}){}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{11}^{-\mathit{1}}
is at most
r^{\prime}
,

\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}\left(Q(z_{12}){}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{11}^{-\mathit{1}},z_{22}-Q(z_{12}){}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{11}^{-\mathit{1}}\right)

is non-zero only if
m_{r^{\prime}+1}=\cdots=m_{r^{\prime\prime}}=0
,
and
\sum_{\mathbf{m}}\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}\left(Q(z_{12}){}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{11}^{-\mathit{1}},z_{22}-Q(z_{12}){}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{11}^{-\mathit{1}}\right)

is non-zero only if
l_{r^{\prime}+j}\leq n_{j}\leq l_{j}
for
1\leq j\leq r^{\prime\prime}-r^{\prime}
and
0\leq n_{j}\leq l_{j}
for
r^{\prime\prime}-r^{\prime}<j\leq r^{\prime\prime}
,
which is proved case-by-case by using the Littlewood–Richardson rule. Therefore we have

\displaystyle(\lambda)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime}}+\mathbf{l}^{\prime},d}\bigg(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\bigg)_{\min\{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}}+\mathbf{l}^{\prime\prime},\mathbf{l}\},d}\big\langle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}(x_{11})^{k}f(x_{22}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}

\displaystyle{}=\bigg(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\bigg)_{\min\{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}}+\mathbf{l}^{\prime\prime},\mathbf{l}\},d}\sum_{\mathbf{l}^{\prime\prime}\leq\mathbf{n}\leq\mathbf{l}}\frac{\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}+\max\{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}}+\mathbf{l}^{\prime\prime},\mathbf{n}\}\big)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}}+\mathbf{n}-\max\{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}}+\mathbf{l}^{\prime\prime},\mathbf{n}\},d}}{\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\big)_{\min\{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}}+\mathbf{l}^{\prime\prime},\mathbf{n}\},d}}

\displaystyle\hphantom{=}{}\ \times\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}(z_{11})^{k}\sum_{\mathbf{m}}\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}\big(Q(z_{12}){}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{11}^{-\mathit{1}},z_{22}-Q(z_{12}){}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{11}^{-\mathit{1}}\big)

\displaystyle{}=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}(z_{11})^{k}\sum_{\mathbf{l}^{\prime\prime}\leq\mathbf{n}\leq\mathbf{l}}\bigg(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}+\min\{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}}+\mathbf{l}^{\prime\prime},\mathbf{n}\}\bigg)_{\min\{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}}+\mathbf{l}^{\prime\prime},\mathbf{l}\}-\min\{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}}+\mathbf{l}^{\prime\prime},\mathbf{n}\},d}

\displaystyle\hphantom{=}{}\ \times\bigg(\!\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}+\max\{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}}+\mathbf{l}^{\prime\prime},\mathbf{n}\}\!\bigg){\vphantom{\biggr)}}_{\min\{\mathbf{n}-\mathbf{l}^{\prime\prime},\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}}\},d}\sum_{\mathbf{m}}\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}\big(Q(z_{12}){}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{11}^{-\mathit{1}},z_{22}-Q(z_{12}){}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{11}^{-\mathit{1}}\big),

and the claim follows.
For general
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}
and
1\leq a\leq r^{\prime}
, this is proved as (1) by using Corollary
2.11
and the
K_{1}
-equivariance.

(3) Again we assume
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}
is of tube type and let
a=r^{\prime}\geq r^{\prime\prime}
. Then in (
5.6
),
by Lemma
5.8
given later,

\tilde{f}_{\min\{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}},\mathbf{l}\},\max\{\mathbf{l}-\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}},\underline{0}_{r^{\prime\prime}}\}}\neq 0

holds, and its coefficient does not depend on
\lambda\in\mathbb{C}
and is non-zero.
Now since all non-zero
\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}
are linearly independent, (
5.6
) is non-zero for all
\lambda\in\mathbb{C}
.
For general
1\leq a\leq r^{\prime}
and
\mathbf{l}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r^{\prime\prime}}
with
l_{a+1}=0
,
we take maximal tripotents
e^{\prime}\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}
of rank
a
and
e^{\prime\prime}\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}
of rank
\min\{a,r^{\prime\prime}\}
.
Then this is proved by reducing to
\mathfrak{p}^{+}(e^{\prime}+e^{\prime\prime})_{2}
using Corollary
2.11
and the
K_{1}
-equivariance.
Next, when
k=0
, by
\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22})\subset\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})
and Corollary
2.10
, or the 3rd equality of Theorem
5.1
,
we have

(\lambda)_{\mathbf{l},d}\big\langle f(x_{22}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}=f(z_{22})\neq 0.

Finally, when
\mathbf{l}=\underline{l}_{a^{\prime}}
with
a^{\prime}>a
, by exchanging the roles of
(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11},k,a)
and
(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22},l,a^{\prime})
,
this is reduced to the
l_{a+1}=0
case.
∎
