To complete the proof of Corollary
5.7
(3), for
\mathbf{l}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}
let

\operatorname{Proj}_{\mathbf{l}}\colon\ \mathcal{P}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})\longrightarrow\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})

be the orthogonal projection.

Lemma 5.8
.

Let
\mathbf{l},\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}
. Then the linear map

\displaystyle\alpha_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}^{\mathbf{l}}\colon\ \mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})

\displaystyle\longrightarrow\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{m}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{n}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}),

\displaystyle f(z)

\displaystyle\longmapsto\tilde{f}_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}(x,y):=(\operatorname{Proj}_{\mathbf{m},x}\otimes\operatorname{Proj}_{\mathbf{n},y})f(x+y)

is injective if and only if the linear map

\beta_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}^{\mathbf{l}}\colon\quad\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{m}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{n}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})\longrightarrow\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}),\qquad g(x,y)\longmapsto\operatorname{Proj}_{\mathbf{l},z}(g(z,z))

is non-zero. Especially, for
k\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
,

\alpha_{\min\{\underline{k}_{r},\mathbf{l}\},\max\{\mathbf{l}-\underline{k}_{r},\underline{0}_{r}\}}^{\mathbf{l}}\colon\ \mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})\longrightarrow\mathcal{P}_{\min\{\underline{k}_{r},\mathbf{l}\}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\max\{\mathbf{l}-\underline{k}_{r},\underline{0}_{r}\}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})

is injective.

Proof.

For
f\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})
,
g_{1}\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{m}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})
,
g_{2}\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{n}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})
, we have

\displaystyle\big\langle(\alpha_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}^{\mathbf{l}}f)(x,y),g_{1}(x)g_{2}(y)\big\rangle_{F,\mathfrak{p}^{+}\oplus\mathfrak{p}^{+}}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\langle f(x+y),g_{1}(x)g_{2}(y)\rangle_{F,\mathfrak{p}^{+}\oplus\mathfrak{p}^{+}}=\overline{g_{1}\bigg(\overline{\frac{\partial}{\partial x}}\bigg)}\overline{g_{2}\bigg(\overline{\frac{\partial}{\partial y}}\bigg)}f(x+y)\biggr|_{x=y=0}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\overline{g_{1}\bigg(\overline{\frac{\partial}{\partial z}}\bigg)}\overline{g_{2}\bigg(\overline{\frac{\partial}{\partial z}}\bigg)}f(z)\biggr|_{z=0}\!\!\!\!=\big\langle f(z),g_{1}(z)g_{2}(z)\big\rangle_{F,\mathfrak{p}^{+}}=\big\langle f(z),(\beta_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}^{\mathbf{l}}(g_{1}\otimes g_{2}))(z)\big\rangle_{F,\mathfrak{p}^{+}},

and hence
\alpha_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}^{\mathbf{l}}
and
\beta_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}^{\mathbf{l}}
are mutually adjoint with respect to the Fischer norms.
Therefore
\alpha_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}^{\mathbf{l}}
is injective if and only if
\beta_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}^{\mathbf{l}}
is surjective,
and since
\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})
is irreducible, this holds if and only if
\beta_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}^{\mathbf{l}}
is non-zero.
Hence the first claim follows. Especially if
\mathbf{m}=\min\{\underline{k}_{r},\mathbf{l}\}
,
\mathbf{n}=\max\{\mathbf{l}-\underline{k}_{r},\underline{0}_{r}\}
,
then for the lowest weight vectors,

\displaystyle\big(\beta_{\min\{\underline{k}_{r},\mathbf{l}\},\max\{\mathbf{l}-\underline{k}_{r},\underline{0}_{r}\}}^{\mathbf{l}}\big(\Delta_{\min\{\underline{k}_{r},\mathbf{l}\}}(x)\otimes\Delta_{\max\{\mathbf{l}-\underline{k}_{r},\underline{0}_{r}\}}(y)\big)\big)(z)=\Delta_{\mathbf{l}}(z)\neq 0

holds, and the second claim follows.
∎

Especially, by (
2.22
) and Corollary
5.7
(1),
for
f_{1}(x_{11})\in\mathcal{P}_{\underline{k}_{a}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11})
,
f_{2}(x_{22})\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22})
we have

\displaystyle\|f_{1}(x_{11})f_{2}(x_{22})\|_{\lambda,x,\mathfrak{p}^{+}}^{2}=\big\langle\big\langle f_{1}(x_{11})f_{2}(x_{22}),{\rm e}^{(x_{11}+x_{22}|\overline{z_{11}+z_{22}})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x,\mathfrak{p}^{+}},f_{1}(z_{11})f_{2}(z_{22})\big\rangle_{F,z,\mathfrak{p}^{+}}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}a\big)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}}+\mathbf{l},d}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k}_{a+r^{\prime\prime}}+\mathbf{l},d}\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}a\big)_{\mathbf{l},d}}\|f_{1}(z_{11})\|_{F,z_{11},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}}^{2}\|f_{2}(z_{22})\|_{F,z_{22},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}}^{2},

(5.7)

and moreover, if
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}
,
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}
are of tube type,
a=r^{\prime}
,
\mathbf{l}=\underline{l}_{r^{\prime\prime}}
and
f_{1}(x_{11})=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}(x_{11})^{k}
,

f_{2}(x_{22})=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}(x_{22})^{l}
, then since

\big\|\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}(z_{11})^{k}\big\|^{2}_{F,z_{11},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}}=\bigg(\frac{n^{\prime}}{r^{\prime}}\bigg)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime}},d},\qquad\big\|\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}(z_{22})^{l}\big\|^{2}_{F,z_{22},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}}=\bigg(\frac{n^{\prime\prime}}{r^{\prime\prime}}\bigg)_{\underline{l}_{r^{\prime\prime}},d}

hold (see
[
11
, Proposition XI.4.1(ii)]
), combining with (
5.4
) we get

\displaystyle\big\|\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}(x_{11})^{k}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}(x_{22})^{l}\big\|^{2}_{\lambda,x,\mathfrak{p}^{+}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\big(\frac{n^{\prime}}{r^{\prime}}\big)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime}},d}\big(\frac{n^{\prime\prime}}{r^{\prime\prime}}\big)_{\underline{l}_{r^{\prime\prime}},d}\big(\lambda+l-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\big)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}},d}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k+l}_{r^{\prime\prime}},d}\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime\prime}\big)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime}},d}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\big(\frac{n^{\prime}}{r^{\prime}}\big)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime}},d}\big(\frac{n^{\prime\prime}}{r^{\prime\prime}}\big)_{\underline{l}_{r^{\prime\prime}},d}\big(\lambda+k-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime\prime}\big)_{\underline{l}_{r^{\prime}},d}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k+l}_{r^{\prime}},d}\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\big)_{\underline{l}_{r^{\prime\prime}},d}}.

5.3
Individual cases

In this section we observe
\big\langle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}(x_{11})^{k}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}(x_{22})^{l},{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}

(Corollary
5.4
) under the explicit realization of
\mathfrak{p}^{+}
.
For
Case
1, if we realize
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}\simeq\mathbb{C}
,
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{12}\simeq\mathbb{C}^{d}
,
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}\simeq\mathbb{C}
as

\displaystyle\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}:=\big\{(0,\dots,0,z,-\sqrt{-1}z)\mid z\in\mathbb{C}\big\}\subset\mathfrak{p}^{+}=\mathbb{C}^{d+2},

\displaystyle\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{12}:=\big\{(z_{1},\dots,z_{d},0,0)\mid z_{j}\in\mathbb{C}\big\}\subset\mathfrak{p}^{+}=\mathbb{C}^{d+2},

\displaystyle\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}:=\big\{(0,\dots,0,z,\sqrt{-1}z)\mid z\in\mathbb{C}\big\}\subset\mathfrak{p}^{+}=\mathbb{C}^{d+2},

and take the maximal tripotent
e=(0,\dots,0,1,0)\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}\oplus\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}\subset\mathfrak{p}^{+}
,
then for
z=(z_{1},\dots,z_{d},\allowbreak z_{d+1},z_{d+2})=(z^{\prime},z_{d+1},z_{d+2})\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}=\mathbb{C}^{d+2}
we have

\displaystyle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}(z_{11})=z_{d+1}+\sqrt{-1}z_{d+2},

\displaystyle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}(z_{22})=z_{d+1}-\sqrt{-1}z_{d+2},

\displaystyle\tilde{\Phi}_{m}^{\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}}\big(Q(z_{12}){}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{11}^{-\mathit{1}},{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{22}^{-\mathit{1}}\big)=\frac{1}{m!}\big(\big(Q(z_{12}){}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{11}^{-\mathit{1}}|{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{22}^{-\mathit{1}}\big)_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}}\big)^{m}=\frac{1}{m!}\bigg({-}\frac{q(z^{\prime})}{z_{d+1}^{2}+z_{d+2}^{2}}\bigg)^{m},

and therefore we get

\displaystyle\big\langle\big(x_{d+1}+\sqrt{-1}x_{d+2}\big)^{k}\big(x_{d+1}-\sqrt{-1}x_{d+2}\big)^{l},{\rm e}^{2q(x,\overline{z})}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{\big(\lambda+\max\{k,l\}-\frac{d}{2}\big)_{\min\{k,l\}}}{(\lambda)_{k+l}\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}\big)_{\min\{k,l\}}}\big(z_{d+1}+\sqrt{-1}z_{d+2}\big)^{k}\big(z_{d+1}-\sqrt{-1}z_{d+2}\big)^{l}

\displaystyle\qquad\hphantom{=}{}\ \times{}_{2}F_{1}\left(\begin{matrix}-k,-l\\
-\lambda-k-l+\frac{d}{2}+1\end{matrix};-\frac{q(z^{\prime})}{z_{d+1}^{2}+z_{d+2}^{2}}\right)\!.

For
Case
3, for
z=\left(\begin{smallmatrix}z_{11}&z_{12}\\
z_{21}&z_{22}\end{smallmatrix}\right)\in M(r,\mathbb{C})=M(r^{\prime},\mathbb{C})\oplus M(r^{\prime},r^{\prime\prime};\mathbb{C})\oplus M(r^{\prime\prime},r^{\prime};\mathbb{C})\oplus M(r^{\prime\prime},\mathbb{C})
, since

\displaystyle Q(z_{12}){}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{11}^{-\mathit{1}}=\begin{pmatrix}0&z_{12}\\
z_{21}&0\end{pmatrix}\begin{pmatrix}z_{11}^{-1}&0\\
0&0\end{pmatrix}\begin{pmatrix}0&z_{12}\\
z_{21}&0\end{pmatrix}=\begin{pmatrix}0&0\\
0&z_{21}z_{11}^{-1}z_{12}\end{pmatrix}\!,

\displaystyle\tilde{\Phi}_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}}\big(Q(z_{12}){}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{11}^{-\mathit{1}},{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{22}^{-\mathit{1}}\big)=\tilde{\Phi}_{\mathbf{m}}^{(2)}\big(z_{21}z_{11}^{-1}z_{12}z_{22}^{-1}\big)=\tilde{\Phi}_{\mathbf{m}}^{(2)}\big(z_{11}^{-1}z_{12}z_{22}^{-1}z_{21}\big),

where
\tilde{\Phi}_{\mathbf{m}}^{(2)}
is as in (
3.2
), we have

\displaystyle\big\langle\operatorname{det}(x_{11})^{k}\operatorname{det}(x_{22})^{l},{\rm e}^{\operatorname{tr}(xz^{*})}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{(\lambda+l-r^{\prime})_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}},2}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k+l}_{r^{\prime\prime}},2}(\lambda-r^{\prime\prime})_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime}},2}}\operatorname{det}(z_{11})^{k}\operatorname{det}(z_{22})^{l}{}_{2}F_{1}^{(2)}\left(\begin{matrix}-k,-l\\
-\lambda\!-\!k\!-\!l\!+\!r^{\prime}\!+\!r^{\prime\prime}\end{matrix};z_{11}^{-1}z_{12}z_{22}^{-1}z_{21}\right)\!,

where
{}_{2}F_{1}^{(2)}
is as in (
3.6
).
This result is extended to non-tube type case. Let
q=q^{\prime}+q^{\prime\prime}
,
s=s^{\prime}+s^{\prime\prime}
, and assume
q^{\prime}\leq s^{\prime}
,
q^{\prime\prime}\leq s^{\prime\prime}
.
For
z=\left(\begin{smallmatrix}z_{11}&z_{12}\\
z_{21}&z_{22}\end{smallmatrix}\right)\in M(q,s;\mathbb{C})=M(q^{\prime},s^{\prime};\mathbb{C})\oplus M(q^{\prime},s^{\prime\prime};\mathbb{C})\oplus M(q^{\prime\prime},s^{\prime};\mathbb{C})\oplus M(q^{\prime\prime},s^{\prime\prime};\mathbb{C})
,
we write
z_{11}=(z_{11}^{\prime},z_{11}^{\prime\prime})\in M(q^{\prime},\mathbb{C})\oplus M(q^{\prime},s^{\prime}-q^{\prime};\mathbb{C})
,

z_{12}=(z_{12}^{\prime},z_{12}^{\prime\prime})\in M(q^{\prime},q^{\prime\prime};\mathbb{C})\oplus M(q^{\prime},s^{\prime\prime}-q^{\prime\prime};\mathbb{C})
,
z_{21}=(z_{21}^{\prime},z_{21}^{\prime\prime})\in M(q^{\prime\prime},q^{\prime};\mathbb{C})\oplus M(q^{\prime\prime},s^{\prime}-q^{\prime};\mathbb{C})
,

z_{22}=(z_{22}^{\prime},z_{22}^{\prime\prime})\in M(q^{\prime\prime},\mathbb{C})\oplus M(q^{\prime\prime},s^{\prime\prime}-q^{\prime\prime};\mathbb{C})
,
and
z^{\prime}=\left(\begin{smallmatrix}z_{11}^{\prime}&z_{12}^{\prime}\\
z_{21}^{\prime}&z_{22}^{\prime}\end{smallmatrix}\right)\in M(q^{\prime}+q^{\prime\prime},\mathbb{C})
.
Then for
w_{11}=(I_{q^{\prime}},0)\in M(q^{\prime},s^{\prime};\mathbb{C})
,
w_{22}=(I_{q^{\prime\prime}},0)\in M(q^{\prime\prime},s^{\prime\prime};\mathbb{C})
, by Corollary
2.11
we have

\displaystyle\big\langle\operatorname{det}\big(x_{11}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{11}\big)^{k}\operatorname{det}\big(x_{22}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{22}\big)^{l},{\rm e}^{\operatorname{tr}(xz^{*})}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x,M(q,s;\mathbb{C})}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\big\langle\operatorname{det}(x_{11}^{\prime})^{k}\operatorname{det}(x_{22}^{\prime})^{l},{\rm e}^{\operatorname{tr}(x^{\prime}(z^{\prime})^{*})}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x^{\prime},M(q^{\prime}+q^{\prime\prime},\mathbb{C})}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{(\lambda+l-q^{\prime})_{\underline{k}_{q^{\prime\prime}},2}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k+l}_{q^{\prime\prime}},2}(\lambda-q^{\prime\prime})_{\underline{k}_{q^{\prime}},2}}\operatorname{det}(z_{11}^{\prime})^{k}\operatorname{det}(z_{22}^{\prime})^{l}

\displaystyle\qquad\hphantom{=}{}\ \times{}_{2}F_{1}^{(2)}\left(\begin{matrix}-k,-l\\
-\lambda-k-l+q^{\prime}+q^{\prime\prime}\end{matrix};(z_{11}^{\prime})^{-1}z_{12}^{\prime}(z_{22}^{\prime})^{-1}z_{21}^{\prime}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{(\lambda+l-q^{\prime})_{\underline{k}_{q^{\prime\prime}},2}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k+l}_{q^{\prime\prime}},2}(\lambda-q^{\prime\prime})_{\underline{k}_{q^{\prime}},2}}\operatorname{det}\big(z_{11}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{11}\big)^{k}\operatorname{det}\big(z_{22}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{22}\big)^{l}

\displaystyle\qquad\hphantom{=}{}\ \times{}_{2}F_{1}^{(2)}\left(\begin{matrix}-k,-l\\
-\lambda-k-l+q^{\prime}+q^{\prime\prime}\end{matrix};\big(z_{11}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{11}\big)^{-1}z_{12}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{22}\big(z_{22}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{22}\big)^{-1}z_{21}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{11}\right)\!.

By the
{\rm GL}(s^{\prime},\mathbb{C})\times{\rm GL}(s^{\prime\prime},\mathbb{C})
-equivariance,
this holds for all
w_{11}\in M(q^{\prime},s^{\prime};\mathbb{C})
and
w_{22}\in M(q^{\prime\prime},s^{\prime\prime};\mathbb{C})
.
When
q^{\prime}\leq s^{\prime}
,
q^{\prime\prime}\geq s^{\prime\prime}
, a similar formula is also proved by reducing to the inner product on
M(q^{\prime}+s^{\prime\prime},\mathbb{C})
.

For
Case
6, since
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}=M(1,5;\mathbb{C})
is not of tube type, the determinant polynomial on
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}
is not defined.
Instead we consider the polynomial
f(x_{22})=\big(x_{22}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{22}\big)^{l}\in\mathcal{P}_{l}(M(1,5;\mathbb{C}))
with
w_{22}\in M(1,5;\mathbb{C})
.
Then we have

\tilde{f}_{m,l-m}(x_{22},y_{22})=\frac{(-1)^{m}(-l)_{m}}{m!}\big(x_{22}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{22}\big)^{m}\big(y_{22}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{22}\big)^{l-m},

and for
z_{11}\in\mathbb{C}
,
z_{12}\in\operatorname{Skew}(5,\mathbb{C})
, we have
Q(z_{12}){}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{11}^{-\mathit{1}}=\frac{1}{z_{11}}\mathbf{Pf}(z_{12})
,
where
\mathbf{Pf}(z_{12})
is defined as in (
3.10
). Therefore we get

\displaystyle\big\langle x_{11}^{k}\big(x_{22}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{22}\big)^{l},{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}

\displaystyle=\frac{(\lambda-3)_{k}\,z_{11}^{k}}{(\lambda)_{(k+l,k),6}(\lambda-3)_{l}}\sum_{m=0}^{l}(-k)_{m}(\lambda+k-3)_{l-m}\frac{(-1)^{m}(-l)_{m}}{m!}\bigg(\frac{\mathbf{Pf}(z_{12}){}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{22}}{z_{11}}\bigg)^{m}\big(z_{22}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{22}\big)^{l-m}

\displaystyle=\frac{(\lambda+k-3)_{l}}{(\lambda)_{k+l}(\lambda-3)_{l}}z_{11}^{k}\big(z_{22}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{22}\big)^{l}\sum_{m=0}^{l}\frac{(-k)_{m}(-l)_{m}}{(-\lambda-k-l+4)_{m}m!}\bigg(\frac{\mathbf{Pf}(z_{12}){}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{22}}{z_{11}(z_{22}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{22})}\bigg)^{m}\!.

We summarize the results for
f(x_{22})=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}(x_{22})^{l}
.
Here
{}_{2}F_{1}^{(d)}
and
{}_{2}F_{1}^{[d]}
are as in (
3.6
) and (
3.7
).

Theorem 5.9
.

1.

Let
\big(\mathfrak{p}^{+},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{12},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}\big)=\big(\mathbb{C}^{d+2},\mathbb{C},\mathbb{C}^{d},\mathbb{C}\big)
. Then for
k,l\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
and for

z=(z^{\prime},z_{d+1},z_{d+2})\allowbreak\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}
, we have

\displaystyle\big\langle\big(x_{d+1}+\sqrt{-1}x_{d+2}\big)^{k}\big(x_{d+1}-\sqrt{-1}x_{d+2}\big)^{l},{\rm e}^{2q(x,\overline{z})}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{\big(\lambda+\max\{k,l\}-\frac{d}{2}\big)_{\min\{k,l\}}}{(\lambda)_{k+l}\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}\big)_{\min\{k,l\}}}\big(z_{d+1}+\sqrt{-1}z_{d+2}\big)^{k}\big(z_{d+1}-\sqrt{-1}z_{d+2}\big)^{l}

\displaystyle\qquad\hphantom{=}{}\ \times{}_{2}F_{1}\left(\begin{matrix}-k,-l\\
-\lambda-k-l+\frac{d}{2}+1\end{matrix};-\frac{q(z^{\prime})}{z_{d+1}^{2}+z_{d+2}^{2}}\right)\!.

2.

Let
\big(\mathfrak{p}^{+},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{12},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}\big)=({\rm Sym}(r,\mathbb{C}),\operatorname{Sym}(r^{\prime},\mathbb{C}),M(r^{\prime},r^{\prime\prime};\mathbb{C}),\operatorname{Sym}(r^{\prime\prime},\mathbb{C}))
with
r=r^{\prime}+r^{\prime\prime}
. Then for
k,l\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
and for
z=\left(\begin{smallmatrix}z_{11}&z_{12}\\
{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{12}&z_{22}\end{smallmatrix}\right)\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}
, we have

\displaystyle\big\langle\operatorname{det}(x_{11})^{k}\operatorname{det}(x_{22})^{l},{\rm e}^{\operatorname{tr}(xz^{*})}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\begin{cases}\displaystyle\frac{\big(\lambda+\max\{k,l\}-\frac{1}{2}r^{\prime}\big)_{\underline{\min\{k,l\}}_{r^{\prime\prime}},1}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k+l}_{r^{\prime\prime}},1}\big(\lambda-\frac{1}{2}r^{\prime\prime}\big)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime}-r^{\prime\prime}},1}\big(\lambda-\frac{1}{2}r^{\prime}\big)_{\underline{\min\{k,l\}}_{r^{\prime\prime}},1}}&(r^{\prime}\geq r^{\prime\prime})\\
\displaystyle\frac{\big(\lambda+\max\{k,l\}-\frac{1}{2}r^{\prime\prime}\big)_{\underline{\min\{k,l\}}_{r^{\prime}},1}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k+l}_{r^{\prime}},1}\big(\lambda-\frac{1}{2}r^{\prime}\big)_{\underline{l}_{r^{\prime\prime}-r^{\prime}},1}\big(\lambda-\frac{1}{2}r^{\prime\prime}\big)_{\underline{\min\{k,l\}}_{r^{\prime}},1}}&(r^{\prime}\leq r^{\prime\prime})\end{cases}

\displaystyle\qquad\hphantom{=}{}\ \times\operatorname{det}(z_{11})^{k}\operatorname{det}(z_{22})^{l}{}_{2}F_{1}^{(1)}\left(\begin{matrix}-k,-l\\
-\lambda-k-l+\frac{r+1}{2}\end{matrix};z_{11}^{-1}z_{12}z_{22}^{-1}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{12}\right)\!.

3.

Let
\big(\mathfrak{p}^{+},\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{11},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{12},\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{22}\big)=(M(q,s;\mathbb{C}),M(q^{\prime},s^{\prime};\mathbb{C}),M(q^{\prime},s^{\prime\prime};\mathbb{C})\oplus M(q^{\prime\prime},s^{\prime};\mathbb{C}),M(q^{\prime\prime},s^{\prime\prime};\mathbb{C}))

with
q=q^{\prime}+q^{\prime\prime}
,
s=s^{\prime}+s^{\prime\prime}
,
q^{\prime}\leq s^{\prime}
. Then for
k,l\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
and
for
z=\left(\begin{smallmatrix}z_{11}&z_{12}\\
z_{21}&z_{22}\end{smallmatrix}\right)\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}
,
w_{11}\in\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{11}
,
w_{22}\in\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{22}
,
if
q^{\prime\prime}\leq s^{\prime\prime}
, then we have

\displaystyle\big\langle\operatorname{det}\big(x_{11}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{11}\big)^{k}\operatorname{det}\big(x_{22}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{22}\big)^{l},{\rm e}^{\operatorname{tr}(xz^{*})}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\begin{cases}\displaystyle\frac{\big(\lambda+\max\{k,l\}-q^{\prime}\big)_{\underline{\min\{k,l\}}_{q^{\prime\prime}},2}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k+l}_{q^{\prime\prime}},2}(\lambda-q^{\prime\prime})_{\underline{k}_{q^{\prime}-q^{\prime\prime}},2}(\lambda-q^{\prime})_{\underline{\min\{k,l\}}_{q^{\prime\prime}},2}}&(q^{\prime}\geq q^{\prime\prime})\\
\displaystyle\frac{(\lambda+\max\{k,l\}-q^{\prime\prime})_{\underline{\min\{k,l\}}_{q^{\prime}},2}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k+l}_{q^{\prime}},2}(\lambda-q^{\prime})_{\underline{l}_{q^{\prime\prime}-q^{\prime}},2}(\lambda-q^{\prime\prime})_{\underline{\min\{k,l\}}_{q^{\prime}},2}}&(q^{\prime}\leq q^{\prime\prime})\end{cases}

\displaystyle\qquad\hphantom{=}{}\ \times\operatorname{det}\big(z_{11}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{11}\big)^{k}\operatorname{det}\big(z_{22}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{22}\big)^{l}

\displaystyle\qquad\hphantom{=}{}\ \times{}_{2}F_{1}^{(2)}\left(\begin{matrix}-k,-l\\
-\lambda-k-l+q^{\prime}+q^{\prime\prime}\end{matrix};\big(z_{11}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{11}\big)^{-1}z_{12}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{22}\big(z_{22}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{22}\big)^{-1}z_{21}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{11}\right)\!.

Similarly, if
q^{\prime\prime}\geq s^{\prime\prime}
, then we have

\displaystyle\big\langle\operatorname{det}\big(x_{11}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{11}\big)^{k}\operatorname{det}\big({}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{22}x_{22}\big)^{l},{\rm e}^{\operatorname{tr}(xz^{*})}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\begin{cases}\displaystyle\frac{(\lambda+\max\{k,l\}-q^{\prime})_{\underline{\min\{k,l\}}_{s^{\prime\prime}},2}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k+l}_{s^{\prime\prime}},2}(\lambda-s^{\prime\prime})_{\underline{k}_{q^{\prime}-s^{\prime\prime}},2}(\lambda-q^{\prime})_{\underline{\min\{k,l\}}_{s^{\prime\prime}},2}}&(q^{\prime}\geq s^{\prime\prime})\\
\displaystyle\frac{(\lambda+\max\{k,l\}-s^{\prime\prime})_{\underline{\min\{k,l\}}_{q^{\prime}},2}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k+l}_{q^{\prime}},2}\left(\lambda-q^{\prime}\right)_{\underline{l}_{s^{\prime\prime}-q^{\prime}},2}(\lambda-s^{\prime\prime})_{\underline{\min\{k,l\}}_{q^{\prime}},2}}&(q^{\prime}\leq s^{\prime\prime})\end{cases}

\displaystyle\qquad\hphantom{=}{}\ \times\operatorname{det}\big(z_{11}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{11}\big)^{k}\operatorname{det}\big({}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{22}z_{22}\big)^{l}

\displaystyle\qquad\hphantom{=}{}\ \times{}_{2}F_{1}^{(2)}\left(\begin{matrix}-k,-l\\
-\lambda-k-l+q^{\prime}+s^{\prime\prime}\end{matrix};\big(z_{11}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{11}\big)^{-1}z_{12}\big({}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{22}z_{22}\big)^{-1}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{22}z_{21}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{11}\right)\!.

4.

Let
\big(\mathfrak{p}^{+},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{12},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}\big)=({\rm Skew}(2r,\mathbb{C}),\operatorname{Skew}(2r^{\prime},\mathbb{C}),M(2r^{\prime},2r^{\prime\prime};\mathbb{C}),\operatorname{Skew}(2r^{\prime\prime},\mathbb{C}))

with
r=r^{\prime}+r^{\prime\prime}
. Then for
k,l\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
and
for
z=\left(\begin{smallmatrix}z_{11}&z_{12}\\
-{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{12}&z_{22}\end{smallmatrix}\right)\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}
, we have

\displaystyle\big\langle\operatorname{Pf}(x_{11})^{k}\operatorname{Pf}(x_{22})^{l},{\rm e}^{\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{tr}(xz^{*})}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\begin{cases}\displaystyle\frac{(\lambda+\max\{k,l\}-2r^{\prime})_{\underline{\min\{k,l\}}_{r^{\prime\prime}},4}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k+l}_{r^{\prime\prime}},4}(\lambda-2r^{\prime\prime})_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime}-r^{\prime\prime}},4}(\lambda-2r^{\prime})_{\underline{\min\{k,l\}}_{r^{\prime\prime}},4}}&(r^{\prime}\geq r^{\prime\prime})\\
\displaystyle\frac{(\lambda+\max\{k,l\}-2r^{\prime\prime})_{\underline{\min\{k,l\}}_{r^{\prime}},4}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k+l}_{r^{\prime}},4}(\lambda-2r^{\prime})_{\underline{l}_{r^{\prime\prime}-r^{\prime}},4}(\lambda-2r^{\prime\prime})_{\underline{\min\{k,l\}}_{r^{\prime}},4}}&(r^{\prime}\leq r^{\prime\prime})\end{cases}

\displaystyle\qquad\hphantom{=}{}\ \times\operatorname{Pf}(z_{11})^{k}\operatorname{Pf}(z_{22})^{l}{}_{2}F_{1}^{[4]}\left(\begin{matrix}-k,-l\\
-\lambda-k-l+2r-1\end{matrix};-z_{11}^{-1}z_{12}z_{22}^{-1}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{12}\right)\!.

5.

Let
\big(\mathfrak{p}^{+},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{12},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}\big)=\big({\rm Herm}(3,\mathbb{O})^{\mathbb{C}},\mathbb{C},M(1,2;\mathbb{O})^{\mathbb{C}},\operatorname{Herm}(2,\mathbb{O})^{\mathbb{C}}\big)
. Then for
k,l\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
and for
z=\left(\begin{smallmatrix}z_{11}&z_{12}\\
{}^{t}\hskip-1.0pt\hat{z}_{12}&z_{22}\end{smallmatrix}\right)\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}\rule[0.0pt]{0.0pt}{11.0pt}
, we have

\displaystyle\big\langle x_{11}^{k}\operatorname{det}(x_{22})^{l},{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}={}

\displaystyle\frac{(\lambda+\max\{k,l\}-8)_{\min\{k,l\}}}{(\lambda)_{k+l}(\lambda-4)_{l}(\lambda-8)_{\min\{k,l\}}}z_{11}^{k}\operatorname{det}(z_{22})^{l}

\displaystyle{}\times{}_{2}F_{1}\left(\begin{matrix}-k,-l\\
-\lambda-k-l+9\end{matrix};\frac{\operatorname{Re}_{\mathbb{O}}\left(z_{12}(z_{22}^{-1}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0pt\hat{z}_{12})\right)}{z_{11}}\right)\!.

6.

Let
\big(\mathfrak{p}^{+},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{12},\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{22}\big)=\big(M(1,2;\mathbb{O})^{\mathbb{C}},\mathbb{C},\operatorname{Skew}(5,\mathbb{C}),M(1,5;\mathbb{C})\big)
. Then for
k,l\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}

and for
z=(z_{11},z_{12},z_{22})\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}
,
w_{22}\in\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{22}
, we have

\displaystyle\big\langle x_{11}^{k}\big(x_{22}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{22}\big)^{l},{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}={}

\displaystyle\frac{(\lambda+\max\{k,l\}-3)_{\min\{k,l\}}}{(\lambda)_{k+l}(\lambda-3)_{\min\{k,l\}}}z_{11}^{k}\big(z_{22}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{22}\big)^{l}

\displaystyle{}\times{}_{2}F_{1}\left(\begin{matrix}-k,-l\\
-\lambda-k-l+4\end{matrix};\frac{\mathbf{Pf}(z_{12}){}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{22}}{z_{11}(z_{22}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{22})}\right)\!,

where
\mathbf{Pf}(z_{12})
is defined as in (
3.10
).

6
Computation for simple
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}

In this section we treat
\big(\mathfrak{p}^{+},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\big)
with
\mathfrak{p}^{+}=\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1}\oplus\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}
such that
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}
is simple.
Let
\dim\mathfrak{p}^{+}=:n
,
\dim\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}=:n_{2}
,
\operatorname{rank}\mathfrak{p}^{+}=:r
,
\operatorname{rank}\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}=:r_{2}
, let
d
,
d_{2}
be the numbers defined in (
2.3
)
for
\mathfrak{p}^{+}
,
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}
respectively, and let
\varepsilon_{2}\in\{1,2\}
be as in (
4.2
).

6.1
Preliminaries for main theorem

First we consider the cases such that
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}(e)_{2}=\mathfrak{p}^{+}(e)_{2}
holds for some (or equivalently any) maximal tripotent
e\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}
, i.e.,

\big(\mathfrak{p}^{+},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\big)
is one of

\big(\mathfrak{p}^{+},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\big)=\begin{cases}\big(M(q,s;\mathbb{C}),M(q,s^{\prime};\mathbb{C}),M(q,s^{\prime\prime};\mathbb{C})\big),\qquad s^{\prime}+s^{\prime\prime}=s,\\
\big({\rm Skew}(s,\mathbb{C}),\operatorname{Skew}(s-1,\mathbb{C}),M(1,s-1;\mathbb{C})\big),\\
\big({\rm Skew}(s,\mathbb{C}),M(1,s-1;\mathbb{C}),\operatorname{Skew}(s-1,\mathbb{C})\big),\\
\big(\mathbb{C}^{2s},\big(\big(1,\sqrt{-1}\big)\mathbb{C}\big)^{s},\big(\big(1,-\sqrt{-1}\big)\mathbb{C}\big)^{s}\big)\simeq\big(\mathbb{C}^{2s},M(1,s;\mathbb{C}),M(1,s;\mathbb{C})\big),\\
\big(M(1,2;\mathbb{O})^{\mathbb{C}},\mathbb{O}^{\mathbb{C}},\mathbb{O}^{\mathbb{C}}\big)\simeq\big(M(1,2;\mathbb{O})^{\mathbb{C}},\mathbb{C}^{8},\mathbb{C}^{8}\big),\end{cases}

so that the corresponding symmetric pairs are

(G,G_{1})=\begin{cases}\big({\rm SU}(q,s),{\rm S}({\rm U}(q,s^{\prime})\times{\rm U}(s^{\prime\prime}))\big),\\
\big({\rm SO}^{*}(2s),{\rm SO}(2)\times{\rm SO}^{*}(2(s-1))\big),\\
\big({\rm SO}^{*}(2s),{\rm U}(1,s-1)\big),\\
\big({\rm SO}_{0}(2,2s),{\rm U}(1,s)\big),\\
\big(E_{6(-14)},{\rm U}(1)\times\operatorname{Spin}_{0}(2,8)\big).\end{cases}

Then for
\mathbf{k}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r_{2}}
, since
\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2})
and
\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})
are generated by

\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}(e)_{2}\big)=\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{+}(e)_{2}\big)
as
K^{\mathbb{C}}_{1}
- and
K^{\mathbb{C}}
-modules respectively,

\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\big)\subset\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{+}\big)
holds, and therefore by Corollary
2.10
, for
f(x_{2})\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2})
we have

\big\langle f(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}=\frac{1}{(\lambda)_{\mathbf{k},d}}f(z_{2}).

(6.1)

Thus in the following we assume
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}(e)_{2}\subsetneq\mathfrak{p}^{+}(e)_{2}
holds for some (or equivalently any) maximal tripotent
e\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}
.
That is, we treat the cases

\displaystyle\big(\mathfrak{p}^{+},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\big)=\begin{cases}\big(\mathbb{C}^{n},\mathbb{C}^{n^{\prime}},\mathbb{C}^{n^{\prime\prime}}\big)&(\textit{Case }1),\\
\big({\rm Sym}(r,\mathbb{C}),\operatorname{Sym}(r^{\prime},\mathbb{C})\oplus\operatorname{Sym}(r^{\prime\prime},\mathbb{C}),M(r^{\prime},r^{\prime\prime};\mathbb{C})\big)&(\textit{Case }2),\\
\big({\rm Skew}(s,\mathbb{C}),\operatorname{Skew}(s^{\prime},\mathbb{C})\oplus\operatorname{Skew}(s^{\prime\prime},\mathbb{C}),M(s^{\prime},s^{\prime\prime};\mathbb{C})\big)&(\textit{Case }3),\\
\big(M(r,\mathbb{C}),\operatorname{Skew}(r,\mathbb{C}),\operatorname{Sym}(r,\mathbb{C})\big)&(\textit{Case }4),\\
\big(M(r,\mathbb{C}),\operatorname{Sym}(r,\mathbb{C}),\operatorname{Skew}(r,\mathbb{C})\big)&(\textit{Case }5),\\
\big({\rm Herm}(3,\mathbb{O})^{\mathbb{C}},M(2,6;\mathbb{C}),\operatorname{Skew}(6,\mathbb{C})\big)&(\textit{Case }6),\\
\big({\rm Herm}(3,\mathbb{O})^{\mathbb{C}},\operatorname{Skew}(6,\mathbb{C}),M(2,6;\mathbb{C})\big)&(\textit{Case }7),\\
\big({\rm Herm}(3,\mathbb{O})^{\mathbb{C}},\mathbb{C}\oplus\operatorname{Herm}(2,\mathbb{O})^{\mathbb{C}},M(1,2;\mathbb{O})^{\mathbb{C}}\big)&(\textit{Case }8),\\
\big(M(1,2;\mathbb{O})^{\mathbb{C}},M(2,4;\mathbb{C}),M(4,2;\mathbb{C})\big)&(\textit{Case }9),\\
\big(M(1,2;\mathbb{O})^{\mathbb{C}},\mathbb{C}\oplus M(1,5;\mathbb{C}),\operatorname{Skew}(5,\mathbb{C})\big)&(\textit{Case }10)\end{cases}

(
n=n^{\prime}+n^{\prime\prime}
,
n\geq 3
,
n^{\prime\prime}\neq 2
for
Case
1,
r=r^{\prime}+r^{\prime\prime}
for
Case
2, and
s=s^{\prime}+s^{\prime\prime}
,
s^{\prime},s^{\prime\prime}\geq 2
for
Case
3).
Then the corresponding symmetric pairs are

(G,G_{1})=\begin{cases}\big({\rm SO}_{0}(2,n),{\rm SO}_{0}(2,n^{\prime})\times{\rm SO}(n^{\prime\prime})\big)&(\textit{Case }1),\\
\big({\rm Sp}(r,\mathbb{R}),\operatorname{Sp}(r^{\prime},\mathbb{R})\times\operatorname{Sp}(r^{\prime\prime},\mathbb{R})\big)&(\textit{Case }2),\\
\big({\rm SO}^{*}(2s),{\rm SO}^{*}(2s^{\prime})\times{\rm SO}^{*}(2s^{\prime\prime})\big)&(\textit{Case }3),\\
\big({\rm SU}(r,r),{\rm SO}^{*}(2r))&(\textit{Case }4\big),\\
\big({\rm SU}(r,r),\operatorname{Sp}(r,\mathbb{R})\big)&(\textit{Case }5),\\
\big(E_{7(-25)},{\rm SU}(2,6)\big)&(\textit{Case }6),\\
\big(E_{7(-25)},{\rm SU}(2)\times{\rm SO}^{*}(12)\big)&(\textit{Case }7),\\
\big(E_{7(-25)},{\rm SL}(2,\mathbb{R})\times\operatorname{Spin}_{0}(2,10)\big)&(\textit{Case }8),\\
\big(E_{6(-14)},{\rm SU}(2,4)\times{\rm SU}(2)\big)&(\textit{Case }9),\\
\big(E_{6(-14)},{\rm SL}(2,\mathbb{R})\times{\rm SU}(1,5)\big)&(\textit{Case }10)\end{cases}

(up to covering), and the numbers
(r,r_{2},d,d_{2},\varepsilon_{2})
are given by

(r,r_{2},d,d_{2},\varepsilon_{2})=\begin{cases}(2,2,n-2,n^{\prime\prime}-2,1)&(\textit{Case }1,\;n^{\prime\prime}\geq 3),\\
(2,1,n-2,-,2)&(\textit{Case }1,\;n^{\prime\prime}=1),\\
(r,\min\{r^{\prime},r^{\prime\prime}\},1,2,2)&(\textit{Case }2),\\
(\lfloor s/2\rfloor,\min\{s^{\prime},s^{\prime\prime}\},4,2,1)&(\textit{Case }3),\\
(r,r,2,1,1)&(\textit{Case }4),\\
(r,\lfloor r/2\rfloor,2,4,2)&(\textit{Case }5),\\
(3,3,8,4,1)&(\textit{Case }6),\\
(3,2,8,2,1)&(\textit{Case }7),\\
(3,2,8,6,1)&(\textit{Case }8),\\
(2,2,6,2,1)&(\textit{Case }9),\\
(2,2,6,4,1)&(\textit{Case }10).\end{cases}

We note that if
r_{2}=1
, then
d_{2}
is not determined uniquely, and any number is allowed.
We also write

(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{12},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22})=\begin{cases}\big({\rm Sym}(r^{\prime},\mathbb{C}),M(r^{\prime},r^{\prime\prime};\mathbb{C}),\operatorname{Sym}(r^{\prime\prime},\mathbb{C})\big)&(\textit{Case }2),\\
\big({\rm Skew}(s^{\prime},\mathbb{C}),M(s^{\prime},s^{\prime\prime};\mathbb{C}),\operatorname{Skew}(s^{\prime\prime},\mathbb{C})\big)&(\textit{Case }3),\\
\big(\mathbb{C},M(1,2;\mathbb{O})^{\mathbb{C}},\operatorname{Herm}(2,\mathbb{O})^{\mathbb{C}}\big)&(\textit{Case }8),\\
\big(\mathbb{C},\operatorname{Skew}(5,\mathbb{C}),M(1,5;\mathbb{C})\big)&(\textit{Case }10).\end{cases}

First we prove the following.

Proposition 6.1
.

1.

Let
\varepsilon_{2}=1
. Then for
l\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
,
\mathcal{P}_{(l,0,\dots,0)}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{2}\big)\subset\mathcal{P}_{(l,0,\dots,0)}(\mathfrak{p}^{\pm})
holds.

2.

Let
\varepsilon_{2}=2
. Then for
0\leq l\leq r_{2}
,
\mathcal{P}_{\underline{1}_{l}}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{2}\big)=\mathcal{P}_{(\smash{\overbrace{\scriptstyle{1,\dots,1}}^{l}},0,\dots,0)}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{2}\big)\subset\mathcal{P}_{\underline{1}_{l}}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}\big)
holds.

Proof.

(1) For
Case
1, since
\mathcal{P}_{(l,0)}(\mathbb{C}^{n^{\prime\prime}})
,
\mathcal{P}_{(l,0)}(\mathbb{C}^{n})
coincide with the spaces of homogeneous harmonic polynomials of degree
l

of
n^{\prime\prime},n
variables respectively,
\mathcal{P}_{(l,0)}(\mathbb{C}^{n^{\prime\prime}})\subset\mathcal{P}_{(l,0)}(\mathbb{C}^{n})
holds.
For other cases, we take a tripotent
e\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}
of rank 2, and set
\mathfrak{p}^{+}(e)_{2}=:\mathfrak{p}^{+\prime}
,
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}(e)_{2}=:\mathfrak{p}^{+\prime}_{2}
.
Then these are of rank 2 and we have
\mathfrak{p}^{+\prime}\simeq\mathbb{C}^{d+2}
,
\mathfrak{p}^{+\prime}_{2}\simeq\mathbb{C}^{d_{2}+2}
, and by
Case
1,

\mathcal{P}_{(l,0)}(\mathfrak{p}^{+\prime}_{2})\subset\mathcal{P}_{(l,0)}(\mathfrak{p}^{+\prime})
holds.
Since
\mathcal{P}_{(l,0,\dots,0)}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})
and
\mathcal{P}_{(l,0,\dots,0)}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\big)
are generated by
\mathcal{P}_{(l,0)}(\mathfrak{p}^{+\prime})

and
\mathcal{P}_{(l,0)}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{+\prime}_{2}\big)
as
K^{\mathbb{C}}
- and
K_{1}^{\mathbb{C}}
-modules respectively, we have

\mathcal{P}_{(l,0,\dots,0)}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2})\subset\mathcal{P}_{(l,0,\dots,0)}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})
.

(2) For
Case
1 with
n^{\prime\prime}=1
, this is proved as in
n^{\prime\prime}\geq 3
cases. For
Case
2, for
0\leq l\leq\min\{r^{\prime},r^{\prime\prime}\}
,
\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}
,
we consider the space of abstract homomorphisms

\displaystyle\operatorname{Hom}_{{\rm U}(r^{\prime})\times{\rm U}(r^{\prime\prime})}\big(\mathcal{P}_{\underline{1}_{l}}(M(r^{\prime},r^{\prime\prime};\mathbb{C})),\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{m}}({\rm Sym}(r,\mathbb{C}))\big)

\displaystyle\qquad{}\simeq\operatorname{Hom}_{{\rm U}(r^{\prime})\times{\rm U}(r^{\prime\prime})}\big(V_{\underline{1}_{l}}^{(r^{\prime})\vee}\boxtimes V_{\underline{1}_{l}}^{(r^{\prime\prime})\vee},V_{2\mathbf{m}}^{(r)\vee}\bigr|_{{\rm U}(r^{\prime})\times{\rm U}(r^{\prime\prime})}\big)

\displaystyle\qquad{}\simeq\operatorname{Hom}_{{\rm U}(r)}\big(V_{2\mathbf{m}}^{(r)\vee},V_{\underline{1}_{l}}^{(r)\vee}\otimes V_{\underline{1}_{l}}^{(r)\vee}\big),

where the last isomorphism follows from
[
12
, Theorem 9.2.3]
. Then since the target space is decomposed as

V_{\underline{1}_{l}}^{(r)\vee}\otimes V_{\underline{1}_{l}}^{(r)\vee}\simeq\bigoplus_{j=0}^{l}V_{(\underline{2}_{j},\underline{1}_{2l-2j})}^{(r)\vee},

the above space is non-zero only when
\mathbf{m}=\underline{1}_{l}
. Finally, for
Case
5, for
0\leq l\leq\lfloor r/2\rfloor
,
\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}
,

\operatorname{Hom}_{{\rm U}(r)}\left(\mathcal{P}_{\underline{1}_{l}}({\rm Skew}(r,\mathbb{C})),\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{m}}(M(r,\mathbb{C}))\right)\simeq\operatorname{Hom}_{{\rm U}(r)}\big(V_{\underline{1}_{2l}}^{(r)\vee},V_{\mathbf{m}}^{(r)\vee}\otimes V_{\mathbf{m}}^{(r)\vee}\big)\neq\{0\}

implies
2|\mathbf{m}|=|\underline{1}_{2l}|=2l
and
m_{j}\leq(\underline{1}_{2l})_{j}
for
1\leq j\leq r
,
that is, we have
\mathbf{m}=\underline{1}_{l}
.
∎

From now on we assume that
\mathfrak{p}^{+}
and
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}
are of tube type, that is, we consider
Case
1,
Case
2 with
r^{\prime}=r^{\prime\prime}
,
Case
3 with
s^{\prime}=s^{\prime\prime}
,

Case
4,
Case
5 with
r=2s
, and
Case
6.
We fix a maximal tripotent
e\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\subset\mathfrak{p}^{+}
, regard
\mathfrak{p}^{+}
,
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}
as Jordan algebras,
and let
\Omega=\Omega^{+}\subset\mathfrak{n}^{+}\subset\mathfrak{p}^{+}
,
\Omega_{2}=\Omega_{2}^{+}\subset\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}\subset\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}
be the corresponding Euclidean real forms
and the symmetric cones. We also write
\Omega^{-}:=Q(\overline{e})\Omega\subset\mathfrak{n}^{-}\subset\mathfrak{p}^{-}
,

\Omega^{-}_{2}:=Q(\overline{e})\Omega_{2}\subset\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{2}\subset\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{2}
.
Then by Theorem
4.4
(1),
when
\varepsilon_{2}=1
, for
f(x_{2})\in\mathcal{P}_{(l,0,\dots,0)}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2})
,
z=z_{1}+z_{2}\in\Omega
we have

\displaystyle\big\langle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(x_{2})^{k}f(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{1}{(\lambda)_{\underline{2k}_{r}+(l,0,\dots,0),d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{-\lambda+\frac{n}{r}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}\bigg(\frac{\partial}{\partial z_{2}}\bigg)^{k}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{\lambda+2k-\frac{n}{r}}f(z_{2})

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{1}{(\lambda)_{(2k+l,\underline{2k}_{r-1}),d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{-\lambda+\frac{n}{r}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}\bigg(\frac{\partial}{\partial z_{2}}\bigg)^{k}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{\lambda+2k-\frac{n}{r}}f(z_{2}),

and when
\varepsilon_{2}=2
, for
f(x_{2})\in\mathcal{P}_{\underline{1}_{l}}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\big)
,
z=z_{1}+z_{2}\in\Omega
we have

\displaystyle\big\langle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(x_{2})^{k}f(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{1}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k}_{r}+\underline{1}_{l},d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{-\lambda+\frac{n}{r}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}\bigg(\frac{1}{2}\frac{\partial}{\partial z_{2}}\bigg)^{k}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{\lambda+k-\frac{n}{r}}f(z_{2})

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{1}{(\lambda)_{(\underline{k+1}_{l},\underline{k}_{r-l}),d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{-\lambda+\frac{n}{r}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}\bigg(\frac{1}{2}\frac{\partial}{\partial z_{2}}\bigg)^{k}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{\lambda+k-\frac{n}{r}}f(z_{2}).

Also, by Proposition
2.4
(1), for
\mu\in\mathbb{C}
we have

\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{-\mu}=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z_{2})^{-\mu}h_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}\big(z_{1},Q(z_{2})^{-1}z_{1}\big)^{-\mu/2}=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})^{-\varepsilon_{2}\mu}h_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}\big(z_{1},Q\big({}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{2}^{-\mathit{1}}\big)z_{1}\big)^{-\mu/2}.

Next we consider the expansion of this.

Proposition 6.2
.

For
(z_{1},w_{2})\in\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{1}\times\mathfrak{p}^{\mp}_{2}
, let
t_{1},\dots,t_{\lfloor r/2\rfloor}
be the roots of the polynomial

t^{\lfloor r/2\rfloor}h_{\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}}\big(t^{-1}z_{1},Q(w_{2})z_{1}\big)^{1/2}\in\mathbb{C}[t]
,
and for
\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{\lfloor r/2\rfloor}
,
define
\tilde{\Phi}_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{1},\mathfrak{p}^{\mp}_{2}}(z_{1},w_{2})\in\mathcal{P}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{1}\big)\otimes\mathcal{P}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{\mp}_{2}\big)
by

\tilde{\Phi}_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{1},\mathfrak{p}^{\mp}_{2}}(z_{1},w_{2}):=\tilde{\Phi}_{\mathbf{m}}^{d}(t_{1},\dots,t_{\lfloor r/2\rfloor}),

where
\tilde{\Phi}_{\mathbf{m}}^{d}(t_{1},\dots,t_{\lfloor r/2\rfloor})
is as in (
2.7
). Then for
|t_{j}|<1
,

h_{\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}}(z_{1},Q(w_{2})z_{1})^{-\mu/2}=\sum_{\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{\lfloor r/2\rfloor}}(\mu)_{\mathbf{m},d}\tilde{\Phi}_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{1},\mathfrak{p}^{\mp}_{2}}(z_{1},w_{2})

holds. Moreover,

1.

If
\varepsilon_{2}=1
, then
\tilde{\Phi}_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{1},\mathfrak{p}^{\mp}_{2}}(z_{1},w_{2})\in\mathcal{P}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{1}\big)\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{m}^{2}}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{\mp}_{2}\big)
holds.

2.

If
\varepsilon_{2}=2
, then
\tilde{\Phi}_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{1},\mathfrak{p}^{\mp}_{2}}(z_{1},w_{2})\in\mathcal{P}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{1}\big)\otimes\mathcal{P}_{2\mathbf{m}}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{\mp}_{2}\big)
holds.

Here we write
\mathbf{m}^{2}=(m_{1},m_{1},m_{2},m_{2},\dots,m_{\lfloor r/2\rfloor},m_{\lfloor r/2\rfloor}(,0))
.
We note that since for
(z_{1},w_{2})\allowbreak=(z_{1},{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{2}^{-\mathit{1}})
with
z=z_{1}+z_{2}\in\Omega
we have

\displaystyle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})^{\varepsilon_{2}}h_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}\big(z_{1},Q\big({}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{2}^{-\mathit{1}}\big)z_{1}\big)^{1/2}=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})^{\varepsilon_{2}}\prod_{j=1}^{\lfloor r/2\rfloor}(1-t_{j})>0,

\displaystyle h_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}\big(z_{1},Q\big({}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{2}^{-\mathit{1}}\big)z_{1}\big)=h_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}\big(z_{1},P\big(z_{2}^{-\mathit{1}}\big)z_{1}\big)=h_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}\big(P\big(z_{2}^{-\mathit{1/2}}\big)z_{1},P\big(z_{2}^{-\mathit{1/2}}\big)z_{1}\big),

and since
\Omega
is connected, we have
0\leq t_{j}<1
, and the above sum converges for
(z_{1},w_{2})=\big(z_{1},{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{2}^{-\mathit{1}}\big)
.

Proof.

First, for
Case
1 with
n^{\prime\prime}\geq 3
, we have
r/2=1
, and for
z_{1}\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1}=\mathbb{C}^{n^{\prime}}
,
w_{2}\in\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{2}=\mathbb{C}^{n^{\prime\prime}}
, since

th_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}\big(t^{-1}z_{1},Q(w_{2})z_{1}\big)^{1/2}=t\bigg(1+\frac{2}{t}q(z_{1})q(w_{2})+\frac{1}{t^{2}}q(z_{1})^{2}q(w_{2})^{2}\bigg)^{1/2}=t+q(z_{1})q(w_{2}),

we have
t=-q(z_{1})q(w_{2})
, and

\displaystyle\tilde{\Phi}_{m}^{\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1},\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{2}}(z_{1},w_{2})=\frac{1}{m!}t^{m}=\frac{1}{m!}(-q(z_{1})q(w_{2}))^{m}\in\mathcal{P}_{(m,m)}(\mathbb{C}^{n^{\prime}})_{z_{1}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{(m,m)}(\mathbb{C}^{n^{\prime\prime}})_{w_{2}},

\displaystyle h_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}(z_{1},Q(w_{2})z_{1})^{-\mu/2}=(1+q(z_{1})q(w_{2}))^{-\mu}=\sum_{m=0}^{\infty}\frac{(\mu)_{m}}{m!}(-q(z_{1})q(w_{2}))^{m}

hold. For
n^{\prime\prime}=1
case this is also proved similarly.
Next we consider
Cases
2, 3. For
Case
2 we have
r=2r^{\prime}
,
\varepsilon_{2}=2
, and for
Case

r=s^{\prime}
,
\varepsilon_{2}=1
.
Then for

,

\pm{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{12}&0\end{smallmatrix}\right)\in\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{2}
with

\operatorname{Skew}(s^{\prime},\mathbb{C})\oplus M(s^{\prime},\mathbb{C})\oplus\operatorname{Skew}(s^{\prime},\mathbb{C})&(\textit{Case }3),\end{cases}

we have

\displaystyle t^{\lfloor r/2\rfloor}h_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}\big(t^{-1}z_{1},Q(w_{2})z_{1}\big)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=t^{\lfloor r/2\rfloor}\operatorname{det}\left(I-t^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}z_{11}&0\\
0&z_{22}\end{pmatrix}\begin{pmatrix}0&\pm w_{12}\\
{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{12}&0\end{pmatrix}\begin{pmatrix}z_{11}&0\\
0&z_{22}\end{pmatrix}\begin{pmatrix}0&\pm w_{12}\\
{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{12}&0\end{pmatrix}\right)^{\varepsilon_{2}/4}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=t^{\lfloor r/2\rfloor}\operatorname{det}\left(I-t^{-1}\begin{pmatrix}\pm z_{11}w_{12}z_{22}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{12}&0\\
0&\pm z_{22}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{12}z_{11}w_{12}\end{pmatrix}\right)^{\varepsilon_{2}/4}

\displaystyle\qquad=t^{\lfloor r/2\rfloor}\operatorname{det}\left(I\mp t^{-1}z_{11}w_{12}z_{22}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{12}\right)^{\varepsilon_{2}/2}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\begin{cases}\operatorname{det}\left(tI-z_{11}w_{12}z_{22}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{12}\right)&(\textit{Case }2),\\
\operatorname{det}\left(tI+z_{11}w_{12}z_{22}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{12}\right)^{1/2}&(\textit{Case }3,\ s^{\prime}\colon\text{even}),\\
\left(t^{-1}\operatorname{det}\left(tI+z_{11}w_{12}z_{22}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{12}\right)\right)^{1/2}&(\textit{Case }3,\ s^{\prime}\colon\text{odd}).\end{cases}

Therefore we have

\tilde{\Phi}_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1},\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{2}}(z_{1},w_{2})=\begin{cases}\tilde{\Phi}_{\mathbf{m}}^{(1)}\left(z_{11}w_{12}z_{22}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{12}\right)=\tilde{\Phi}_{\mathbf{m}}^{\operatorname{Sym}(r^{\prime},\mathbb{C})}\left(z_{11},w_{12}z_{22}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{12}\right)&(\textit{Case }2),\\
\tilde{\Phi}_{\mathbf{m}}^{[4]}\left(-z_{11}w_{12}z_{22}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{12}\right)=\tilde{\Phi}_{\mathbf{m}}^{\operatorname{Skew}(s^{\prime},\mathbb{C})}\left(z_{11},w_{12}z_{22}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{12}\right)&(\textit{Case }3),\end{cases}

(6.2)

where
\tilde{\Phi}_{\mathbf{m}}^{(1)}
,
\tilde{\Phi}_{\mathbf{m}}^{[4]}
and
\tilde{\Phi}_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}}
(with

\displaystyle\hphantom{h_{\mathfrak{p}^+}(z_1,Q(w_2)z_1)^{-\mu/2}}{}=\sum_{\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r^{\prime}}}(\mu)_{\mathbf{m},1}\tilde{\Phi}_{\mathbf{m}}^{(1)}\big(z_{11}w_{12}z_{22}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{12}\big)\hskip 36.98857pt(\textit{Case }2),

\displaystyle\hphantom{h_{\mathfrak{p}^+}(z_1,Q(w_2)z_1)^{-\mu/2}}{}=\sum_{\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{\lfloor s^{\prime}/2\rfloor}}(\mu)_{\mathbf{m},4}\tilde{\Phi}_{\mathbf{m}}^{[4]}\big({-}z_{11}w_{12}z_{22}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{12}\big)\qquad(\textit{Case }3).

Since as polynomials of
z_{11}
,

,

&\qquad{}\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{m}}({\rm Sym}(r^{\prime},\mathbb{C}))_{z_{11}}\boxtimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{m}}({\rm Sym}(r^{\prime},\mathbb{C}))_{z_{22}}\simeq V_{2\mathbf{m}}^{(r^{\prime})\vee}\boxtimes V_{2\mathbf{m}}^{(r^{\prime})\vee}\hskip 28.45274pt(\textit{Case }2),\\

hold, by the

- resp.

\displaystyle\tilde{\Phi}_{\mathbf{m}}^{(1)}\big(z_{11}w_{12}z_{22}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{12}\big)\in\mathcal{P}_{2\mathbf{m}}(M(r^{\prime},\mathbb{C}))_{w_{12}}\simeq V_{2\mathbf{m}}^{(r^{\prime})}\boxtimes V_{2\mathbf{m}}^{(r^{\prime})}\qquad

\displaystyle(\textit{Case }2),

\displaystyle\tilde{\Phi}_{\mathbf{m}}^{[4]}\big({-}z_{11}w_{12}z_{22}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{12}\big)\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{m}^{2}}(M(s^{\prime},\mathbb{C}))_{w_{12}}\simeq V_{\mathbf{m}^{2}}^{(s^{\prime})}\boxtimes V_{\mathbf{m}^{2}}^{(s^{\prime})}\qquad

\displaystyle(\textit{Case }3)

hold. Next we consider

. Then for

we have

\displaystyle t^{\lfloor r/2\rfloor}h_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}\big(t^{-1}z_{1},Q(w_{2})z_{1}\big)^{1/2}=t^{\lfloor r/2\rfloor}\operatorname{det}\big(I-t^{-1}z_{1}w_{2}z_{1}w_{2}\big)^{1/2},

\displaystyle\tilde{\Phi}_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1},\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{2}}(z_{1},w_{2})=\tilde{\Phi}_{\mathbf{m}}^{[2]}(z_{1}w_{2}z_{1}w_{2}),

\displaystyle(\textit{Case }4),

\displaystyle\tilde{\Phi}_{\mathbf{m}}^{[2]}(z_{1}J_{2s}z_{1}J_{2s})\in(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{m}^{2}}({\rm Sym}(2s,\mathbb{C})))^{\operatorname{Sp}(s,\mathbb{C})}\simeq\big(V_{2\mathbf{m}^{2}}^{(2s)\vee}\big)^{\operatorname{Sp}(s,\mathbb{C})}\qquad

-invariance we get

\displaystyle\tilde{\Phi}_{\mathbf{m}}^{[2]}(z_{1}w_{2}z_{1}w_{2})

\displaystyle\in\begin{cases}\big(\mathcal{P}_{2\mathbf{m}}({\rm Skew}(r,\mathbb{C}))_{z_{1}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{m}^{2}}({\rm Sym}(r,\mathbb{C}))_{w_{2}}\big)^{{\rm GL}(r,\mathbb{C})}&(\textit{Case }4),\\
\big(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{m}^{2}}({\rm Sym}(2s,\mathbb{C}))_{z_{1}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{2\mathbf{m}}({\rm Skew}(2s,\mathbb{C}))_{w_{2}}\big)^{{\rm GL}(2s,\mathbb{C})}&(\textit{Case }5)\end{cases}

\displaystyle\simeq\big(V_{2\mathbf{m}^{2}}^{(r)\vee}\otimes V_{2\mathbf{m}^{2}}^{(r)}\big)^{{\rm GL}(r,\mathbb{C})}.

Finally, for
Case
6, we have
r=3
, and for
z_{1}\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1}=M(2,6;\mathbb{C})
,
w_{2}\in\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{2}=\operatorname{Skew}(6,\mathbb{C})
, since

\displaystyle th_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}\big(t^{-1}z_{1},Q(w_{2})z_{1}\big)^{1/2}

\displaystyle=th_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1}}(t^{-1}z_{1},Q(w_{2})z_{1})^{1/2}=t\operatorname{det}\big(I-t^{-1}z_{1}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0pt\big(J_{2}z_{1}w_{2}^{\#}\big)\big)^{1/2}

\displaystyle=t\operatorname{det}\big(I-t^{-1}z_{1}w_{2}^{\#}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{1}J_{2}\big)^{1/2}=\operatorname{Pf}\big(tJ_{2}+z_{1}w_{2}^{\#}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{1}\big)

\displaystyle=t+\operatorname{Pf}\big(z_{1}w_{2}^{\#}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{1}\big),

where
w_{2}^{\#}
is defined as in (
3.9
), we have
t=-\operatorname{Pf}\big(z_{1}w_{2}^{\#}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{1}\big)
, and

\displaystyle\tilde{\Phi}_{m}^{\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1},\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{2}}(z_{1},w_{2})=\frac{1}{m!}t^{m}=\frac{1}{m!}\big({-}\operatorname{Pf}\big(z_{1}w_{2}^{\#}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{1}\big)\big)^{m},

(6.3)

\displaystyle h_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}(z_{1},Q(w_{2})z_{1})^{-\mu/2}=\big(1+\operatorname{Pf}\big(z_{1}w_{2}^{\#}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{1}\big)\big)^{-\mu}=\sum_{m=0}^{\infty}\frac{(\mu)_{m}}{m!}\big({-}\operatorname{Pf}\big(z_{1}w_{2}^{\#}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{1}\big)\big)^{m}.

Since
\frac{1}{m!}\big({-}\operatorname{Pf}\big(z_{1}w_{2}^{\#}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{1}\big)\big)^{m}
is rewritten as

\displaystyle\frac{1}{m!}\big({-}\operatorname{Pf}\big(z_{1}w_{2}^{\#}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{1}\big)\big)^{m}=\tilde{\Phi}_{m}^{[4]}\big(z_{1}w_{2}^{\#}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{1}J\big)=\tilde{\Phi}_{(m,0,0)}^{[4]}\big(w_{2}^{\#}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{1}Jz_{1}\big),

by the
{\rm S}({\rm GL}(2,\mathbb{C})\times{\rm GL}(6,\mathbb{C}))
-invariance we conclude that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{m!}\big({-}\operatorname{Pf}\big(z_{1}w_{2}^{\#}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{1}\big)\big)^{m}

\displaystyle\qquad{}\in\big(\mathcal{P}_{(m,m)}(M(2,6;\mathbb{C}))_{z_{1}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{(m,m,0)}({\rm Skew}(6,\mathbb{C}))_{w_{2}}\big)^{{\rm S}({\rm GL}(2,\mathbb{C})\times{\rm GL}(6,\mathbb{C}))}

\displaystyle\qquad{}\simeq\big(\big(V_{(m,m)}^{(2)\vee}\boxtimes V_{(0,0,0,0,-m,-m)}^{(6)\vee}\big)\otimes\big(V_{(m,m)}^{(2)}\boxtimes V_{(0,0,0,0,-m,-m)}^{(6)}\big)\big)^{{\rm S}({\rm GL}(2,\mathbb{C})\times{\rm GL}(6,\mathbb{C}))}

holds.
∎

Using
\tilde{\Phi}_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{1},\mathfrak{p}^{\mp}_{2}}(z_{1},w_{2})
,
for
\alpha,\beta,\gamma\in\mathbb{C}
with
\gamma\notin\bigcup_{j=1}^{\lfloor r/2\rfloor}\big(\frac{d}{2}(j-1)-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}\big)
we define

\displaystyle{}_{0}F_{1}^{\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{1},\mathfrak{p}^{\mp}_{2}}(\gamma;z_{1},w_{2}):=\sum_{\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{\lfloor r/2\rfloor}}\frac{1}{(\gamma)_{\mathbf{m},d}}\tilde{\Phi}_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{1},\mathfrak{p}^{\mp}_{2}}(z_{1},w_{2}),

(6.4)

\displaystyle{}_{2}F_{1}^{\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{1},\mathfrak{p}^{\mp}_{2}}\left(\begin{matrix}\alpha,\beta\\
\gamma\end{matrix};z_{1},w_{2}\right):=\sum_{\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{\lfloor r/2\rfloor}}\frac{(\alpha)_{\mathbf{m},d}(\beta)_{\mathbf{m},d}}{(\gamma)_{\mathbf{m},d}}\tilde{\Phi}_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{1},\mathfrak{p}^{\mp}_{2}}(z_{1},w_{2}).

(6.5)

Next we consider the expansion of
\tilde{\Phi}_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{1},\mathfrak{p}^{\mp}_{2}}(z_{1},w_{2})f\big({}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{2}^{-\mathit{1}}\big)
.
When
\varepsilon_{2}=1
, we have
r=r_{2}
. For
f(x_{2})\in\mathcal{P}_{(l,0,\dots,0)}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{2}\big)
and for
\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{\lfloor r/2\rfloor}
,

\mathbf{l}\in(\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0})^{\lceil r/2\rceil}
with
|\mathbf{l}|=l
,
we define
F_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{l}}^{1}[f](z_{1},w_{2})\in\mathcal{P}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{1}\big)_{z_{1}}\otimes\mathcal{P}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{\mp}_{2}\big)_{w_{2}}
by

\displaystyle\tilde{\Phi}_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{1},\mathfrak{p}^{\mp}_{2}}(z_{1},w_{2})f\big({}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{2}^{-\mathit{1}}\big)=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{l}\in(\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0})^{\lceil r/2\rceil}\\
|\mathbf{l}|=l\end{subarray}}F_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{l}}^{1}[f](z_{1},w_{2}),

\displaystyle F_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{l}}^{1}[f](z_{1},w_{2})

\displaystyle\qquad{}\in\begin{cases}\mathcal{P}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{1}\big)_{z_{1}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\begin{subarray}{c}(m_{1},m_{1}-l_{1},m_{2},m_{2}-l_{2},\dots,m_{r/2},m_{r/2}-l_{r/2})\end{subarray}}\big(\mathfrak{p}_{2}^{\mp}\big)_{w_{2}},&r\colon\text{even},\\
\mathcal{P}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{1}\big)_{z_{1}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\begin{subarray}{c}(m_{1},m_{1}-l_{1},m_{2},m_{2}-l_{2},\dots,m_{\lfloor r/2\rfloor},m_{\lfloor r/2\rfloor}-l_{\lfloor r/2\rfloor},-l_{\lceil r/2\rceil})\end{subarray}}\big(\mathfrak{p}_{2}^{\mp}\big)_{w_{2}},&r\colon\text{odd}.\end{cases}

Then
F_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{l}}^{1}[f](z_{1},w_{2})\neq 0
holds only if
0\leq l_{j}\leq m_{j}-m_{j+1}
(
1\leq j<\lfloor r/2\rfloor
) and

0\leq l_{\lfloor r/2\rfloor}\leq m_{\lfloor r/2\rfloor}
(only when
r
is odd).
Similarly, when
\varepsilon_{2}=2
, we have
r=2r_{2}
. For
f(x_{2})\in\mathcal{P}_{\underline{1}_{l}}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{2}\big)
and for
\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r_{2}}
,

\mathbf{l}\in\{0,1\}^{r_{2}}
with
|\mathbf{l}|=l
, we define
F_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{l}}^{2}[f](z_{1},w_{2})\in\mathcal{P}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{1}\big)_{z_{1}}\otimes\mathcal{P}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{\mp}_{2}\big)_{w_{2}}
by

\displaystyle\tilde{\Phi}_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{1},\mathfrak{p}^{\mp}_{2}}(z_{1},w_{2})f\big({}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{2}^{-\mathit{1}}\big)=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{l}\in\{0,1\}^{r_{2}}\\
|\mathbf{l}|=l\end{subarray}}F_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{l}}^{2}[f](z_{1},w_{2}),

\displaystyle F_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{l}}^{2}[f](z_{1},w_{2})\in\mathcal{P}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{1}\big)_{z_{1}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{2\mathbf{m}-\mathbf{l}}\big(\mathfrak{p}_{2}^{\mp}\big)_{w_{2}}.

Then
F_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{l}}^{2}[f](z_{1},w_{2})\neq 0
holds only if
2\mathbf{m}-\mathbf{l}\in\mathbb{Z}_{+}^{r_{2}}
, or equivalently
\mathbf{m}-\mathbf{l}\in\mathbb{Z}_{+}^{r_{2}}
.
Then for
\mu\in\mathbb{C}
we have

\displaystyle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{-\mu}f(z_{2})

\displaystyle=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})^{-\varepsilon_{2}\mu}h_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}\big(z_{1},Q(z_{2})^{-1}z_{1}\big)^{-\mu/2}f(z_{2})

\displaystyle=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})^{-\varepsilon_{2}\mu}\sum_{\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{\lfloor r/2\rfloor}}(\mu)_{\mathbf{m},d}\tilde{\Phi}_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1},\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{2}}\big(z_{1},{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{2}^{-\mathit{1}}\big)f(z_{2})

\displaystyle=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})^{-\varepsilon_{2}\mu}\sum_{\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{\lfloor r/2\rfloor}}\sum_{\mathbf{l}}(\mu)_{\mathbf{m},d}F_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{l}}^{\varepsilon_{2}}[f]\big(z_{1},{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{2}^{-\mathit{1}}\big).

(6.6)

6.2
Main theorem

We continue to assume
\mathfrak{p}^{+}
,
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}
are of tube type.
When
\varepsilon_{2}=1
, for
f(x_{2})\in\mathcal{P}_{(l,0,\dots,0)}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{2}\big)
,
\mu,\nu\in\mathbb{C}

and for
z=z_{1}+z_{2}\in\Omega^{\pm}\subset\mathfrak{n}^{\pm}\subset\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}
, let

\displaystyle F_{1}^{l}\left(\begin{matrix}\nu\\
\mu\end{matrix};f;z\right):\hskip-3.0pt

\displaystyle=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})^{-\nu}\sum_{\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{\lfloor r/2\rfloor}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{l}\in(\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0})^{\lceil r/2\rceil}\\
|\mathbf{l}|=l\end{subarray}}F_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{l}}^{1}[f]\big(z_{1},{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{2}^{-\mathit{1}}\big)

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}{}\times\begin{cases}\displaystyle\frac{(\nu)_{\mathbf{m},d}\big(\nu-\frac{d_{2}}{2}-(\underline{0}_{r/2-1},l)\big)_{\mathbf{m}-\mathbf{l}+(\underline{0}_{r/2-1},l),d}}{\big(\mu-\frac{d_{2}}{2}-(\underline{0}_{r/2-1},l)\big)_{\mathbf{m}-\mathbf{l}+(\underline{0}_{r/2-1},l),d}},&r\colon\text{even},\\[4.30554pt]
\displaystyle\frac{(\nu)_{\mathbf{m},d}\big(\nu-\frac{d_{2}}{2}\big)_{\mathbf{m}-\mathbf{l}^{\prime},d}\big(\nu-\frac{d_{2}}{2}(r-1)-l\big)_{l-l_{\lceil r/2\rceil}}}{\big(\mu-\frac{d_{2}}{2}\big)_{\mathbf{m}-\mathbf{l}^{\prime},d}\big(\mu-\frac{d_{2}}{2}(r-1)-l\big)_{l-l_{\lceil r/2\rceil}}},&r\colon\text{odd},\end{cases}

(6.7)

where for odd
r
, for
\mathbf{l}=(l_{1},\dots,l_{\lfloor r/2\rfloor},l_{\lceil r/2\rceil})
let
\mathbf{l}^{\prime}:=(l_{1},\dots,l_{\lfloor r/2\rfloor})
.
Similarly, when
\varepsilon_{2}=2
, for
f(x_{2})\in\mathcal{P}_{\underline{1}_{l}}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{2}\big)
,
\mu,\nu\in\mathbb{C}

and for
z=z_{1}+z_{2}\in\Omega^{\pm}\subset\mathfrak{n}^{\pm}\subset\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}
, let

\displaystyle F_{2}^{l}\left(\begin{matrix}\nu\\
\mu\end{matrix};f;z\right):=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})^{-2\nu}

\displaystyle\qquad{}\times\sum_{\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r_{2}}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{l}\in\{0,1\}^{r_{2}}\\
|\mathbf{l}|=l\end{subarray}}\frac{(\nu)_{\mathbf{m},d}\big(\nu+\bigl(\underline{\frac{1}{2}}_{r_{2}-l},\underline{-\frac{1}{2}}_{l}\bigr)\big)_{\mathbf{m}-\mathbf{l}+(\underline{0}_{r_{2}-l},\underline{1}_{l}),d}}{\big(\mu+\bigl(\underline{\frac{1}{2}}_{r_{2}-l},\underline{-\frac{1}{2}}_{l}\bigr)\big)_{\mathbf{m}-\mathbf{l}+(\underline{0}_{r_{2}-l},\underline{1}_{l}),d}}F_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{l}}^{2}[f]\big(z_{1},{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{2}^{-\mathit{1}}\big).

(6.8)

F_{\varepsilon_{2}}^{l}\left(\begin{smallmatrix}\nu\\
\mu\end{smallmatrix};f;z\right)
becomes a polynomial on
\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}

if
\nu\in-\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{2}}\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
.
Now we want to prove the following.

Theorem 6.3
.

Assume
\mathfrak{p}^{+}
,
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}
are of tube type.

1.

Suppose
\varepsilon_{2}=1
. Let
k,l\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
,
f(x_{2})\in\mathcal{P}_{(l,0,\dots,0)}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\big)
.
Then for
\operatorname{Re}\lambda>\frac{2n}{r}-1
and for
z=z_{1}+z_{2}\in\Omega\subset\mathfrak{n}^{+}\subset\mathfrak{p}^{+}
, we have

\displaystyle\big\langle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(x_{2})^{k}f(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{1}{(\lambda)_{(2k+l,\underline{2k}_{r-1}),d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{-\lambda+\frac{n}{r}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}\bigg(\frac{\partial}{\partial z_{2}}\bigg)^{k}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{\lambda+2k-\frac{n}{r}}f(z_{2})

\displaystyle\qquad{}=C_{1}^{r_{2}}(\lambda,k,l)\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{-\lambda+\frac{n}{r}}F_{1}^{l}\left(\begin{matrix}-\lambda-k+\frac{n}{r}\\
-\lambda-2k+\frac{n}{r}\end{matrix};f;z\right)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=C_{1}^{r_{2}}(\lambda,k,l)F_{1}^{l}\left(\begin{matrix}-k\\
-\lambda-2k+\frac{n}{r}\end{matrix};f;z\right)\!,

where

C_{1}^{r_{2}}(\lambda,k,l)=\begin{cases}\displaystyle\frac{\big(\lambda+k-\frac{d}{4}r_{2}+\frac{d_{2}}{2}+(l,\underline{0}_{r_{2}/2-1})\big)_{\underline{k}_{r_{2}/2},d}}{(\lambda)_{(2k+l,\underline{2k}_{r_{2}/2-1},\underline{k}_{r_{2}/2}),d}},&r_{2}\colon\text{even},\\[8.61108pt]
\displaystyle\frac{\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}\big\lfloor\frac{r_{2}}{2}\big\rfloor+\max\{2k,k+l\}\big)_{\min\{k,l\}}\big(\lambda+k-\frac{d}{2}\big\lceil\frac{r_{2}}{2}\big\rceil+\frac{d_{2}}{2}\big)_{\underline{k}_{\lfloor r_{2}/2\rfloor},d}}{(\lambda)_{(2k+l,\underline{2k}_{\lfloor r_{2}/2\rfloor-1},\min\{2k,k+l\},\underline{k}_{\lfloor r_{2}/2\rfloor}),d}},\hskip-52.0pt&\\
&r_{2}\colon\text{odd}.\end{cases}

2.

Suppose
\varepsilon_{2}=2
. Let
k\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
,
0\leq l<r_{2}
,
f(x_{2})\in\mathcal{P}_{\underline{1}_{l}}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\big)
.
Then for
\operatorname{Re}\lambda>\frac{2n}{r}-1
and for
z=z_{1}+z_{2}\in\Omega\subset\mathfrak{n}^{+}\subset\mathfrak{p}^{+}
, we have

\displaystyle\big\langle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(x_{2})^{k}f(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{1}{(\lambda)_{(\underline{k+1}_{l},\underline{k}_{r-l}),d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{-\lambda+\frac{n}{r}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}\bigg(\frac{1}{2}\frac{\partial}{\partial z_{2}}\bigg)^{k}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{\lambda+k-\frac{n}{r}}f(z_{2})

\displaystyle\qquad{}=C_{2}^{r_{2}}(\lambda,k,l)\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{-\lambda+\frac{n}{r}}F_{2}^{l}\left(\begin{matrix}-\lambda-\frac{k}{2}+\frac{n}{r}\\
-\lambda-k+\frac{n}{r}\end{matrix};f;z\right)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=C_{2}^{r_{2}}(\lambda,k,l)F_{2}^{l}\left(\begin{matrix}-\frac{k}{2}\\
-\lambda-k+\frac{n}{r}\end{matrix};f;z\right)\!,

where

C_{2}^{r_{2}}(\lambda,k,l)=\frac{\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r_{2}+\big(\underline{\big\lfloor\frac{k}{2}\big\rfloor+\frac{1}{2}}_{l},\underline{\big\lceil\frac{k}{2}\big\rceil-\frac{1}{2}}_{r_{2}-l}\big)\big)_{(\underline{\lceil k/2\rceil}_{l},\underline{\lfloor k/2\rfloor}_{r_{2}-l}),d}}{(\lambda)_{(\underline{k+1}_{l},\underline{k}_{r_{2}-l},\underline{\lceil k/2\rceil}_{l},\underline{\lfloor k/2\rfloor}_{r_{2}-l}),d}}.

The 1st equalities are nothing but Theorem
4.4
(1). Hence by putting
-\lambda-\frac{2k}{\varepsilon_{2}}+\frac{n}{r}=:\mu
,
it suffices to show

\displaystyle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{\mu+\frac{2k}{\varepsilon_{2}}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}\bigg(\frac{\partial}{\partial z_{2}}\bigg)^{k}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{-\mu}f(z_{2})

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\begin{cases}\displaystyle(-1)^{kr_{2}}\left(\mu-(\underline{0}_{r_{2}-1},l)\right)_{\underline{k}_{r_{2}},d_{2}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{\mu+2k}F_{1}^{l}\left(\begin{matrix}\mu+k\\
\mu\end{matrix};f;z\right),&\varepsilon_{2}=1,\\
\displaystyle(-1)^{kr_{2}}\left(2\mu-(\underline{0}_{r_{2}-l},\underline{1}_{l})\right)_{\underline{k}_{r_{2}},d_{2}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{\mu+k}F_{2}^{l}\left(\begin{matrix}\mu+\frac{k}{2}\\
\mu\end{matrix};f;z\right),&\varepsilon_{2}=2,\end{cases}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\begin{cases}\displaystyle(-1)^{kr_{2}}\left(\mu-(\underline{0}_{r_{2}-1},l)\right)_{\underline{k}_{r_{2}},d_{2}}F_{1}^{l}\left(\begin{matrix}-k\\
\mu\end{matrix};f;z\right),&\varepsilon_{2}=1,\\
\displaystyle(-1)^{kr_{2}}\left(2\mu-(\underline{0}_{r_{2}-l},\underline{1}_{l})\right)_{\underline{k}_{r_{2}},d_{2}}F_{2}^{l}\left(\begin{matrix}-\frac{k}{2}\\
\mu\end{matrix};f;z\right),&\varepsilon_{2}=2.\end{cases}

(6.9)

Indeed, when
\varepsilon_{2}=1
and
r_{2}
is even, since
r=r_{2}
and
d=2d_{2}
or
r=2
hold, we have

\displaystyle(-1)^{kr_{2}}\big(\mu-(\underline{0}_{r_{2}-1},l)\big)_{\underline{k}_{r_{2}},d_{2}}=(-1)^{kr}(\mu)_{\underline{k}_{r/2},d}\bigg(\mu-\frac{d_{2}}{2}-(\underline{0}_{r/2-1},l)\bigg)_{\underline{k}_{r/2},d}

\displaystyle{}=\bigg({-}\mu-k+\frac{d}{2}\bigg(\frac{r}{2}-1\bigg)+1\bigg)_{\underline{k}_{r/2},d}\bigg({-}\mu-k+\frac{d}{2}\bigg(\frac{r}{2}-1\bigg)+1+\frac{d_{2}}{2}+(l,\underline{0}_{r/2-1})\bigg)_{\underline{k}_{r/2},d}

\displaystyle{}=\bigg(\lambda+k-\frac{d}{2}\frac{r}{2}\bigg)_{\underline{k}_{r/2},d}\bigg(\lambda+k-\frac{d}{2}\frac{r}{2}+\frac{d_{2}}{2}+(l,\underline{0}_{r/2-1})\bigg)_{\underline{k}_{r/2},d},

and hence

\displaystyle\frac{(-1)^{kr_{2}}(\mu-(\underline{0}_{r_{2}-1},l))_{\underline{k}_{r_{2}},d_{2}}}{(\lambda)_{(2k+l,\underline{2k}_{r-1}),d}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\big(\lambda+k-\frac{d}{2}\frac{r}{2}\big)_{\underline{k}_{r/2},d}\big(\lambda+k-\frac{d}{2}\frac{r}{2}+\frac{d_{2}}{2}+(l,\underline{0}_{r/2-1})\big)_{\underline{k}_{r/2},d}}{(\lambda)_{(2k+l,\underline{2k}_{r/2-1}),d}\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}\frac{r}{2}\big)_{\underline{2k}_{r/2},d}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\big(\lambda+k-\frac{d}{2}\frac{r}{2}+\frac{d_{2}}{2}+(l,\underline{0}_{r/2-1})\big)_{\underline{k}_{r/2},d}}{(\lambda)_{(2k+l,\underline{2k}_{r/2-1}),d}\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}\frac{r}{2}\big)_{\underline{k}_{r/2},d}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\big(\lambda+k-\frac{d}{4}r_{2}+\frac{d_{2}}{2}+(l,\underline{0}_{r_{2}/2-1})\big)_{\underline{k}_{r_{2}/2},d}}{(\lambda)_{(2k+l,\underline{2k}_{r_{2}/2-1},\underline{k}_{r_{2}/2}),d}}

holds. Similarly, when
\varepsilon_{2}=1
and
r_{2}
is odd, we have

\displaystyle(-1)^{kr_{2}}\big(\mu-(\underline{0}_{r_{2}-1},l)\big)_{\underline{k}_{r_{2}},d_{2}}={}(-1)^{kr}\big(\mu-(\underline{0}_{\lfloor r/2\rfloor},l)\big)_{\underline{k}_{\lceil r/2\rceil},d}\bigg(\mu-\frac{d_{2}}{2}\bigg)_{\underline{k}_{\lfloor r/2\rfloor},d}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\bigg({-}\mu-k+\frac{d}{2}\bigg(\bigg\lceil\frac{r}{2}\bigg\rceil-1\bigg)+1+(l,\underline{0}_{\lfloor r/2\rfloor})\bigg)_{\underline{k}_{\lceil r/2\rceil},d}

\displaystyle\qquad\hphantom{=}\ \times\bigg({-}\mu-k+\frac{d}{2}\bigg(\bigg\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\bigg\rfloor-1\bigg)+1+\frac{d_{2}}{2}\bigg)_{\underline{k}_{\lfloor r/2\rfloor},d}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\bigg(\lambda+k-\frac{d}{2}\bigg\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\bigg\rfloor+(l,\underline{0}_{\lfloor r/2\rfloor})\bigg)_{\underline{k}_{\lceil r/2\rceil},d}\bigg(\lambda+k-\frac{d}{2}\bigg\lceil\frac{r}{2}\bigg\rceil+\frac{d_{2}}{2}\bigg)_{\underline{k}_{\lfloor r/2\rfloor},d},

and hence

\displaystyle\frac{(-1)^{kr_{2}}\big(\mu-(\underline{0}_{r_{2}-1},l)\big)_{\underline{k}_{r_{2}},d_{2}}}{(\lambda)_{(2k+l,\underline{2k}_{r-1}),d}}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{\big(\lambda+k-\frac{d}{2}\big\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\big\rfloor+(l,\underline{0}_{\lfloor r/2\rfloor})\big)_{\underline{k}_{\lceil r/2\rceil},d}\big(\lambda+k-\frac{d}{2}\big\lceil\frac{r}{2}\big\rceil+\frac{d_{2}}{2}\big)_{\underline{k}_{\lfloor r/2\rfloor},d}}{(\lambda)_{(2k+l,\underline{2k}_{\lfloor r/2\rfloor-1}),d}\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}\big\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\big\rfloor\big)_{\underline{2k}_{\lceil r/2\rceil},d}}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}\big\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\big\rfloor+\max\{2k,k+l\}\big)_{\min\{k,l\}}\big(\lambda+k-\frac{d}{2}\big\lceil\frac{r}{2}\big\rceil+\frac{d_{2}}{2}\big)_{\underline{k}_{\lfloor r/2\rfloor},d}}{(\lambda)_{(2k+l,\underline{2k}_{\lfloor r/2\rfloor-1}),d}\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}\big\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\big\rfloor\big)_{(\min\{2k,k+l\},\underline{k}_{\lfloor r/2\rfloor}),d}}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}\big\lfloor\frac{r_{2}}{2}\big\rfloor+\max\{2k,k+l\}\big)_{\min\{k,l\}}\big(\lambda+k-\frac{d}{2}\big\lceil\frac{r_{2}}{2}\big\rceil+\frac{d_{2}}{2}\big)_{\underline{k}_{\lfloor r_{2}/2\rfloor},d}}{(\lambda)_{(2k+l,\underline{2k}_{\lfloor r_{2}/2\rfloor-1},\min\{2k,k+l\},\underline{k}_{\lfloor r_{2}/2\rfloor}),d}}

holds. Similarly, when
\varepsilon_{2}=2
, since
r=2r_{2}
and
d_{2}=2d
or
r_{2}=1
hold, we have

\displaystyle\frac{(-1)^{kr_{2}}}{2^{kr_{2}}}\big(2\mu-(\underline{0}_{r_{2}-l},\underline{1}_{l})\big)_{\underline{k}_{r_{2}},d_{2}}={}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2^{kr_{2}}}\big({-}2\mu-k+d(r_{2}-1)+1+(\underline{1}_{l},\underline{0}_{r_{2}-l})\big)_{\underline{k}_{r_{2}},2d}

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2^{kr_{2}}}\big(2\lambda+k-dr_{2}-1+(\underline{1}_{l},\underline{0}_{r_{2}-l})\big)_{\underline{k}_{r_{2}},2d}

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle\bigg(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r_{2}+\bigg(\underline{\bigg\lceil\frac{k}{2}\bigg\rceil}_{l},\underline{\left\lfloor\frac{k}{2}\right\rfloor}_{r_{2}-l}\bigg)\bigg)_{(\underline{\lfloor k/2\rfloor}_{l},\underline{\lceil k/2\rceil}_{r_{2}-l}),d}

\displaystyle\times\!\bigg(\!\lambda\!-\!\frac{d}{2}r_{2}\!+\!\bigg(\underline{\bigg\lfloor\frac{k}{2}\bigg\rfloor\!+\!\frac{1}{2}}_{l},\underline{\left\lceil\frac{k}{2}\right\rceil-\frac{1}{2}}_{r_{2}-l}\bigg)\!\bigg)_{(\underline{\lceil k/2\rceil}_{l},\underline{\lfloor k/2\rfloor}_{r_{2}-l}),d}\!,

and hence we get

\displaystyle\frac{(-1)^{kr_{2}}\big(2\mu-(\underline{0}_{r_{2}-l},\underline{1}_{l})\big)_{\underline{k}_{r_{2}},d_{2}}}{2^{kr_{2}}(\lambda)_{(\underline{k+1}_{l},\underline{k}_{r-l}),d}}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{1}{(\lambda)_{(\underline{k+1}_{l},\underline{k}_{r_{2}-l}),d}\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r_{2}\big)_{\underline{k}_{r_{2}},d}}\bigg(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r_{2}+\bigg(\underline{\bigg\lceil\frac{k}{2}\bigg\rceil}_{l},\underline{\bigg\lfloor\frac{k}{2}\bigg\rfloor}_{r_{2}-l}\bigg)\bigg)_{(\underline{\lfloor k/2\rfloor}_{l},\underline{\lceil k/2\rceil}_{r_{2}-l}),d}

\displaystyle\qquad\hphantom{=}{}\ \times\bigg(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r_{2}+\bigg(\underline{\bigg\lfloor\frac{k}{2}\bigg\rfloor+\frac{1}{2}}_{l},\underline{\bigg\lceil\frac{k}{2}\bigg\rceil-\frac{1}{2}}_{r_{2}-l}\bigg)\bigg)_{(\underline{\lceil k/2\rceil}_{l},\underline{\lfloor k/2\rfloor}_{r_{2}-l}),d}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r_{2}+\big(\underline{\big\lfloor\frac{k}{2}\big\rfloor+\frac{1}{2}}_{l},\underline{\big\lceil\frac{k}{2}\big\rceil-\frac{1}{2}}_{r_{2}-l}\big)\big)_{(\underline{\lceil k/2\rceil}_{l},\underline{\lfloor k/2\rfloor}_{r_{2}-l}),d}}{(\lambda)_{(\underline{k+1}_{l},\underline{k}_{r_{2}-l}),d}\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r_{2}\big)_{(\underline{\lceil k/2\rceil}_{l},\underline{\lfloor k/2\rfloor}_{r_{2}-l}),d}}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r_{2}+\big(\underline{\big\lfloor\frac{k}{2}\big\rfloor+\frac{1}{2}}_{l},\underline{\big\lceil\frac{k}{2}\big\rceil-\frac{1}{2}}_{r_{2}-l}\big)\big)_{(\underline{\lceil k/2\rceil}_{l},\underline{\lfloor k/2\rfloor}_{r_{2}-l}),d}}{(\lambda)_{(\underline{k+1}_{l},\underline{k}_{r_{2}-l},\underline{\lceil k/2\rceil}_{l},\underline{\lfloor k/2\rfloor}_{r_{2}-l}),d}}.

To prove (
6.9
), let
\mathcal{B}_{\lambda}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}\colon C^{2}(\Omega)\to C(\Omega)\otimes\mathfrak{p}^{+}
be the Bessel operator given by

\mathcal{B}_{\lambda}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}f(z):=\sum_{\alpha,\beta}\frac{1}{2}P(e_{\alpha},e_{\beta})z\frac{\partial^{2}f}{\partial z_{\alpha}\partial z_{\beta}}(z)+\lambda\sum_{\alpha}e_{\alpha}\frac{\partial f}{\partial z_{\alpha}}(z),

where
\{e_{\alpha}\}\subset\mathfrak{n}^{+}\subset\mathfrak{p}^{+}
is a basis of
\mathfrak{n}^{+}
, and
\{z_{\alpha}\}
is the coordinate of
\mathfrak{p}^{+}
for the dual basis

\{e_{\alpha}^{\vee}\}\subset\mathfrak{n}^{+}\subset\mathfrak{p}^{+}
of
\{e_{\alpha}\}
with respect to
(\cdot|\cdot)_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}
.
This
\mathcal{B}_{\lambda}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}
and
\mathcal{B}_{\tau}^{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}(w)
(
w\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}
) in (
2.20
) are related as

(w|\mathcal{B}_{\lambda}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}f)_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}=\mathcal{B}_{\chi^{-\lambda}}^{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}(w)f
.
Also, let
\operatorname{Proj}_{1}\colon\mathfrak{p}^{+}\to\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1}
be the orthogonal projection,
let
L\subset K^{\mathbb{C}}
be the real form given in Section
2.4
,
and let
\Gamma_{r_{2}}^{d_{2}}
be as (
4.1
).

Proposition 6.4
.

Let
f(z_{2})
be as before, and let
\mu,\nu\in\mathbb{C}
.

1.

For
\operatorname{Re}\mu>\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{2}}\big(\frac{2n_{2}}{r_{2}}-1\big)
,
\operatorname{Re}\nu>\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{2}}\big(\frac{n_{2}}{r_{2}}-1\big)

and for
z=z_{1}+z_{2}\in\Omega
,
a_{2}\in\Omega_{2}
with
z_{1}+a_{2}\in\Omega
, we have

\displaystyle F_{\varepsilon_{2}}^{l}\left(\begin{matrix}\nu\\
\mu\end{matrix};f;z\right)={}

\displaystyle\begin{cases}\displaystyle\frac{\Gamma_{r_{2}}^{d_{2}}(\mu-(\underline{0}_{r_{2}-1},l))}{\Gamma_{r_{2}}^{d_{2}}(\nu-(\underline{0}_{r_{2}-1},l))}\int_{\Omega_{2}}{\rm e}^{-(y_{2}|z_{2})_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(y_{2})^{\nu-\mu}&(\varepsilon_{2}=1)\\[8.61108pt]
\displaystyle\frac{\Gamma_{r_{2}}^{d_{2}}(2\mu-(\underline{0}_{r_{2}-l},\underline{1}_{l}))}{\Gamma_{r_{2}}^{d_{2}}(2\nu-(\underline{0}_{r_{2}-l},\underline{1}_{l}))}\int_{\Omega_{2}}{\rm e}^{-(y_{2}|z_{2})_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(y_{2})^{2(\nu-\mu)}&(\varepsilon_{2}=2)\end{cases}

\displaystyle{}\times\bigg(\frac{1}{(2\pi\sqrt{-1})^{n_{2}}}\int_{a_{2}+\sqrt{-1}\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}\!\!\!\!{\rm e}^{(x_{2}|y_{2})_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z_{1}+x_{2})^{-\mu}f(x_{2})\,{\rm d}x_{2}\bigg){\rm d}y_{2}.

2.

For
g\in K_{1}^{\mathbb{C}}\cap L
and for
z=z_{1}+z_{2}\in\Omega
, we have

F_{\varepsilon_{2}}^{l}\left(\begin{matrix}\nu\\
\mu\end{matrix};f(g\cdot);z\right)=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(ge)^{\varepsilon_{2}\nu}F_{\varepsilon_{2}}^{l}\left(\begin{matrix}\nu\\
\mu\end{matrix};f;gz\right)\!.

3.

F_{\varepsilon_{2}}^{l}\left(\begin{smallmatrix}\nu\\
\mu\end{smallmatrix};f;z\right)
satisfies the differential equation

\operatorname{Proj}_{1}\left(\mathcal{B}_{-\mu+2\nu+\frac{n}{r}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}F_{\varepsilon_{2}}^{l}\left(\begin{matrix}\nu\\
\mu\end{matrix};f;z\right)\right)=0.

Proof.

(1) By (
6.6
) we have

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega_{2}}{\rm e}^{-(y_{2}|z_{2})_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(y_{2})^{\varepsilon_{2}(\nu-\mu)}

\displaystyle\qquad\hphantom{=}{}\ \times\bigg(\frac{1}{(2\pi\sqrt{-1})^{n_{2}}}\int_{a_{2}+\sqrt{-1}\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}{\rm e}^{(x_{2}|y_{2})_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z_{1}+x_{2})^{-\mu}f(x_{2})\,{\rm d}x_{2}\bigg){\rm d}y_{2}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\sum_{\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{\lfloor r/2\rfloor}}\sum_{\mathbf{l}}(\mu)_{\mathbf{m},d}\int_{\Omega_{2}}{\rm e}^{-(y_{2}|z_{2})_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(y_{2})^{\varepsilon_{2}(\nu-\mu)}

\displaystyle\qquad\hphantom{=}{}\ \times\bigg(\frac{1}{(2\pi\sqrt{-1})^{n_{2}}}\int_{a_{2}+\sqrt{-1}\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}{\rm e}^{(x_{2}|y_{2})_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(x_{2})^{-\varepsilon_{2}\mu}F_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{l}}^{\varepsilon_{2}}[f]\big(z_{1},{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptx_{2}^{-\mathit{1}}\big)\,{\rm d}x_{2}\bigg){\rm d}y_{2}.

When
\varepsilon_{2}=1
, since
r=r_{2}
and
d=2d_{2}
or
r=2
hold, by Theorem
4.2
we have

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega_{2}}{\rm e}^{-(y_{2}|z_{2})_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(y_{2})^{\nu-\mu}

\displaystyle\qquad\hphantom{=}{}\ \times\bigg(\frac{1}{(2\pi\sqrt{-1})^{n_{2}}}\int_{a_{2}+\sqrt{-1}\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}{\rm e}^{(x_{2}|y_{2})_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(x_{2})^{-\mu}F_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{l}}^{1}[f]\big(z_{1},{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptx_{2}^{-\mathit{1}}\big)\,{\rm d}x_{2}\bigg){\rm d}y_{2}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{1}{\Gamma_{r_{2}}^{d_{2}}(\mu+(m_{1},m_{1}-l_{1},m_{2},m_{2}-l_{2},\dots,m_{\lfloor r/2\rfloor},m_{\lfloor r/2\rfloor}-l_{\lfloor r/2\rfloor}(,-l_{\lceil r/2\rceil})))}

\displaystyle\qquad\hphantom{=}{}\ \times\int_{\Omega_{2}}{\rm e}^{-(y_{2}|z_{2})_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(y_{2})^{\nu-\frac{n_{2}}{r_{2}}}F_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{l}}^{1}[f](z_{1},Q(\overline{e})y_{2})\,{\rm d}y_{2}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{\Gamma_{r_{2}}^{d_{2}}(\nu+(m_{1},m_{1}-l_{1},m_{2},m_{2}-l_{2},\dots,m_{\lfloor r/2\rfloor},m_{\lfloor r/2\rfloor}-l_{\lfloor r/2\rfloor}(,-l_{\lceil r/2\rceil})))}{\Gamma_{r_{2}}^{d_{2}}(\mu+(m_{1},m_{1}-l_{1},m_{2},m_{2}-l_{2},\dots,m_{\lfloor r/2\rfloor},m_{\lfloor r/2\rfloor}-l_{\lfloor r/2\rfloor}(,-l_{\lceil r/2\rceil})))}

\displaystyle\qquad\hphantom{=}{}\ \times\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(y_{2})^{-\nu}F_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{l}}^{1}[f]\big(z_{1},{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{2}^{-\mathit{1}}\big)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{\Gamma_{r_{2}}^{d_{2}}(\nu-(\underline{0}_{r_{2}-1},l))}{\Gamma_{r_{2}}^{d_{2}}(\mu-(\underline{0}_{r_{2}-1},l))}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(y_{2})^{-\nu}F_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{l}}^{1}[f]\big(z_{1},{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{2}^{-\mathit{1}}\big)

\displaystyle\qquad\hphantom{=}{}\ \times\frac{(\nu-(\underline{0}_{r_{2}-1},l))_{(m_{1},m_{1}-l_{1},m_{2},m_{2}-l_{2},\dots,m_{\lfloor r/2\rfloor},m_{\lfloor r/2\rfloor}-l_{\lfloor r/2\rfloor}(,-l_{\lceil r/2\rceil}))+(\underline{0}_{r-1},l),d_{2}}}{(\mu-(\underline{0}_{r_{2}-1},l))_{(m_{1},m_{1}-l_{1},m_{2},m_{2}-l_{2},\dots,m_{\lfloor r/2\rfloor},m_{\lfloor r/2\rfloor}-l_{\lfloor r/2\rfloor}(,-l_{\lceil r/2\rceil}))+(\underline{0}_{r-1},l),d_{2}}}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{\Gamma_{r_{2}}^{d_{2}}(\nu-(\underline{0}_{r_{2}-1},l))}{\Gamma_{r_{2}}^{d_{2}}(\mu-(\underline{0}_{r_{2}-1},l))}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(y_{2})^{-\nu}F_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{l}}^{1}[f]\big(z_{1},{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{2}^{-\mathit{1}}\big)

\displaystyle\qquad\hphantom{=}{}\ \times\begin{cases}\displaystyle\frac{(\nu)_{\mathbf{m},d}\big(\nu-\frac{d_{2}}{2}-(\underline{0}_{r/2-1},l)\big)_{\mathbf{m}-\mathbf{l}+(\underline{0}_{r/2-1},l),d}}{(\mu)_{\mathbf{m},d}\big(\mu-\frac{d_{2}}{2}-(\underline{0}_{r/2-1},l)\big)_{\mathbf{m}-\mathbf{l}+(\underline{0}_{r/2-1},l),d}},&r\colon\text{even},\\
\displaystyle\frac{(\nu)_{\mathbf{m},d}\big(\nu-\frac{d_{2}}{2}\big)_{\mathbf{m}-\mathbf{l}^{\prime},d}\big(\nu-\frac{d_{2}}{2}(r-1)-l\big)_{l-l_{\lceil r/2\rceil}}}{(\mu)_{\mathbf{m},d}\big(\mu-\frac{d_{2}}{2}\big)_{\mathbf{m}-\mathbf{l}^{\prime},d}\big(\mu-\frac{d_{2}}{2}(r-1)-l\big)_{l-l_{\lceil r/2\rceil}}},&r\colon\text{odd}.\end{cases}

Similarly, when
\varepsilon_{2}=2
, since
d_{2}=2d
or
r_{2}=1
holds and
\mathbf{l}\in\{0,1\}^{r_{2}}
, again by Theorem
4.2
we have

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega_{2}}{\rm e}^{-(y_{2}|z_{2})_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(y_{2})^{2(\nu-\mu)}

\displaystyle\qquad\hphantom{=}{}\ \times\bigg(\frac{1}{(2\pi\sqrt{-1})^{n_{2}}}\int_{a_{2}+\sqrt{-1}\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}{\rm e}^{(x_{2}|y_{2})_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(x_{2})^{-2\mu}F_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{l}}^{2}[f]\big(z_{1},{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptx_{2}^{-\mathit{1}}\big)\,{\rm d}x_{2}\bigg){\rm d}y_{2}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{\Gamma_{r_{2}}^{d_{2}}(2\nu+2\mathbf{m}-\mathbf{l})}{\Gamma_{r_{2}}^{d_{2}}(2\mu+2\mathbf{m}-\mathbf{l})}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(y_{2})^{-2\nu}F_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{l}}^{2}[f]\big(z_{1},{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{2}^{-\mathit{1}}\big)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{\Gamma_{r_{2}}^{d_{2}}(2\nu\!-\!(\underline{0}_{r_{2}-l},\underline{1}_{l}))}{\Gamma_{r_{2}}^{d_{2}}(2\mu\!-\!(\underline{0}_{r_{2}-l},\underline{1}_{l}))}\frac{(2\nu\!-\!(\underline{0}_{r_{2}-l},\underline{1}_{l}))_{2\mathbf{m}-\mathbf{l}+(\underline{0}_{r_{2}-l},\underline{1}_{l}),d_{2}}}{(2\mu\!-\!(\underline{0}_{r_{2}-l},\underline{1}_{l}))_{2\mathbf{m}-\mathbf{l}+(\underline{0}_{r_{2}-l},\underline{1}_{l}),d_{2}}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(y_{2})^{-2\nu}F_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{l}}^{2}[f]\big(z_{1},{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{2}^{-\mathit{1}}\big)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{\Gamma_{r_{2}}^{d_{2}}(2\nu-(\underline{0}_{r_{2}-l},\underline{1}_{l}))}{\Gamma_{r_{2}}^{d_{2}}(2\mu-(\underline{0}_{r_{2}-l},\underline{1}_{l}))}\frac{(\nu)_{\mathbf{m},d}\big(\nu+\bigl(\underline{\frac{1}{2}}_{r_{2}-l},\underline{-\frac{1}{2}}_{l}\bigr)\big)_{\mathbf{m}-\mathbf{l}+(\underline{0}_{r_{2}-l},\underline{1}_{l}),d}}{(\mu)_{\mathbf{m},d}\big(\mu+\bigl(\underline{\frac{1}{2}}_{r_{2}-l},\underline{-\frac{1}{2}}_{l}\bigr)\big)_{\mathbf{m}-\mathbf{l}+(\underline{0}_{r_{2}-l},\underline{1}_{l}),d}}

\displaystyle\qquad\hphantom{=}{}\ \times\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(y_{2})^{-2\nu}F_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{l}}^{2}[f]\big(z_{1},{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{2}^{-\mathit{1}}\big).

Hence we get the desired formulas.

(2) When
\operatorname{Re}\mu>\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{2}}\big(\frac{2n_{2}}{r_{2}}-1\big)
,
\operatorname{Re}\nu>\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{2}}\big(\frac{n_{2}}{r_{2}}-1\big)
,
in the integral expression (1), by changing the variables
x_{2}
and
y_{2}
to
g^{-1}x_{2}
and
g^{\top}y_{2}
respectively,
we can easily get the desired formula. By analytic continuation this holds for all
\mu
,
\nu
.

(3) Again it suffices to prove when
\operatorname{Re}\mu>\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{2}}\big(\frac{2n_{2}}{r_{2}}-1\big)
,

\operatorname{Re}\nu>\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{2}}\big(\frac{n_{2}}{r_{2}}-1\big)
.
Let
\{e_{1,\alpha}\}\subset\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1}\cap\mathfrak{n}^{+}\subset\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1}
and
\{e_{2,\alpha}\}\subset\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}\subset\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}
be
orthonormal bases with respect to
(\cdot|\cdot)_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}
and
(\cdot|\cdot)_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}
respectively, so that

\big\{e_{1,\alpha},\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{2}}e_{2,\alpha}\big\},\{e_{1,\alpha},e_{2,\alpha}\}\subset\mathfrak{n}^{+}\subset\mathfrak{p}^{+}

are mutually dual with respect to
(\cdot|\cdot)_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}
.
Let
\{z_{1,\alpha},z_{2,\alpha}\}
be the coordinates for
\{e_{1,\alpha},e_{2,\alpha}\}
.
Then the projection of the Bessel operator is written as

\displaystyle\operatorname{Proj}_{1}(\mathcal{B}_{\lambda}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}})=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\alpha,\beta}P(e_{1,\alpha},e_{1,\beta})z_{1}\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial z_{1,\alpha}\partial z_{1,\beta}}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{2}}\sum_{\alpha,\beta}P(e_{1,\alpha},e_{2,\beta})z_{2}\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial z_{1,\alpha}\partial z_{2,\beta}}

\displaystyle\hphantom{\operatorname{Proj}_1(\mathcal{B}_\lambda^{\mathfrak{n}^+})=}{}+\frac{1}{2\varepsilon_{2}^{2}}\sum_{\alpha,\beta}P(e_{2,\alpha},e_{2,\beta})z_{1}\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial z_{2,\alpha}\partial z_{2,\beta}}+\lambda\sum_{\alpha}e_{1,\alpha}\frac{\partial}{\partial z_{1,\alpha}}.

Let
\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{-\mu}f(z_{2})=:g(z)
. When we operate the 3rd term to the integral expression (1), we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\varepsilon_{2}^{2}}\sum_{\alpha,\beta}P(e_{2,\alpha},e_{2,\beta})z_{1}\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial z_{2,\alpha}\partial z_{2,\beta}}

\displaystyle\qquad\hphantom{=}{}\ \times\int_{\Omega_{2}}{\rm e}^{-(y_{2}|z_{2})_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(y_{2})^{\varepsilon_{2}(\nu-\mu)}\bigg(\int_{a_{2}+\sqrt{-1}\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}{\rm e}^{(x_{2}|y_{2})_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}}g(z_{1},x_{2})\,{\rm d}x_{2}\bigg){\rm d}y_{2}
