\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{2}^{2}}\int_{\Omega_{2}}(P(y_{2})z_{1}){\rm e}^{-(y_{2}|z_{2})_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(y_{2})^{\varepsilon_{2}(\nu-\mu)}\bigg(\int_{a_{2}+\sqrt{-1}\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}{\rm e}^{(x_{2}|y_{2})_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}}g(z_{1},x_{2})\,{\rm d}x_{2}\bigg){\rm d}y_{2}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\int_{\Omega_{2}}{\rm e}^{-(y_{2}|z_{2})_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(y_{2})^{\varepsilon_{2}(\nu-\mu)}

\displaystyle\qquad\hphantom{=}{}\ \times\biggl(\int_{a_{2}+\sqrt{-1}\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}\biggl(\frac{1}{2\varepsilon_{2}^{2}}\sum_{\alpha,\beta}P(e_{2,\alpha},e_{2,\beta})z_{1}\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial x_{2,\alpha}\partial x_{2,\beta}}{\rm e}^{(x_{2}|y_{2})_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}}\biggr)\,g(z_{1},x_{2})\,{\rm d}x_{2}\biggr){\rm d}y_{2}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\int_{\Omega_{2}}{\rm e}^{-(y_{2}|z_{2})_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(y_{2})^{\varepsilon_{2}(\nu-\mu)}

\displaystyle\qquad\hphantom{=}{}\ \times\biggl(\int_{a_{2}+\sqrt{-1}\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}{\rm e}^{(x_{2}|y_{2})_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}}\,\biggl(\frac{1}{2\varepsilon_{2}^{2}}\sum_{\alpha,\beta}P(e_{2,\alpha},e_{2,\beta})z_{1}\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial x_{2,\alpha}\partial x_{2,\beta}}g(z_{1},x_{2})\biggr){\rm d}x_{2}\biggr){\rm d}y_{2}.

Similarly, the 2nd term is computed as

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{2}}\sum_{\alpha,\beta}P(e_{1,\alpha},e_{2,\beta})z_{2}\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial z_{1,\alpha}\partial z_{2,\beta}}

\displaystyle\qquad\hphantom{=}{}\ \times\int_{\Omega_{2}}{\rm e}^{-(y_{2}|z_{2})_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(y_{2})^{\varepsilon_{2}(\nu-\mu)}\bigg(\int_{a_{2}+\sqrt{-1}\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}{\rm e}^{(x_{2}|y_{2})_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}}g(z_{1},x_{2})\,{\rm d}x_{2}\bigg){\rm d}y_{2}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=-\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{2}}\sum_{\alpha}\int_{\Omega_{2}}(P(e_{1,\alpha},y_{2})z_{2}){\rm e}^{-(y_{2}|z_{2})_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(y_{2})^{\varepsilon_{2}(\nu-\mu)}

\displaystyle\qquad\hphantom{=}{}\ \times\bigg(\int_{a_{2}+\sqrt{-1}\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}{\rm e}^{(x_{2}|y_{2})_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}}\frac{\partial g}{\partial z_{1,\alpha}}(z_{1},x_{2})\,{\rm d}x_{2}\bigg){\rm d}y_{2}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{2}}\sum_{\alpha,\beta}\int_{\Omega_{2}}\bigg(P(e_{1,\alpha},y_{2})e_{2,\beta}\frac{\partial}{\partial y_{2,\beta}}{\rm e}^{-(y_{2}|z_{2})_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}}\bigg)\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(y_{2})^{\varepsilon_{2}(\nu-\mu)}

\displaystyle\qquad\hphantom{=}{}\ \times\bigg(\int_{a_{2}+\sqrt{-1}\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}{\rm e}^{(x_{2}|y_{2})_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}}\frac{\partial g}{\partial z_{1,\alpha}}(z_{1},x_{2})\,{\rm d}x_{2}\bigg){\rm d}y_{2}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=-\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{2}}\sum_{\alpha,\beta}\int_{\Omega_{2}}{\rm e}^{-(y_{2}|z_{2})_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}}\,\biggl(\frac{\partial}{\partial y_{2,\beta}}P(e_{1,\alpha},y_{2})e_{2,\beta}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(y_{2})^{\varepsilon_{2}(\nu-\mu)}

\displaystyle\qquad\hphantom{=}{}\ \times\bigg(\int_{a_{2}+\sqrt{-1}\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}{\rm e}^{(x_{2}|y_{2})_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}}\frac{\partial g}{\partial z_{1,\alpha}}(z_{1},x_{2})\,{\rm d}x_{2}\bigg)\biggr){\rm d}y_{2}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=-\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{2}}\sum_{\alpha}\int_{\Omega_{2}}{\rm e}^{-(y_{2}|z_{2})_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(y_{2})^{\varepsilon_{2}(\nu-\mu)}\,\biggl(\int_{a_{2}+\sqrt{-1}\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}\biggl(\sum_{\beta}P(e_{1,\alpha},e_{2,\beta})e_{2,\beta}

\displaystyle\qquad\hphantom{=}\ {}+P(e_{1,\alpha},y_{2})x_{2}+\varepsilon_{2}(\nu-\mu)P(e_{1,\alpha},y_{2})y_{2}^{-\mathit{1}}\biggr)\,{\rm e}^{(x_{2}|y_{2})_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}}\frac{\partial g}{\partial z_{1,\alpha}}(z_{1},x_{2})\,{\rm d}x_{2}\biggr){\rm d}y_{2}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=-\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{2}}\sum_{\alpha}\int_{\Omega_{2}}{\rm e}^{-(y_{2}|z_{2})_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(y_{2})^{\varepsilon_{2}(\nu-\mu)}\,\biggl(\int_{a_{2}+\sqrt{-1}\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}\biggl(\sum_{\beta}\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{2,\beta}}P(e_{1,\alpha},e_{2,\beta})

\displaystyle\qquad\hphantom{=}{}\ \times x_{2}{\rm e}^{(x_{2}|y_{2})_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}}\!+\!2\varepsilon_{2}(\nu\!-\!\mu)e_{1,\alpha}{\rm e}^{(x_{2}|y_{2})_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}}\!\biggr)\frac{\partial g}{\partial z_{1,\alpha}}(z_{1},x_{2}){\rm d}x_{2}\!\biggr){\rm d}y_{2}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\int_{\Omega_{2}}{\rm e}^{-(y_{2}|z_{2})_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(y_{2})^{\varepsilon_{2}(\nu-\mu)}\,\biggl(\int_{a_{2}+\sqrt{-1}\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}{\rm e}^{(x_{2}|y_{2})_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}}

\displaystyle\qquad\hphantom{=}{}\ \times\biggl(\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{2}}\sum_{\alpha,\beta}P(e_{1,\alpha},e_{2,\beta})x_{2}\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial z_{1,\alpha}\partial x_{2,\beta}}+2(\mu-\nu)\sum_{\alpha}e_{1,\alpha}\frac{\partial}{\partial z_{1,\alpha}}\biggr)\,g(z_{1},x_{2})\,{\rm d}x_{2}\biggr){\rm d}y_{2},

where we have used
[
11
, Proposition III.4.2(ii)]
at the 4th equality. Therefore we have

\displaystyle\operatorname{Proj}_{1}\big(\mathcal{B}_{-\mu+2\nu+\frac{n}{r},z}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}\big)\int_{\Omega_{2}}{\rm e}^{-(y_{2}|z_{2})_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(y_{2})^{\varepsilon_{2}(\nu-\mu)}\bigg(\!\int_{a_{2}+\sqrt{-1}\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}{\rm e}^{(x_{2}|y_{2})_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}}g(z_{1},x_{2})\,{\rm d}x_{2}\!\bigg){\rm d}y_{2}

\displaystyle{}=\int_{\Omega_{2}}\!\!{\rm e}^{-(y_{2}|z_{2})_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(y_{2})^{\varepsilon_{2}(\nu-\mu)}\bigg(\!\int_{a_{2}+\sqrt{-1}\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}\!\!{\rm e}^{(x_{2}|y_{2})_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}}\operatorname{Proj}_{1}(\mathcal{B}_{\mu+\frac{n}{r},z_{1}+x_{2}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}})g(z_{1},x_{2})\,{\rm d}x_{2}\!\bigg){\rm d}y_{2}.

Now by
[
11
, Proof of Proposition XV.2.4]
we have

\mathcal{B}_{\mu+\frac{n}{r}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}g(z)=\mathcal{B}_{\mu+\frac{n}{r}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{-\mu}f(z_{2})=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{-\mu}\mathcal{B}_{-\mu+\frac{n}{r}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}f(z_{2}),

and by Corollary
2.10
, (
2.21
) and Proposition
6.1
, for
\operatorname{Re}\lambda>\frac{2n}{r}-1
we have

\displaystyle\operatorname{Proj}_{1}(\mathcal{B}_{\lambda})f(z_{2})

\displaystyle=\operatorname{Proj}_{1}(\mathcal{B}_{\lambda})\times\begin{cases}(\lambda)_{l}\bigl\langle f(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\bigr\rangle_{\lambda,x}&(\varepsilon_{2}=1)\\
(\lambda)_{\underline{1}_{l},d}\bigl\langle f(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\bigr\rangle_{\lambda,x}&(\varepsilon_{2}=2)\end{cases}

\displaystyle=0,

and by analytic continuation this holds for all
\lambda\in\mathbb{C}
. Hence

\operatorname{Proj}_{1}\bigl(\mathcal{B}_{-\mu+2\nu+\frac{n}{r}}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}F_{\varepsilon_{2}}^{l}\big(\begin{smallmatrix}\nu\\
\mu\end{smallmatrix};f;z\big)\bigr)=0
holds.
∎

Proof of Theorem
6.3
.

First we prove the 1st equality of (
6.9
). For
\operatorname{Re}\mu>\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{2}}\big(\frac{2n_{2}}{r_{2}}-1\big)
,
by the Laplace and the inverse Laplace transforms and Proposition
6.4
(1) we have

\displaystyle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}\bigg(\frac{\partial}{\partial z_{2}}\bigg)^{k}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{-\mu}f(z_{2})=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}\bigg(\frac{\partial}{\partial z_{2}}\bigg)^{k}\int_{\Omega_{2}}{\rm e}^{-(y_{2}|z_{2})_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}}

\displaystyle\qquad\hphantom{=}{}\ \times\bigg(\frac{1}{(2\pi\sqrt{-1})^{n_{2}}}\int_{a_{2}+\sqrt{-1}\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}{\rm e}^{(x_{2}|z_{2})_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z_{1}+x_{2})^{-\mu}f(x_{2})\,{\rm d}x_{2}\bigg){\rm d}y_{2}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\int_{\Omega_{2}}{\rm e}^{-(y_{2}|z_{2})_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(-y_{2})^{k}

\displaystyle\qquad\hphantom{=}{}\ \times\bigg(\frac{1}{(2\pi\sqrt{-1})^{n_{2}}}\int_{a_{2}+\sqrt{-1}\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}{\rm e}^{(x_{2}|z_{2})_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z_{1}+x_{2})^{-\mu}f(x_{2})\,{\rm d}x_{2}\bigg){\rm d}y_{2}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\begin{cases}\displaystyle(-1)^{kr_{2}}\frac{\Gamma_{r_{2}}^{d_{2}}(\mu+k-(\underline{0}_{r_{2}-1},l))}{\Gamma_{r_{2}}^{d_{2}}(\mu-(\underline{0}_{r_{2}-1},l))}F_{1}^{l}\left(\begin{matrix}\mu+k\\
\mu\end{matrix};f;z\right)\!,&\varepsilon_{2}=1,\\
\displaystyle(-1)^{kr_{2}}\frac{\Gamma_{r_{2}}^{d_{2}}(2\mu+k-(\underline{0}_{r_{2}-l},\underline{1}_{l}))}{\Gamma_{r_{2}}^{d_{2}}(2\mu-(\underline{0}_{r_{2}-l},\underline{1}_{l}))}F_{2}^{l}\left(\begin{matrix}\mu+\frac{k}{2}\\
\mu\end{matrix};f;z\right)\!,&\varepsilon_{2}=2,\end{cases}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\begin{cases}\displaystyle(-1)^{kr_{2}}\left(\mu-(\underline{0}_{r_{2}-1},l)\right)_{\underline{k}_{r_{2}},d_{2}}F_{1}^{l}\left(\begin{matrix}\mu+k\\
\mu\end{matrix};f;z\right)\!,&\varepsilon_{2}=1,\\
\displaystyle(-1)^{kr_{2}}\left(2\mu-(\underline{0}_{r_{2}-l},\underline{1}_{l})\right)_{\underline{k}_{r_{2}},d_{2}}F_{2}^{l}\left(\begin{matrix}\mu+\frac{k}{2}\\
\mu\end{matrix};f;z\right)\!,&\varepsilon_{2}=2.\end{cases}

Hence the 1st equality of (
6.9
) and the 2nd equalities of Theorem
6.3
are proved.
Next we consider the 3rd equalities of Theorem
6.3
. By Proposition
6.4
(2), (3), we have

\displaystyle F_{\varepsilon_{2}}^{l}\left(\begin{matrix}-\frac{k}{\varepsilon_{2}}\\[3.0pt]
-\lambda-\frac{2k}{\varepsilon_{2}}+\frac{n}{r}\end{matrix};f(g\cdot);z\right)=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(ge)^{-k}F_{\varepsilon_{2}}^{l}\left(\begin{matrix}-\frac{k}{\varepsilon_{2}}\\[3.0pt]
-\lambda-\frac{2k}{\varepsilon_{2}}+\frac{n}{r}\end{matrix};f;gz\right)\!,\qquad g\in K_{1}^{\mathbb{C}},

\displaystyle\operatorname{Proj}_{1}\left(\mathcal{B}_{\lambda}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}F_{\varepsilon_{2}}^{l}\left(\begin{matrix}-\frac{k}{\varepsilon_{2}}\\[3.0pt]
-\lambda-\frac{2k}{\varepsilon_{2}}+\frac{n}{r}\end{matrix};f;gz\right)\right)=0,

and
F_{\varepsilon_{2}}^{l}\left(\begin{smallmatrix}-\frac{k}{\varepsilon_{2}}\\
-\lambda-\frac{2k}{\varepsilon_{2}}+\frac{n}{r}\end{smallmatrix};f;z\right)
is a polynomial on
\mathfrak{p}^{+}
. Hence the linear map

\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(x_{2})^{k}f(x_{2})\longmapsto F_{\varepsilon_{2}}^{l}\left(\begin{matrix}-\frac{k}{\varepsilon_{2}}\\[3.0pt]
-\lambda-\frac{2k}{\varepsilon_{2}}+\frac{n}{r}\end{matrix};f;z\right)

sits in

\begin{cases}\operatorname{Hom}_{K_{1}}\big(\mathcal{P}_{(k+l,\underline{k}_{r_{2}-1})}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}),\mathcal{P}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})\cap\mathrm{Sol}(\operatorname{Proj}_{1}(\mathcal{B}_{\lambda}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}))\big),&\varepsilon_{2}=1,\\
\operatorname{Hom}_{K_{1}}\big(\mathcal{P}_{(\underline{k+1}_{l},\underline{k}_{r_{2}-l})}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}),\mathcal{P}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})\cap\mathrm{Sol}(\operatorname{Proj}_{1}(\mathcal{B}_{\lambda}^{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}))\big),&\varepsilon_{2}=2.\end{cases}

By Theorem
2.13
these spaces are 1-dimensional if
\lambda>\frac{2n}{r}-1
, and hence by (
2.21
),
this is proportional to
\bigl\langle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(x_{2})^{k}f(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\bigr\rangle_{\lambda,x}
.
By comparing the values at
z_{1}=0
, we get the desired formulas.
∎

(
6.9
) holds for all
\mu\in\mathbb{C}
. Especially if we put
\lambda=\frac{n}{r}-j
, or
\mu=j-\frac{2k}{\varepsilon_{2}}
, then we have

F_{\varepsilon_{2}}^{l}\left(\begin{matrix}-\frac{k}{\varepsilon_{2}}\\[3.0pt]
j-\frac{2k}{\varepsilon_{2}}\end{matrix};f;z\right)=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{j}F_{\varepsilon_{2}}^{l}\left(\begin{matrix}j-\frac{k}{\varepsilon_{2}}\\[3.0pt]
j-\frac{2k}{\varepsilon_{2}}\end{matrix};f;z\right)\!,

(6.10)

and if
j\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
,
j\leq\lfloor k/\varepsilon_{2}\rfloor
, then

F_{\varepsilon_{2}}^{l}\left(\begin{smallmatrix}-\frac{k}{\varepsilon_{2}}\\
j-\frac{2k}{\varepsilon_{2}}\end{smallmatrix};f;z\right)
,

F_{\varepsilon_{2}}^{l}\left(\begin{smallmatrix}j-\frac{k}{\varepsilon_{2}}\\
j-\frac{2k}{\varepsilon_{2}}\end{smallmatrix};f;z\right)

and
\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(z)^{j}
are polynomials. If
l=0
or
r=2
, then this also follows from (
2.16
) and the argument below.

Next we consider special cases. If
l=0
and
f(x_{2})=1
, then we have

F_{\mathbf{m},\underline{0}_{\lceil r/2\rceil}}^{\varepsilon_{2}}[1](z_{1},w_{2})=\tilde{\Phi}_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1},\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{2}}(z_{1},w_{2}).

Similarly, if
\varepsilon_{2}=1
and
r=2
with general
l
, then we have

F_{m,l}^{1}[f](z_{1},w_{2})=\tilde{\Phi}_{m}^{\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1},\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{2}}(z_{1},w_{2})f\big({}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{2}^{-\mathit{1}}\big)=\frac{1}{m!}\left(\frac{1}{2}(z_{1}|Q(w_{2})z_{1})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}\right)^{m}f\big({}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{2}^{-\mathit{1}}\big).

Therefore, the results become simpler for these cases by using the notations in (
6.4
), (
6.5
).

Corollary 6.5
.

Assume
\mathfrak{p}^{+}
,
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}
are of tube type.

1.

Suppose
\varepsilon_{2}=1
. Then for
k\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
,
\operatorname{Re}\lambda>\frac{2n}{r}-1
and for
z=z_{1}+z_{2}\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}
, we have

\displaystyle\big\langle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(x_{2})^{k},{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}={}

\displaystyle\frac{\big(\lambda+k-\frac{d}{2}\big\lceil\frac{r_{2}}{2}\big\rceil+\frac{d_{2}}{2}\big)_{\underline{k}_{\lfloor r_{2}/2\rfloor},d}}{(\lambda)_{(\underline{2k}_{\lfloor r_{2}/2\rfloor},\underline{k}_{\lceil r_{2}/2\rceil}),d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})^{k}

\displaystyle\times{}_{2}F_{1}^{\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1},\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{2}}\left(\begin{matrix}-k,-k-\frac{d_{2}}{2}\\
-\lambda-2k+\frac{n}{r}-\frac{d_{2}}{2}\end{matrix};z_{1},{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{2}^{-\mathit{1}}\right)\!.

2.

Suppose
\varepsilon_{2}=1
,
r=2
. Then for
k,l\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
,
f(x_{2})\in\mathcal{P}_{(l,0)}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2})
,
\operatorname{Re}\lambda>n-1
and for

z=z_{1}+z_{2}\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}
, we have

\displaystyle\big\langle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(x_{2})^{k}f(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}={}

\displaystyle\frac{\big(\lambda+k+l-\frac{d-d_{2}}{2}\big)_{k}}{(\lambda)_{(2k+l,k),d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})^{k}f(z_{2})

\displaystyle{}\times\,{}_{2}F_{1}\left(\begin{matrix}-k,-k-l-\frac{d_{2}}{2}\\
-\lambda-2k-l+\frac{n}{2}-\frac{d_{2}}{2}\end{matrix};\frac{\big(z_{1}|Q(z_{2})^{-1}z_{1}\big)_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}{2}\right)\!.

3.

Suppose
\varepsilon_{2}=2
. Then for
k\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
,
\operatorname{Re}\lambda>\frac{2n}{r}-1
and for
z=z_{1}+z_{2}\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}
, we have

\displaystyle\big\langle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(x_{2})^{k},{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}={}

\displaystyle\frac{\big(\lambda+\big\lceil\frac{k}{2}\big\rceil-\frac{d}{2}r_{2}-\frac{1}{2}\big)_{\underline{\lfloor k/2\rfloor}_{r_{2}},d}}{(\lambda)_{(\underline{k}_{r_{2}},\underline{\lfloor k/2\rfloor}_{r_{2}}),d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})^{k}

\displaystyle\times{}_{2}F_{1}^{\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1},\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{2}}\left(\begin{matrix}-\frac{k}{2},-\frac{k-1}{2}\\
-\lambda-k+\frac{n}{r}+\frac{1}{2}\end{matrix};z_{1},{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{2}^{-\mathit{1}}\right)\!.

Therefore by Remark
2.8
,
the inner product
\big\langle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(x_{2})^{k},{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}
is
given by using a Heckman–Opdam’s multivariate hypergeometric polynomial of type
BC_{\lfloor r/2\rfloor}
.

6.3
Computation of poles

By Theorem
6.3
, we can compute the poles of
\big\langle F(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}

for
F(x_{2})\in\mathcal{P}_{(k+l,\underline{k}_{a-1})}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\big)
or
F(x_{2})\in\mathcal{P}_{(\underline{k+1}_{l},\underline{k}_{a-l})}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\big)

with
1\leq a\leq r_{2}
, without the assumption that
\mathfrak{p}^{+}
and
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}
are of tube type.

Corollary 6.6
.

1.

Suppose
\varepsilon_{2}=1
. Let
k,l\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
,
2\leq a\leq r_{2}
,
F(x_{2})\in\mathcal{P}_{(k+l,\underline{k}_{a-1})}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\big)
.
Then as functions of
\lambda
,

\displaystyle(\lambda)_{(2k+l,\underline{2k}_{a/2-1},\underline{k}_{a/2}),d}\big\langle F(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}\hskip 86.7809pt\text{if}\quad a\colon\text{even},

\displaystyle(\lambda)_{(2k+l,\underline{2k}_{\lfloor a/2\rfloor-1},\min\{2k,k+l\},\underline{k}_{\lfloor a/2\rfloor}),d}\big\langle F(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}\qquad\text{if}\quad a\colon\text{odd}

are holomorphically continued for all
\lambda\in\mathbb{C}
. Moreover, when
F(x_{2})\neq 0
, if
a
is even or
k=0
or
l=0
,
then these give non-zero polynomials in
z\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}
for all
\lambda\in\mathbb{C}
.
In addition, for
z_{1}=0
we have

\displaystyle\big\langle F(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{(x_{2}|\overline{z_{2}})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\begin{cases}\displaystyle\frac{\big(\lambda+k-\frac{d}{4}a+\frac{d_{2}}{2}+(l,\underline{0}_{a/2-1})\big)_{\underline{k}_{a/2},d}}{(\lambda)_{(2k+l,\underline{2k}_{a/2-1},\underline{k}_{a/2}),d}}F(z_{2}),&a\colon\text{even},\\
\displaystyle\frac{\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}\big\lfloor\frac{a}{2}\big\rfloor+\max\{2k,k+l\}\big)_{\min\{k,l\}}\big(\lambda+k-\frac{d}{2}\big\lceil\frac{a}{2}\big\rceil+\frac{d_{2}}{2}\big)_{\underline{k}_{\lfloor a/2\rfloor},d}}{(\lambda)_{(2k+l,\underline{2k}_{\lfloor a/2\rfloor-1},\min\{2k,k+l\},\underline{k}_{\lfloor a/2\rfloor}),d}}F(z_{2}),\hskip-40.0pt&\\
&a\colon\text{odd}.\end{cases}

2.

Suppose
\varepsilon_{2}=2
. Let
k\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
,
0\leq l<a\leq r_{2}
,
F(x_{2})\in\mathcal{P}_{(\underline{k+1}_{l},\underline{k}_{a-l})}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\big)
.
Then as a function of
\lambda
,

(\lambda)_{(\underline{k+1}_{l},\underline{k}_{a-l},\underline{\lceil k/2\rceil}_{l},\underline{\lfloor k/2\rfloor}_{a-l}),d}\big\langle F(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}

is holomorphically continued for all
\lambda\in\mathbb{C}
, and when
F(x_{2})\neq 0
,
this gives a non-zero polynomial in
z\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}
for all
\lambda\in\mathbb{C}
.
In addition, for
z_{1}=0
we have

\displaystyle\big\langle F(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{(x_{2}|\overline{z_{2}})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}=\frac{\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}a+\big(\underline{\big\lfloor\frac{k}{2}\big\rfloor+\frac{1}{2}}_{l},\underline{\left\lceil\frac{k}{2}\right\rceil-\frac{1}{2}}_{a-l}\big)\big)_{(\underline{\lceil k/2\rceil}_{l},\underline{\lfloor k/2\rfloor}_{a-l}),d}}{(\lambda)_{(\underline{k+1}_{l},\underline{k}_{a-l},\underline{\lceil k/2\rceil}_{l},\underline{\lfloor k/2\rfloor}_{a-l}),d}}F(z_{2}).

Proof.

(1) Let
\varepsilon_{2}=1
. First we assume
\mathfrak{p}^{+}
,
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}
are of tube type,
a=r_{2}
and let
F(x_{2})=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(x_{2})^{k}f(x_{2})
.
We put
\mu:=-\lambda-2k+\frac{n}{r}
. Then by Theorem
6.3
(1), we have

\displaystyle(\lambda)_{(2k+l,\underline{2k}_{a/2-1},\underline{k}_{a/2}),d}\big\langle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(x_{2})^{k}f(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=(-1)^{kr/2}\bigg(\mu-\frac{d_{2}}{2}-(\underline{0}_{r/2-1},l)\bigg)_{\underline{k}_{r/2},d}F_{1}^{l}\left(\begin{matrix}-k\\
\mu\end{matrix};f;z\right)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=(-1)^{kr/2}\bigg(\mu-\frac{d_{2}}{2}-(\underline{0}_{r/2-1},l)\bigg)_{\underline{k}_{r/2},d}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})^{k}

\displaystyle\qquad\hphantom{=}{}\ \times\sum_{\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r/2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{l}\in(\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0})^{r/2}\\
|\mathbf{l}|=l\end{subarray}}\frac{(-k)_{\mathbf{m},d}\big({-}k-\frac{d_{2}}{2}-(\underline{0}_{r/2-1},l)\big)_{\mathbf{m}-\mathbf{l}+(\underline{0}_{r/2-1},l),d}}{\big(\mu-\frac{d_{2}}{2}-(\underline{0}_{r/2-1},l)\big)_{\mathbf{m}-\mathbf{l}+(\underline{0}_{r/2-1},l),d}}F_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{l}}^{1}[f]\big(z_{1},{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{2}^{-\mathit{1}}\big)

for even
r=r_{2}
, and

\displaystyle(\lambda)_{(2k+l,\underline{2k}_{\lfloor a/2\rfloor-1},\min\{2k,k+l\},\underline{k}_{\lfloor a/2\rfloor}),d}\big\langle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(x_{2})^{k}f(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=(-1)^{k\lfloor r/2\rfloor+\min\{k,l\}}\bigg(\mu-\frac{d_{2}}{2}\bigg)_{\underline{k}_{\lfloor r/2\rfloor},d}\bigg(\mu-\frac{d_{2}}{2}(r-1)-l\bigg)_{\min\{k,l\}}F_{1}^{l}\left(\begin{matrix}-k\\
\mu\end{matrix};f;z\right)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=(-1)^{k\lfloor r/2\rfloor+\min\{k,l\}}\bigg(\mu-\frac{d_{2}}{2}\bigg)_{\underline{k}_{\lfloor r/2\rfloor},d}\bigg(\mu-\frac{d_{2}}{2}(r-1)-l\bigg)_{\min\{k,l\}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})^{k}

\displaystyle\qquad\hphantom{=}{}\ \times\sum_{\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{\lfloor r/2\rfloor}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{l}\in(\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0})^{\lceil r/2\rceil}\\
|\mathbf{l}|=l\end{subarray}}\frac{(-k)_{\mathbf{m},d}\big({-}k-\frac{d_{2}}{2}\big)_{\mathbf{m}-\mathbf{l}^{\prime},d}\big({-}k-\frac{d_{2}}{2}(r-1)-l\big)_{l-l_{\lceil r/2\rceil}}}{\big(\mu-\frac{d_{2}}{2}\big)_{\mathbf{m}-\mathbf{l}^{\prime},d}\big(\mu-\frac{d_{2}}{2}(r-1)-l\big)_{l-l_{\lceil r/2\rceil}}}

\displaystyle\qquad\hphantom{=}{}\ \times F_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{l}}^{1}[f]\big(z_{1},{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{2}^{-\mathit{1}}\big)

for odd
r=r_{2}
. Then
(-k)_{\mathbf{m},d}F_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{l}}^{1}[f](z_{1},{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{2}^{-\mathit{1}})
is non-zero only if

\begin{cases}\mathbf{m}\leq\underline{k}_{r/2},\quad\mathbf{m}-\mathbf{l}+(\underline{0}_{r/2-1},l)\leq\underline{k}_{r/2},&r\colon\text{even},\\
\mathbf{m}\leq\underline{k}_{\lfloor r/2\rfloor},\quad\mathbf{m}-\mathbf{l}^{\prime}\leq\underline{k}_{\lfloor r/2\rfloor},\quad l-l_{\lceil r/2\rceil}=|\mathbf{l}^{\prime}|\leq\min\{k,l\},&r\colon\text{odd},\end{cases}

where
\mathbf{k}\leq\mathbf{l}
means
k_{j}\leq l_{j}
for all
j
, and
\mathbf{l}=(\mathbf{l}^{\prime},l_{\lceil r/2\rceil})
for odd
r
.
Hence the above formulas are holomorphic for all
\mu\in\mathbb{C}
.
Moreover, all non-zero
F_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{l}}^{1}[f]
are linearly independent, and if
r
is even or
k=0
or
l=0
, then the coefficient of

F_{\underline{k}_{\lfloor r/2\rfloor},(\underline{0}_{\lceil r/2\rceil-1},l)}^{1}[f]\big(z_{1},{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{2}^{-\mathit{1}}\big)=\tilde{\Phi}_{\underline{k}_{\lfloor r/2\rfloor}}^{\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1},\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{2}}\big(z_{1},{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{2}^{-\mathit{1}}\big)f(z_{2})

is non-zero for all
\mu\in\mathbb{C}
.
Hence the above formulas are non-zero for all
\mu\in\mathbb{C}
when
f(x_{2})\neq 0
and
r
is even or
k=0
or
l=0
.
The values at
z_{1}=0
are clear.

Next we consider general
\mathfrak{p}^{+}
,
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}
and general
2\leq a\leq r_{2}
.
We take a tripotent
e^{\prime}\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}
of rank
a
, and let
\mathfrak{p}^{+\prime}:=\mathfrak{p}^{+}(e^{\prime})_{2}
,
\mathfrak{p}^{+\prime}_{2}:=\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\cap\mathfrak{p}^{+\prime}
.
For
z\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}
, let
z^{\prime}\in\mathfrak{p}^{+\prime}
be the projection of
z
onto
\mathfrak{p}^{+\prime}
.
We take
f(x_{2}^{\prime})\in\mathcal{P}_{(l,0,\dots,0)}(\mathfrak{p}^{+\prime}_{2})
,
and let
F(x_{2})=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+\prime}_{2}}(x_{2}^{\prime})^{k}f(x_{2}^{\prime})\in\mathcal{P}_{(k+l,\underline{k}_{a-1})}(\mathfrak{p}^{+\prime}_{2})\subset\mathcal{P}_{(k+l,\underline{k}_{a-1})}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2})
.
Then by Corollary
2.11
, as a function of
\lambda
,

\displaystyle(\lambda)_{(2k+l,\underline{2k}_{\lfloor a/2\rfloor-1},(\min\{2k,k+l\},)\underline{k}_{\lfloor a/2\rfloor}),d}\big\langle F(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x,\mathfrak{p}^{+}}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=(\lambda)_{(2k+l,\underline{2k}_{\lfloor a/2\rfloor-1},(\min\{2k,k+l\},)\underline{k}_{\lfloor a/2\rfloor}),d}\big\langle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+\prime}_{2}}(x_{2}^{\prime})^{k}f(x_{2}^{\prime}),{\rm e}^{(x^{\prime}|\overline{z^{\prime}})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+\prime}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x^{\prime},\mathfrak{p}^{+\prime}}

is entirely holomorphic on
\mathbb{C}
, and by the
K_{1}
-equivariance, this holds for all
F(x_{2})\!\in\!\mathcal{P}_{(\!k{+}l,\underline{k}_{a{-}1}\!)}\!\big(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\!\big)
.
The proof of the results for
z_{1}=0
is similar.

(2) Next let
\varepsilon_{2}=1
. Again we assume
\mathfrak{p}^{+}
,
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}
are of tube type,
a=r_{2}
and let
F(x_{2})=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(x_{2})^{k}f(x_{2})
.
We put
\mu:=-\lambda-k+\frac{n}{r}
. Then by Theorem
6.3
(2), we have

\displaystyle(\lambda)_{(\underline{k+1}_{l},\underline{k}_{a-l},\underline{\lceil k/2\rceil}_{l},\underline{\lfloor k/2\rfloor}_{a-l}),d}\big\langle F(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=(-1)^{\lceil k/2\rceil l+\lfloor k/2\rfloor(r_{2}-l)}\bigg(\mu+\bigg(\underline{\frac{1}{2}}_{r_{2}-l},\underline{-\frac{1}{2}}_{l}\bigg)\bigg)_{(\underline{\lfloor k/2\rfloor}_{r_{2}-l},\underline{\lceil k/2\rceil}_{l}),d}F_{2}^{l}\left(\begin{matrix}-k/2\\
\mu\end{matrix};f;z\right)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=(-1)^{\lceil k/2\rceil l+\lfloor k/2\rfloor(r_{2}-l)}\left(\mu+\left(\underline{\frac{1}{2}}_{r_{2}-l},\underline{-\frac{1}{2}}_{l}\right)\right)_{(\underline{\lfloor k/2\rfloor}_{r_{2}-l},\underline{\lceil k/2\rceil}_{l}),d}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})^{k}

\displaystyle\qquad\hphantom{=}{}\ \times\sum_{\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r_{2}}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{l}\in\{0,1\}^{r_{2}}\\
|\mathbf{l}|=l\end{subarray}}\frac{\big({-}\frac{k}{2}\big)_{\mathbf{m},d}\big({-}\frac{k}{2}+\bigl(\underline{\frac{1}{2}}_{r_{2}-l},\underline{-\frac{1}{2}}_{l}\bigr)\big)_{\mathbf{m}-\mathbf{l}+(\underline{0}_{r_{2}-l},\underline{1}_{l}),d}}{\big(\mu+\bigl(\underline{\frac{1}{2}}_{r_{2}-l},\underline{-\frac{1}{2}}_{l}\bigr)\big)_{\mathbf{m}-\mathbf{l}+(\underline{0}_{r_{2}-l},\underline{1}_{l}),d}}F_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{l}}^{2}[f]\big(z_{1},{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{2}^{-\mathit{1}}\big).

Then
\big({-}\frac{k}{2}\big)_{\mathbf{m},d}\big({-}\frac{k}{2}+\bigl(\underline{\frac{1}{2}}_{r_{2}-l},\underline{-\frac{1}{2}}_{l}\bigr)\big)_{\mathbf{m}-\mathbf{l}+(\underline{0}_{r_{2}-l},\underline{1}_{l}),d}F_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{l}}^{2}[f]\big(z_{1},{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{2}^{-\mathit{1}}\big)
is non-zero only if

\mathbf{m}\leq\left(\underline{\left\lceil\frac{k}{2}\right\rceil}_{l},\underline{\left\lfloor\frac{k}{2}\right\rfloor}_{r_{2}-l}\right)\!,\qquad\mathbf{m}-\mathbf{l}+(\underline{0}_{r_{2}-l},\underline{1}_{l})\leq\left(\underline{\left\lfloor\frac{k}{2}\right\rfloor}_{r_{2}-l},\underline{\left\lceil\frac{k}{2}\right\rceil}_{l}\right)\!.

Hence the above formula is holomorphic for all
\mu\in\mathbb{C}
.
Moreover, all non-zero
F_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{l}}^{2}[f]
are linearly independent, and the coefficient of

\begin{cases}F_{\underline{k/2}_{r_{2}},(\underline{0}_{r_{2}-l},\underline{1}_{l})}^{2}[f]\big(z_{1},{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{2}^{-\mathit{1}}\big),&k\colon\text{even},\\
F^{2}_{(\underline{\lceil k/2\rceil}_{l},\underline{\lfloor k/2\rfloor}_{r_{2}-l}),(\underline{1}_{l},\underline{0}_{r_{2}-l})}[f]\big(z_{1},{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{2}^{-\mathit{1}}\big),&k\colon\text{odd}\end{cases}

is non-zero for all
\mu\in\mathbb{C}
. If
f(x_{2})\neq 0
, when
k
is even,

F_{\underline{k/2}_{r_{2}},(\underline{0}_{r_{2}-l},\underline{1}_{l})}^{2}[f](z_{1},w_{2})=\tilde{\Phi}_{\underline{k/2}_{r_{2}}}^{\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1},\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{2}}(z_{1},w_{2})f\big({}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{2}^{-\mathit{1}}\big)\neq 0

holds, and when
k
is odd,

F^{2}_{(\underline{\lceil k/2\rceil}_{l},\underline{\lfloor k/2\rfloor}_{r_{2}-l}),(\underline{1}_{l},\underline{0}_{r_{2}-l})}[f](z_{1},w_{2})\neq 0

also holds by Lemma
6.7
given later.
Hence the above formula is non-zero for all
\mu\in\mathbb{C}
. The values at
z_{1}=0
are clear.
For general
\mathfrak{p}^{+}
,
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}
and general
a\leq r_{2}
, this is also proved as the case (1).
∎

To complete the proof of Corollary
6.6
(2), we prove the following.

Lemma 6.7
.

Let
\varepsilon_{2}=2
and let
f(x_{2})\in\mathcal{P}_{\underline{1}_{l}}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\big)
,
f(x_{2})\neq 0
.
Then for
k\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
,
\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{l}
with
m_{l}\geq 1
,

F_{(\mathbf{m},\underline{0}_{r_{2}-l})+\underline{k}_{r_{2}},(\underline{1}_{l},\underline{0}_{r_{2}-l})}^{2}[f](z_{1},w_{2})
gives a non-zero polynomial.

Proof.

When
\varepsilon_{2}=2
,
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1}
automatically is of tube type, and for
k\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
,
\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r_{2}}
, we have

\displaystyle\tilde{\Phi}_{\mathbf{m}+\underline{k}_{r_{2}}}^{\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1},\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{2}}(z_{1},w_{2})=c_{k,\mathbf{m}}\tilde{\Phi}_{\underline{k}_{r_{2}}}^{\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1},\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{2}}(z_{1},w_{2})\tilde{\Phi}_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1},\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{2}}(z_{1},w_{2}),

\displaystyle F_{\mathbf{m}+\underline{k}_{r_{2}},\mathbf{l}}^{2}[f](z_{1},w_{2})=c_{k,\mathbf{m}}\tilde{\Phi}_{\underline{k}_{r_{2}}}^{\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1},\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{2}}(z_{1},w_{2})F_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{l}}^{2}[f](z_{1},w_{2})

for some
c_{k,\mathbf{m}}\neq 0
. Hence we may assume
k=0
. By the definition of
F_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{l}}^{2}[f]
, we have

\displaystyle\tilde{\Phi}_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1},\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{2}}(z_{1},w_{2})f\big({}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{2}^{-\mathit{1}}\big)\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{2}}(w_{2})=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{l}\in\{0,1\}^{r_{2}}\\
|\mathbf{l}|=r_{2}-l\end{subarray}}F_{\mathbf{m},\underline{1}_{r_{2}}-\mathbf{l}}^{2}[f](z_{1},w_{2})\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{2}}(w_{2}),

\displaystyle F_{\mathbf{m},\underline{1}_{r_{2}}-\mathbf{l}}^{2}[f](z_{1},w_{2})\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{2}}(w_{2})\in\mathcal{P}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1})_{z_{1}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{2\mathbf{m}+\mathbf{l}}(\mathfrak{p}_{2}^{-})_{w_{2}}.

Now let
\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{l}
with
m_{l}\geq 1
. If we operate
\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{2}}\big(\frac{\partial}{\partial w_{2}}\big)
, then since only
\mathbf{l}=(\underline{0}_{l},\underline{1}_{r_{2}-l})
term remains, by
[
11
, Proposition VII.1.6]
we have

\displaystyle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{2}}\bigg(\frac{\partial}{\partial w_{2}}\bigg)\tilde{\Phi}_{(\mathbf{m},\underline{0}_{r_{2}-l})}^{\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1},\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{2}}(z_{1},w_{2})f\big({}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{2}^{-\mathit{1}}\big)\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{2}}(w_{2})

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\bigg(\frac{n_{2}}{r_{2}}+(2\mathbf{m}-\underline{1}_{l},\underline{0}_{r_{2}-l})\bigg)_{\underline{1}_{r_{2}},d_{2}}F_{(\mathbf{m},\underline{0}_{r_{2}-l}),(\underline{1}_{l},\underline{0}_{r_{2}-l})}^{2}[f](z_{1},w_{2}).

Now we take mutually orthogonal tripotents
e^{\prime},e^{\prime\prime}\in\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}\subset\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}
of rank
l
and
r_{2}-l
respectively such that
e^{\prime}+e^{\prime\prime}=e
holds.
Let
\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{2}(e^{\prime})_{2}=\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{2}(e^{\prime\prime})_{0}=:\mathfrak{p}^{\pm\prime}_{2}
,
\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{2}(e^{\prime})_{0}=\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{2}(e^{\prime\prime})_{2}=:\mathfrak{p}^{\pm\prime\prime}_{2}
,
and for
w_{2}\in\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{2}
, let
w_{2}^{\prime}\in\mathfrak{p}^{\pm\prime}_{2}
,
w_{2}^{\prime\prime}\in\mathfrak{p}^{\pm\prime\prime}
be the orthogonal projections.
Also let
\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{1}\cap(\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}(e^{\prime})_{2})=:\mathfrak{p}^{\pm\prime}_{1}
. First we consider the case
f(x_{2})=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+\prime}_{2}}(x_{2}^{\prime})
.
Then if
z_{1}=z_{1}^{\prime}\in\mathfrak{p}^{+\prime}_{1}
, by (
4.3
) we have

\displaystyle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{2}}\bigg(\frac{\partial}{\partial w_{2}}\bigg)\tilde{\Phi}_{(\mathbf{m},\underline{0}_{r_{2}-l})}^{\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1},\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{2}}(z_{1}^{\prime},w_{2})\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+\prime}_{2}}\big(\big({}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{2}^{-\mathit{1}}\big)^{\prime}\big)\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{2}}(w_{2})

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-\prime}_{2}}\bigg(\frac{\partial}{\partial w_{2}^{\prime}}\bigg)\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-\prime\prime}_{2}}\bigg(\frac{\partial}{\partial w_{2}^{\prime\prime}}\bigg)\tilde{\Phi}_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{p}^{+\prime}_{1},\mathfrak{p}^{-\prime}_{2}}(z_{1}^{\prime},w_{2}^{\prime})\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-\prime\prime}_{2}}(w_{2}^{\prime\prime})

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\bigg(\frac{n_{2}^{\prime}}{l}-1+\mathbf{m}\bigg)_{\underline{1}_{l},d_{2}}\bigg(\frac{n_{2}^{\prime\prime}}{r_{2}-l}\bigg)_{\underline{1}_{r_{2}-l},d_{2}}\tilde{\Phi}_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{p}^{+\prime}_{1},\mathfrak{p}^{-\prime}_{2}}(z_{1}^{\prime},w_{2}^{\prime})\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-\prime}_{2}}(w_{2}^{\prime})^{-1}\neq 0,

where
n_{2}^{\prime}:=\dim\mathfrak{p}^{+\prime}_{2}
,
n_{2}^{\prime\prime}:=\dim\mathfrak{p}^{+\prime\prime}_{2}
, and hence
F_{(\mathbf{m},\underline{0}_{r_{2}-l}),(\underline{1}_{l},\underline{0}_{r_{2}-l})}^{2}[\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+\prime}_{2}}]\neq 0
holds. For general
f(x_{2})\neq 0
,
F_{(\mathbf{m},\underline{0}_{r_{2}-l}),(\underline{1}_{l},\underline{0}_{r_{2}-l})}^{2}[f]\neq 0

follows from the
K_{1}^{\mathbb{C}}
-equivariance of
F_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{l}}^{2}
.
∎

Especially, by (
2.22
), for
F(x_{2})\!\in\!\mathcal{P}_{(k+l,\underline{k}_{a-1})}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\big)
(when
\varepsilon_{2}=1
) or
for
F(x_{2})\!\in\!\mathcal{P}_{(\underline{k+1}_{l},\underline{k}_{a-l})}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\big)
(when
\varepsilon_{2}=2
) we have

\displaystyle\|F(x_{2})\|_{\lambda,x,\mathfrak{p}^{+}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\big\langle\big\langle F(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{(x_{2}|\overline{z_{2}})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x,\mathfrak{p}^{+}},F(z_{2})\big\rangle_{F,z,\mathfrak{p}^{+}}

\displaystyle=\bigg\langle\big\langle F(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{(x_{2}|\overline{z_{2}})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x,\mathfrak{p}^{+}},F\bigg(\frac{z_{2}}{\varepsilon_{2}}\bigg)\bigg\rangle_{F,z_{2},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}}

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}\displaystyle\frac{\big(\lambda+k\!-\frac{d}{4}a\!+\frac{d_{2}}{2}+(l,\underline{0}_{a/2-1})\big)_{\underline{k}_{a/2},d}}{(\lambda)_{(2k+l,\underline{2k}_{a/2-1},\underline{k}_{a/2}),d}}\|F(z_{2})\|_{F,z_{2},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}}^{2},\quad\varepsilon_{2}=1,\ a\colon\text{even},\\
\displaystyle\frac{\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}\big\lfloor\frac{a}{2}\big\rfloor+\max\{2k,k+l\}\big)_{\min\{k,l\}}\big(\lambda\!+\!k\!-\!\frac{d}{2}\big\lceil\frac{a}{2}\big\rceil\!+\!\frac{d_{2}}{2}\big)_{\underline{k}_{\lfloor a/2\rfloor},d}}{(\lambda)_{(2k+l,\underline{2k}_{\lfloor a/2\rfloor-1},\min\{2k,k+l\},\underline{k}_{\lfloor a/2\rfloor}),d}}\\
\hskip 159.33542pt{}\times\|F(z_{2})\|_{F,z_{2},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}}^{2},\quad\varepsilon_{2}=1,\ a\colon\text{odd},\\
\displaystyle\frac{\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}a+\big(\underline{\big\lfloor\frac{k}{2}\big\rfloor+\frac{1}{2}}_{l},\underline{\left\lceil\frac{k}{2}\right\rceil-\frac{1}{2}}_{a-l}\big)\big)_{(\underline{\lceil k/2\rceil}_{l},\underline{\lfloor k/2\rfloor}_{a-l}),d}}{(\lambda)_{(\underline{k+1}_{l},\underline{k}_{a-l},\underline{\lceil k/2\rceil}_{l},\underline{\lfloor k/2\rfloor}_{a-l}),d}}\bigg\|F\bigg(\frac{z_{2}}{\sqrt{2}}\bigg)\bigg\|_{F,z_{2},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}}^{2},\\
\hskip 270.30118pt\varepsilon_{2}=2.\end{cases}\!\!\!

Moreover, if
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}
is of tube type,
a=r_{2}
,
l=0
and
F(x_{2})=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(x_{2})^{k}
,
then since

\big\|\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(z_{2})^{k}\big\|^{2}_{F,z_{2},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}}=\bigg(\frac{n_{2}}{r_{2}}\bigg)_{\underline{k}_{r_{2}},d_{2}}

holds (see
[
11
, Proposition XI.4.1(ii)]
), we get

\big\|\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(x_{2})^{k}\big\|_{\lambda,x,\mathfrak{p}^{+}}^{2}=\begin{cases}\displaystyle\frac{\big(\frac{n_{2}}{r_{2}}\big)_{\underline{k}_{r_{2}},d_{2}}\big(\lambda+k-\frac{d}{2}\big\lceil\frac{r_{2}}{2}\big\rceil+\frac{d_{2}}{2}\big)_{\underline{k}_{\lfloor r_{2}/2\rfloor},d}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{2k}_{\lfloor r_{2}/2\rfloor},d}\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}\big\lfloor\frac{r_{2}}{2}\big\rfloor\big)_{\underline{k}_{\lceil r_{2}/2\rceil},d}},&\varepsilon_{2}=1,\\[12.91663pt]
\displaystyle\frac{\big(\frac{n_{2}}{r_{2}}\big)_{\underline{k}_{r_{2}},d_{2}}\big(\lambda+\big\lceil\frac{k}{2}\big\rceil-\frac{d}{2}r_{2}-\frac{1}{2}\big)_{\underline{\lfloor k/2\rfloor}_{r_{2}},d}}{2^{kr_{2}}(\lambda)_{\underline{k}_{r_{2}},d}\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r_{2}\big)_{\underline{\lfloor k/2\rfloor}_{r_{2}},d}},&\varepsilon_{2}=2.\end{cases}

6.4
Individual cases

In this section we observe the computation of inner products (Corollary
6.5
) under the explicit realization of
\mathfrak{p}^{+}
.
For
Case
1,
\mathfrak{p}^{+}=\mathbb{C}^{n}=\mathbb{C}^{n^{\prime}}\oplus\mathbb{C}^{n^{\prime\prime}}
with
n^{\prime\prime}\geq 3
, we write
k+l=:k_{1}
,
k=:k_{2}
,

q(x_{2})^{k}f(x_{2})=:F(x_{2})\in\mathcal{P}_{(k_{1},k_{2})}(\mathbb{C}^{n^{\prime\prime}})
. Then for
z=(z_{1},z_{2})\in\mathbb{C}^{n^{\prime}}\oplus\mathbb{C}^{n^{\prime\prime}}
,
since
\frac{1}{2}(z_{1}|Q(z_{2})^{-1}z_{1})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}=-\frac{q(z_{1})}{q(z_{2})}
holds,
by Corollary
6.5
(2) we have

\displaystyle\big\langle F(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{2q(x,\overline{z})}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}=\frac{\big(\lambda+k_{1}-\frac{n^{\prime}}{2}\big)_{k_{2}}}{(\lambda)_{k_{1}+k_{2}}\big(\lambda\!-\!\frac{n-2}{2}\big)_{k_{2}}}{}_{2}F_{1}\left(\begin{matrix}-k_{2},-k_{1}-\frac{n^{\prime\prime}}{2}+1\\
-\lambda\!-\!k_{1}\!-\!k_{2}\!+\!\frac{n^{\prime}}{2}\!+\!1\end{matrix};-\frac{q(z_{1})}{q(z_{2})}\right)F(z_{2}).

(6.11)

Similarly, when
n^{\prime\prime}=1
, for
F(x_{2})=x_{2}^{k}\in\mathcal{P}_{k}(\mathbb{C})
, by Corollary
6.5
(3) we have

\displaystyle\big\langle x_{2}^{k},{\rm e}^{2q(x,\overline{z})}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}

\displaystyle=\frac{\big(\lambda+\big\lceil\frac{k}{2}\big\rceil-\frac{n-1}{2}\big)_{\lfloor k/2\rfloor}}{(\lambda)_{k}\big(\lambda-\frac{n-2}{2}\big)_{\lfloor k/2\rfloor}}{}_{2}F_{1}\left(\begin{matrix}-\frac{k}{2},-\frac{k-1}{2}\\
-\lambda-k+\frac{n+1}{2}\end{matrix};-\frac{q(z_{1})}{q(z_{2})}\right)z_{2}^{k}

\displaystyle=\frac{\big(\lambda+\big\lceil\frac{k}{2}\big\rceil-\frac{n^{\prime}}{2}\big)_{\lfloor k/2\rfloor}}{(\lambda)_{k}\big(\lambda-\frac{n-2}{2}\big)_{\lfloor k/2\rfloor}}{}_{2}F_{1}\left(\begin{matrix}-\left\lceil\frac{k}{2}\right\rceil+\frac{1}{2},-\left\lfloor\frac{k}{2}\right\rfloor\\
-\lambda-k+\frac{n^{\prime}}{2}+1\end{matrix};-\frac{q(z_{1})}{q(z_{2})}\right)z_{2}^{k}.

Then by putting
\mathcal{P}_{k}(\mathbb{C})=:\mathcal{P}_{(\lceil k/2\rceil,\lfloor k/2\rfloor)}\big(\mathbb{C}^{1}\big)
, this result is included in (
6.11
).
Similarly, when
n^{\prime\prime}=2
, by Theorem
5.9
(1), for
(k_{1},k_{2})\in(\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0})^{2}
,
F(x_{2})\in\mathcal{P}_{(k_{1},k_{2})}\big(\mathbb{C}^{2}\big)
we have

\big\langle F(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{2q(x,\overline{z})}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}=\frac{\big(\lambda+k_{1}-\frac{n-2}{2}\big)_{k_{2}}}{(\lambda)_{k_{1}+k_{2}}\big(\lambda-\frac{n-2}{2}\big)_{k_{2}}}{}_{2}F_{1}\left(\begin{matrix}-k_{1},-k_{2}\\
-\lambda-k_{1}-k_{2}+\frac{n^{\prime}}{2}+1\end{matrix};-\frac{q(z_{1})}{q(z_{2})}\right)F(z_{2}),

and this is also included in (
6.11
).

Next, for
Case
2,
\mathfrak{p}^{+}=\operatorname{Sym}(r,\mathbb{C})=\operatorname{Sym}(r^{\prime},\mathbb{C})\oplus M(r^{\prime},r^{\prime\prime};\mathbb{C})\oplus\operatorname{Sym}(r^{\prime\prime},\mathbb{C})
,
we write
z=\left(\begin{smallmatrix}z_{11}&z_{12}\\
{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{12}&z_{22}\end{smallmatrix}\right)
.
When
r^{\prime}=r^{\prime\prime}
, we regard
\mathfrak{p}^{+}=\operatorname{Sym}(2r^{\prime},\mathbb{C})
as a Jordan algebra by using the maximal tripotent

e=\left(\begin{smallmatrix}0&I_{r^{\prime}}\\
I_{r^{\prime}}&0\end{smallmatrix}\right)
. Then since

\tilde{\Phi}_{\mathbf{m}}^{\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1},\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{2}}\big(z_{1},{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{2}^{-\mathit{1}}\big)=\tilde{\Phi}_{\mathbf{m}}^{(1)}\big(z_{11}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{12}^{-1}z_{22}z_{12}^{-1}\big)

holds (see (
6.2
)), by Corollary
6.5
(3) we have

\displaystyle\big\langle\operatorname{det}(x_{12})^{k},{\rm e}^{\operatorname{tr}(xz^{*})}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{\big(\lambda+\big\lceil\frac{k}{2}\big\rceil-\frac{r^{\prime}+1}{2}\big)_{\underline{\lfloor k/2\rfloor}_{r^{\prime}},1}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime}},1}\big(\lambda-\frac{r^{\prime}}{2}\big)_{\underline{\lfloor k/2\rfloor}_{r^{\prime}},1}}\operatorname{det}(z_{12})^{k}{}_{2}F_{1}^{(1)}\left(\begin{matrix}-\frac{k}{2},-\frac{k-1}{2}\\
-\lambda-k+r^{\prime}+1\end{matrix};z_{11}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{12}^{-1}z_{22}z_{12}^{-1}\right)\!,

where
{}_{2}F_{1}^{(1)}
is as in (
3.6
). This result is generalized to
r^{\prime}\neq r^{\prime\prime}
case. We assume
r^{\prime}\leq r^{\prime\prime}
,
and we write
z_{12}=(z_{12}^{\prime},z_{12}^{\prime\prime})\in M(r^{\prime},\mathbb{C})\oplus M(r^{\prime},r^{\prime\prime}-r^{\prime};\mathbb{C})
,

z_{22}=\left(\begin{smallmatrix}z_{22}^{\prime}&z_{22}^{\prime\prime}\\
{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{22}^{\prime\prime}&z_{22}^{\prime\prime\prime}\end{smallmatrix}\right)\in\operatorname{Sym}(r^{\prime},\mathbb{C})\oplus M(r^{\prime},r^{\prime\prime}-r^{\prime};\mathbb{C})\oplus\operatorname{Sym}(r^{\prime\prime}-r^{\prime},\mathbb{C})
,
and
z^{\prime}=\left(\begin{smallmatrix}z_{11}&z_{12}^{\prime}\\
{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{12}^{\prime}&z_{22}^{\prime}\end{smallmatrix}\right)
.
Then for
w_{12}=(I_{r^{\prime}},0)\in M(r^{\prime},r^{\prime\prime};\mathbb{C})
, by Corollary
2.11
we have

\displaystyle\big\langle\operatorname{det}(x_{12}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{12})^{k},{\rm e}^{\operatorname{tr}(xz^{*})}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x,\operatorname{Sym}(r,\mathbb{C})}=\big\langle\operatorname{det}(x_{12}^{\prime})^{k},{\rm e}^{\operatorname{tr}(x^{\prime}(z^{\prime})^{*})}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x^{\prime},\operatorname{Sym}(2r^{\prime},\mathbb{C})}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{\big(\lambda+\big\lceil\frac{k}{2}\big\rceil-\frac{r^{\prime}+1}{2}\big)_{\underline{\lfloor k/2\rfloor}_{r^{\prime},1}}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime}},1}\big(\lambda-\frac{r^{\prime}}{2}\big)_{\underline{\lfloor k/2\rfloor}_{r^{\prime}},1}}\operatorname{det}(z_{12}^{\prime})^{k}{}_{2}F_{1}^{(1)}\left(\begin{matrix}-\frac{k}{2},-\frac{k-1}{2}\\
-\lambda-k+r^{\prime}+1\end{matrix};z_{11}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0pt(z_{12}^{\prime})^{-1}z_{22}^{\prime}(z_{12}^{\prime})^{-1}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{\big(\lambda+\big\lceil\frac{k}{2}\big\rceil-\frac{r^{\prime}+1}{2}\big)_{\underline{\lfloor k/2\rfloor}_{r^{\prime}},1}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime}},1}\big(\lambda-\frac{r^{\prime}}{2}\big)_{\underline{\lfloor k/2\rfloor}_{r^{\prime}},1}}\operatorname{det}\big(z_{12}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{12}\big)^{k}

\displaystyle\qquad\hphantom{=}{}\ \times{}_{2}F_{1}^{(1)}\left(\begin{matrix}-\frac{k}{2},-\frac{k-1}{2}\\
-\lambda-k+r^{\prime}+1\end{matrix};z_{11}\big(w_{12}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{12}\big)^{-1}w_{12}z_{22}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{12}\big(z_{12}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{12}\big)^{-1}\right)\!.

Then by the
{\rm GL}(r^{\prime},\mathbb{C})\times{\rm GL}(r^{\prime\prime},\mathbb{C})
-equivariance, this holds for all
w_{12}\in M(r^{\prime},r^{\prime\prime};\mathbb{C})
.
Similarly, the result for
Case
3 is generalized to
s^{\prime}\neq s^{\prime\prime}
case.

Next, for
Case
6, for
z_{1}\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1}=M(2,6;\mathbb{C})
,
z_{2}\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}=\operatorname{Skew}(6,\mathbb{C})
, by (
6.3
) we have

\tilde{\Phi}_{m}^{\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1},\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{2}}\big(z_{1},{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{2}^{-\mathit{1}}\big)=\frac{1}{m!}\big({-}\operatorname{Pf}\big(z_{1}\big({}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{2}^{-1}\big)^{\#}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{1}\big)\big)^{m}=\frac{1}{m!}\bigg(\frac{\operatorname{Pf}\big(z_{1}z_{2}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{1}\big)}{\operatorname{Pf}(z_{2})}\bigg)^{m}\!,

and hence

\displaystyle\big\langle\operatorname{Pf}(x_{2})^{k},{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}=\frac{(\lambda+k-6)_{k}}{(\lambda)_{(2k,k,k),8}}\operatorname{Pf}(z_{2})^{k}{}_{2}F_{1}\left(\begin{matrix}-k,-k-2\\
-\lambda-2k+7\end{matrix};\frac{\operatorname{Pf}\big(z_{1}z_{2}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{1}\big)}{\operatorname{Pf}(z_{2})}\right)

holds. We summarize the results for
Cases
1–6. Here
{}_{2}F_{1}^{(d)}
and
{}_{2}F_{1}^{[d]}
are as in (
3.6
) and (
3.7
).

Theorem 6.8
.

1.

Let
\big(\mathfrak{p}^{+},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\big)=(\mathbb{C}^{n},\mathbb{C}^{n^{\prime}},\mathbb{C}^{n^{\prime\prime}})
with
n=n^{\prime}+n^{\prime\prime}
. Then for

\begin{cases}(k_{1},k_{2})\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{2},&n^{\prime\prime}\geq 3,\\
(k_{1},k_{2})\in(\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0})^{2},&n^{\prime\prime}=2,\\
(k_{1},k_{2})=\big(\big\lceil\frac{k}{2}\big\rceil,\big\lfloor\frac{k}{2}\big\rfloor\big),\quad k\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&n^{\prime\prime}=1,\end{cases}

for
F(x_{2})\in\mathcal{P}_{(k_{1},k_{2})}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2})
and for
z=z_{1}+z_{2}\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}
, we have

\displaystyle\big\langle F(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{2q(x,\overline{z})}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}=\frac{\big(\lambda+k_{1}-\frac{n^{\prime}}{2}\big)_{k_{2}}}{(\lambda)_{k_{1}+k_{2}}\big(\lambda-\frac{n-2}{2}\big)_{k_{2}}}{}_{2}F_{1}\left(\begin{matrix}-k_{2},-k_{1}-\frac{n^{\prime\prime}}{2}+1\\
-\lambda\!-\!k_{1}\!-\!k_{2}\!+\!\frac{n^{\prime}}{2}\!+\!1\end{matrix};-\frac{q(z_{1})}{q(z_{2})}\right)F(z_{2}).

2.

Let
\big(\mathfrak{p}^{+},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11},\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{12},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}\big)=({\rm Sym}(r,\mathbb{C}),\operatorname{Sym}(r^{\prime},\mathbb{C}),M(r^{\prime},r^{\prime\prime};\mathbb{C}),\operatorname{Sym}(r^{\prime\prime},\mathbb{C}))
with
r=r^{\prime}+r^{\prime\prime}
,
r^{\prime}\leq r^{\prime\prime}
.
Then for
k\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
and for
z=\left(\begin{smallmatrix}z_{11}&z_{12}\\
{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{12}&z_{22}\end{smallmatrix}\right)\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}
,

w_{12}\in\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{12}
, we have

\displaystyle\big\langle\operatorname{det}\big(x_{12}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{12}\big)^{k},{\rm e}^{\operatorname{tr}(xz^{*})}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}=\frac{\big(\lambda+\big\lceil\frac{k}{2}\big\rceil-\frac{r^{\prime}+1}{2}\big)_{\underline{\lfloor k/2\rfloor}_{r^{\prime}},1}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime}},1}\big(\lambda-\frac{r^{\prime}}{2}\big)_{\underline{\lfloor k/2\rfloor}_{r^{\prime}},1}}\operatorname{det}\big(z_{12}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{12}\big)^{k}

\displaystyle\qquad{}\times{}_{2}F_{1}^{(1)}\left(\begin{matrix}-\frac{k}{2},-\frac{k-1}{2}\\
-\lambda-k+r^{\prime}+1\end{matrix};z_{11}\big(w_{12}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{12}\big)^{-1}w_{12}z_{22}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{12}\big(z_{12}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{12}\big)^{-1}\right)\!.

3.

Let
\big(\mathfrak{p}^{+},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11},\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{12},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}\big)=({\rm Skew}(s,\mathbb{C}),\operatorname{Skew}(s^{\prime},\mathbb{C}),M(s^{\prime},s^{\prime\prime};\mathbb{C}),\operatorname{Skew}(s^{\prime\prime},\mathbb{C}))
with
s=s^{\prime}+s^{\prime\prime}
,
s^{\prime}\leq s^{\prime\prime}
.
Then for
k\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
and for
z=\left(\begin{smallmatrix}z_{11}&z_{12}\\
-{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{12}&z_{22}\end{smallmatrix}\right)\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}
,
w_{12}\in\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{12}
,
we have

\displaystyle\big\langle\operatorname{det}\big(x_{12}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{12}\big)^{k},{\rm e}^{\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{tr}(xz^{*})}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}=\frac{\big(\lambda+k-2\big\lceil\frac{s^{\prime}}{2}\big\rceil+1\big)_{\underline{k}_{\lfloor s^{\prime}/2\rfloor},4}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{2k}_{\lfloor s^{\prime}/2\rfloor},4}\big(\lambda-2\big\lfloor\frac{s^{\prime}}{2}\big\rfloor\big)_{\underline{k}_{\lceil s^{\prime}/2\rceil},4}}\operatorname{det}\big(z_{12}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{12}\big)^{k}

\displaystyle\qquad{}\times{}_{2}F_{1}^{[4]}\left(\begin{matrix}-k,-k-1\\
-\lambda-2k+2s^{\prime}-2\end{matrix};-z_{11}\big(w_{12}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{12}\big)^{-1}w_{12}z_{22}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{12}\big(z_{12}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{12}\big)^{-1}\right)\!.

4.

Let
\big(\mathfrak{p}^{+},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\big)=(M(r,\mathbb{C}),\operatorname{Skew}(r,\mathbb{C}),\operatorname{Sym}(r,\mathbb{C})\big)
. Then for
k\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
and for
z=z_{1}+z_{2}\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}
, we have

\displaystyle\big\langle\operatorname{det}(x_{2})^{k},{\rm e}^{\operatorname{tr}(xz^{*})}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}={}

\displaystyle\frac{\big(\lambda+k-\big\lceil\frac{r}{2}\big\rceil+\frac{1}{2}\big)_{\underline{k}_{\lfloor r/2\rfloor},2}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{2k}_{\lfloor r/2\rfloor},2}\big(\lambda-\big\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\big\rfloor\big)_{\underline{k}_{\lceil r/2\rceil},2}}

\displaystyle\times\operatorname{det}(z_{2})^{k}{}_{2}F_{1}^{[2]}\left(\begin{matrix}-k,-k-\frac{1}{2}\\
-\lambda-2k+r-\frac{1}{2}\end{matrix};z_{1}z_{2}^{-1}z_{1}z_{2}^{-1}\right)\!.

5.

Let
\big(\mathfrak{p}^{+},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\big)=(M(2s,\mathbb{C}),\operatorname{Sym}(2s,\mathbb{C}),\operatorname{Skew}(2s,\mathbb{C}))
. Then for
k\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
and for
z=z_{1}+z_{2}\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}
, we have

\displaystyle\big\langle\operatorname{Pf}(x_{2})^{k},{\rm e}^{\operatorname{tr}(xz^{*})}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}={}

\displaystyle\frac{\big(\lambda+\big\lceil\frac{k}{2}\big\rceil-s-\frac{1}{2}\big)_{\underline{\lfloor k/2\rfloor}_{s},2}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k}_{s},2}(\lambda-s)_{\underline{\lfloor k/2\rfloor}_{s},2}}

\displaystyle\times\operatorname{Pf}(z_{2})^{k}{}_{2}F_{1}^{[2]}\left(\begin{matrix}-\frac{k}{2},-\frac{k-1}{2}\\
-\lambda-k+2s+\frac{1}{2}\end{matrix};z_{1}z_{2}^{-1}z_{1}z_{2}^{-1}\right)\!.

6.

Let
\big(\mathfrak{p}^{+},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\big)=\big({\rm Herm}(3,\mathbb{O})^{\mathbb{C}},M(2,6;\mathbb{C}),\operatorname{Skew}(6,\mathbb{C})\big)
. Then for
k\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
and for
z=z_{1}+z_{2}\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}
, we have

\displaystyle\big\langle\operatorname{Pf}(x_{2})^{k},{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}=\frac{(\lambda+k-6)_{k}}{(\lambda)_{2k}(\lambda\!-\!4)_{k}(\lambda\!-\!8)_{k}}\!\operatorname{Pf}(z_{2})^{k}{}_{2}F_{1}\!\left(\begin{matrix}-k,-k-2\\
-\lambda\!-\!2k\!+\!7\end{matrix};\frac{\operatorname{Pf}\big(z_{1}z_{2}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{1}\big)}{\operatorname{Pf}(z_{2})}\right)\!.

7
Poles of inner products and submodules

In this section we apply the results on the poles of
\big\langle F(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda}
to determining
which
\big(\mathfrak{g},\widetilde{K}\big)
-submodule of
({\rm d}\tau_{\lambda},\mathcal{O}_{\lambda}(D)_{\widetilde{K}})
contains
the
\big(\mathfrak{g}_{1},\widetilde{K}_{1}\big)
-submodule generated by
F(x_{2})
.
For
\lambda>p-1
,
f=\sum_{\mathbf{m}}f_{\mathbf{m}}
,
g=\sum_{\mathbf{m}}g_{\mathbf{m}}\in\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}(D)_{\widetilde{K}}=\mathcal{P}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})=\bigoplus_{\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{m}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})
,
by
[
10
]
,
[
9
, Part III, Corollary V.3.9]
the inner product is given by

\displaystyle\langle f,g\rangle_{\lambda}=\sum_{\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}}\frac{1}{(\lambda)_{\mathbf{m},d}}\langle f_{\mathbf{m}},g_{\mathbf{m}}\rangle_{F}=\sum_{\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}}\frac{1}{\prod_{j=1}^{r}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}(j-1)\right)_{m_{j}}}\langle f_{\mathbf{m}},g_{\mathbf{m}}\rangle_{F}.

This is meromorphically continued for all
\lambda\in\mathbb{C}
, and is positive definite on
\mathcal{P}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})
for
\lambda>\frac{d}{2}(r-1)
.
If
\lambda
is a pole, then
\mathcal{P}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})
becomes reducible
as a
\big(\mathfrak{g},\widetilde{K}\big)
-module, and for
i=1,2,\dots,r
,
\lambda\in\frac{d}{2}(i-1)-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
,

M_{i}(\lambda)=M_{i}^{\mathfrak{g}}(\lambda):=\bigoplus_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}\\
m_{i}\leq\frac{d}{2}(i-1)-\lambda\end{subarray}}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{m}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})\subset\mathcal{P}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})=\mathcal{O}_{\lambda}(D)_{\widetilde{K}}

is a
\big(\mathfrak{g},\widetilde{K}\big)
-submodule of
\mathcal{O}_{\lambda}(D)_{\widetilde{K}}
.
Especially, the submodule
M_{i}\big(\frac{d}{2}(i-1)\big)

(1\leq i\leq r)
and the quotient
\mathcal{O}_{\lambda}(D)_{\widetilde{K}}/M_{r}(\lambda)

are infinitesimally unitary (see
[
10
]
).
For
f(x)\in\mathcal{P}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})
,
\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r}
, if

(\lambda)_{\mathbf{m},d}\langle f,g\rangle_{\lambda}
is holomorphic for all
\lambda\in\mathbb{C}
for any
g\in\mathcal{P}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})
,
or equivalently by (
2.22
), if

(\lambda)_{\mathbf{m},d}\big\langle f(x),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}

(7.1)

is holomorphic for all
\lambda\in\mathbb{C}
, then for
1\leq i\leq r
,

f(x)\in M_{i}(\lambda)\qquad\text{holds if}\quad\lambda\in\frac{d}{2}(i-1)-m_{i}-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},

and moreover, for any Lie subalgebra
\mathfrak{g}_{1}\subset\mathfrak{g}
,

{\rm d}\tau_{\lambda}(\mathcal{U}(\mathfrak{g}_{1}))f(x)\subset M_{i}(\lambda)\qquad\text{holds if}\quad\lambda\in\frac{d}{2}(i-1)-m_{i}-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}.

In addition, if (
7.1
) gives a non-zero polynomial in
z\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}
for all
\lambda\in\mathbb{C}
, then the converse also holds.

For example, let
\mathfrak{p}^{+}=\mathbb{C}^{n}
with
n\geq 3
. Then we have
d=n-2
, and as an
\big(\mathfrak{so}(2,n),\widetilde{\rm SO}(2)\times{\rm SO}(n)\big)
-module,

\mathcal{O}_{\lambda}(D)_{\widetilde{K}}
is reducible if and only if
\lambda\in-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}\cup\big(\frac{n-2}{2}-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}\big)
.
For these
\lambda
we have the submodules

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}_{\lambda}(D)_{\widetilde{K}}\supset M_{2}(\lambda)\supset M_{1}(\lambda)\supset\{0\},\qquad

\displaystyle n\colon\text{even},\quad\lambda\in\mathbb{Z},\quad\lambda\leq 0,

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}_{\lambda}(D)_{\widetilde{K}}\supset M_{2}(\lambda)\supset\{0\},\qquad

\displaystyle n\colon\text{even},\quad\lambda\in\mathbb{Z},\quad 1\leq\lambda\leq{\textstyle\frac{n-2}{2}},

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}_{\lambda}(D)_{\widetilde{K}}\supset M_{1}(\lambda)\supset\{0\},\qquad

\displaystyle n\colon\text{odd},\quad\lambda\in\mathbb{Z},\quad\lambda\leq 0,

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}_{\lambda}(D)_{\widetilde{K}}\supset M_{2}(\lambda)\supset\{0\},\qquad

\displaystyle n\colon\text{odd},\quad\lambda\in\mathbb{Z}+{\textstyle\frac{1}{2}},\quad\lambda\leq{\textstyle\frac{n-2}{2}}.

We consider
\mathbb{C}^{n}=\mathbb{C}^{n^{\prime}}\oplus\mathbb{C}^{n^{\prime\prime}}
. If
n^{\prime\prime}\geq 3
, then for
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{2}
,
F(x_{2})\in\mathcal{P}_{(k_{1},k_{2})}\big(\mathbb{C}^{n^{\prime\prime}}\big)
,

F(x_{2})\neq 0
, by Corollary
6.6
(1) or Theorem
6.8
(1),

(\lambda)_{(k_{1}+k_{2},k_{2}),n-2}\big\langle F(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{2q(x,\overline{z})}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}

is non-zero holomorphic for all
\lambda\in\mathbb{C}
. If
n^{\prime\prime}=2
, then for
(k_{1},k_{2})\in(\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0})^{2}
,
F(x_{2})\in\mathcal{P}_{(k_{1},k_{2})}\big(\mathbb{C}^{2}\big)
,

F(x_{2})\neq 0
, by Corollary
5.7
or Theorem
5.9
(1),

(\lambda)_{(k_{1}+k_{2},\min\{k_{1},k_{2}\}),n-2}\big\langle F(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{2q(x,\overline{z})}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}

is non-zero holomorphic for all
\lambda\in\mathbb{C}
. If
n^{\prime\prime}=1
, then for
k\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
,
F(x_{2})\in\mathcal{P}_{k}(\mathbb{C})
,

F(x_{2})\neq 0
, by Corollary
6.6
(2) or Theorem
6.8
(1),

(\lambda)_{(k,\lfloor k/2\rfloor),n-2}\big\langle F(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{2q(x,\overline{z})}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}

is non-zero holomorphic for all
\lambda\in\mathbb{C}
. Similarly, we consider

\mathbb{C}^{2s}=\big(\big(1,\sqrt{-1}\big)\mathbb{C}\big)^{s}\oplus\big(\big(1,-\sqrt{-1}\big)\mathbb{C}\big)^{s}\simeq M(1,s;\mathbb{C})\oplus M(1,s;\mathbb{C}).

Then for
k\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
,
F(x_{2})\in\mathcal{P}_{k}(M(1,s;\mathbb{C}))
,
F(x_{2})\neq 0
, by (
6.1
),

(\lambda)_{k}\big\langle F(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{2q(x,\overline{z})}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}

is non-zero holomorphic for all
\lambda\in\mathbb{C}
. Therefore the following holds.

Theorem 7.1
.

1.

Let
n^{\prime\prime}\geq 3
,
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{2}
. Then for
i=1,2
,

{\rm d}\tau_{\lambda}(\mathcal{U}(\mathfrak{so}(2,n^{\prime})\oplus\mathfrak{so}(n^{\prime\prime})))\mathcal{P}_{(k_{1},k_{2})}\big(\mathbb{C}^{n^{\prime\prime}}\big)\subset M_{i}^{\mathfrak{so}(2,n)}(\lambda)

holds if and only if

\lambda\in\begin{cases}-(k_{1}+k_{2})-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&i=1,\\
\frac{n-2}{2}-k_{2}-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&i=2.\end{cases}

2.

Let
n^{\prime\prime}=2
,
(k_{1},k_{2})\in(\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0})^{2}
. Then for
i=1,2
,

{\rm d}\tau_{\lambda}(\mathcal{U}(\mathfrak{so}(2,n^{\prime})\oplus\mathfrak{so}(2)))\mathcal{P}_{(k_{1},k_{2})}\big(\mathbb{C}^{2}\big)\subset M_{i}^{\mathfrak{so}(2,n)}(\lambda)

holds if and only if

\lambda\in\begin{cases}-(k_{1}+k_{2})-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&i=1,\\
\frac{n-2}{2}-\min\{k_{1},k_{2}\}-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&i=2.\end{cases}

3.

Let
n^{\prime\prime}=1
,
k\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
. Then for
i=1,2
,

{\rm d}\tau_{\lambda}(\mathcal{U}(\mathfrak{so}(2,n^{\prime})))\mathcal{P}_{k}(\mathbb{C})\subset M_{i}^{\mathfrak{so}(2,n)}(\lambda)

holds if and only if

\lambda\in\begin{cases}-k-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&i=1,\\
\frac{n-2}{2}-\left\lfloor\frac{k}{2}\right\rfloor-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&i=2.\end{cases}

4.

Suppose
n=2s
and let
k\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
. Then for
i=1,2
,

{\rm d}\tau_{\lambda}(\mathcal{U}(\mathfrak{u}(1,s)))\mathcal{P}_{k}(M(1,s;\mathbb{C}))\subset M_{i}^{\mathfrak{so}(2,n)}(\lambda)

holds if and only if

\lambda\in\begin{cases}-k-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&i=1,\\
\frac{n-2}{2}-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&i=2.\end{cases}

Next we consider
\mathfrak{p}^{+}=\operatorname{Sym}(r,\mathbb{C})
. Then we have
d=1
, and for
r\geq 2
, as an
\big(\mathfrak{sp}(r,\mathbb{R}),\widetilde{{\rm U}}(r)\big)
-module,

\mathcal{O}_{\lambda}(D)_{\widetilde{K}}
is reducible if and only if
\lambda\in\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{Z}
,
\lambda\leq\frac{1}{2}(r-1)
.
For these
\lambda
we have the submodules

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}_{\lambda}(D)_{\widetilde{K}}\supset M_{2\left\lceil\frac{r}{2}\right\rceil-1}(\lambda)\supset M_{2\left\lceil\frac{r}{2}\right\rceil-3}(\lambda)\supset\cdots\supset M_{\max\{2\lambda,0\}+1}(\lambda)\supset\{0\},\qquad

\displaystyle\lambda\in\mathbb{Z},

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}_{\lambda}(D)_{\widetilde{K}}\supset M_{2\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor}(\lambda)\supset M_{2\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor-2}(\lambda)\supset\cdots\supset M_{\max\{2\lambda,1\}+1}(\lambda)\supset\{0\},\qquad

\displaystyle\lambda\in\mathbb{Z}+{\textstyle\frac{1}{2}}.

We consider

\operatorname{Sym}(r,\mathbb{C})=\operatorname{Sym}(r^{\prime},\mathbb{C})\oplus M(r^{\prime},r^{\prime\prime};\mathbb{C})\oplus\operatorname{Sym}(r^{\prime\prime},\mathbb{C})
.
Then for
1\leq a\leq r^{\prime}
,
k\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
,
\mathbf{l}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r^{\prime\prime}}
and for

F(x_{11},x_{22})\in\mathcal{P}_{\underline{k}_{a}}({\rm Sym}(r^{\prime},\mathbb{C}))\boxtimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}({\rm Sym}(r^{\prime\prime},\mathbb{C}))
,
F(x_{11},x_{22})\neq 0
,
by Corollary
5.7
,

(\lambda)_{(\underline{k}_{a}+\mathbf{l}^{\prime},\min\{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}}+\mathbf{l}^{\prime\prime},\mathbf{l}\}),1}\big\langle F(x_{11},x_{22}),{\rm e}^{\operatorname{tr}(xz^{*})}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}

is holomorphic for all
\lambda\in\mathbb{C}
, where
\mathbf{l}^{\prime}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{a}
,
\mathbf{l}^{\prime\prime}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r^{\prime\prime}}
are defined as in (
5.5
),
and this is non-zero if
l_{a+1}=0
or
k=0
or
\mathbf{l}=\underline{l}_{a^{\prime}}
(
l\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
,
1\leq a^{\prime}\leq r^{\prime\prime}
).
Similarly, if
r^{\prime}\leq r^{\prime\prime}
, then for
0\leq l<a\leq r^{\prime}
,
k\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
and for

F(x_{12})\in\mathcal{P}_{(\underline{k+1}_{l},\underline{k}_{a-l})}(M(r^{\prime},r^{\prime\prime};\mathbb{C}))
,
F(x_{12})\neq 0
, by Corollary
6.6
(2),

(\lambda)_{(\underline{k+1}_{l},\underline{k}_{a-l},\underline{\lceil k/2\rceil}_{l},\underline{\lfloor k/2\rfloor}_{a-l}),1}\big\langle F(x_{12}),{\rm e}^{\operatorname{tr}(xz^{*})}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}

is non-zero holomorphic for all
\lambda\in\mathbb{C}
. Therefore the following holds.

Theorem 7.2
.

1.

Let
1\leq a\leq r^{\prime}
,
k\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
,
\mathbf{l}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r^{\prime\prime}}
. Then for
1\leq i\leq r
,

{\rm d}\tau_{\lambda}(\mathcal{U}(\mathfrak{u}(r^{\prime},r^{\prime\prime})))\mathcal{P}_{\underline{k}_{a}}({\rm Sym}(r^{\prime},\mathbb{C}))\boxtimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}({\rm Sym}(r^{\prime\prime},\mathbb{C}))\subset M_{i}^{\mathfrak{sp}(r,\mathbb{R})}(\lambda)

holds if
a\geq r^{\prime\prime}
and

\lambda\in\begin{cases}\frac{1}{2}(i-1)-(k+l_{i})-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&1\leq i\leq r^{\prime\prime},\\
\frac{1}{2}(i-1)-k-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&r^{\prime\prime}+1\leq i\leq a,\\
\frac{1}{2}(i-1)-\min\{k,l_{i-a}\}-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&a+1\leq i\leq a+r^{\prime\prime},\\
\frac{1}{2}(i-1)-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&a+r^{\prime\prime}+1\leq i\leq r,\end{cases}

or if
a<r^{\prime\prime}
and

\lambda\in\begin{cases}\frac{1}{2}(i-1)-(k+l_{i})-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&1\leq i\leq a,\\
\frac{1}{2}(i-1)-\min\{k+l_{i},l_{i-a}\}-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&a+1\leq i\leq r^{\prime\prime},\\
\frac{1}{2}(i-1)-\min\{k,l_{i-a}\}-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&r^{\prime\prime}+1\leq i\leq a+r^{\prime\prime},\\
\frac{1}{2}(i-1)-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&a+r^{\prime\prime}+1\leq i\leq r.\end{cases}

If
l_{a+1}=0
or
k=0
or
\mathbf{l}=\underline{l}_{a^{\prime}}

(l\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
,
1\leq a^{\prime}\leq r^{\prime\prime})
, then the converse also holds.

2.

Suppose
r^{\prime}\leq r^{\prime\prime}
and let
0\leq l<a\leq r^{\prime}
,
k\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
. Then for
1\leq i\leq r
,

{\rm d}\tau_{\lambda}(\mathcal{U}(\mathfrak{sp}(r^{\prime},\mathbb{R})\oplus\mathfrak{sp}(r^{\prime\prime},\mathbb{R})))\mathcal{P}_{(\underline{k+1}_{l},\underline{k}_{a-l})}(M(r^{\prime},r^{\prime\prime};\mathbb{C}))\subset M_{i}^{\mathfrak{sp}(r,\mathbb{R})}(\lambda)

holds if and only if

\lambda\in\begin{cases}\frac{1}{2}(i-1)-(k+1)-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&1\leq i\leq l,\\
\frac{1}{2}(i-1)-k-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&l+1\leq i\leq a,\\
\frac{1}{2}(i-1)-\left\lceil\frac{k}{2}\right\rceil-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&a+1\leq i\leq a+l,\\
\frac{1}{2}(i-1)-\left\lfloor\frac{k}{2}\right\rfloor-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&a+l+1\leq i\leq 2a,\\
\frac{1}{2}(i-1)-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&2a+1\leq i\leq r.\end{cases}

Next we consider
\mathfrak{p}^{+}=M(q,s;\mathbb{C})
. Then we have
d=2
, and as an
(\mathfrak{su}(q,s),{\rm S}({\rm U}(q)\times{\rm U}(s))^{\sim})
-module,

\mathcal{O}_{\lambda}(D)_{\widetilde{K}}
is reducible if and only if
\lambda\in\mathbb{Z}
,
\lambda\leq\min\{q,s\}-1
.
For these
\lambda
we have the submodules

\mathcal{O}_{\lambda}(D)_{\widetilde{K}}\supset M_{\min\{q,s\}}(\lambda)\supset M_{\min\{q,s\}-1}(\lambda)\supset\cdots\supset M_{\max\{\lambda,0\}+1}(\lambda)\supset\{0\}.

We consider
M(q,s;\mathbb{C})=M(q^{\prime},s^{\prime};\mathbb{C})\oplus M(q^{\prime},s^{\prime\prime};\mathbb{C})\oplus M(q^{\prime\prime},s^{\prime};\mathbb{C})\oplus M(q^{\prime\prime},s^{\prime\prime};\mathbb{C})
with
q^{\prime\prime}s^{\prime\prime}\neq 0
.
If
q^{\prime}s^{\prime}\neq 0
, then for
1\leq a\leq\min\{q^{\prime},s^{\prime}\}
,
k\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
,
\mathbf{l}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{\min\{q^{\prime\prime},s^{\prime\prime}\}}
and for

F(x_{11},x_{22})\in\mathcal{P}_{\underline{k}_{a}}(M(q^{\prime},s^{\prime};\mathbb{C}))\boxtimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}(M(q^{\prime\prime},s^{\prime\prime};\mathbb{C}))
,
F(x_{11},x_{22})\neq 0
,
by Corollary
5.7
,

(\lambda)_{(\underline{k}_{a}+\mathbf{l}^{\prime},\min\{\underline{k}_{\min\{q^{\prime\prime},s^{\prime\prime}\}}+\mathbf{l}^{\prime\prime},\mathbf{l}\}),2}\big\langle F(x_{11},x_{22}),{\rm e}^{\operatorname{tr}(xz^{*})}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}

is holomorphic for all
\lambda\in\mathbb{C}
, where
\mathbf{l}^{\prime}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{a}
,
\mathbf{l}^{\prime\prime}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{\min\{q^{\prime\prime},s^{\prime\prime}\}}
are defined as in (
5.5
),
and this is non-zero if
l_{a+1}=0
or
k=0
or
\mathbf{l}=\underline{l}_{a^{\prime}}
(
l\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
,
1\leq a^{\prime}\leq\min\{q^{\prime\prime},s^{\prime\prime}\}
).
If
q^{\prime}s^{\prime}=0
, then the poles are determined by (
6.1
), and this case is contained in the above formula with
a=0
.
Similarly, when
q=s=r
, we consider
M(r,\mathbb{C})=\operatorname{Skew}(r,\mathbb{C})\oplus\operatorname{Sym}(r,\mathbb{C})
.
Then for
k,l\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
,
2\leq a\leq r
and for
F(x_{2})\in\mathcal{P}_{(k+l,\underline{k}_{a-1})}({\rm Sym}(r,\mathbb{C}))
,
F(x_{2})\neq 0
,
by Corollary
6.6
(1),

\displaystyle(\lambda)_{(2k+l,\underline{2k}_{a/2-1},\underline{k}_{a/2}),2}\big\langle F(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{\operatorname{tr}(xz^{*})}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}\qquad

\displaystyle\text{if}\quad a\colon\text{even},

\displaystyle(\lambda)_{(2k+l,\underline{2k}_{\lfloor a/2\rfloor-1},\min\{2k,k+l\},\underline{k}_{\lfloor a/2\rfloor}),2}\big\langle F(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{\operatorname{tr}(xz^{*})}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}\qquad

\displaystyle\text{if}\quad a\colon\text{odd}

are holomorphic for all
\lambda\in\mathbb{C}
, and these are non-zero if
a
is even or
k=0
or
l=0
.
Similarly, for
0\leq l<a\leq\lfloor r/2\rfloor
,
k\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
and for

F(x_{2})\in\mathcal{P}_{(\underline{k+1}_{l},\underline{k}_{a-l})}({\rm Skew}(r,\mathbb{C}))
,
F(x_{2})\neq 0
, by Corollary
6.6
(2),

(\lambda)_{(\underline{k+1}_{l},\underline{k}_{a-l},\underline{\lceil k/2\rceil}_{l},\underline{\lfloor k/2\rfloor}_{a-l}),2}\big\langle F(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{\operatorname{tr}(xz^{*})}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}

is non-zero holomorphic for all
\lambda\in\mathbb{C}
. Therefore the following holds.

Theorem 7.3
.

1.

Let
0\leq a\leq\min\{q^{\prime},s^{\prime}\}
,
k\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
,
\mathbf{l}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{\min\{q^{\prime\prime},s^{\prime\prime}\}}
. Then for
1\leq i\leq\min\{q,s\}
,

{\rm d}\tau_{\lambda}(\mathcal{U}(\mathfrak{s}(\mathfrak{u}(q^{\prime},s^{\prime\prime})\oplus\mathfrak{u}(q^{\prime\prime},s^{\prime}))))\mathcal{P}_{\underline{k}_{a}}(M(q^{\prime},s^{\prime};\mathbb{C}))\boxtimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}(M(q^{\prime\prime},s^{\prime\prime};\mathbb{C}))\subset M_{i}^{\mathfrak{su}(q,s)}(\lambda)

holds if
a\geq\min\{q^{\prime\prime},s^{\prime\prime}\}
and

\lambda\in\begin{cases}i-1-(k+l_{i})-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&1\leq i\leq\min\{q^{\prime\prime},s^{\prime\prime}\},\\
i-1-k-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&\min\{q^{\prime\prime},s^{\prime\prime}\}+1\leq i\leq a,\\
i-1-\min\{k,l_{i-a}\}-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&a+1\leq i\leq a+\min\{q^{\prime\prime},s^{\prime\prime}\},\\
i-1-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&a+\min\{q^{\prime\prime},s^{\prime\prime}\}+1\leq i\leq\min\{q,s\},\end{cases}

or if
a<\min\{q^{\prime\prime},s^{\prime\prime}\}
and

\lambda\in\begin{cases}i-1-(k+l_{i})-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&1\leq i\leq a,\\
i-1-\min\{k+l_{i},l_{i-a}\}-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&a+1\leq i\leq\min\{q^{\prime\prime},s^{\prime\prime}\},\\
i-1-\min\{k,l_{i-a}\}-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&\min\{q^{\prime\prime},s^{\prime\prime}\}+1\leq i\leq a+\min\{q^{\prime\prime},s^{\prime\prime}\},\\
i-1-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&a+\min\{q^{\prime\prime},s^{\prime\prime}\}+1\leq i\leq\min\{q,s\}.\end{cases}

If
l_{a+1}=0
or
k=0
or
\mathbf{l}=\underline{l}_{a^{\prime}}

(l\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
,
1\leq a^{\prime}\leq\min\{q^{\prime\prime},s^{\prime\prime}\})
,
then the converse also holds.

2.

Suppose
q=s=r
and let
k,l\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
,
2\leq a\leq r
. Then for
1\leq i\leq r
,

{\rm d}\tau_{\lambda}(\mathcal{U}(\mathfrak{so}^{*}(2r)))\mathcal{P}_{(k+l,\underline{k}_{a-1})}({\rm Sym}(r,\mathbb{C}))\subset M_{i}^{\mathfrak{su}(r,r)}(\lambda)

holds if

\lambda\in\begin{cases}i-1-(2k+l)-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&i=1,\\
i-1-2k-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&2\leq i\leq\lfloor a/2\rfloor,\\
i-1-\min\{2k,k+l\}-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&a\colon\text{odd},\ i=\lceil a/2\rceil,\\
i-1-k-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&\lceil a/2\rceil+1\leq i\leq a,\\
i-1-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&a+1\leq i\leq r.\end{cases}

If
a
is even or
k=0
or
l=0
, then the converse also holds.

3.

Again suppose
q=s=r
and let
0\leq l<a\leq\lfloor r/2\rfloor
,
k\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
. Then for
1\leq i\leq r
,

{\rm d}\tau_{\lambda}(\mathcal{U}(\mathfrak{sp}(r,\mathbb{R})))\mathcal{P}_{(\underline{k+1}_{l},\underline{k}_{a-l})}({\rm Skew}(r,\mathbb{C}))\subset M_{i}^{\mathfrak{su}(r,r)}(\lambda)

holds if and only if

\lambda\in\begin{cases}i-1-(k+1)-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&1\leq i\leq l,\\
i-1-k-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&l+1\leq i\leq a,\\
i-1-\left\lceil\frac{k}{2}\right\rceil-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&a+1\leq i\leq a+l,\\
i-1-\left\lfloor\frac{k}{2}\right\rfloor-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&a+l+1\leq i\leq 2a,\\
i-1-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&2a+1\leq i\leq r.\end{cases}

Next we consider
\mathfrak{p}^{+}=\operatorname{Skew}(s,\mathbb{C})
. Then we have
d=4
, and as an
\big(\mathfrak{so}^{*}(2s),\widetilde{{\rm U}}(s)\big)
-module,

\mathcal{O}_{\lambda}(D)_{\widetilde{K}}
is reducible if and only if
\lambda\in\mathbb{Z}
,
\lambda\leq 2(\lfloor s/2\rfloor-1)
.
For these
\lambda
we have the submodules

\mathcal{O}_{\lambda}(D)_{\widetilde{K}}\supset M_{\left\lfloor\frac{s}{2}\right\rfloor}(\lambda)\supset M_{\left\lfloor\frac{s}{2}\right\rfloor-1}(\lambda)\supset\cdots\supset M_{\max\left\{\left\lceil\frac{\lambda}{2}\right\rceil,0\right\}+1}(\lambda)\supset\{0\}.

We consider
\operatorname{Skew}(s,\mathbb{C})=\operatorname{Skew}(s^{\prime},\mathbb{C})\oplus M(s^{\prime},s^{\prime\prime};\mathbb{C})\oplus\operatorname{Skew}(s^{\prime\prime},\mathbb{C})
with
s^{\prime\prime}\geq 2
.
If
s^{\prime}\geq 2
, then for
1\leq a\leq\lfloor s^{\prime}/2\rfloor
,
k\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
,
\mathbf{l}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{\lfloor s^{\prime\prime}/2\rfloor}
and for

F(x_{11},x_{22})\in\mathcal{P}_{\underline{k}_{a}}({\rm Skew}(s^{\prime},\mathbb{C}))\boxtimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}({\rm Skew}(s^{\prime\prime},\mathbb{C}))
,
F(x_{11},x_{22})\neq 0
,
by Corollary
5.7
,

(\lambda)_{(\underline{k}_{a}+\mathbf{l}^{\prime},\min\{\underline{k}_{\lfloor s^{\prime\prime}/2\rfloor}+\mathbf{l}^{\prime\prime},\mathbf{l}\}),4}\big\langle F(x_{11},x_{22}),{\rm e}^{\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{tr}(xz^{*})}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}

is holomorphic for all
\lambda\in\mathbb{C}
, where
\mathbf{l}^{\prime}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{a}
,
\mathbf{l}^{\prime\prime}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{\lfloor s^{\prime\prime}/2\rfloor}
are defined as in (
5.5
),
and this is non-zero if
l_{a+1}=0
or
k=0
or
\mathbf{l}=\underline{l}_{a^{\prime}}
(
l\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
,
1\leq a^{\prime}\leq\lfloor s^{\prime\prime}/2\rfloor
).
Similarly, if
2\leq s^{\prime}\leq s^{\prime\prime}
, then for
k,l\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
,
2\leq a\leq s^{\prime}
and for

F(x_{12})\in\mathcal{P}_{(k+l,\underline{k}_{a-1})}(M(s^{\prime},s^{\prime\prime};\mathbb{C}))
,
F(x_{12})\neq 0
, by Corollary
6.6
(1),

\displaystyle(\lambda)_{(\underline{2k}_{a/2},\underline{k}_{a/2})+(l,0,\dots,0),4}\big\langle F(x_{12}),{\rm e}^{\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{tr}(xz^{*})}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x},\qquad

\displaystyle\text{if }a\colon\text{even},

\displaystyle(\lambda)_{(\underline{2k}_{\lfloor a/2\rfloor},\min\{2k,k+l\},\underline{k}_{\lfloor a/2\rfloor})+(l,0,\dots,0),4}\big\langle F(x_{12}),{\rm e}^{\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{tr}(xz^{*})}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x},\qquad

\displaystyle\text{if }a\colon\text{odd}

are holomorphic for all
\lambda\in\mathbb{C}
, and these are non-zero if
a
is even or
k=0
or
l=0
.
If
s^{\prime}=1
, then the poles are determined by (
6.1
),
and these cases are contained in the above formulas with
a=0
for the former case, and with
a=1
,
k=0
for the latter case.
Therefore the following holds.

Theorem 7.4
.

1.

Let
0\leq a\leq\lfloor s^{\prime}/2\rfloor
,
k\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
,
\mathbf{l}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{\lfloor s^{\prime\prime}/2\rfloor}
. Then for
1\leq i\leq\lfloor s/2\rfloor
,

{\rm d}\tau_{\lambda}(\mathcal{U}(\mathfrak{u}(s^{\prime},s^{\prime\prime})))\mathcal{P}_{\underline{k}_{a}}({\rm Skew}(s^{\prime},\mathbb{C}))\boxtimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}({\rm Skew}(s^{\prime\prime},\mathbb{C}))\subset M_{i}^{\mathfrak{so}^{*}(2s)}(\lambda)

holds if
a\geq\lfloor s^{\prime\prime}/2\rfloor
and

\lambda\in\begin{cases}2(i-1)-(k+l_{i})-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&1\leq i\leq\lfloor s^{\prime\prime}/2\rfloor,\\
2(i-1)-k-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&\lfloor s^{\prime\prime}/2\rfloor+1\leq i\leq a,\\
2(i-1)-\min\{k,l_{i-a}\}-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&a+1\leq i\leq a+\lfloor s^{\prime\prime}/2\rfloor,\\
2(i-1)-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&a+\lfloor s^{\prime\prime}/2\rfloor+1\leq i\leq\lfloor s/2\rfloor,\end{cases}

or if
a<\lfloor s^{\prime\prime}/2\rfloor
and

\lambda\in\begin{cases}2(i-1)-(k+l_{i})-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&1\leq i\leq a,\\
2(i-1)-\min\{k+l_{i},l_{i-a}\}-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&a+1\leq i\leq\lfloor s^{\prime\prime}/2\rfloor,\\
2(i-1)-\min\{k,l_{i-a}\}-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&\lfloor s^{\prime\prime}/2\rfloor+1\leq i\leq a+\lfloor s^{\prime\prime}/2\rfloor,\\
2(i-1)-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&a+\lfloor s^{\prime\prime}/2\rfloor+1\leq i\leq\lfloor s/2\rfloor.\end{cases}

If
l_{a+1}=0
or
k=0
or
\mathbf{l}=\underline{l}_{a^{\prime}}

(l\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
,
1\leq a^{\prime}\leq\lfloor s^{\prime\prime}/2\rfloor)
,
then the converse also holds.

2.

Suppose
s^{\prime}\leq s^{\prime\prime}
and let
k,l\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
,
1\leq a\leq s^{\prime}
. If
a=1
we put
k=0
. Then for
1\leq i\leq\lfloor s/2\rfloor
,

{\rm d}\tau_{\lambda}(\mathcal{U}(\mathfrak{so}^{*}(2s^{\prime})\oplus\mathfrak{so}^{*}(2s^{\prime\prime})))\mathcal{P}_{(k+l,\underline{k}_{a-1})}(M(s^{\prime},s^{\prime\prime};\mathbb{C}))\subset M_{i}^{\mathfrak{so}^{*}(2s)}(\lambda)

holds if

\lambda\in\begin{cases}2(i-1)-(2k+l)-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&i=1,\\
2(i-1)-2k-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&2\leq i\leq\lfloor a/2\rfloor,\\
2(i-1)-\min\{2k,k+l\}-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&a\geq 3\colon\text{odd},\ i=\lceil a/2\rceil,\\
2(i-1)-k-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&\lceil a/2\rceil+1\leq i\leq a,\\
2(i-1)-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&a+1\leq i\leq\lfloor s/2\rfloor.\end{cases}

If
a
is even or
k=0
or
l=0
, then the converse also holds.

Next we consider
\mathfrak{p}^{+}=\operatorname{Herm}(3,\mathbb{O})^{\mathbb{C}}
. Then
d=8
, and as an
\big(\mathfrak{e}_{7(-25)},\widetilde{{\rm U}}(1)\times E_{6}\big)
-module,

\mathcal{O}_{\lambda}(D)_{\widetilde{K}}
is reducible if and only if
\lambda\in\mathbb{Z}
,
\lambda\leq 8
.
For these
\lambda
we have the submodules

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}_{\lambda}(D)_{\widetilde{K}}\supset M_{3}(\lambda)\supset M_{2}(\lambda)\supset M_{1}(\lambda)\supset\{0\},\qquad

\displaystyle\lambda\in\mathbb{Z},\quad\lambda\leq 0,

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}_{\lambda}(D)_{\widetilde{K}}\supset M_{3}(\lambda)\supset M_{2}(\lambda)\supset\{0\},\qquad

\displaystyle\lambda\in\mathbb{Z},\quad 1\leq\lambda\leq 4,

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}_{\lambda}(D)_{\widetilde{K}}\supset M_{3}(\lambda)\supset\{0\},\qquad

\displaystyle\lambda\in\mathbb{Z},\quad 5\leq\lambda\leq 8.

We consider

\displaystyle\operatorname{Herm}(3,\mathbb{O})^{\mathbb{C}}=\mathbb{C}\oplus M(1,2;\mathbb{O})^{\mathbb{C}}\oplus\operatorname{Herm}(2,\mathbb{O})^{\mathbb{C}}=M(2,6;\mathbb{C})\oplus\operatorname{Skew}(6,\mathbb{C}).

Then for
k\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
,
\mathbf{l}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{2}
and for
F(x_{11},x_{22})\in\mathcal{P}_{k}(\mathbb{C})\boxtimes\mathcal{P}_{(l_{1},l_{2})}\big({\rm Herm}(2,\mathbb{O})^{\mathbb{C}}\big)
,

F(x_{11},x_{22})\neq 0
, by Corollary
5.7
,

(\lambda)_{(k+l_{1},\min\{k+l_{2},l_{1}\},l_{2}),8}\big\langle F(x_{11},x_{22}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}

is holomorphic for all
\lambda\in\mathbb{C}
, and this is non-zero if
k=0
or
\mathbf{l}=\underline{l}_{a^{\prime}}
(
l\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
,
a^{\prime}=1,2
).
Similarly, for
k,l\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
and for
F(x_{2})\in\mathcal{P}_{(k+l,k,k)}({\rm Skew}(6,\mathbb{C}))
,
F(x_{2})\neq 0
,
by Corollary
6.6
(1),

(\lambda)_{(2k+l,\min\{2k,k+l\},k),8}\big\langle F(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}

is holomorphic for all
\lambda\in\mathbb{C}
, and this is non-zero if
k=0
or
l=0
.
Similarly, for
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}=\operatorname{Skew}(6,\mathbb{C})
,
M(2,6;\mathbb{C})
or
M(1,2;\mathbb{O})^{\mathbb{C}}
,
for
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{2}
and for
F(x_{2})\in\mathcal{P}_{(k_{1},k_{2})}(\mathfrak{p}_{2}^{+})
,
F(x_{2})\neq 0
, again by Corollary
6.6
(1),

(\lambda)_{(k_{1}+k_{2},k_{2}),8}\big\langle F(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}

is non-zero holomorphic for all
\lambda\in\mathbb{C}
. Therefore the following holds.

Theorem 7.5
.

1.

Let
k\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
,
\mathbf{l}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{2}
. Then for
i=1,2,3
,

{\rm d}\tau_{\lambda}(\mathcal{U}(\mathfrak{u}(1)\oplus\mathfrak{e}_{6(-14)}))\mathcal{P}_{k}(\mathbb{C})\boxtimes\mathcal{P}_{(l_{1},l_{2})}\big({\rm Herm}(2,\mathbb{O})^{\mathbb{C}}\big)\subset M_{i}^{\mathfrak{e}_{7(-25)}}(\lambda)

holds if

\lambda\in\begin{cases}-(k+l_{1})-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&\quad i=1,\\
4-\min\{k+l_{2},l_{1}\}-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&\quad i=2,\\
8-l_{2}-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&\quad i=3.\end{cases}

If
k=0
or
\mathbf{l}=(l,0)
,
(l,l)

(l\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0})
, then the converse also holds.

2.

Let
k,l\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
. Then for
i=1,2,3
,

{\rm d}\tau_{\lambda}(\mathcal{U}(\mathfrak{su}(2,6)))\mathcal{P}_{(k+l,k,k)}({\rm Skew}(6,\mathbb{C}))\subset M_{i}^{\mathfrak{e}_{7(-25)}}(\lambda)

holds if

\lambda\in\begin{cases}-(2k+l)-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&i=1,\\
4-\min\{2k,k+l\}-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&i=2,\\
8-k-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&i=3.\end{cases}

If
k=0
or
l=0
, then the converse also holds.

3.

Let
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{2}
. Then for
i=1,2,3
,

\begin{cases}{\rm d}\tau_{\lambda}(\mathcal{U}(\mathfrak{su}(2,6)))\mathcal{P}_{(k_{1},k_{2},0)}({\rm Skew}(6,\mathbb{C}))\\
{\rm d}\tau_{\lambda}(\mathcal{U}(\mathfrak{su}(2)\oplus\mathfrak{so}^{*}(12)))\mathcal{P}_{(k_{1},k_{2})}(M(2,6;\mathbb{C}))\\
{\rm d}\tau_{\lambda}(\mathcal{U}(\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathbb{R})\oplus\mathfrak{so}(2,10)))\mathcal{P}_{(k_{1},k_{2})}\big(M(1,2;\mathbb{O})^{\mathbb{C}}\big)\end{cases}\subset M_{i}^{\mathfrak{e}_{7(-25)}}(\lambda)

holds if and only if

\lambda\in\begin{cases}-(k_{1}+k_{2})-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&i=1,\\
4-k_{2}-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&i=2,\\
8-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&i=3.\end{cases}

Finally we consider
\mathfrak{p}^{+}=M(1,2;\mathbb{O})^{\mathbb{C}}
. Then we have
d=6
, and as an
\big(\mathfrak{e}_{6(-14)},\widetilde{{\rm U}}(1)\times\operatorname{Spin}(10)\big)
-module,

\mathcal{O}_{\lambda}(D)_{\widetilde{K}}
is reducible if and only if
\lambda\in\mathbb{Z}
,
\lambda\leq 3
.
For these
\lambda
we have the submodules

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}_{\lambda}(D)_{\widetilde{K}}\supset M_{2}(\lambda)\supset M_{1}(\lambda)\supset\{0\},\qquad

\displaystyle\lambda\in\mathbb{Z},\quad\lambda\leq 0,

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}_{\lambda}(D)_{\widetilde{K}}\supset M_{2}(\lambda)\supset\{0\},\qquad

\displaystyle\lambda\in\mathbb{Z},\quad 1\leq\lambda\leq 3.

We consider

\displaystyle M(1,2;\mathbb{O})^{\mathbb{C}}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{O}^{\mathbb{C}}\oplus\mathbb{O}^{\mathbb{C}}\simeq\mathbb{C}^{8}\oplus\mathbb{C}^{8}=\mathbb{C}\oplus\operatorname{Skew}(5,\mathbb{C})\oplus M(1,5;\mathbb{C})

\displaystyle=M(2,4;\mathbb{C})\oplus M(4,2;\mathbb{C}).

Then for
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{2}
and for
F(x_{2})\in\mathcal{P}_{(k_{1},k_{2})}\big(\mathbb{C}^{8}\big)
,
F(x_{2})\neq 0
, by (
6.1
),

(\lambda)_{(k_{1},k_{2}),6}\big\langle F(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}
