is non-zero holomorphic for all
\lambda\in\mathbb{C}
. Similarly, for
k,l\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
and for
F(x_{11},x_{22})\in\mathcal{P}_{k}(\mathbb{C})\boxtimes\mathcal{P}_{l}(M(1,5;\mathbb{C}))
,
F(x_{11},x_{22})\neq 0
, by Corollary
5.7
or Theorem
5.9
(6),

(\lambda)_{(k+l,\min\{k,l\}),6}\big\langle F(x_{11},x_{22}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}

is non-zero holomorphic for all
\lambda\in\mathbb{C}
. Similarly, for
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}=M(4,2;\mathbb{C})
or
\operatorname{Skew}(5,\mathbb{C})
, for
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{2}
and for

F(x_{2})\in\mathcal{P}_{(k_{1},k_{2})}\big(\mathfrak{p}_{2}^{+}\big)
,
F(x_{2})\neq 0
, by Corollary
6.6
(1)

(\lambda)_{(k_{1}+k_{2},k_{2}),6}\big\langle F(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}}\big\rangle_{\lambda,x}

is non-zero holomorphic for all
\lambda\in\mathbb{C}
. Therefore we have the following.

Theorem 7.6
.

1.

Let
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{2}
. Then for
i=1,2
,

{\rm d}\tau_{\lambda}(\mathcal{U}(\mathfrak{u}(1)\oplus\mathfrak{so}(2,8)))\mathcal{P}_{(k_{1},k_{2})}\big(\mathbb{C}^{8}\big)\subset M_{i}^{\mathfrak{e}_{6(-14)}}(\lambda)

holds if and only if

\lambda\in\begin{cases}-k_{1}-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&i=1,\\
3-k_{2}-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&i=2.\end{cases}

2.

Let
k,l\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
. Then for
i=1,2
,

{\rm d}\tau_{\lambda}(\mathcal{U}(\mathfrak{u}(1)\oplus\mathfrak{so}^{*}(10)))\mathcal{P}_{k}(\mathbb{C})\boxtimes\mathcal{P}_{l}(M(1,5;\mathbb{C}))\subset M_{i}^{\mathfrak{e}_{6(-14)}}(\lambda)

holds if and only if

\lambda\in\begin{cases}-(k+l)-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&i=1,\\
3-\min\{k,l\}-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&i=2.\end{cases}

3.

Let
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{2}
. Then for
i=1,2
,

\begin{cases}{\rm d}\tau_{\lambda}(\mathcal{U}(\mathfrak{su}(2,4)\oplus\mathfrak{su}(2)))\mathcal{P}_{(k_{1},k_{2})}(M(4,2;\mathbb{C}))\\
{\rm d}\tau_{\lambda}(\mathcal{U}(\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathbb{R})\oplus\mathfrak{su}(1,5)))\mathcal{P}_{(k_{1},k_{2})}({\rm Skew}(5,\mathbb{C}))\end{cases}\subset M_{i}^{\mathfrak{e}_{6(-14)}}(\lambda)

holds if and only if

\lambda\in\begin{cases}-(k_{1}+k_{2})-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&i=1,\\
3-k_{2}-\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},&i=2.\end{cases}

8
Intertwining operators

In this section we apply the results on the inner products to the construction of intertwining operators between holomorphic discrete series
representations.

Let
G
be a connected Hermitian Lie group, with the complexification
G^{\mathbb{C}}
.
We fix a Cartan involution on
G
, and let
K\subset G
,
K^{\mathbb{C}}\subset G^{\mathbb{C}}
be the corresponding
maximal compact subgroup and its complexification.
Let
G_{1}\subset G
be a connected subgroup, with the complexification
G_{1}^{\mathbb{C}}\subset G^{\mathbb{C}}
,
such that
(G,G_{1})
is a symmetric pair of holomorphic type.
Without loss of generality we may assume that
G_{1}
is stable under the Cartan involution of
G
,
and let
K_{1}\subset G_{1}
,
K^{\mathbb{C}}_{1}\subset G^{\mathbb{C}}_{1}
be the corresponding maximal compact subgroup and its complexification.
Their corresponding Lie algebras are denoted by the corresponding lowercase frakturs,
and let
\mathfrak{g}^{\mathbb{C}}=\mathfrak{p}^{+}\oplus\mathfrak{k}^{\mathbb{C}}\oplus\mathfrak{p}^{-}
,
\mathfrak{g}^{\mathbb{C}}_{1}=\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1}\oplus\mathfrak{k}^{\mathbb{C}}_{1}\oplus\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{1}
be the gradings with respect to
the adjoint action of
\mathfrak{z}\big(\mathfrak{k}^{\mathbb{C}}\big)
. Then
\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}
,
\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{1}
have Jordan triple system structures.
Let
\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{2}=\big(\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{1}\big)^{\bot}\subset\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}
, and let
D\subset\mathfrak{p}^{+}
,
D_{1}\subset\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1}
be the bounded symmetric domains,
so that
G/K\simeq D
,
G_{1}/K_{1}\simeq D_{1}
hold.

For
\lambda>\frac{d}{2}(r-1)
, we consider the unitary representation
\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}(D)\subset\mathcal{O}(D)

of the universal covering group
\widetilde{G}
with the inner product
\langle\cdot,\cdot\rangle_{\lambda}
.
Then for the
\widetilde{K}
-finite part we have
\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}(D)_{\widetilde{K}}=\mathcal{P}(\mathfrak{p}^{+})
, and this is holomorphic discrete if
\lambda>p-1
.
Similarly, for a unitary representation
(\rho,W)=\big(\chi_{1}^{-\varepsilon_{1}\lambda}\otimes\rho_{0},W\big)=\big(\big(\chi^{-\lambda}|_{\widetilde{K}_{1}}\big)\otimes\rho_{0},W\big)
of
\widetilde{K}_{1}
, we consider the unitary representation

\mathcal{H}_{\varepsilon_{1}\lambda}(D_{1},W)\subset\mathcal{O}(D_{1},W)
of
\widetilde{G}_{1}
if it exists.
Then
\mathcal{H}_{\varepsilon_{1}\lambda}(D_{1},W)
appears in the decomposition of
\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}(D)|_{\widetilde{G}_{1}}

if and only if
(\rho_{0},W)
appears in the decomposition of
\mathcal{P}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2})
, and then there exists a symmetry breaking operator

\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,\rho_{0}}\colon\ \mathcal{H}_{\lambda}(D)|_{\widetilde{G}_{1}}\longrightarrow\mathcal{H}_{\varepsilon_{1}\lambda}(D_{1},W),

which is unique up to scalar multiple for
\lambda>\frac{d}{2}(r-1)
by the multiplicity-freeness of
\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}(D)|_{\widetilde{G}_{1}}
.
By Theorem
2.12
(1), this operator is given by substituting the differential operator to the polynomial

F^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,\rho_{0}}(z)=F^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,\rho_{0}}(z_{1},z_{2})\in\mathcal{P}(\mathfrak{p}^{-},W)
,

F^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,\rho_{0}}(z):=\big\langle{\rm e}^{(x|z)_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}},\mathrm{K}(\overline{x_{2}})^{*}\big\rangle_{\lambda,\mathfrak{p}^{+},x}=\big\langle\mathrm{K}(x_{2}),{\rm e}^{(x|\overline{z})_{\mathfrak{p}^{-}}},\big\rangle_{\lambda,\mathfrak{p}^{-},x},

where
\mathrm{K}(x_{2})\in\mathcal{P}\bigl(\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{2},\operatorname{Hom}_{\mathbb{C}}\big(\chi^{-\lambda},W\otimes\chi_{1}^{-\varepsilon_{1}\lambda}\big)\bigr)^{\widetilde{K}_{1}^{\mathbb{C}}}\simeq(\mathcal{P}(\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{2})\otimes W)^{K_{1}^{\mathbb{C}}}
.
Therefore, the construction of symmetry breaking operators are reduced to Theorems
5.1
and
6.3
.

8.1
Case
\boldsymbol{\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}}
is non-simple

First we consider the cases such that
\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{2}=\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{11}\oplus\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{22}
is non-simple, and write
\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{1}=\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{12}
.
We assume
\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{11}
is of tube type. That is, we consider

\displaystyle\big(\mathfrak{p}^{\pm},\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{11},\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{12},\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{22}\big)

\displaystyle\quad{}=\begin{cases}\big(\mathbb{C}^{d+2},\mathbb{C},\mathbb{C}^{d},\mathbb{C}\big)&(\textit{Case }1),\\
\big({\rm Sym}(r,\mathbb{C}),\operatorname{Sym}(r^{\prime},\mathbb{C}),M(r^{\prime},r^{\prime\prime};\mathbb{C}),\operatorname{Sym}(r^{\prime\prime},\mathbb{C})\big)&(\textit{Case }2),\\
\big(M(q,s;\mathbb{C}),M(r^{\prime},r^{\prime};\mathbb{C}),M(r^{\prime},s^{\prime\prime};\mathbb{C})\oplus M(q^{\prime\prime},r^{\prime};\mathbb{C}),M(q^{\prime\prime},s^{\prime\prime};\mathbb{C})\big)&(\textit{Case }3),\\
\big({\rm Skew}(s,\mathbb{C}),\operatorname{Skew}(2r^{\prime},\mathbb{C}),M(2r^{\prime},s^{\prime\prime};\mathbb{C}),\operatorname{Skew}(s^{\prime\prime},\mathbb{C})\big)&(\textit{Case }4),\\
\big({\rm Herm}(3,\mathbb{O})^{\mathbb{C}},\mathbb{C},M(1,2;\mathbb{O})^{\mathbb{C}},\operatorname{Herm}(2,\mathbb{O})^{\mathbb{C}}\big)&(\textit{Case }5),\\
\big(M(1,2;\mathbb{O})^{\mathbb{C}},\mathbb{C},\operatorname{Skew}(5,\mathbb{C}),M(1,5;\mathbb{C})\big)&(\textit{Case }6)\end{cases}\!\!\!

(8.1)

with
r=r^{\prime}+r^{\prime\prime}
for
Case
2,
q=r^{\prime}+q^{\prime\prime}
,
s=r^{\prime}+s^{\prime\prime}
for
Case
3, and
s=2r^{\prime}+s^{\prime\prime}
for
Case
4.
Then the corresponding symmetric pairs are

(G,G_{1})=\begin{cases}({\rm SO}_{0}(2,d+2),{\rm SO}_{0}(2,d)\times{\rm SO}(2))&(\textit{Case }1),\\
(\operatorname{Sp}(r,\mathbb{R}),{\rm U}(r^{\prime},r^{\prime\prime}))&(\textit{Case }2),\\
({\rm U}(q,s),{\rm U}(r^{\prime},s^{\prime\prime})\times{\rm U}(q^{\prime\prime},r^{\prime}))&(\textit{Case }3),\\
({\rm SO}^{*}(2s),{\rm U}(2r^{\prime},s^{\prime\prime}))&(\textit{Case }4),\\
(E_{7(-25)},{\rm U}(1)\times E_{6(-14)})&(\textit{Case }5),\\
(E_{6(-14)},{\rm U}(1)\times{\rm SO}^{*}(10))&(\textit{Case }6)\end{cases}

(up to covering). Here, for
Case
3 we consider
{\rm U}(q,s)
instead of
{\rm SU}(q,s)
.
Let
\dim\mathfrak{p}^{+}=:n
,
\dim\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}=:n^{\prime}
,
\dim\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}=:n^{\prime\prime}
,
\operatorname{rank}\mathfrak{p}^{+}=:r
,
\operatorname{rank}\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}=:r^{\prime}
,
\operatorname{rank}\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}=:r^{\prime\prime}
,
let
d
be the number defined in (
2.3
), and take
\varepsilon_{12}\in\{1,2\}
such that

(x_{12}|\overline{y_{12}})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}=\varepsilon_{12}(x_{12}|\overline{y_{12}})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{12}},\qquad x_{12},y_{12}\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{12},

so that we have

(r,r^{\prime},r^{\prime\prime},d,\varepsilon_{12})=\begin{cases}(2,1,1,d,1)&(\textit{Case }1,\;d\geq 2),\\
(2,1,1,1,2)&(\textit{Case }1,\;d=1),\\
(r,r^{\prime},r^{\prime\prime},1,2)&(\textit{Case }2),\\
(\min\{q,s\},r^{\prime},\min\{q^{\prime\prime},s^{\prime\prime}\},2,1)&(\textit{Case }3),\\
(\lfloor s/2\rfloor,r^{\prime},\lfloor s^{\prime\prime}/2\rfloor,4,1)&(\textit{Case }4),\\
(3,1,2,8,1)&(\textit{Case }5),\\
(2,1,1,6,1)&(\textit{Case }6).\end{cases}

For
j=1,2
, let
\mathfrak{k}_{jj}:=\big[\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{jj},\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{jj}\big]\cap\mathfrak{k}
, let
K_{jj}
be the corresponding connected subgroup of
G_{1}
,
and let
\Sigma_{jj}\subset\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{jj}
be the Bergman–Shilov boundary of the bounded symmetric domain,
with the normalized
K_{jj}
-invariant measure, so that
\Sigma_{jj}\simeq K_{jj}/K_{L_{jj}}M_{jj}
holds
for some
K_{L_{jj}}M_{jj}\subset K_{jj}
.

As an example, we consider
Case
2. Then according to the decomposition of
\mathcal{P}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}\big)\boxtimes\mathcal{P}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}\big)

under
K_{1}={\rm U}(r^{\prime})\times{\rm U}(r^{\prime\prime})
,

\mathcal{P}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}\big)\boxtimes\mathcal{P}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}\big)=\bigoplus_{\mathbf{k}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r^{\prime}}}\bigoplus_{\mathbf{l}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r^{\prime\prime}}}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{k}}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}\big)\boxtimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}\big)\simeq\bigoplus_{\mathbf{k}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r^{\prime}}}\bigoplus_{\mathbf{l}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r^{\prime\prime}}}V_{2\mathbf{k}}^{(r^{\prime})\vee}\boxtimes V_{2\mathbf{l}}^{(r^{\prime\prime})},

\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}(D_{\operatorname{Sp}(r,\mathbb{R})})
for
\lambda>\frac{r-1}{2}
is decomposed under
\widetilde{G}_{1}
as

\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}(D_{\operatorname{Sp}(r,\mathbb{R})})|_{\widetilde{G}_{1}}\simeq\sideset{}{{}^{\oplus}}{\sum}_{\mathbf{k}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r^{\prime}}}\sideset{}{{}^{\oplus}}{\sum}_{\mathbf{l}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r^{\prime\prime}}}\mathcal{H}_{\lambda+\lambda}\big(D_{{\rm U}(r^{\prime},r^{\prime\prime})},V_{2\mathbf{k}}^{(r^{\prime})\vee}\boxtimes V_{2\mathbf{l}}^{(r^{\prime\prime})}\big),

where
\mathcal{H}_{\lambda+\lambda}(D_{{\rm U}(r^{\prime},r^{\prime\prime})},V^{\prime}\boxtimes V^{\prime\prime})
is defined by using the character (
3.1
)
of
\widetilde{{\rm U}}(r^{\prime})\times\widetilde{{\rm U}}(r^{\prime\prime})\subset\widetilde{{\rm U}}(r^{\prime},r^{\prime\prime})
.
In the following let
\mathbf{k}=\underline{k}_{r^{\prime}}
, and consider the symmetry breaking operator

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}_{\lambda,k,\mathbf{l}}^{\downarrow}\colon\ \mathcal{H}_{\lambda}(D_{\operatorname{Sp}(r,\mathbb{R})})\longrightarrow{}

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{\lambda+\lambda}\big(D_{{\rm U}(r^{\prime},r^{\prime\prime})},V_{\underline{2k}_{r^{\prime}}}^{(r^{\prime})\vee}\boxtimes V_{2\mathbf{l}}^{(r^{\prime\prime})}\big)

\displaystyle\simeq{}

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{(\lambda+2k)+\lambda}\big(D_{{\rm U}(r^{\prime},r^{\prime\prime})},\mathbb{C}\boxtimes V_{2\mathbf{l}}^{(r^{\prime\prime})}\big).

We take a vector-valued polynomial

\mathrm{K}_{\mathbf{l}}(x_{22})\in\bigl(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}(\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{22})\otimes V_{2\mathbf{l}}^{(r^{\prime\prime})}\bigr)^{K_{22}}
, normalized such that

\big\|(v,\mathrm{K}_{\mathbf{l}}(\overline{x_{22}}))_{V_{2\mathbf{l}}^{(r^{\prime\prime})}}\big\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{22}),x_{22}}^{2}=|v|_{V_{2\mathbf{l}}^{(r^{\prime\prime})}}^{2},\qquad v\in V_{2\mathbf{l}}^{(r^{\prime\prime})},

or equivalently,

\big|\big\langle f(x_{22}),\overline{\mathrm{K}_{\mathbf{l}}(\overline{x_{22}})}\big\rangle_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{22}),x_{22}}\big|_{V_{2\mathbf{l}}^{(r^{\prime\prime})}}^{2}=\|f(x_{22})\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{22}),x_{22}}^{2},\qquad f(x_{22})\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}\big).

Using this, we define the polynomials

\tilde{\mathrm{K}}_{\mathbf{l},\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}^{\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{22}}(x_{22},y_{22})\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{m}}(\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{22})\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{n}}(\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{22})\otimes V_{2\mathbf{l}}^{(r^{\prime\prime})}
by

\sum_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r^{\prime\prime}}}\tilde{\mathrm{K}}_{\mathbf{l},\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}^{\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{22}}(x_{22},y_{22})=\mathrm{K}_{\mathbf{l}}(x_{22}+y_{22}).

Then the polynomial
F^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,\rho_{0}}(z)=F^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,\mathbf{l}}(z)\in\mathcal{P}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{-},V_{2\mathbf{l}}^{(r^{\prime\prime})}\big)
is computed by using Theorem
5.1
. We renormalize this as

\displaystyle F^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,\mathbf{l}}(z):\!={}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\big(\frac{n^{\prime}}{r^{\prime}}\big)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime}},d}\big(\frac{n^{\prime\prime}}{r^{\prime\prime}}\big)_{\mathbf{l},d}\big(\lambda+k-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\big)_{\mathbf{l},d}}

\displaystyle\times\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{11}}(z_{11})^{k}\sum_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r^{\prime\prime}}}(-k)_{\mathbf{m},d}\bigg(\lambda+k-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\bigg)_{\mathbf{n},d}\tilde{\mathrm{K}}_{\mathbf{l},\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}^{\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{22}}\big(Q(z_{12}){}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{11}^{-\mathit{1}},z_{22}\big)

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle\frac{\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{11}}(z_{11})^{k}}{\big(\frac{n^{\prime}}{r^{\prime}}\big)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime}},d}\big(\frac{n^{\prime\prime}}{r^{\prime\prime}}\big)_{\mathbf{l},d}}\sum_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r^{\prime\prime}}}\frac{(-k)_{\mathbf{m},d}}{\big(\lambda+k-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}+\mathbf{n}\big)_{\mathbf{l}-\mathbf{n},d}}\tilde{\mathrm{K}}_{\mathbf{l},\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}^{\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{22}}\big(Q(z_{12}){}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{11}^{-\mathit{1}},z_{22}\big),

with
d=1
. Then the linear map

\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,\mathbf{l}}=F^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,\mathbf{l}}\big(\frac{\partial}{\partial x}\big)\bigr|_{x_{11}=0,x_{22}=0}

becomes a symmetry breaking operator, and for
f(x_{22})\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22})
, by
[
9
, Part III, Corollary V.3.5]
we have

\displaystyle\big(\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,\mathbf{l}}\big(\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(x_{11})^{k}f(x_{22})\big)\big)(x_{12})

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{1}{\big(\frac{n^{\prime}}{r^{\prime}}\big)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime}},d}\big(\frac{n^{\prime\prime}}{r^{\prime\prime}}\big)_{\mathbf{l},d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{11}}\bigg(\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{11}}\bigg)^{k}\mathrm{K}_{\mathbf{l}}\bigg(\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{22}}\bigg)\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}(x_{11})^{k}f(x_{22})\bigg|_{x_{11}=0,x_{22}=0}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{1}{\big(\frac{n^{\prime}}{r^{\prime}}\big)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime}},d}\big(\frac{n^{\prime\prime}}{r^{\prime\prime}}\big)_{\mathbf{l},d}}\big\langle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}(x_{11})^{k}f(x_{22}),\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}(x_{11})^{k}\overline{\mathrm{K}_{\mathbf{l}}(\overline{x_{22}})}\big\rangle_{F,\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}\oplus\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22},x_{11},x_{22}}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\big\langle f(x_{22}),\overline{\mathrm{K}_{\mathbf{l}}(\overline{x_{22}})}\big\rangle_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{22}),x_{22}}.

Hence by the normalization of
\mathrm{K}_{\mathbf{l}}(x_{22})
we have

\displaystyle\big\|\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,\mathbf{l}}\big(\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(x_{11})^{k}f(x_{22})\big)(x_{12})\big\|_{\mathcal{H}_{(\lambda+2k)+\lambda}(D_{{\rm U}(r^{\prime},r^{\prime\prime})},\mathbb{C}\boxtimes V_{2\mathbf{l}}^{(r^{\prime\prime})})}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\big|\big\langle f(x_{22}),\overline{\mathrm{K}_{\mathbf{l}}(\overline{x_{22}})}\big\rangle_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{22}),x_{22}}\big|_{V_{2\mathbf{l}}^{(r^{\prime\prime})}}^{2}=\big\|f(x_{22})\big\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{22}),x_{22}}^{2}.

On the other hand, by (
5.7
) and again by
[
9
, Part III, Corollary V.3.5]
we have

\big\|\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}}(x_{11})^{k}f(x_{22})\big\|_{\lambda,\mathfrak{p}^{+},x}^{2}=\frac{\big(\frac{n^{\prime}}{r^{\prime}}\big)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime}},d}\big(\frac{n^{\prime\prime}}{r^{\prime\prime}}\big)_{\mathbf{l},d}\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\big)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}}+\mathbf{l},d}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k}_{r}+\mathbf{l},d}\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\big)_{\mathbf{l},d}}\big\|f(x_{22})\big\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{22}),x_{22}}^{2}.

Now since
\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,\mathbf{l}}
is an isometry up to constant on the subspace of
\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}(D_{\operatorname{Sp}(r,\mathbb{R})})

isomorphic to
\mathcal{H}_{(\lambda+2k)+\lambda}\big(D_{{\rm U}(r^{\prime},r^{\prime\prime})},\mathbb{C}\boxtimes V_{2\mathbf{l}}^{(r^{\prime\prime})}\big)
,
and identically zero on its orthogonal complement, we have

\big\|\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,\mathbf{l}}\big\|_{\mathrm{op}}^{-2}=\frac{\big(\frac{n^{\prime}}{r^{\prime}}\big)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime}},d}\big(\frac{n^{\prime\prime}}{r^{\prime\prime}}\big)_{\mathbf{l},d}\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\big)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}}+\mathbf{l},d}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k}_{r}+\mathbf{l},d}\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\big)_{\mathbf{l},d}}.

Similar results also hold for the other cases. Now we summarize the results so far.

Theorem 8.1
.

Let
\big(\mathfrak{p}^{\pm},\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{11},\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{12},\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{22}\big)
be as in
(\ref{list_nonsimple})
.
For
\mathbf{l}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r^{\prime\prime}}
, let
V_{\mathbf{l}}
be the representation of
K_{22}
isomorphic to
\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22})
, and
we take a vector-valued polynomial
\mathrm{K}_{\mathbf{l}}(x_{22})\in\bigl(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{l}}(\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{22})\otimes V_{\mathbf{l}}\bigr)^{K_{22}}
such that

\big\|(v,\mathrm{K}_{\mathbf{l}}(\overline{x_{22}}))_{V_{\mathbf{l}}}\big\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{22}),x_{22}}^{2}=|v|_{V_{\mathbf{l}}}^{2},\qquad v\in V_{\mathbf{l}}.

We define the polynomials

\tilde{\mathrm{K}}_{\mathbf{l},\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}^{\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{22}}(x_{22},y_{22})\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{m}}(\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{22})\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{n}}(\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{22})\otimes V_{\mathbf{l}}
by

\sum_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r^{\prime\prime}}}\tilde{\mathrm{K}}_{\mathbf{l},\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}^{\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{22}}(x_{22},y_{22})=\mathrm{K}_{\mathbf{l}}(x_{22}+y_{22}).

Using these, for
\lambda>\frac{d}{2}(r-1)
,
k\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
, we define

F^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,\mathbf{l}}(z)=F^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,\mathbf{l}}(z_{11},z_{12},z_{22})\in\mathcal{P}(\mathfrak{p}^{-},V_{\mathbf{l}})
by

F^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,\mathbf{l}}(z):=\frac{\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{11}}(z_{11})^{k}}{\left(\frac{n^{\prime}}{r^{\prime}}\right)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime}},d}\left(\frac{n^{\prime\prime}}{r^{\prime\prime}}\right)_{\mathbf{l},d}}\sum_{\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}\in\mathbb{Z}_{++}^{r^{\prime\prime}}}\frac{(-k)_{\mathbf{m},d}}{\left(\lambda+k-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}+\mathbf{n}\right)_{\mathbf{l}-\mathbf{n},d}}\tilde{\mathrm{K}}_{\mathbf{l},\mathbf{m},\mathbf{n}}^{\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{22}}\big(Q(z_{12}){}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{11}^{-\mathit{1}},z_{22}\big).

Then the linear map

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,\mathbf{l}}\colon\left\{\hskip-4.0pt\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}(D_{{\rm SO}_{0}(2,d+2)})&\longrightarrow\mathcal{H}_{\lambda+k+l}(D_{{\rm SO}_{0}(2,d)})\boxtimes\mathbb{C}_{-k+l}&(\textit{Case }1),\\
\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}(D_{\operatorname{Sp}(r,\mathbb{R})})&\longrightarrow\mathcal{H}_{(\lambda+2k)+\lambda}\big(D_{{\rm U}(r^{\prime},r^{\prime\prime})},\mathbb{C}\boxtimes V_{2\mathbf{l}}^{(r^{\prime\prime})}\big)&(\textit{Case }2),\\
\mathcal{H}_{\lambda_{1}+\lambda_{2}}(D_{{\rm U}(q,s)})&\longrightarrow\mathcal{H}_{(\lambda_{1}+k)+\lambda_{2}}\big(D_{{\rm U}(r^{\prime},s^{\prime\prime})},\mathbb{C}\boxtimes V_{\mathbf{l}}^{(s^{\prime\prime})}\big)&\\
&\qquad{}\mathbin{\hat{\boxtimes}}\mathcal{H}_{\lambda_{1}+(\lambda_{2}+k)}\big(D_{{\rm U}(q^{\prime\prime},r^{\prime})},V_{\mathbf{l}}^{(q^{\prime\prime})\vee}\boxtimes\mathbb{C}\big)&(\textit{Case }3),\\
\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}(D_{{\rm SO}^{*}(2s)})&\longrightarrow\mathcal{H}_{\left(\frac{\lambda}{2}+k\right)+\frac{\lambda}{2}}\big(D_{{\rm U}(2r^{\prime},s^{\prime\prime})},\mathbb{C}\boxtimes V_{\mathbf{l}^{2}}^{(s^{\prime\prime})}\big)&(\textit{Case }4),\\
\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}(D_{E_{7(-25)}})&\longrightarrow\mathcal{H}_{\lambda+k+\frac{|\mathbf{l}|}{2}}\big(D_{E_{6(-14)}},V_{(l_{1}-l_{2},0,0,0,0)}^{[10]\vee}\big)\boxtimes\chi_{{\rm U}(1)}^{-\lambda+2k-2|\mathbf{l}|}&(\textit{Case }5),\\
\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}(D_{E_{6(-14)}})&\longrightarrow\mathcal{H}_{\lambda+k}\big(D_{{\rm SO}^{*}(10)},V_{\left(\frac{l}{2},\frac{l}{2},\frac{l}{2},\frac{l}{2},-\frac{l}{2}\right)}^{(5)\vee}\big)\boxtimes\chi_{{\rm U}(1)}^{-\lambda+3k-3l}&(\textit{Case }6),\end{array}\right.

\displaystyle(\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,\mathbf{l}}f)(x_{12})=F^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,\mathbf{l}}\bigg(\frac{\partial}{\partial x}\bigg)f(x)\bigg|_{x_{11}=0,x_{22}=0}

intertwines the
\widetilde{G}_{1}
-action, where we put
\lambda_{1}+\lambda_{2}=\lambda
for Case
3
, and its operator norm is given by

\|\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,\mathbf{l}}\|^{-2}_{\mathrm{op}}=\frac{\left(\frac{n^{\prime}}{r^{\prime}}\right)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime}},d}\left(\frac{n^{\prime\prime}}{r^{\prime\prime}}\right)_{\mathbf{l},d}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\right)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}}+\mathbf{l},d}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k}_{r}+\mathbf{l},d}\left(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\right)_{\mathbf{l},d}}.

For holographic operators
\mathcal{F}^{\uparrow}_{\lambda,k,\mathbf{l}}
, see
[
33
, Theorems 5.7(2), 5.10 and 5.19(1)]
.
Next we assume
\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{22}
is also of tube type, that is, we consider
Case
1,
Case
2,
Case
3 with
q^{\prime\prime}=s^{\prime\prime}=r^{\prime\prime}
,
Case

, and
Case

Then by Theorem
5.1
and (

) we have

\displaystyle F_{\lambda,k,\mathbf{l}}^{\downarrow}(z)={}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\big(\frac{n^{\prime}}{r^{\prime}}\big)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime}},d}\big(\frac{n^{\prime\prime}}{r^{\prime\prime}}\big)_{\mathbf{l},d}}\frac{\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\big)_{\mathbf{l},d}}{\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\big)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}},d}\big(\lambda+k-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}\big)_{\mathbf{l},d}}

\displaystyle\times\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}}(z)^{-\lambda+\frac{n}{r}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{22}}\bigg(\frac{\partial}{\partial z_{22}}\bigg)^{k}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}}(z)^{\lambda+k-\frac{n}{r}}\mathrm{K}_{\mathbf{l}}(z_{22})

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle\frac{\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}}(z)^{-\lambda+\frac{n}{r}}}{\big(\frac{n^{\prime}}{r^{\prime}}\big)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime}},d}\big(\frac{n^{\prime\prime}}{r^{\prime\prime}}\big)_{\mathbf{l},d}\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime}+\mathbf{l}\big)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}},d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{22}}\bigg(\frac{\partial}{\partial z_{22}}\bigg)^{k}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}}(z)^{\lambda+k-\frac{n}{r}}\mathrm{K}_{\mathbf{l}}(z_{22}).

Moreover, if
\mathbf{l}=\underline{l}_{r^{\prime\prime}}
, then as in Corollary
5.4
, the intertwining operators become easier.
Here
{}_{0}F_{1}^{\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}}
and

are as in (

) and (

).

Theorem 8.2
.

Assume
\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}
,
\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{11}
,
\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{22}
are of tube type.
For
\lambda>\frac{d}{2}(r-1)
,
k,l\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
,
we define
F^{\uparrow}_{\lambda,k,l}(x_{11},x_{22};z_{12})\in\mathcal{O}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}\times\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}\times\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{12}\big)
by

F^{\uparrow}_{\lambda,k,l}(x_{11},x_{22};z_{12}):=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}(x_{11})^{k}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}(x_{22})^{l}{}_{0}F_{1}^{\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}}\bigg(\lambda+k+l-\frac{d-2}{2};x_{11},Q(z_{12})x_{22}\bigg),

and
F^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,\mathbf{l}}(z)=F^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,\mathbf{l}}(z_{11},z_{12},z_{22})\in\mathcal{P}(\mathfrak{p}^{-})
by

F^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,l}(z):=\frac{\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{11}}(z_{11})^{k}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{22}}(z_{22})^{l}}{\left(\frac{n^{\prime}}{r^{\prime}}\right)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime}},d}\left(\frac{n^{\prime\prime}}{r^{\prime\prime}}\right)_{\underline{l}_{r^{\prime\prime}},d}}{}_{2}F_{1}^{\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}}\left(\begin{matrix}-k,-l\\
-\lambda-k-l+\frac{n}{r}\end{matrix};{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{11}^{-\mathit{1}},Q(z_{12}){}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{22}^{-\mathit{1}}\right)\!.

Then the linear map

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}^{\uparrow}_{\lambda,k,l}\colon\left\{\hskip-4.0pt\begin{array}[]{lll}\bigl(\mathcal{H}_{\lambda+k+l}(D_{{\rm SO}_{0}(2,d)})\boxtimes\mathbb{C}_{-k+l}\bigr)_{\widetilde{K}_{1}}&\longrightarrow\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}(D_{{\rm SO}_{0}(2,d+2)})_{\widetilde{K}}&(\textit{Case }1),\\
\mathcal{H}_{(\lambda+2k)+(\lambda+2l)}(D_{{\rm U}(r^{\prime},r^{\prime\prime})})_{\widetilde{K}_{1}}&\longrightarrow\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}(D_{\operatorname{Sp}(r,\mathbb{R})})_{\widetilde{K}}&(\textit{Case }2),\\
\bigl(\mathcal{H}_{(\lambda_{1}+k)+(\lambda_{2}+l)}(D_{{\rm U}(r^{\prime},r^{\prime\prime})})&&\\
\hskip 17.0pt{}\mathbin{\hat{\boxtimes}}\mathcal{H}_{(\lambda_{1}+l)+(\lambda_{2}+k)}(D_{{\rm U}(r^{\prime\prime},r^{\prime})})\bigr)_{\widetilde{K}_{1}}&\longrightarrow\mathcal{H}_{\lambda_{1}+\lambda_{2}}(D_{{\rm U}(r,r)})_{\widetilde{K}}&(\textit{Case }3),\\
\mathcal{H}_{\left(\frac{\lambda}{2}+k\right)+\left(\frac{\lambda}{2}+l\right)}(D_{{\rm U}(2r^{\prime},2r^{\prime\prime})})_{\widetilde{K}_{1}}&\longrightarrow\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}(D_{{\rm SO}^{*}(4r)})_{\widetilde{K}}&(\textit{Case }4),\\
\bigl(\mathcal{H}_{\lambda+k+l}(D_{E_{6(-14)}})\boxtimes\chi_{{\rm U}(1)}^{-\lambda+2k-4l}\bigr)_{\widetilde{K}_{1}}&\longrightarrow\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}(D_{E_{7(-25)}})_{\widetilde{K}}&(\textit{Case }5),\end{array}\right.

\displaystyle(\mathcal{F}^{\uparrow}_{\lambda,k,l}f)(x)=F^{\uparrow}_{\lambda,k,l}\left(x_{11},x_{22};\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{12}}\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{12}}\right)f(x_{12}),

intertwines the
\big(\mathfrak{g}_{1},\widetilde{K}_{1}\big)
-action, and the linear map

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,l}\colon\left\{\hskip-4.0pt\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}(D_{{\rm SO}_{0}(2,d+2)})&\longrightarrow\mathcal{H}_{\lambda+k+l}(D_{{\rm SO}_{0}(2,d)})\boxtimes\mathbb{C}_{-k+l}&(\textit{Case }1),\\
\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}(D_{\operatorname{Sp}(r,\mathbb{R})})&\longrightarrow\mathcal{H}_{(\lambda+2k)+(\lambda+2l)}(D_{{\rm U}(r^{\prime},r^{\prime\prime})})&(\textit{Case }2),\\
\mathcal{H}_{\lambda_{1}+\lambda_{2}}(D_{{\rm U}(r,r)})&\longrightarrow\mathcal{H}_{(\lambda_{1}+k)+(\lambda_{2}+l)}(D_{{\rm U}(r^{\prime},r^{\prime\prime})})&\\
&\hskip 35.0pt{}\mathbin{\hat{\boxtimes}}\mathcal{H}_{(\lambda_{1}+l)+(\lambda_{2}+k)}(D_{{\rm U}(r^{\prime\prime},r^{\prime})})&(\textit{Case }3),\\
\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}(D_{{\rm SO}^{*}(4r)})&\longrightarrow\mathcal{H}_{\left(\frac{\lambda}{2}+k\right)+\left(\frac{\lambda}{2}+l\right)}(D_{{\rm U}(2r^{\prime},2r^{\prime\prime})})&(\textit{Case }4),\\
\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}(D_{E_{7(-25)}})&\longrightarrow\mathcal{H}_{\lambda+k+l}(D_{E_{6(-14)}})\boxtimes\chi_{{\rm U}(1)}^{-\lambda+2k-4l}&(\textit{Case }5),\end{array}\right.

\displaystyle\big(\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,l}f\big)(x_{12})=F^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,l}\bigg(\frac{\partial}{\partial x}\bigg)f(x)\bigg|_{x_{11}=0,x_{22}=0}

intertwines the
\widetilde{G}_{1}
-action, where we put
\lambda_{1}+\lambda_{2}=\lambda
for Case
3
. Their operator norms are given by

\displaystyle\big\|\mathcal{F}^{\uparrow}_{\lambda,k,l}\big\|^{2}_{\mathrm{op}}=\big\|\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,l}\big\|^{-2}_{\mathrm{op}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\big(\frac{n^{\prime}}{r^{\prime}}\big)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime}},d}\big(\frac{n^{\prime\prime}}{r^{\prime\prime}}\big)_{\underline{l}_{r^{\prime\prime}},d}\big(\lambda+l-\frac{1}{2}r^{\prime}\big)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}},d}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k+l}_{r^{\prime\prime}},d}\big(\lambda-\frac{1}{2}r^{\prime\prime}\big)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime}},d}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\big(\frac{n^{\prime}}{r^{\prime}}\big)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime}},d}\big(\frac{n^{\prime\prime}}{r^{\prime\prime}}\big)_{\underline{l}_{r^{\prime\prime}},d}\big(\lambda+k-\frac{1}{2}r^{\prime\prime}\big)_{\underline{l}_{r^{\prime}},d}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k+l}_{r^{\prime}},d}\big(\lambda-\frac{1}{2}r^{\prime}\big)_{\underline{l}_{r^{\prime\prime}},d}}.

Here we normalize
\frac{\partial}{\partial x}
and
\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{12}}
with respect to the bilinear forms

(\cdot|\cdot)_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}\colon\mathfrak{p}^{+}\times\mathfrak{p}^{-}\to\mathbb{C}
and
(\cdot|\cdot)_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{12}}\colon\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{12}\times\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{12}\to\mathbb{C}
respectively,
so that
\frac{\partial}{\partial x}\bigr|_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{12}}=\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{12}}\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{12}}
holds.

For
Case
3, the above result is generalized to the case such that
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}=M(q^{\prime},s^{\prime};\mathbb{C})
,
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}=M(q^{\prime\prime},s^{\prime\prime};\mathbb{C})
are of non-tube type,
as in Theorem
5.9
(3).
When
q^{\prime}\leq s^{\prime}
, let
\mathcal{P}_{\underline{k}_{q^{\prime}}}(M(q^{\prime},s^{\prime};\mathbb{C}))_{R}
be the representation of
{\rm GL}(s^{\prime},\mathbb{C})
given by

g.f(x_{11}):=f(x_{11}g),\qquad g\in{\rm GL}(s^{\prime},\mathbb{C}),\quad f\in\mathcal{P}_{\underline{k}_{q^{\prime}}}(M(q^{\prime},s^{\prime};\mathbb{C})),

(8.2)

so that
\mathcal{P}_{\underline{k}_{q^{\prime}}}(M(q^{\prime},s^{\prime};\mathbb{C}))_{R}\simeq V_{\underline{k}_{q^{\prime}}}^{(s^{\prime})}
holds.
Similarly, when
q^{\prime}\geq s^{\prime}
, let
\mathcal{P}_{\underline{k}_{s^{\prime}}}(M(q^{\prime},s^{\prime};\mathbb{C}))_{L}
be the representation of
{\rm GL}(q^{\prime},\mathbb{C})
given by

g.f(x_{11}):=f\big(g^{-1}x_{11}\big),\qquad g\in{\rm GL}(q^{\prime},\mathbb{C}),\quad f\in\mathcal{P}_{\underline{k}_{s^{\prime}}}(M(q^{\prime},s^{\prime};\mathbb{C})),

so that
\mathcal{P}_{\underline{k}_{s^{\prime}}}(M(q^{\prime},s^{\prime};\mathbb{C}))_{L}\simeq V_{\underline{k}_{s^{\prime}}}^{(q^{\prime})\vee}
holds.
We consider the Fischer inner product
\langle\cdot,\cdot\rangle_{F}
on
\mathcal{P}_{\underline{k}_{q^{\prime}}}(M(q^{\prime},s^{\prime};\mathbb{C}))_{R}
and

\mathcal{P}_{\underline{k}_{s^{\prime}}}(M(q^{\prime},s^{\prime};\mathbb{C}))_{L}
. Especially if
q^{\prime}=s^{\prime}
, then the identification
\mathbb{C}_{-k}\to\mathcal{P}_{\underline{k}_{q^{\prime}}}(M(q^{\prime},\mathbb{C}))
,

1\mapsto\operatorname{det}(x_{11})^{k}
has the operator norm
\sqrt{(q^{\prime})_{\underline{k}_{q^{\prime}},2}}
.
Then the intertwining operators are given as follows. Here
{}_{0}F_{1}^{(2)}
and
{}_{2}F_{1}^{(2)}
are as in (
3.4
) and (
3.6
).

Theorem 8.3
.

We consider
\big(\mathfrak{p}^{\pm},\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{11},\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{12},\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{21},\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{22}\big)=(M(q,s;\mathbb{C}),M(q^{\prime},s^{\prime};\mathbb{C}),M(q^{\prime},s^{\prime\prime};\mathbb{C}),\linebreak M(q^{\prime\prime},s^{\prime};\mathbb{C}),M(q^{\prime\prime},s^{\prime\prime};\mathbb{C}))
with
q=q^{\prime}+q^{\prime\prime}
,
s=s^{\prime}+s^{\prime\prime}
. We assume
q^{\prime}\leq s^{\prime}
.
For
\lambda=\lambda_{1}+\lambda_{2}>\min\{q,s\}-1
,
k,l\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
, we define

F^{\uparrow}_{\lambda,k,l}(x_{11},x_{22};z_{12},z_{21},y_{11},y_{22})\in\mathcal{O}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}\times\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}\times\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{12}\times\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{21}\times\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{11}\times\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{22}\big)
by

\displaystyle F^{\uparrow}_{\lambda,k,l}(x_{11},x_{22};z_{12},z_{21},y_{11},y_{22})

\displaystyle\qquad{}:=\begin{cases}\displaystyle\frac{\operatorname{det}\big(x_{11}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0pty_{11}\big)^{k}\operatorname{det}\big(x_{22}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0pty_{22}\big)^{l}}{(q^{\prime})_{\underline{k}_{q^{\prime}},2}(q^{\prime\prime})_{\underline{l}_{q^{\prime\prime}},2}}{}_{0}F_{1}^{(2)}\big(\lambda+k+l;x_{11}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{21}x_{22}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{12}\big),&q^{\prime\prime}\leq s^{\prime\prime},\\[12.91663pt]
\displaystyle\frac{\operatorname{det}\big(x_{11}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0pty_{11}\big)^{k}\operatorname{det}\big({}^{t}\hskip-1.0pty_{22}x_{22}\big)^{l}}{(q^{\prime})_{\underline{k}_{q^{\prime}},2}(s^{\prime\prime})_{\underline{l}_{s^{\prime\prime}},2}}{}_{0}F_{1}^{(2)}\big(\lambda+k+l;x_{11}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{21}x_{22}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{12}\big),&q^{\prime\prime}\geq s^{\prime\prime},\end{cases}

and
F^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,l}(z;w_{11},w_{22})=F^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,l}\big(\!\left(\begin{smallmatrix}z_{11}&z_{12}\\
z_{21}&z_{22}\end{smallmatrix}\right);w_{11},w_{22}\big)\in\mathcal{P}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{-}\times\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}\times\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}\big)
by

\displaystyle F^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,l}(z;w_{11},w_{22})

\displaystyle\qquad{}:=\begin{cases}\displaystyle\frac{\operatorname{det}\big(z_{11}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{11}\big)^{k}\operatorname{det}\big(z_{22}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{22}\big)^{l}}{(q^{\prime})_{\underline{k}_{q^{\prime}},2}(q^{\prime\prime})_{\underline{l}_{q^{\prime\prime}},2}}\vskip 2.84526pt\\
\ \displaystyle\times\,{}_{2}F_{1}^{(2)}\left(\begin{matrix}-k,-l\\
-\lambda\!-\!k\!-\!l\!+\!q^{\prime}\!+\!q^{\prime\prime}\end{matrix};\big(z_{11}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{11}\big)^{-1}z_{12}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{22}\big(z_{22}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{22}\big)^{-1}z_{21}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{11}\right)\!,&q^{\prime\prime}\leq s^{\prime\prime},\\
\displaystyle\frac{\operatorname{det}\big(z_{11}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{11}\big)^{k}\operatorname{det}\big({}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{22}z_{22}\big)^{l}}{(q^{\prime})_{\underline{k}_{q^{\prime}},2}(s^{\prime\prime})_{\underline{l}_{s^{\prime\prime}},2}}\vskip 2.84526pt\\
\ \displaystyle\times\,{}_{2}F_{1}^{(2)}\left(\begin{matrix}-k,-l\\
-\lambda\!-\!k\!-\!l\!+\!q^{\prime}\!+\!s^{\prime\prime}\end{matrix};\big(z_{11}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{11}\big)^{-1}z_{12}\big({}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{22}z_{22}\big)^{-1}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{22}z_{21}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{11}\right)\!,&q^{\prime\prime}\geq s^{\prime\prime}.\end{cases}

Then the linear map

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}^{\uparrow}_{\lambda,k,l}\colon\ \begin{cases}\bigl(\mathcal{H}_{(\lambda_{1}+k)+\lambda_{2}}(D_{{\rm U}(q^{\prime},s^{\prime\prime})},\mathbb{C}\boxtimes\mathcal{P}_{\underline{l}_{q^{\prime\prime}}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22})_{R})\\
\qquad{}\mathbin{\hat{\boxtimes}}\mathcal{H}_{(\lambda_{1}+l)+\lambda_{2}}(D_{{\rm U}(q^{\prime\prime},s^{\prime})},\mathbb{C}\boxtimes\mathcal{P}_{\underline{k}_{q^{\prime}}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11})_{R})\bigr)_{\widetilde{K}_{1}},\hskip 28.45274ptq^{\prime\prime}\leq s^{\prime\prime},\\[8.61108pt]
\bigl(\mathcal{H}_{(\lambda_{1}+k)+(\lambda_{2}+l)}(D_{{\rm U}(q^{\prime},s^{\prime\prime})})\mathbin{\hat{\boxtimes}}\mathcal{H}_{\lambda_{1}+\lambda_{2}}(D_{{\rm U}(q^{\prime\prime},s^{\prime})},\mathcal{P}_{\underline{l}_{s^{\prime\prime}}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22})_{L}\boxtimes\mathcal{P}_{\underline{k}_{q^{\prime}}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11})_{R})\bigr)_{\widetilde{K}_{1}},\\
\hskip 261.76535ptq^{\prime\prime}\geq s^{\prime\prime}\end{cases}

\displaystyle\qquad{}\longrightarrow\mathcal{H}_{\lambda_{1}+\lambda_{2}}(D_{{\rm U}(q,s)})_{\widetilde{K}},

\displaystyle\big(\mathcal{F}^{\uparrow}_{\lambda,k,l}f\big)(x)=F^{\uparrow}_{\lambda,k,l}\bigg(x_{11},x_{22};\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{12}},\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{21}},\frac{\partial}{\partial w_{11}},\frac{\partial}{\partial w_{22}}\bigg)f(x_{12},x_{21},w_{11},w_{22})\bigg|_{w_{11}=0,\,w_{22}=0}

intertwines the
\big(\mathfrak{u}(q^{\prime},s^{\prime\prime})\oplus\mathfrak{u}(q^{\prime\prime},s^{\prime}),\widetilde{{\rm U}}(q^{\prime})\times\widetilde{{\rm U}}(s^{\prime\prime})\times\widetilde{{\rm U}}(q^{\prime\prime})\times\widetilde{{\rm U}}(s^{\prime})\big)
-action,
and the linear map

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,l}\colon\ \mathcal{H}_{\lambda_{1}+\lambda_{2}}(D_{{\rm U}(q,s)})\!\longrightarrow\!\begin{cases}\mathcal{H}_{(\lambda_{1}+k)+\lambda_{2}}\big(D_{{\rm U}(q^{\prime},s^{\prime\prime})},\mathbb{C}\boxtimes\mathcal{P}_{\underline{l}_{q^{\prime\prime}}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22})_{R}\big)\\
\quad{}\mathbin{\hat{\boxtimes}}\mathcal{H}_{(\lambda_{1}+l)+\lambda_{2}}\big(D_{{\rm U}(q^{\prime\prime},s^{\prime})},\mathbb{C}\boxtimes\mathcal{P}_{\underline{k}_{q^{\prime}}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11})_{R}\big),\hskip 34.1433ptq^{\prime\prime}\leq s^{\prime\prime},\\
\mathcal{H}_{(\lambda_{1}+k)+(\lambda_{2}+l)}\big(D_{{\rm U}(q^{\prime},s^{\prime\prime})}\big)\\
\quad{}\mathbin{\hat{\boxtimes}}\mathcal{H}_{\lambda_{1}+\lambda_{2}}\big(D_{{\rm U}(q^{\prime\prime},s^{\prime})},\mathcal{P}_{\underline{l}_{s^{\prime\prime}}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22})_{L}\boxtimes\mathcal{P}_{\underline{k}_{q^{\prime}}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11})_{R}\big),\ \ q^{\prime\prime}\geq s^{\prime\prime},\end{cases}\!\!\!\!\!\!

\displaystyle\big(\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,l}f\big)(x_{12},x_{21},w_{11},w_{22})=F^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,l}\bigg(\frac{\partial}{\partial x};w_{11},w_{22}\bigg)f(x)\bigg|_{x_{11}=0,\,x_{22}=0}

intertwines the
\widetilde{{\rm U}}(q^{\prime},s^{\prime\prime})\times\widetilde{{\rm U}}(q^{\prime\prime},s^{\prime})
-action. Their operator norms are given by

\displaystyle\big\|\mathcal{F}^{\uparrow}_{\lambda,k,l}\big\|^{2}_{\mathrm{op}}=\big\|\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,l}\big\|^{-2}_{\mathrm{op}}=\begin{cases}\displaystyle\frac{(\lambda+l-q^{\prime})_{\underline{k}_{q^{\prime\prime}},2}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k+l}_{q^{\prime\prime}},2}(\lambda-q^{\prime\prime})_{\underline{k}_{q^{\prime}},2}}=\frac{(\lambda+k-q^{\prime\prime})_{\underline{l}_{q^{\prime}},2}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k+l}_{q^{\prime}},2}(\lambda-q^{\prime})_{\underline{l}_{q^{\prime\prime}},2}},&q^{\prime\prime}\leq s^{\prime\prime},\\[8.61108pt]
\displaystyle\frac{(\lambda+l-q^{\prime})_{\underline{k}_{s^{\prime\prime}},2}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k+l}_{s^{\prime\prime}},2}(\lambda-s^{\prime\prime})_{\underline{k}_{q^{\prime}},2}}=\frac{(\lambda+k-s^{\prime\prime})_{\underline{l}_{q^{\prime}},2}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{k+l}_{q^{\prime}},2}(\lambda-q^{\prime})_{\underline{l}_{s^{\prime\prime}},2}},&q^{\prime\prime}\geq s^{\prime\prime}.\end{cases}

Except for the results on operator norms, Theorems
8.1
,
8.2
and
8.3
hold
without the assumption
\lambda>\frac{d}{2}(r-1)
, if
\lambda
is not a pole and if we replace
\mathcal{H}
with
\mathcal{O}
.

We return to the case
\mathfrak{p}^{+}
,
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}
,
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}
are of tube type. If
\lambda=\frac{n}{r}-a
with
a=1,2,\dots,\min\{k,l_{r^{\prime\prime}}\}
,
then by Corollary
5.6
we have

\displaystyle F_{\frac{n}{r}-a,k,\mathbf{l}}^{\downarrow}(z)

\displaystyle=\frac{\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}}(z)^{a}}{\big(\frac{n^{\prime}}{r^{\prime}}\big)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime}},d}\big(\frac{n^{\prime\prime}}{r^{\prime\prime}}\big)_{\mathbf{l},d}\big(\frac{n^{\prime\prime}}{r^{\prime\prime}}-a+\mathbf{l}\big)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime\prime}},d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{22}}\left(\frac{\partial}{\partial z_{22}}\right)^{k}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}}(z)^{k-a}\mathrm{K}_{\mathbf{l}}(z_{22})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\big(\frac{n^{\prime}}{r^{\prime}}\big)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime}},d}\big(\frac{n^{\prime\prime}}{r^{\prime\prime}}\big)_{\mathbf{l},d}\big(\frac{n^{\prime\prime}}{r^{\prime\prime}}\!+\!\mathbf{l}\big)_{\underline{k-a}_{r^{\prime\prime}},d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{22}}\bigg(\frac{\partial}{\partial z_{22}}\bigg)^{k-a}\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}}(z)^{k}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{22}}(z_{22})^{-a}\mathrm{K}_{\mathbf{l}}(z_{22})

\displaystyle=\frac{\big(\frac{n^{\prime}}{r^{\prime}}\big)_{\underline{k-a}_{r^{\prime}},d}\big(\frac{n^{\prime\prime}}{r^{\prime\prime}}\big)_{\mathbf{l}-\underline{a}_{r^{\prime\prime}},d}}{\big(\frac{n^{\prime}}{r^{\prime}}\big)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime}},d}\big(\frac{n^{\prime\prime}}{r^{\prime\prime}}\big)_{\mathbf{l},d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}}(z)^{a}F_{\frac{n}{r}+a,k-a,\mathbf{l}-\underline{a}_{r^{\prime\prime}}}^{\downarrow}(z)

\displaystyle=\frac{(-1)^{ar}}{(-k)_{\underline{a}_{r^{\prime}},d}(-\mathbf{l}^{\vee})_{\underline{a}_{r^{\prime\prime}},d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}}(z)^{a}F_{\frac{n}{r}+a,k-a,\mathbf{l}-\underline{a}_{r^{\prime\prime}}}^{\downarrow}(z),

where
\mathbf{l}^{\vee}=(l_{r^{\prime\prime}},\dots,l_{1})
.
Next we additionally assume
r^{\prime}\leq r^{\prime\prime}
and
\mathbf{l}=\underline{l}_{r^{\prime\prime}}
.
Then at
\lambda=\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime\prime}-k-l+a
with
a=1,2,\dots,\min\{k,l\}
, by Proposition
2.6
(2) we have

\displaystyle\lim_{\lambda\to\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime\prime}-k-l+a}\bigg({-}\lambda-k-l+\frac{n}{r}\bigg)_{\underline{a}_{r^{\prime}},d}F^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,l}(z)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\lim_{\lambda\to\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime\prime}-k-l+a}\frac{\big({-}\lambda-k-l+\frac{n}{r}\big)_{\underline{a}_{r^{\prime}},d}}{\big(\frac{n^{\prime}}{r^{\prime}}\big)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime}},d}\big(\frac{n^{\prime\prime}}{r^{\prime\prime}}\big)_{\underline{l}_{r^{\prime\prime}},d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{11}}(z_{11})^{k}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{22}}(z_{22})^{l}

\displaystyle\qquad\hphantom{=}{}\ \times{}_{2}F_{1}^{\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}}\left(\begin{matrix}-k,-l\\
-\lambda-k-l+\frac{n}{r}\end{matrix};{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{11}^{-\mathit{1}},Q(z_{12}){}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{22}^{-\mathit{1}}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{(-k)_{\underline{a}_{r^{\prime}},d}(-l)_{\underline{a}_{r^{\prime}},d}}{\big(\frac{n^{\prime}}{r^{\prime}}\big)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime}},d}\big(\frac{n^{\prime\prime}}{r^{\prime\prime}}\big)_{\underline{l}_{r^{\prime\prime}},d}\big(\frac{n^{\prime}}{r^{\prime}}\big)_{\underline{a}_{r^{\prime}},d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{11}}(z_{11})^{k}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{22}}(z_{22})^{l}

\displaystyle\qquad\hphantom{=}{}\ \times\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{11}}({}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{11}^{-\mathit{1}})^{a}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{11}}\left(Q(z_{12}){}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{22}^{-\mathit{1}}\right)^{a}{}_{2}F_{1}^{\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}}\left(\begin{matrix}-k+a,-l+a\\
\frac{n^{\prime}}{r^{\prime}}+a\end{matrix};{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{11}^{-\mathit{1}},Q(z_{12}){}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{22}^{-\mathit{1}}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{\big(\frac{n^{\prime}}{r^{\prime}}+k-a\big)_{\underline{a}_{r^{\prime}},d}\big(\frac{n^{\prime}}{r^{\prime}}+l-a\big)_{\underline{a}_{r^{\prime}},d}}{\big(\frac{n^{\prime}}{r^{\prime}}\big)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime}},d}\big(\frac{n^{\prime\prime}}{r^{\prime\prime}}\big)_{\underline{l}_{r^{\prime\prime}},d}\big(\frac{n^{\prime}}{r^{\prime}}\big)_{\underline{a}_{r^{\prime}},d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{11}}(z_{11})^{k-a}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{22}}(z_{22})^{l}

\displaystyle\qquad\hphantom{=}{}\ \times\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{11}}\big(Q(z_{12}){}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{22}^{-\mathit{1}}\big)^{a}{}_{2}F_{1}^{\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}}\left(\begin{matrix}-k+a,-l+a\\
\frac{n^{\prime}}{r^{\prime}}+a\end{matrix};{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{11}^{-\mathit{1}},Q(z_{12}){}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{22}^{-\mathit{1}}\right)\!.

Especially, when
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{12}
is also of tube type, let
\dim\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{12}=:n_{12}
,
\operatorname{rank}\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{12}=:r_{12}
.
Then
r^{\prime}=r^{\prime\prime}
and
\frac{d}{2}r^{\prime\prime}=\frac{n_{12}}{\varepsilon_{12}r_{12}}
hold.
Let
\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{12}}(z_{12})
be the polynomial on
\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{12}
satisfying

\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{11}}(Q(z_{12})x_{22})=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{12}}(z_{12})^{\varepsilon_{12}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{22}}(x_{22}),\qquad z_{12}\in\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{12},\quad x_{22}\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}.

Then we have

\displaystyle\lim_{\lambda\to\frac{n_{12}}{\varepsilon_{12}r_{12}}-k-l+a}\bigg({-}\lambda-k-l+\frac{n}{r}\bigg)_{\underline{a}_{r^{\prime}},d}F^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,l}(z)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{11}}(z_{11})^{k-a}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{22}}(z_{22})^{l-a}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{12}}(z_{12})^{\varepsilon_{12}a}}{\big(\frac{n^{\prime}}{r^{\prime}}\big)_{\underline{k-a}_{r^{\prime}},d}\big(\frac{n^{\prime\prime}}{r^{\prime\prime}}\big)_{\underline{l-a}_{r^{\prime\prime}},d}\big(\frac{n^{\prime}}{r^{\prime}}\big)_{\underline{a}_{r^{\prime}},d}}{}_{2}F_{1}^{\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}}\left(\begin{matrix}-k\!+\!a,-l\!+\!a\\
\frac{n^{\prime}}{r^{\prime}}+a\end{matrix};{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{11}^{-\mathit{1}},Q(z_{12}){}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{22}^{-\mathit{1}}\!\right)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{1}{\big(\frac{n^{\prime}}{r^{\prime}}\big)_{\underline{a}_{r^{\prime}},d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{12}}(z_{12})^{\varepsilon_{12}a}F^{\downarrow}_{\frac{n_{12}}{\varepsilon_{12}r_{12}}-k-l+a,k-a,l-a}(z).

Therefore the following holds.

Theorem 8.4
.

1.

Assume
\mathfrak{p}^{+}
,
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}
,
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}
are of tube type. If
\lambda=\frac{n}{r}-a
,
a=1,2,\dots,\allowbreak\min\{k,l_{r^{\prime\prime}}\}
,
then we have

\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}_{\frac{n}{r}-a,k,\mathbf{l}}=\frac{(-1)^{ar}}{(-k)_{\underline{a}_{r^{\prime}},d}(-\mathbf{l}^{\vee})_{\underline{a}_{r^{\prime\prime}},d}}\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}_{\frac{n}{r}+a,k-a,\mathbf{l}-\underline{a}_{r^{\prime\prime}}}\circ\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}}\bigg(\frac{\partial}{\partial x}\bigg)^{a}.

2.

Assume
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{12}
is also of tube type, and let
\mathbf{l}=\underline{l}_{r^{\prime\prime}}
.
At
\lambda=\frac{n_{12}}{\varepsilon_{12}r_{12}}-k-l+a
,
a=1,2,\allowbreak\dots,\min\{k,l\}
, we have

\displaystyle\lim_{\lambda\to\frac{n_{12}}{\varepsilon_{12}r_{12}}-k-l+a}\bigg({-}\lambda-k-l+\frac{n}{r}\bigg)_{\underline{a}_{r^{\prime}},d}\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,l}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{1}{\big(\frac{n^{\prime}}{r^{\prime}}\big)_{\underline{a}_{r^{\prime}},d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{12}}\bigg(\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{12}}\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{12}}\bigg)^{\varepsilon_{12}a}\circ\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}_{\frac{n_{12}}{\varepsilon_{12}r_{12}}-k-l+a,k-a,l-a}.

This theorem is regarded as an analogue of the functional identities
[
27
, Corollaries 12.7(2) and 12.8(3)]
.
Here, for individual cases, when we take a maximal tripotent
e\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{11}\oplus\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{22}
suitably,

\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}}(z)
and
\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{12}}(z_{12})
(for
r^{\prime}=r^{\prime\prime}
case) are given by

\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}}(z)=q(z),\qquad\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{12}}(z_{12})^{\varepsilon_{12}}=-q(z_{12})

for
Case
1,

\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}}\begin{pmatrix}z_{11}&z_{12}\\
{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{12}&z_{22}\end{pmatrix}=\operatorname{det}\begin{pmatrix}z_{11}&z_{12}\\
{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{12}&z_{22}\end{pmatrix}\!,\qquad\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{12}}\begin{pmatrix}0&z_{12}\\
{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{12}&0\end{pmatrix}=\operatorname{det}(z_{12})

for
Case
2,

\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}}\begin{pmatrix}z_{11}&z_{12}\\
z_{21}&z_{22}\end{pmatrix}=\operatorname{det}\begin{pmatrix}z_{11}&z_{12}\\
z_{21}&z_{22}\end{pmatrix}\!,\qquad\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{12}}\begin{pmatrix}0&z_{12}\\
z_{21}&0\end{pmatrix}=\operatorname{det}(z_{12})\operatorname{det}(z_{21})

for
Case
3,

\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}}\begin{pmatrix}z_{11}&z_{12}\\
-{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{12}&z_{22}\end{pmatrix}=\operatorname{Pf}\begin{pmatrix}z_{11}&z_{12}\\
-{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{12}&z_{22}\end{pmatrix}\!,\qquad\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{12}}\begin{pmatrix}0&z_{12}\\
-{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{12}&0\end{pmatrix}=\operatorname{det}(z_{12})

for
Case
4, and

\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}}\begin{pmatrix}z_{11}&z_{12}\\
{}^{t}\hskip-1.0pt\hat{z}_{12}&z_{22}\end{pmatrix}=\operatorname{det}\begin{pmatrix}z_{11}&z_{12}\\
{}^{t}\hskip-1.0pt\hat{z}_{12}&z_{22}\end{pmatrix}

for
Case
5. By
[
1
, Theorem 6.4]
, if
\mathfrak{p}^{+}
is of tube type, then for
a\in\mathbb{Z}_{>0}
,

\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}}\bigg(\frac{\partial}{\partial x}\bigg)^{a}\colon\ \mathcal{O}_{\frac{n}{r}-a}(D)\longrightarrow\mathcal{O}_{\frac{n}{r}+a}(D)

(8.3)

intertwines the
\widetilde{G}
-action (see also
[
14
, Propositions 1.2 and 2.2]
,
[
39
, Lemma 7.1, equations (8.6)–(8.8)]
), and similar for
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{12}
. Especially, the both sides of Theorem
8.4
(1), (2) intertwine the
\widetilde{G}_{1}
-action.

8.2
Case
\boldsymbol{\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}}
is simple

Next we consider the cases such that
\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{2}
is simple and
\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}
,
\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{2}
are of tube type.
That is, we consider

(\mathfrak{p}^{\pm},\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{1},\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{2})=\begin{cases}(\mathbb{C}^{n},\mathbb{C}^{n^{\prime}},\mathbb{C}^{n^{\prime\prime}}),\quad n=n^{\prime}+n^{\prime\prime},\quad n^{\prime\prime}\geq 3&(\textit{Case }1),\\
(\mathbb{C}^{n},\mathbb{C}^{n-1},\mathbb{C}),\quad n\geq 3&(\textit{Case }1^{\prime}),\\
({\rm Sym}(2r^{\prime},\mathbb{C}),\operatorname{Sym}(r^{\prime},\mathbb{C})\oplus\operatorname{Sym}(r^{\prime},\mathbb{C}),M(r^{\prime},\mathbb{C}))&(\textit{Case }2),\\
({\rm Skew}(2s^{\prime},\mathbb{C}),\operatorname{Skew}(s^{\prime},\mathbb{C})\oplus\operatorname{Skew}(s^{\prime},\mathbb{C}),M(s^{\prime},\mathbb{C}))&(\textit{Case }3),\\
(M(r,\mathbb{C}),\operatorname{Skew}(r,\mathbb{C}),\operatorname{Sym}(r,\mathbb{C}))&(\textit{Case }4),\\
(M(2s,\mathbb{C}),\operatorname{Sym}(2s,\mathbb{C}),\operatorname{Skew}(2s,\mathbb{C}))&(\textit{Case }5),\\
\big({\rm Herm}(3,\mathbb{O})^{\mathbb{C}},M(2,6;\mathbb{C}),\operatorname{Skew}(6,\mathbb{C})\big)&(\textit{Case }6).\end{cases}

(8.4)

Then the corresponding symmetric pairs are

(G,G_{1})=\begin{cases}({\rm SO}_{0}(2,n),{\rm SO}_{0}(2,n^{\prime})\times{\rm SO}(n^{\prime\prime}))&(\textit{Case }1),\\
({\rm SO}_{0}(2,n),{\rm SO}_{0}(2,n-1))&(\textit{Case }1^{\prime}),\\
(\operatorname{Sp}(2r^{\prime},\mathbb{R}),\operatorname{Sp}(r^{\prime},\mathbb{R})\times\operatorname{Sp}(r^{\prime},\mathbb{R}))&(\textit{Case }2),\\
({\rm SO}^{*}(4s^{\prime}),{\rm SO}^{*}(2s^{\prime})\times{\rm SO}^{*}(2s^{\prime}))&(\textit{Case }3),\\
({\rm SU}(r,r),{\rm SO}^{*}(2r))&(\textit{Case }4),\\
({\rm SU}(2s,2s),\operatorname{Sp}(2s,\mathbb{R}))&(\textit{Case }5),\\
(E_{7(-25)},{\rm SU}(2,6))&(\textit{Case }6)\end{cases}

(up to covering). Let
\dim\mathfrak{p}^{+}=:n
,
\dim\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}=:n_{2}
,
\operatorname{rank}\mathfrak{p}^{+}=:r
,
\operatorname{rank}\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}=:r_{2}
,
let
d
,

,

be the numbers defined in (
2.3
) for
\mathfrak{p}^{+}
,
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1}
,
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}
respectively, and take
\varepsilon_{1},\varepsilon_{2}\in\{1,2\}
such that

(x_{j}|\overline{y_{j}})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}=\varepsilon_{j}(x_{j}|\overline{y_{j}})_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{j}},\qquad x_{j},y_{j}\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{j},\qquad j=1,2.

Also let
\delta_{1}
,
\delta_{2}
be the numbers defined by

\delta_{j}:=\begin{cases}0&\big(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{j}\colon\text{not simple}\big),\\
d_{j}&\big(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{j}\colon\text{simple},\;\varepsilon_{j}=1\big),\\
-1&\big(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{j}\colon\text{simple},\;\varepsilon_{j}=2\big),\end{cases}\qquad j=1,2.

Then we have

(r,r_{2},d,d_{2},\varepsilon_{1},\varepsilon_{2},\delta_{1},\delta_{2})=\begin{cases}(2,2,n-2,n^{\prime\prime}-2,1,1,n^{\prime}-2,n^{\prime\prime}-2)&(\textit{Case }1,\;n^{\prime}\geq 2),\\
(2,2,n-2,n^{\prime\prime}-2,2,1,-1,n^{\prime\prime}-2)&(\textit{Case }1,\;n^{\prime}=1),\\
(2,1,n-2,-,1,2,n-3,-1)&(\textit{Case }1^{\prime}),\\
(2r^{\prime},r^{\prime},1,2,1,2,0,-1)&(\textit{Case }2),\\
(s^{\prime},s^{\prime},4,2,1,1,0,2)&(\textit{Case }3),\\
(r,r,2,1,2,1,-1,1)&(\textit{Case }4),\\
(2s,s,2,4,1,2,1,-1)&(\textit{Case }5),\\
(3,3,8,4,1,1,2,4)&(\textit{Case }6).\end{cases}

We note that if
r_{2}=1
, then
d_{2}
is not determined uniquely, and any number is allowed.
Let
\Sigma_{2}\subset\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}
be the Bergman–Shilov boundary of the bounded symmetric domain, with the normalized

-invariant measure,
so that
\Sigma_{2}\simeq K_{1}/K_{L_{2}}
holds for some

. For these cases,
for
(\rho_{0},W)\simeq\mathcal{P}_{(k+l,\underline{k}_{r_{2}-1})}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\big)=\mathcal{P}_{(l,0,\dots,0)}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2})\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(x_{2})^{k}
(
\varepsilon_{2}=1
) or
for
(\rho_{0},W)\simeq\mathcal{P}_{(\underline{k+1}_{l},\underline{k}_{r_{2}-l})}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\big)=\mathcal{P}_{\underline{1}_{l}}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\big)\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(x_{2})^{k}

(
\varepsilon_{2}=2
),

F^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,\rho_{0}}(z)=F^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,l}(z)
is computable by using Theorem
6.3
.

Theorem 8.5
.

Let
\big(\mathfrak{p}^{\pm},\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{1},\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{2}\big)
be as in
(\ref{list_simple})
.
For
l\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}

(\varepsilon_{2}=1)
or

(\varepsilon_{2}=2)

be the representation of

isomorphic to
\mathcal{P}_{(l,0,\dots,0)}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\big)

(\varepsilon_{2}=1)

\mathcal{P}_{\underline{1}_{l}}(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2})

(\varepsilon_{2}=2)
, and
we take a vector-valued polynomial
\mathrm{K}_{l}(x_{2})\in\bigl(\mathcal{P}(\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{2})\otimes V_{l}\bigr)^{K_{1}}
such that

\big\|(v,\mathrm{K}_{l}(\overline{x_{2}}))_{V_{l}}\big\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{2}),x_{2}}^{2}=|v|_{V_{l}}^{2},\qquad v\in V_{l}.

For
\lambda>\frac{d}{2}(r-1)
,

,
we define
F^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,l}(z)=F^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,l}(z_{1},z_{2})\in\mathcal{P}(\mathfrak{p}^{-},V_{l})
by

F^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,l}(z):=\begin{cases}\displaystyle\frac{1}{\big(\frac{n_{2}}{r_{2}}\big)_{(k+l,\underline{k}_{r_{2}-1}),d_{2}}}F_{1}^{l}\left(\begin{matrix}-k\\
-\lambda-2k+\frac{n}{r}\end{matrix};\mathrm{K}_{l};z\right)\!,&\varepsilon_{2}=1,\\[18.0pt]
\displaystyle\frac{2^{kr_{2}+l}}{\big(\frac{n_{2}}{r_{2}}\big)_{(\underline{k+1}_{l},\underline{k}_{r_{2}-l}),d_{2}}}F_{2}^{l}\left(\begin{matrix}-\frac{k}{2}\\
-\lambda-k+\frac{n}{r}\end{matrix};\mathrm{K}_{l};z\right)\!,&\varepsilon_{2}=2,\end{cases}

by using
F_{\varepsilon_{2}}^{l}\left(\begin{smallmatrix}\nu\\
\mu\end{smallmatrix};f;z\right)
in (
6.7
), (
6.8
).
Then the linear map

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,l}\colon\left\{\hskip-4.0pt\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}(D_{{\rm SO}_{0}(2,n)})&\longrightarrow\mathcal{H}_{\lambda+2k+l}\big(D_{{\rm SO}_{0}(2,n^{\prime})}\big)\boxtimes V_{(l,0,\dots,0)}^{[n^{\prime\prime}]\vee}&(\textit{Case }1),\\
\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}(D_{{\rm SO}_{0}(2,n)})&\longrightarrow\mathcal{H}_{\lambda+k}\big(D_{{\rm SO}_{0}(2,n-1)}\big)&(\textit{Case }1^{\prime}),\\

&\hskip 29.0pt{}\mathbin{\hat{\boxtimes}}\mathcal{H}_{\lambda+k}\big(D_{\operatorname{Sp}(r^{\prime},\mathbb{R})},V_{\underline{1}_{l}}^{(r^{\prime})\vee}\big)&(\textit{Case }2),\\
\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}(D_{{\rm SO}^{*}(4s^{\prime})})&\longrightarrow\mathcal{H}_{\lambda+2k}\big(D_{{\rm SO}^{*}(2s^{\prime})},V_{(l,0,\dots,0)}^{(s^{\prime})\vee}\big)\\
&\hskip 29.0pt{}\mathbin{\hat{\boxtimes}}\mathcal{H}_{\lambda+2k}\big(D_{{\rm SO}^{*}(2s^{\prime})},V_{(l,0,\dots,0)}^{(s^{\prime})\vee}\big)&(\textit{Case }3),\\
\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}(D_{{\rm SU}(r,r)})&\longrightarrow\mathcal{H}_{2\lambda+4k}\big(D_{{\rm SO}^{*}(2r)},V_{(2l,0,\dots,0)}^{(r)\vee}\big)&(\textit{Case }4),\\
\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}(D_{{\rm SU}(2s,2s)})&\longrightarrow\mathcal{H}_{\lambda+k}\big(D_{\operatorname{Sp}(2s,\mathbb{R})},V_{\underline{1}_{2l}}^{(2s)\vee}\big)&(\textit{Case }5),\\
\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}(D_{E_{7(-25)}})&\longrightarrow\mathcal{H}_{\lambda+2k}\big(D_{{\rm SU}(2,6)},\mathbb{C}\boxtimes V_{(l,l,l,l,0,0)}^{(6)}\big)&(\textit{Case }6),\end{array}\right.

\displaystyle\big(\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,l}f\big)(x_{1})=\left.F^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,l}\left(\frac{\partial}{\partial x}\right)f(x)\right|_{x_{2}=0}

intertwines the
\widetilde{G}_{1}
-action, and its operator norm is given by

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,l}\|^{-2}_{\mathrm{op}}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\begin{cases}\displaystyle\frac{\big(\frac{n_{2}}{r_{2}}\big)_{(k+l,\underline{k}_{r_{2}-1}),d_{2}}\big(\lambda+k-\frac{d}{4}r_{2}+\frac{d_{2}}{2}+(l,\underline{0}_{r_{2}/2-1})\big)_{\underline{k}_{r_{2}/2},d}}{(\lambda)_{(2k+l,\underline{2k}_{r_{2}/2-1},\underline{k}_{r_{2}/2}),d}},&\hskip-45.0pt\varepsilon_{2}=1,\;r_{2}\colon\text{even},\\
\displaystyle\frac{\big(\frac{n_{2}}{r_{2}}\big)_{(k+l,\underline{k}_{r_{2}-1}),d_{2}}\big(\lambda\!-\!\frac{d}{2}\big\lfloor\frac{r_{2}}{2}\big\rfloor\!+\!\max\{2k,k+l\}\big)_{\min\{k,l\}}\big(\lambda\!+\!k\!-\!\frac{d}{2}\big\lceil\frac{r_{2}}{2}\big\rceil\!+\!\frac{d_{2}}{2}\big)_{\underline{k}_{\lfloor r_{2}/2\rfloor},d}}{(\lambda)_{(2k+l,\underline{2k}_{\lfloor r_{2}/2\rfloor-1},\min\{2k,k+l\},\underline{k}_{\lfloor r_{2}/2\rfloor}),d}},\hskip-55.0pt&\\
&\hskip-40.0pt\varepsilon_{2}=1,\;r_{2}\colon\text{odd},\\
\displaystyle\frac{\big(\frac{n_{2}}{r_{2}}\big)_{(\underline{k+1}_{l},\underline{k}_{r_{2}-l}),d_{2}}\big(\lambda\!-\!\frac{d}{2}r_{2}\!+\!\big(\underline{\big\lfloor\frac{k}{2}\big\rfloor\!+\!\frac{1}{2}}_{l},\underline{\big\lceil\frac{k}{2}\big\rceil\!-\!\frac{1}{2}}_{r_{2}-l}\big)\big)_{(\underline{\lceil k/2\rceil}_{l},\underline{\lfloor k/2\rfloor}_{r_{2}-l}),d}}{2^{kr_{2}+l}(\lambda)_{(\underline{k+1}_{l},\underline{k}_{r_{2}-l},\underline{\lceil k/2\rceil}_{l},\underline{\lfloor k/2\rfloor}_{r_{2}-l}),d}},&\varepsilon_{2}=2.\end{cases}

For holographic operators
\mathcal{F}^{\uparrow}_{\lambda,k,l}
, see
[

, Theorems 5.7, 5.12, 5.17 and 5.19]
.
Especially if

, then as in Corollary

, the intertwining operators become easier.
Here
{}_{0}F_{1}^{\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{1},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}}
