Theorem 8.6
.

Let
\big(\mathfrak{p}^{\pm},\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{1},\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{2}\big)
be as in (
8.4
). For
\lambda>\frac{d}{2}(r-1)
,
k\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
,
we define
F^{\uparrow}_{\lambda,k,0}(x_{2};z_{1})\in\mathcal{O}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}\times\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{1}\big)
by

F^{\uparrow}_{\lambda,k,0}(x_{2};z_{1}):=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(x_{2})^{k}{}_{0}F_{1}^{\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{1},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}}\bigg(\lambda+\frac{2k}{\varepsilon_{2}}-\frac{\delta_{1}}{2};z_{1},x_{2}\bigg),

and
F^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,0}(z)=F^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,0}(z_{1},z_{2})\in\mathcal{P}(\mathfrak{p}^{-})
by

F^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,0}(z):=\frac{\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{2}}(z_{2})^{k}}{\big(\frac{n_{2}}{r_{2}}\big)_{\underline{k}_{r_{2}},d_{2}}}\times\begin{cases}\displaystyle{}_{2}F_{1}^{\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{1},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}}\left(\begin{matrix}-k,-k-\frac{d_{2}}{2}\\
-\lambda-2k+\frac{n}{r}-\frac{d_{2}}{2}\end{matrix};z_{1},{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{2}^{-\mathit{1}}\right)\!,&\varepsilon_{2}=1,\\
\displaystyle 2^{kr_{2}}{}_{2}F_{1}^{\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{1},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}}\left(\begin{matrix}-\frac{k}{2},-\frac{k-1}{2}\\
-\lambda-k+\frac{n}{r}+\frac{1}{2}\end{matrix};z_{1},{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{2}^{-\mathit{1}}\right)\!,&\varepsilon_{2}=2.\end{cases}

Then the linear map

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}^{\uparrow}_{\lambda,k,0}\colon\ \left\{\hskip-4.0pt\begin{array}[]{lll}\bigl(\mathcal{H}_{\lambda+2k}\big(D_{{\rm SO}_{0}(2,n^{\prime})}\big)\boxtimes\mathbb{C}\bigr)_{\widetilde{K}_{1}}&\longrightarrow\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}\big(D_{{\rm SO}_{0}(2,n)}\big)_{\widetilde{K}}&(\text{Case }1),\\
\mathcal{H}_{\lambda+k}\big(D_{{\rm SO}_{0}(2,n-1)}\big)_{\widetilde{K}_{1}}&\longrightarrow\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}\big(D_{{\rm SO}_{0}(2,n)}\big)_{\widetilde{K}}&(\text{Case }1^{\prime}),\\
\bigl(\mathcal{H}_{\lambda+k}\big(D_{{\rm Sp}(r^{\prime},\mathbb{R})}\big)\mathbin{\hat{\boxtimes}}\mathcal{H}_{\lambda+k}\big(D_{{\rm Sp}(r^{\prime},\mathbb{R})}\big)\bigr)_{\widetilde{K}_{1}}&\longrightarrow\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}\big(D_{{\rm Sp}(2r^{\prime},\mathbb{R})}\big)_{\widetilde{K}}&(\text{Case }2),\\
\bigl(\mathcal{H}_{\lambda+2k}\big(D_{{\rm SO}^{*}(2s^{\prime})}\big)\mathbin{\hat{\boxtimes}}\mathcal{H}_{\lambda+2k}\big(D_{{\rm SO}^{*}(2s^{\prime})}\big)\bigr)_{\widetilde{K}_{1}}&\longrightarrow\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}\big(D_{{\rm SO}^{*}(4s^{\prime})}\big)_{\widetilde{K}}&(\text{Case }3),\\
\mathcal{H}_{2\lambda+4k}\big(D_{{\rm SO}^{*}(2r)}\big)_{\widetilde{K}_{1}}&\longrightarrow\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}\big(D_{{\rm SU}(r,r)}\big)_{\widetilde{K}}&(\text{Case }4),\\
\mathcal{H}_{\lambda+k}\big(D_{{\rm Sp}(2s,\mathbb{R})}\big)_{\widetilde{K}_{1}}&\longrightarrow\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}\big(D_{{\rm SU}(2s,2s)}\big)_{\widetilde{K}}&(\text{Case }5),\\
\mathcal{H}_{\lambda+2k}\big(D_{{\rm SU}(2,6)}\big)_{\widetilde{K}_{1}}&\longrightarrow\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}\big(D_{E_{7(-25)}}\big)_{\widetilde{K}}&(\text{Case }6),\end{array}\right.

\displaystyle\big(\mathcal{F}^{\uparrow}_{\lambda,k,0}f\big)(x)=F^{\uparrow}_{\lambda,k,0}\bigg(x_{2};\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{1}}\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{1}}\bigg)f(x_{1})

intertwines the
\big(\mathfrak{g}_{1},\widetilde{K}_{1}\big)
-action, and the linear map

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,0}\colon\ \left\{\hskip-4.0pt\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}\big(D_{{\rm SO}_{0}(2,n)}\big)&\longrightarrow\mathcal{H}_{\lambda+2k}\big(D_{{\rm SO}_{0}(2,n^{\prime})}\big)\boxtimes\mathbb{C}&(\text{Case }1),\\
\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}\big(D_{{\rm SO}_{0}(2,n)}\big)&\longrightarrow\mathcal{H}_{\lambda+k}\big(D_{{\rm SO}_{0}(2,n-1)}\big)&(\text{Case }1^{\prime}),\\
\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}\big(D_{{\rm Sp}(2r^{\prime},\mathbb{R})}\big)&\longrightarrow\mathcal{H}_{\lambda+k}\big(D_{{\rm Sp}(r^{\prime},\mathbb{R})}\big)\mathbin{\hat{\boxtimes}}\mathcal{H}_{\lambda+k}\big(D_{{\rm Sp}(r^{\prime},\mathbb{R})}\big)&(\text{Case }2),\\
\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}\big(D_{{\rm SO}^{*}(4s^{\prime})}\big)&\longrightarrow\mathcal{H}_{\lambda+2k}\big(D_{{\rm SO}^{*}(2s^{\prime})}\big)\mathbin{\hat{\boxtimes}}\mathcal{H}_{\lambda+2k}\big(D_{{\rm SO}^{*}(2s^{\prime})}\big)&(\text{Case }3),\\
\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}\big(D_{{\rm SU}(r,r)}\big)&\longrightarrow\mathcal{H}_{2\lambda+4k}\big(D_{{\rm SO}^{*}(2r)}\big)&(\text{Case }4),\\
\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}\big(D_{{\rm SU}(2s,2s)}\big)&\longrightarrow\mathcal{H}_{\lambda+k}\big(D_{{\rm Sp}(2s,\mathbb{R})}\big)&(\text{Case }5),\\
\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}\big(D_{E_{7(-25)}}\big)&\longrightarrow\mathcal{H}_{\lambda+2k}\big(D_{{\rm SU}(2,6)}\big)&(\text{Case }6),\end{array}\right.

\displaystyle\big(\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,0}f\big)(x_{1})=\left.F^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,0}\left(\frac{\partial}{\partial x}\right)f(x)\right|_{x_{2}=0}

intertwines the
\widetilde{G}_{1}
-action. Their operator norms are given by

\big\|\mathcal{F}^{\uparrow}_{\lambda,k,0}\big\|^{2}_{\mathrm{op}}=\big\|\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,0}\big\|^{-2}_{\mathrm{op}}=\begin{cases}\displaystyle\frac{\big(\frac{n_{2}}{r_{2}}\big)_{\underline{k}_{r_{2}},d_{2}}\big(\lambda+k-\frac{d}{2}\big\lceil\frac{r_{2}}{2}\big\rceil+\frac{d_{2}}{2}\big)_{\underline{k}_{\lfloor r_{2}/2\rfloor},d}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{2k}_{\lfloor r_{2}/2\rfloor},d}\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}\big\lfloor\frac{r_{2}}{2}\big\rfloor\big)_{\underline{k}_{\lceil r_{2}/2\rceil},d}},&\varepsilon_{2}=1,\\
\displaystyle\frac{\big(\frac{n_{2}}{r_{2}}\big)_{\underline{k}_{r_{2}},d_{2}}\big(\lambda+\big\lceil\frac{k}{2}\big\rceil-\frac{d}{2}r_{2}-\frac{1}{2}\big)_{\underline{\lfloor k/2\rfloor}_{r_{2}},d}}{2^{kr_{2}}(\lambda)_{\underline{k}_{r_{2}},d}\big(\lambda-\frac{d}{2}r_{2}\big)_{\underline{\lfloor k/2\rfloor}_{r_{2}},d}},&\varepsilon_{2}=2.\end{cases}

Here we normalize
\frac{\partial}{\partial x}
and
\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{1}}
with respect to the bilinear forms

(\cdot|\cdot)_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}\colon\mathfrak{p}^{+}\times\mathfrak{p}^{-}\to\mathbb{C}
and
(\cdot|\cdot)_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1}}\colon\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1}\times\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{1}\to\mathbb{C}
respectively,
so that
\frac{\partial}{\partial x}\bigr|_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1}}=\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{1}}\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{1}}
holds.

For
Cases
2, 3, the above results are generalized to the cases
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}=M(r^{\prime},r^{\prime\prime};\mathbb{C})
,
M(s^{\prime},s^{\prime\prime};\mathbb{C})
are of non-tube type,
as in Theorem
6.8
(2), (3). Here
{}_{0}F_{1}^{(1)}
,
{}_{0}F_{1}^{[4]}
,
{}_{2}F_{1}^{(1)}
and
{}_{2}F_{1}^{[4]}
are as in
(
3.4
)–(
3.7
), and
\mathcal{P}_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime}}}(M(r^{\prime},r^{\prime\prime};\mathbb{C}))_{R}
is the representation of
{\rm GL}(r^{\prime\prime},\mathbb{C})
given in (
8.2
)
with the Fischer inner product.

Theorem 8.7
.

Let

\big(\mathfrak{p}^{\pm},\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{11},\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{12},\mathfrak{p}^{\pm}_{22}\big)=\begin{cases}({\rm Sym}(r,\mathbb{C}),\operatorname{Sym}(r^{\prime},\mathbb{C}),M(r^{\prime},r^{\prime\prime};\mathbb{C}),\operatorname{Sym}(r^{\prime\prime},\mathbb{C}))&(\textit{Case }2),\\
({\rm Skew}(s,\mathbb{C}),\operatorname{Skew}(s^{\prime},\mathbb{C}),M(s^{\prime},s^{\prime\prime};\mathbb{C}),\operatorname{Skew}(s^{\prime\prime},\mathbb{C}))&(\textit{Case }3),\end{cases}

with
r=r^{\prime}+r^{\prime\prime}
,
r^{\prime}\leq r^{\prime\prime}
or
s=s^{\prime}+s^{\prime\prime}
,
s^{\prime}\leq s^{\prime\prime}
respectively.
For
\lambda>\frac{r-1}{2}
or
\lambda>2\big(\big\lfloor\frac{s}{2}\big\rfloor-1\big)
respectively and for
k\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
, we define

F^{\uparrow}_{\lambda,k,0}(x_{12};z_{11},z_{22},y_{12})\in\mathcal{P}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{12}\times\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{11}\times\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{22}\times\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{12}\big)
by

F^{\uparrow}_{\lambda,k,0}(x_{12};z_{11},z_{22},y_{12}):=\begin{cases}\displaystyle\frac{\operatorname{det}(x_{12}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0pty_{12})^{k}}{(r^{\prime})_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime}},2}}{}_{0}F_{1}^{(1)}\big(\lambda+k;z_{11}x_{12}z_{22}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptx_{12}\big)&(\textit{Case }2),\\[8.61108pt]
\displaystyle\frac{\operatorname{det}(x_{12}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0pty_{12})^{k}}{(s^{\prime})_{\underline{k}_{s^{\prime}},2}}{}_{0}F_{1}^{[4]}\big(\lambda+2k;-z_{11}x_{12}z_{22}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptx_{12}\big)&(\textit{Case }3),\end{cases}

and
F^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,0}(z;w_{12})=F^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,0}\left(\left(\begin{smallmatrix}z_{11}&z_{12}\\
\pm{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{12}&z_{22}\end{smallmatrix}\right);w_{12}\right)\in\mathcal{P}\big(\mathfrak{p}^{-}\times\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{12}\big)
by

\displaystyle F^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,0}(z;w_{12})

\displaystyle\qquad:=\begin{cases}\displaystyle\frac{2^{kr^{\prime}}\operatorname{det}(z_{12}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{12})^{k}}{(r^{\prime})_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime}},2}}{}_{2}F_{1}^{(1)}\!\left(\begin{matrix}-\frac{k}{2},-\frac{k-1}{2}\\
-\lambda\!-\!k\!+\!r^{\prime}\!+\!1\end{matrix};z_{11}(w_{12}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{12})^{-1}w_{12}z_{22}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{12}(z_{12}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{12})^{-1}\right)\\
\hskip 330.0pt(\textit{Case }2),\\
\displaystyle\frac{\operatorname{det}(z_{12}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{12})^{k}}{(s^{\prime})_{\underline{k}_{s^{\prime}},2}}{}_{2}F_{1}^{[4]}\!\left(\begin{matrix}-k,-k-1\\
-\lambda\!-\!2k\!+\!2s^{\prime}\!-\!2\end{matrix};-z_{11}(w_{12}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{12})^{-1}w_{12}z_{22}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{12}(z_{12}{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptw_{12})^{-1}\right)\\
\hskip 330.0pt(\textit{Case }3).\end{cases}\!\!\!

Then the linear maps

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}^{\uparrow}_{\lambda,k,0}\colon\begin{cases}\bigl(\mathcal{H}_{\lambda+k}(D_{\operatorname{Sp}(r^{\prime},\mathbb{R})})\mathbin{\hat{\boxtimes}}\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}(D_{\operatorname{Sp}(r^{\prime\prime},\mathbb{R})},\mathcal{P}_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime}}}(M(r^{\prime},r^{\prime\prime};\mathbb{C}))_{R})\bigr)_{\widetilde{K}_{1}}\longrightarrow\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}(D_{\operatorname{Sp}(r,\mathbb{R})})_{\widetilde{K}}\\
\hskip 337.0pt(\textit{Case }2),\hskip-12.0pt\\
\bigl(\mathcal{H}_{\lambda+2k}(D_{{\rm SO}^{*}(2s^{\prime})})\mathbin{\hat{\boxtimes}}\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}(D_{{\rm SO}^{*}(2s^{\prime\prime})},\mathcal{P}_{\underline{k}_{s^{\prime}}}(M(s^{\prime},s^{\prime\prime};\mathbb{C}))_{R})\bigr)_{\widetilde{K}_{1}}\longrightarrow\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}(D_{{\rm SO}^{*}(2s)})_{\widetilde{K}}\hskip-10.0pt\\
\hskip 337.0pt(\textit{Case }3),\hskip-12.0pt\end{cases}

\displaystyle\big(\mathcal{F}^{\uparrow}_{\lambda,k,0}f\big)(x)=F^{\uparrow}_{\lambda,k,0}\bigg(x_{12};\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{11}},\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{22}},\frac{\partial}{\partial w_{12}}\bigg)f(x_{11},x_{22},w_{12})\bigg|_{w_{12}=0}

intertwine the
(\mathfrak{sp}(r^{\prime},\mathbb{R})\oplus\mathfrak{sp}(r^{\prime\prime},\mathbb{R}),\widetilde{{\rm U}}(r^{\prime})\times\widetilde{{\rm U}}(r^{\prime\prime}))
- or

(\mathfrak{so}^{*}(2s^{\prime})\oplus\mathfrak{so}^{*}(2s^{\prime\prime}),\widetilde{{\rm U}}(s^{\prime})\times\widetilde{{\rm U}}(s^{\prime\prime}))
-action respectively,
and the linear maps

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,0}\colon\ \begin{cases}\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}(D_{\operatorname{Sp}(r,\mathbb{R})})\longrightarrow\mathcal{H}_{\lambda+k}(D_{\operatorname{Sp}(r^{\prime},\mathbb{R})})\mathbin{\hat{\boxtimes}}\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}(D_{\operatorname{Sp}(r^{\prime\prime},\mathbb{R})},\mathcal{P}_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime}}}(M(r^{\prime},r^{\prime\prime};\mathbb{C}))_{R})\\
\hskip 310.0pt(\textit{Case }2),\\
\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}(D_{{\rm SO}^{*}(2s)})\longrightarrow\mathcal{H}_{\lambda+2k}(D_{{\rm SO}^{*}(2s^{\prime})})\mathbin{\hat{\boxtimes}}\mathcal{H}_{\lambda}(D_{{\rm SO}^{*}(2s^{\prime\prime})},\mathcal{P}_{\underline{k}_{s^{\prime}}}(M(s^{\prime},s^{\prime\prime};\mathbb{C}))_{R})\\
\hskip 310.0pt(\textit{Case }3),\end{cases}

\displaystyle\big(\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,0}f\big)(x_{11},x_{22},w_{12})=F^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,0}\bigg(\frac{\partial}{\partial x};w_{12}\bigg)f(x)\bigg|_{x_{12}=0}

intertwine the
\widetilde{\operatorname{Sp}}(r^{\prime},\mathbb{R})\times\widetilde{\operatorname{Sp}}(r^{\prime\prime},\mathbb{R})
- or

\widetilde{{\rm SO}^{*}}(2s^{\prime})\times\widetilde{{\rm SO}^{*}}(2s^{\prime\prime})
-action respectively. Their operator norms are given by

\big\|\mathcal{F}^{\uparrow}_{\lambda,k,0}\big\|^{2}_{\mathrm{op}}=\big\|\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,0}\big\|^{-2}_{\mathrm{op}}=\begin{cases}\displaystyle\frac{\big(\lambda+\big\lceil\frac{k}{2}\big\rceil-\frac{r^{\prime}+1}{2}\big)_{\underline{\lfloor k/2\rfloor}_{r^{\prime}},1}}{2^{kr^{\prime}}(\lambda)_{\underline{k}_{r^{\prime}},1}\big(\lambda-\frac{r^{\prime}}{2}\big)_{\underline{\lfloor k/2\rfloor}_{r^{\prime}},1}}&(\textit{Case }2),\\[8.61108pt]
\displaystyle\frac{\big(\lambda+k-2\big\lceil\frac{s^{\prime}}{2}\big\rceil+1\big)_{\underline{k}_{\lfloor s^{\prime}/2\rfloor},4}}{(\lambda)_{\underline{2k}_{\lfloor s^{\prime}/2\rfloor},4}\big(\lambda-2\big\lfloor\frac{s^{\prime}}{2}\big\rfloor\big)_{\underline{k}_{\lceil s^{\prime}/2\rceil},4}}&(\textit{Case }3).\end{cases}

Except for the results on operator norms, Theorems
8.5
,
8.6
and
8.7
hold
without the assumption
\lambda>\frac{d}{2}(r-1)
,
if
\lambda
is not a pole and if we replace
\mathcal{H}
with
\mathcal{O}
.

Remark 8.8
.

For
Case

1^{\prime}
, since
F^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,0}(z)
is written as

\displaystyle F^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,0}(z)

\displaystyle=\frac{2^{k}}{k!}(z_{2})^{k}{}_{2}F_{1}\left(\begin{matrix}-\frac{k}{2},-\frac{k-1}{2}\\
-\lambda-k+\frac{n}{2}+\frac{1}{2}\end{matrix};-\frac{q(z_{1})}{(z_{2})^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{2^{k}}{k!}(z_{2})^{k}\sum_{m=0}^{\lfloor k/2\rfloor}\frac{(-k)_{2m}}{\left(-\lambda-k+\frac{n-1}{2}+1\right)_{m}m!}\left(-\frac{q(z_{1})}{(2z_{2})^{2}}\right)^{m}

\displaystyle=\frac{(-q(z_{1}))^{k/2}}{\left(\lambda-\frac{n-1}{2}\right)_{k}}\sum_{m=0}^{\lfloor k/2\rfloor}\frac{(-1)^{m}\left(\lambda-\frac{n-1}{2}\right)_{k-m}}{(k-2m)!m!}\bigg(\frac{2z_{2}}{\sqrt{-q(z_{1})}}\bigg)^{k-2m}

\displaystyle=\frac{(-q(z_{1}))^{k/2}}{\left(\lambda-\frac{n-1}{2}\right)_{k}}C_{k}^{\lambda-\frac{n-1}{2}}\bigg(\frac{2z_{2}}{\sqrt{-q(z_{1})}}\bigg)

by using the Gegenbauer polynomial
C_{k}^{\alpha}(t)
, the symmetry breaking operator
\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,0}
coincides with
the holomorphic version of Juhl’s operator (see
[
17
]
,
[
25
, Theorem 6.3]
) up to constant multiple.
Similarly, for
Case

2
with
r^{\prime}=1
, since
F^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,0}(z;w_{12})
is again written by using the Gegenbauer polynomial

C^{\lambda-1}_{k}(t)
, the symmetry breaking operator
\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,0}
coincides with the result by Kobayashi–Pevzner

[
25
, Theorem 7.1]
up to constant multiple. Moreover, for
Case

2
with
r^{\prime}=r^{\prime\prime}
, combining with Proposition
2.6
(1),

F^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,0}(z)
coincides with the result by Ibukiyama–Kuzumaki–Ochiai
[
13
, Theorem 7.5]
up to constant multiple,
and
\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,0}
coincides with the differential operator in
[
13
]
which is related to the Siegel modular forms.
In addition, similar functions for
Cases

1
and
1^{\prime}
also appear in the restriction of complementary series representations to some subgroups
as inclusion maps from discrete summands, since these maps are given by the Fourier transforms of the symmetry breaking operators
(Kobayashi–Speh
[
27
, Chapter 15]
, Möllers–Oshima
[
31
]
).

We return to the case
\mathfrak{p}^{+}
,
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}
are of tube type.
When
\varepsilon_{2}=1
, if
\lambda=\frac{n}{r}-a
with
a=1,2,\dots,k
,
then by (
6.10
) we have

\displaystyle\bigg(\frac{n_{2}}{r_{2}}\bigg)_{(k+l,\underline{k}_{r_{2}-1}),d_{2}}F^{\downarrow}_{\frac{n}{r}-a,k,l}(z)

\displaystyle=F_{1}^{l}\left(\begin{matrix}-k\\
a-2k\end{matrix};\mathrm{K}_{l};z\right)=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}}(z)^{a}F_{1}^{l}\left(\begin{matrix}a-k\\
a-2k\end{matrix};\mathrm{K}_{l};z\right)

\displaystyle=\bigg(\frac{n_{2}}{r_{2}}\bigg)_{(k-a+l,\underline{k-a}_{r_{2}-1}),d_{2}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}}(z)^{a}F^{\downarrow}_{\frac{n}{r}+a,k-a,l}(z),

and when
\varepsilon_{2}=2
, if
\lambda=\frac{n}{r}-a
with
a=1,2,\dots,\left\lfloor\frac{k}{2}\right\rfloor
, then we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2^{kr_{2}+l}}\bigg(\frac{n_{2}}{r_{2}}\bigg)_{(\underline{k+1}_{l},\underline{k}_{r_{2}-l}),d_{2}}F^{\downarrow}_{\frac{n}{r}-a,k,l}(z)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=F_{2}^{l}\left(\begin{matrix}-\frac{k}{2}\\
a-k\end{matrix};\mathrm{K}_{l};z\right)=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}}(z)^{a}F_{2}^{l}\left(\begin{matrix}a-\frac{k}{2}\\
a-k\end{matrix};\mathrm{K}_{l};z\right)

\displaystyle\qquad=\frac{1}{2^{(k-2a)r_{2}+l}}\bigg(\frac{n_{2}}{r_{2}}\bigg)_{(\underline{k-2a+1}_{l},\underline{k-2a}_{r_{2}-l}),d_{2}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}}(z)^{a}F^{\downarrow}_{\frac{n}{r}+a,k-2a,l}(z).

Therefore we have

F^{\downarrow}_{\frac{n}{r}-a,k,l}(z)=\begin{cases}\displaystyle\frac{(-1)^{ar_{2}}}{(-k-(\underline{0}_{r_{2}-1},l))_{\underline{a}_{r_{2}},d_{2}}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}}(z)^{a}F^{\downarrow}_{\frac{n}{r}+a,k-a,l}(z),&\varepsilon_{2}=1,\\
\displaystyle\frac{2^{2ar_{2}}}{(-k-(\underline{0}_{r_{2}-l},\underline{1}_{l}))_{\underline{2a}_{r_{2}},d_{2}}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}}(z)^{a}F^{\downarrow}_{\frac{n}{r}+a,k-2a,l}(z),&\varepsilon_{2}=2.\end{cases}

Next we assume
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1}
is also of tube type, so that
r
is even. Let
\dim\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1}=:n_{1}
,
\operatorname{rank}\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1}=:r_{1}
,
and let
\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{1}}(z_{1})
be the polynomial on
\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{1}
satisfying

t^{r/2}h_{\mathfrak{p}^{+}}\big(Q(x_{2})z_{1},-t^{-1}z_{1}\big)^{1/2}\big|_{t=0}=\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{1}}(z_{1})^{\varepsilon_{1}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{+}_{2}}(x_{2})^{\varepsilon_{2}},\qquad x_{2}\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2},\quad z_{1}\in\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{1}.

Suppose
l=0
, and for
a=1,2,\dots,\lfloor k/\varepsilon_{2}\rfloor
we consider

\lim_{\lambda\to\frac{n_{1}}{\varepsilon_{1}r_{1}}-\frac{2k}{\varepsilon_{2}}+a}\bigg({-}\lambda-\frac{2k}{\varepsilon_{2}}+\frac{n}{r}-\frac{\delta_{2}}{2}\bigg)_{\underline{a}_{r/2},d}F^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,0}(z).

By case-by-case consideration, we have

\frac{n}{r}-\frac{\delta_{2}}{2}-\bigg(\frac{d}{2}\bigg(\frac{r}{2}-1\bigg)+1\bigg)=\frac{d}{4}r-\frac{\delta_{2}}{2}=\frac{n_{1}}{\varepsilon_{1}r_{1}}.

Therefore by Proposition
2.6
(2) we have

\displaystyle\lim_{\lambda\to\frac{n_{1}}{\varepsilon_{1}r_{1}}-\frac{2k}{\varepsilon_{2}}+a}\bigg({-}\lambda-\frac{2k}{\varepsilon_{2}}+\frac{n}{r}-\frac{\delta_{2}}{2}\bigg)_{\underline{a}_{r/2},d}F^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,0}(z)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\lim_{\lambda\to\frac{n_{1}}{\varepsilon_{1}r_{1}}-\frac{2k}{\varepsilon_{2}}+a}\frac{\varepsilon_{2}^{kr_{2}}\big({-}\lambda-\frac{2k}{\varepsilon_{2}}+\frac{n}{r}-\frac{\delta_{2}}{2}\big)_{\underline{a}_{r/2},d}}{\big(\frac{n_{2}}{r_{2}}\big)_{\underline{k}_{r_{2}},d_{2}}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{2}}(z_{2})^{k}

\displaystyle\qquad\hphantom{=}{}\ \times{}_{2}F_{1}^{\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{1},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}}\left(\begin{matrix}-\frac{k}{\varepsilon_{2}},-\frac{k}{\varepsilon_{2}}-\frac{\delta_{2}}{2}\\
-\lambda-\frac{2k}{\varepsilon_{2}}+\frac{n}{r}-\frac{\delta_{2}}{2}\end{matrix};z_{1},{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{2}^{-\mathit{1}}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{\varepsilon_{2}^{kr_{2}}\big({-}\frac{k}{\varepsilon_{2}}\big)_{\underline{a}_{r/2},d}\big({-}\frac{k}{\varepsilon_{2}}-\frac{\delta_{2}}{2}\big)_{\underline{a}_{r/2},d}}{\big(\frac{n_{2}}{r_{2}}\big)_{\underline{k}_{r_{2}},d_{2}}\big(\frac{d}{2}\big(\frac{r}{2}-1\big)+1\big)_{\underline{a}_{r/2},d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{2}}(z_{2})^{k}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{1}}(z_{1})^{\varepsilon_{1}a}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{2}}(z_{2})^{-\varepsilon_{2}a}

\displaystyle\qquad\hphantom{=}{}\ \times{}_{2}F_{1}^{\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{1},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}}\left(\begin{matrix}-\frac{k}{\varepsilon_{2}}+a,-\frac{k}{\varepsilon_{2}}+a-\frac{\delta_{2}}{2}\\[2.0pt]
\frac{d}{2}\big(\frac{r}{2}-1\big)+1+a\end{matrix};z_{1},{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{2}^{-\mathit{1}}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{\varepsilon_{2}^{(k-2a)r_{2}}(-k)_{\underline{\varepsilon_{2}a}_{r_{2}},d_{2}}}{\big(\frac{n_{2}}{r_{2}}\big)_{\underline{k}_{r_{2}},d_{2}}\big(\frac{d}{2}\big(\frac{r}{2}-1\big)+1\big)_{\underline{a}_{r/2},d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{1}}(z_{1})^{\varepsilon_{1}a}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{2}}(z_{2})^{k-\varepsilon_{2}a}

\displaystyle\qquad\hphantom{=}{}\ \times{}_{2}F_{1}^{\mathfrak{p}^{-}_{1},\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}}\left(\begin{matrix}-\frac{k}{\varepsilon_{2}}+a,-\frac{k}{\varepsilon_{2}}+a-\frac{\delta_{2}}{2}\\
a-\frac{n_{1}}{\varepsilon_{1}r_{1}}+\frac{n}{r}-\frac{\delta_{2}}{2}\end{matrix};z_{1},{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{2}^{-\mathit{1}}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{(-k)_{\underline{\varepsilon_{2}a}_{r_{2}},d_{2}}\big(\frac{n_{2}}{r_{2}}\big)_{\underline{k-\varepsilon_{2}a}_{r_{2}},d_{2}}}{\big(\frac{n_{2}}{r_{2}}\big)_{\underline{k}_{r_{2}},d_{2}}\big(\frac{d}{2}\big(\frac{r}{2}-1\big)+1\big)_{\underline{a}_{r/2},d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{1}}(z_{1})^{\varepsilon_{1}a}F^{\downarrow}_{\frac{n_{1}}{\varepsilon_{1}r_{1}}-\frac{2k}{\varepsilon_{2}}+a,k-\varepsilon_{2}a,0}(z)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{1}{\left(\frac{d}{2}\left(\frac{r}{2}-1\right)+1\right)_{\underline{a}_{r/2},d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{1}}(z_{1})^{\varepsilon_{1}a}F^{\downarrow}_{\frac{n_{1}}{\varepsilon_{1}r_{1}}-\frac{2k}{\varepsilon_{2}}+a,k-\varepsilon_{2}a,0}(z).

Similarly, we consider the case
\varepsilon_{2}=1
and
r=2
, with general
l
.
Then
\frac{n}{2}-\frac{d_{2}}{2}-1=\frac{n_{1}}{2}
holds, and for
a=1,2,\dots,k
we have

\displaystyle\lim_{\lambda\to\frac{n_{1}}{2}-2k-l+a}\bigg({-}\lambda-2k-l+\frac{n}{2}-\frac{d_{2}}{2}\bigg)_{a}F^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,l}(z)

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\lim_{\lambda\to\frac{n_{1}}{2}-2k-l+a}\frac{\big({-}\lambda-2k-l+\frac{n}{2}-\frac{d_{2}}{2}\big)_{a}}{\big(\frac{n_{2}}{2}\big)_{(k+l,k),d_{2}}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{2}}(z_{2})^{k}

\displaystyle\qquad\hphantom{=}{}\ \times{}_{2}F_{1}\!\left(\begin{matrix}-k,-k-l-\frac{d_{2}}{2}\\
-\lambda-2k-l+\frac{n}{2}-\frac{d_{2}}{2}\end{matrix};\frac{\big(z_{1}|Q(z_{2})^{-1}z_{1}\big)_{\mathfrak{p}^{-}}}{2}\right)\mathrm{K}_{l}(z_{2})

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{(-k)_{a}\big({-}k-l-\frac{d_{2}}{2}\big)_{a}}{\big(\frac{n_{2}}{2}\big)_{k+l}(1)_{k}a!}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{2}}(z_{2})^{k}

\displaystyle\qquad\hphantom{=}{}\ \times\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{1}}(z_{1})^{\varepsilon_{1}a}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{2}}(z_{2})^{-a}{}_{2}F_{1}\!\left(\!\begin{matrix}-k+a,-k-l+a-\frac{d_{2}}{2}\\
a+1\end{matrix};\frac{\big(z_{1}|Q(z_{2})^{-1}z_{1}\big)_{\mathfrak{p}^{-}}}{2}\!\right)\!\mathrm{K}_{l}(z_{2})

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{1}{\left(\frac{n_{2}}{2}\right)_{k+l-a}(1)_{k-a}a!}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{1}}(z_{1})^{\varepsilon_{1}a}

\displaystyle\qquad\hphantom{=}{}\ \times\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{2}}(z_{2})^{k-a}{}_{2}F_{1}\!\left(\begin{matrix}-k+a,-k-l+a-\frac{d_{2}}{2}\\
a-\frac{n_{1}}{2}+\frac{n}{2}-\frac{d_{2}}{2}\end{matrix};\frac{\big(z_{1}|Q(z_{2})^{-1}z_{1}\big)_{\mathfrak{p}^{-}}}{2}\right)\mathrm{K}_{l}(z_{2})

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{1}{a!}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{1}}(z_{1})^{\varepsilon_{1}a}F^{\downarrow}_{\frac{n_{1}}{2}-2k-l+a,k-a,l}(z).

Therefore the following holds.

Theorem 8.9
.

1.

Assume
\mathfrak{p}^{+}
,
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}
are of tube type. If
\lambda=\frac{n}{r}-a
with

a=1,2,\dots,\lfloor k/\varepsilon_{2}\rfloor
, then we have

\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}_{\frac{n}{r}-a,k,l}=\begin{cases}\displaystyle\frac{(-1)^{ar_{2}}}{(-k-(\underline{0}_{r_{2}-1},l))_{\underline{a}_{r_{2}},d_{2}}}\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}_{\frac{n}{r}+a,k-a,l}\circ\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}}\bigg(\frac{\partial}{\partial x}\bigg)^{a},&\varepsilon_{2}=1,\\[8.61108pt]
\displaystyle\frac{2^{2ar_{2}}}{(-k-(\underline{0}_{r_{2}-l},\underline{1}_{l}))_{\underline{2a}_{r_{2}},d_{2}}}\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}_{\frac{n}{r}+a,k-2a,l}\circ\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}}\bigg(\frac{\partial}{\partial x}\bigg)^{a},&\varepsilon_{2}=2.\end{cases}

2.

Assume
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1}
is also of tube type, and let
l=0
. Then at
\lambda=\frac{n_{1}}{\varepsilon_{1}r_{1}}-\frac{2k}{\varepsilon_{2}}+a

with
a=1,2,\dots,\lfloor k/\varepsilon_{2}\rfloor
, we have

\displaystyle\lim_{\lambda\to\frac{n_{1}}{\varepsilon_{1}r_{1}}-\frac{2k}{\varepsilon_{2}}+a}\left(-\lambda-\frac{2k}{\varepsilon_{2}}+\frac{n}{r}-\frac{\delta_{2}}{2}\right)_{\underline{a}_{r/2},d}\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,0}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{1}{\big(\frac{d}{2}\big(\frac{r}{2}-1\big)+1\big)_{\underline{a}_{r/2},d}}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{1}}\bigg(\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{1}}\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{1}}\bigg)^{\varepsilon_{1}a}\circ\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}_{\frac{n_{1}}{\varepsilon_{1}r_{1}}-\frac{2k}{\varepsilon_{2}}+a,k-\varepsilon_{2}a,0}.

3.

Again assume
\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{1}
is also of tube type, and
\varepsilon_{2}=1
,
r=2
. Then at
\lambda=\frac{n_{1}}{2}-2k-l+a

with
a=1,2,\dots,k
, we have

\displaystyle\lim_{\lambda\to\frac{n_{1}}{2}-2k-l+a}\bigg({-}\lambda-2k-l+\frac{n}{2}-\frac{d_{2}}{2}\bigg)_{a}\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}_{\lambda,k,l}

\displaystyle\qquad{}=\frac{1}{a!}\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{1}}\bigg(\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{1}}\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{1}}\bigg)^{\varepsilon_{1}a}\circ\mathcal{F}^{\downarrow}_{\frac{n_{1}}{2}-2k-l+a,k-a,l}.

This theorem is again regarded as an analogue of the functional identities
[
27
, Corollaries 12.7(2) and 12.8(3)]
.
Here, for individual cases, when we take a maximal tripotent
e\in\mathfrak{p}^{+}_{2}
suitably,
\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}}(z)
and
\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}_{1}^{-}}(z_{1})
are given by

\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}}(z)=q(z),\qquad\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{1}}(z_{1})^{\varepsilon_{1}}=-q(z_{1})

for
Cases
1 and
1^{\prime}
,

\displaystyle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}}\begin{pmatrix}z_{11}&z_{12}\\
{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{12}&z_{22}\end{pmatrix}=\operatorname{det}\left(\begin{pmatrix}z_{11}&z_{12}\\
{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{12}&z_{22}\end{pmatrix}\begin{pmatrix}0&I\\
I&0\end{pmatrix}\right)=(-1)^{r^{\prime}}\operatorname{det}\begin{pmatrix}z_{11}&z_{12}\\
{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{12}&z_{22}\end{pmatrix}\!,

\displaystyle\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{1}}\begin{pmatrix}z_{11}&0\\
0&z_{22}\end{pmatrix}=\operatorname{det}(z_{11})\operatorname{det}(z_{22})

for
Case
2,

\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}}\begin{pmatrix}z_{11}&z_{12}\\
-{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{12}&z_{22}\end{pmatrix}=\operatorname{Pf}\begin{pmatrix}z_{11}&z_{12}\\
-{}^{t}\hskip-1.0ptz_{12}&z_{22}\end{pmatrix}\!,\qquad\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{1}}\begin{pmatrix}z_{11}&0\\
0&z_{22}\end{pmatrix}=\operatorname{Pf}(z_{11})\operatorname{Pf}(z_{22})

for
Case
3 (with
s^{\prime}=2r^{\prime}
for
\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{1}}(z_{1})
),

\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}}(z_{1}+z_{2})=\operatorname{det}(z_{1}+z_{2}),\qquad\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{1}}(z_{1})=\sqrt{-1}^{s}\operatorname{Pf}(z_{1})

for
Case
4 (with
r=2s
for
\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{1}}(z_{1})
),

\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}}(z_{1}+z_{2})=\operatorname{det}(z_{1}+z_{2}),\qquad\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}_{1}}(z_{1})=(-1)^{s}\operatorname{det}(z_{1})

for
Case
5, and

\operatorname{det}_{\mathfrak{n}^{-}}((z_{1},z_{2}))=\operatorname{det}((z_{1},z_{2}))

under the identification (
3.8
) for
Case
6. Again by
[
1
, Theorem 6.4]
, (
8.3
) intertwines the
\widetilde{G}
-action if
\mathfrak{p}^{+}
is of tube type,
and hence the both sides of Theorem
8.9
(1)–(3) intertwine the
\widetilde{G}_{1}
-action.
